::: section
## [1. ]{.secno}[Motivations]{.content}[](#motivations){.self-link} {#motivations .heading .settled level="1"}

*This section is non-normative.*

Web Applications traditionally assume that the network is reachable.
This assumption pervades the platform. HTML documents are loaded over
HTTP and traditionally fetch all of their sub-resources via subsequent
HTTP requests. This places web content at a disadvantage versus other
technology stacks.

The [service worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker
link-type="dfn"} is designed first to redress this balance by providing
a Web Worker context, which can be started by a runtime when navigations
are about to occur. This event-driven worker is registered against an
origin and a path (or pattern), meaning it can be consulted when
navigations occur to that location. Events that correspond to network
requests are dispatched to the worker and the responses generated by the
worker may override default network stack behavior. This puts the
[service worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker①
link-type="dfn"}, conceptually, between the network and a document
renderer, allowing the [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker②
link-type="dfn"} to provide content for documents, even while offline.

Web developers familiar with previous attempts to solve the offline
problem have reported a deficit of flexibility in those solutions. As a
result, the [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker③
link-type="dfn"} is highly procedural, providing a maximum of
flexibility at the price of additional complexity for developers. Part
of this complexity arises from the need to keep [service
workers](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker④
link-type="dfn"} responsive in the face of a single-threaded execution
model. As a result, APIs exposed by [service
workers](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑤
link-type="dfn"} are almost entirely asynchronous, a pattern familiar in
other JavaScript contexts but accentuated here by the need to avoid
blocking document and resource loading.

Developers using the [HTML5 Application
Cache](https://www.w3.org/TR/2014/REC-html5-20141028/browsers.html#appcache)
have also [reported that several
attributes](https://alistapart.com/article/application-cache-is-a-douchebag/)
of the design contribute to [unrecoverable
errors](https://alistapart.com/article/application-cache-is-a-douchebag/#section6).
A key design principle of the [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑥
link-type="dfn"} is that errors should *always* be recoverable. Many
details of the update process of [service
workers](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑦
link-type="dfn"} are designed to avoid these hazards.

[Service workers](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑧
link-type="dfn"} are started and kept alive by their relationship to
events, not documents. This design borrows heavily from developer and
vendor experience with [shared
workers](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/workers.html#shared-workers-and-the-sharedworker-interface)
and [Chrome Background
Pages](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/extensions/mv2/background-pages).
A key lesson from these systems is the necessity to time-limit the
execution of background processing contexts, both to conserve resources
and to ensure that background context loss and restart is top-of-mind
for developers. As a result, [service
workers](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑨
link-type="dfn"} bear more than a passing resemblance to [Chrome Event
Pages](https://developer.chrome.com/docs/apps/event_pages), the
successor to Background Pages. [Service
workers](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker①⓪
link-type="dfn"} may be started by user agents *without an attached
document* and may be killed by the user agent at nearly any time.
Conceptually, [service
workers](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker①①
link-type="dfn"} can be thought of as Shared Workers that can start,
process events, and die without ever handling messages from documents.
Developers are advised to keep in mind that [service
workers](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker①②
link-type="dfn"} may be started and killed many times a second.

[Service workers](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker①③
link-type="dfn"} are generic, event-driven, time-limited script contexts
that run at an origin. These properties make them natural endpoints for
a range of runtime services that may outlive the context of a particular
document, e.g. handling push notifications, background data
synchronization, responding to resource requests from other origins, or
receiving centralized updates to expensive-to-calculate data (e.g.,
geolocation or gyroscope).
:::

:::::::::::::::: section
## [2. ]{.secno}[Model]{.content}[](#model){.self-link} {#model .heading .settled level="2"}

::::: section
### [2.1. ]{.secno}[Service Worker]{.content}[](#service-worker-concept){.self-link} {#service-worker-concept .heading .settled level="2.1"}

A [service worker]{#dfn-service-worker .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-for=""
dfn-type="dfn" export=""} is a type of [web
worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/workers.html#workers){#ref-for-workers
link-type="dfn"}. A [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker①④
link-type="dfn"} executes in the registering [service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client
link-type="dfn"}'s
[origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#concept-origin){#ref-for-concept-origin
link-type="dfn"}.

A [service worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker①⑤
link-type="dfn"} has an associated [state]{#dfn-state .dfn .dfn-paneled
dfn-for="service worker" dfn-type="dfn" export=""}, which is one of
\"`parsed`\", \"`installing`\", \"`installed`\", \"`activating`\",
\"`activated`\", and \"`redundant`\". It is initially \"`parsed`\".

A [service worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker①⑥
link-type="dfn"} has an associated [script url]{#dfn-script-url .dfn
.dfn-paneled dfn-for="service worker" dfn-type="dfn" export=""} (a
[URL](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url){#ref-for-concept-url
link-type="dfn"}).

A [service worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker①⑦
link-type="dfn"} has an associated [type]{#dfn-type .dfn .dfn-paneled
dfn-for="service worker" dfn-type="dfn" export=""} which is either
\"`classic`\" or \"`module`\". Unless stated otherwise, it is
\"`classic`\".

A [service worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker①⑧
link-type="dfn"} has an associated [containing service worker
registration]{#dfn-containing-service-worker-registration .dfn
.dfn-paneled dfn-for="service worker" dfn-type="dfn" export=""
lt="registration|containing service worker registration"} (a [service
worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration
link-type="dfn"}), which contains itself.

A [service worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker①⑨
link-type="dfn"} has an associated [global
object]{#dfn-service-worker-global-object .dfn .dfn-paneled
dfn-for="service worker" dfn-type="dfn" export=""} (a
[`ServiceWorkerGlobalScope`{.idl}](#serviceworkerglobalscope){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope
link-type="idl"} object or null).

A [service worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker②⓪
link-type="dfn"} has an associated [script
resource]{#dfn-script-resource .dfn .dfn-paneled
dfn-for="service worker" dfn-type="dfn" export=""} (a
[script](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-script){#ref-for-concept-script
link-type="dfn"}), which represents its own script resource. It is
initially set to null.

A [script resource](#dfn-script-resource){#ref-for-dfn-script-resource
link-type="dfn"} has an associated [has ever been evaluated
flag]{#dfn-has-ever-been-evaluated-flag .dfn .dfn-paneled
dfn-for="script resource" dfn-type="dfn" export=""}. It is initially
unset.

A [script resource](#dfn-script-resource){#ref-for-dfn-script-resource①
link-type="dfn"} has an associated [policy
container]{#dfn-policy-container .dfn .dfn-paneled
dfn-for="script resource" dfn-type="dfn" export=""} (a [policy
container](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#policy-container){#ref-for-policy-container
link-type="dfn"}). It is initially a new policy container.

A [service worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker②①
link-type="dfn"} has an associated [script resource
map]{#dfn-script-resource-map .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-for="service worker"
dfn-type="dfn" export=""} which is an [ordered
map](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ordered-map){#ref-for-ordered-map
link-type="dfn"} where the keys are
[URLs](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url){#ref-for-concept-url①
link-type="dfn"} and the values are
[responses](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response){#ref-for-concept-response
link-type="dfn"}.

A [service worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker②②
link-type="dfn"} has an associated [set of used
scripts]{#dfn-set-of-used-scripts .dfn .dfn-paneled
dfn-for="service worker" dfn-type="dfn" export=""} (a
[set](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ordered-set){#ref-for-ordered-set
link-type="dfn"}) whose
[item](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-item){#ref-for-list-item
link-type="dfn"} is a
[URL](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url){#ref-for-concept-url②
link-type="dfn"}. It is initially a new
[set](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ordered-set){#ref-for-ordered-set①
link-type="dfn"}.

[Note:]{.marker} The [set of used
scripts](#dfn-set-of-used-scripts){#ref-for-dfn-set-of-used-scripts
link-type="dfn"} is only used to prune unused resources from a new
worker's map after installation, that were populated based on the old
worker's map during the update check.

A [service worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker②③
link-type="dfn"} has an associated [skip waiting
flag]{#dfn-skip-waiting-flag .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-for="service worker"
dfn-type="dfn" export=""}. Unless stated otherwise it is unset.

A [service worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker②④
link-type="dfn"} has an associated [classic scripts imported
flag]{#dfn-classic-scripts-imported-flag .dfn .dfn-paneled
dfn-for="service worker" dfn-type="dfn" export=""}. It is initially
unset.

A [service worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker②⑤
link-type="dfn"} has an associated [set of event types to
handle]{#dfn-set-of-event-types-to-handle .dfn .dfn-paneled
dfn-for="service worker" dfn-type="dfn" export=""} (a
[set](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ordered-set){#ref-for-ordered-set②
link-type="dfn"}) whose
[item](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-item){#ref-for-list-item①
link-type="dfn"} is an [event
listener](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event-listener){#ref-for-concept-event-listener
link-type="dfn"}'s event type. It is initially a new
[set](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ordered-set){#ref-for-ordered-set③
link-type="dfn"}.

A [service worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker②⑥
link-type="dfn"} has an associated [set of extended
events]{#dfn-set-of-extended-events .dfn .dfn-paneled
dfn-for="service worker" dfn-type="dfn" export=""} (a
[set](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ordered-set){#ref-for-ordered-set④
link-type="dfn"}) whose
[item](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-item){#ref-for-list-item②
link-type="dfn"} is an
[`ExtendableEvent`{.idl}](#extendableevent){#ref-for-extendableevent
link-type="idl"}. It is initially a new
[set](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ordered-set){#ref-for-ordered-set⑤
link-type="dfn"}.

A [service worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker②⑦
link-type="dfn"} has an associated [start
status]{#service-worker-start-status .dfn .dfn-paneled
dfn-for="service worker" dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""} which can be null
or a
[Completion](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-completion-record-specification-type){#ref-for-sec-completion-record-specification-type
link-type="dfn"}. It is initially null.

A [service worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker②⑧
link-type="dfn"} has an associated [all fetch listeners are empty
flag]{#service-worker-all-fetch-listeners-are-empty-flag .dfn
.dfn-paneled dfn-for="service worker" dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}. It is
initially unset.

A [service worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker②⑨
link-type="dfn"} has an associated [list of router
rules]{#service-worker-list-of-router-rules .dfn .dfn-paneled
dfn-for="service worker" dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""} (a
[list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list){#ref-for-list
link-type="dfn"} of
[`RouterRule`{.idl}](#dictdef-routerrule){#ref-for-dictdef-routerrule
link-type="idl"}s). It is initially an empty
[list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list){#ref-for-list①
link-type="dfn"}.

A [service worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker③⓪
link-type="dfn"} is said to be [running]{#service-worker-running .dfn
.dfn-paneled dfn-for="service worker" dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""} if its
[event
loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-loop){#ref-for-event-loop
link-type="dfn"} is running.

A [service worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker③①
link-type="dfn"} has an associated [\[\[service worker
queue\]\]]{#service-worker-service-worker-queue .dfn .dfn-paneled
dfn-for="service worker" dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""} (a [parallel
queue](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#parallel-queue){#ref-for-parallel-queue
link-type="dfn"}).

::: section
#### [2.1.1. ]{.secno}[Lifetime]{.content}[](#service-worker-lifetime){.self-link} {#service-worker-lifetime .heading .settled level="2.1.1"}

The lifetime of a [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker③②
link-type="dfn"} is tied to the execution lifetime of events and not
references held by [service worker
clients](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client①
link-type="dfn"} to the
[`ServiceWorker`{.idl}](#serviceworker){#ref-for-serviceworker
link-type="idl"} object.

A user agent *may*
[terminate](#terminate-service-worker){#ref-for-terminate-service-worker
link-type="dfn"} [service
workers](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker③③
link-type="dfn"} at any time it:

- Has no event to handle.

- Detects abnormal operation: such as infinite loops and tasks exceeding
  imposed time limits (if any) while handling the events.
:::

::: section
#### [2.1.2. ]{.secno}[Events]{.content}[](#service-worker-events){.self-link} {#service-worker-events .heading .settled level="2.1.2"}

The Service Workers specification defines [service worker
events]{#dfn-service-worker-events .dfn .dfn-paneled
dfn-for="service worker" dfn-type="dfn" export=""} (each of which is an
[event](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event){#ref-for-concept-event
link-type="dfn"}) that include (see the
[list](#execution-context-events)):

- [Lifecycle events]{#dfn-lifecycle-events .dfn .dfn-paneled
  dfn-for="service worker" dfn-type="dfn" export=""}:
  [`install`{.idl}](#service-worker-global-scope-install-event){#ref-for-service-worker-global-scope-install-event
  .idl-code link-type="event"} and
  [`activate`{.idl}](#service-worker-global-scope-activate-event){#ref-for-service-worker-global-scope-activate-event
  .idl-code link-type="event"}.

- [Functional events]{#dfn-functional-events .dfn .dfn-paneled
  dfn-for="service worker" dfn-type="dfn" export=""}:
  [`fetch`{.idl}](#service-worker-global-scope-fetch-event){#ref-for-service-worker-global-scope-fetch-event
  .idl-code link-type="event"} and the
  [events](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event){#ref-for-concept-event①
  link-type="dfn"} defined by other specifications that
  [extend](#extensibility) the Service Workers specification. (See the
  [list](#execution-context-events).)

- [`message`{.idl}](#eventdef-serviceworkerglobalscope-message){#ref-for-eventdef-serviceworkerglobalscope-message
  .idl-code link-type="event"} and
  [`messageerror`{.idl}](#eventdef-serviceworkerglobalscope-messageerror){#ref-for-eventdef-serviceworkerglobalscope-messageerror
  .idl-code link-type="event"}.
:::
:::::

:::: section
### [2.2. ]{.secno}[Service Worker Timing]{.content}[](#service-worker-timing){.self-link} {#service-worker-timing .heading .settled level="2.2"}

Service workers mark certain points in time that are later exposed by
the [navigation
timing](https://w3c.github.io/navigation-timing/#dom-performancenavigationtiming){#ref-for-dom-performancenavigationtiming
.idl-code link-type="interface"} API and [resource
timing](https://w3c.github.io/resource-timing/#dom-performanceresourcetiming){#ref-for-dom-performanceresourcetiming
.idl-code link-type="interface"} API.

A [service worker timing info]{#service-worker-timing-info .dfn
.dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" export=""} is a
[struct](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct){#ref-for-struct
link-type="dfn"}. It has the following
[items](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct-item){#ref-for-struct-item
link-type="dfn"}:

::: section

[start time]{#service-worker-timing-info-start-time .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-for="service worker timing info" dfn-type="dfn" export=""}

:   A
    [`DOMHighResTimeStamp`{.idl}](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp){#ref-for-dom-domhighrestimestamp
    link-type="idl"}, initially 0.

[fetch event dispatch time]{#service-worker-timing-info-fetch-event-dispatch-time .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-for="service worker timing info" dfn-type="dfn" export=""}

:   A
    [`DOMHighResTimeStamp`{.idl}](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp){#ref-for-dom-domhighrestimestamp①
    link-type="idl"}, initially 0.

[worker router evaluation start]{#service-worker-timing-info-worker-router-evaluation-start .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-for="service worker timing info" dfn-type="dfn" export=""}

:   A
    [`DOMHighResTimeStamp`{.idl}](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp){#ref-for-dom-domhighrestimestamp②
    link-type="idl"}, initially 0.

[worker cache lookup start]{#service-worker-timing-info-worker-cache-lookup-start .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-for="service worker timing info" dfn-type="dfn" export=""}

:   A
    [`DOMHighResTimeStamp`{.idl}](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp){#ref-for-dom-domhighrestimestamp③
    link-type="idl"}, initially 0.

[worker matched router source]{#service-worker-timing-info-worker-matched-router-source .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-for="service worker timing info" dfn-type="dfn" export=""}

:   A
    [`DOMString`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMString){#ref-for-idl-DOMString
    link-type="idl"}, initially an empty string.

[worker final router source]{#service-worker-timing-info-worker-final-router-source .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-for="service worker timing info" dfn-type="dfn" export=""}

:   A
    [`DOMString`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMString){#ref-for-idl-DOMString①
    link-type="idl"}, initially an empty string.
:::
::::

:::: section
### [2.3. ]{.secno}[Service Worker Registration]{.content}[](#service-worker-registration-concept){.self-link} {#service-worker-registration-concept .heading .settled level="2.3"}

A [service worker registration]{#dfn-service-worker-registration .dfn
.dfn-paneled dfn-for="" dfn-type="dfn" export=""} is a tuple of a [scope
url](#dfn-scope-url){#ref-for-dfn-scope-url link-type="dfn"}, a [storage
key](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#storage-key){#ref-for-storage-key
link-type="dfn"}, and a set of [service
workers](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker③④
link-type="dfn"}, an [installing
worker](#dfn-installing-worker){#ref-for-dfn-installing-worker
link-type="dfn"}, a [waiting
worker](#dfn-waiting-worker){#ref-for-dfn-waiting-worker
link-type="dfn"}, and an [active
worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker link-type="dfn"}.
A user agent *may* enable many [service worker
registrations](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration①
link-type="dfn"} at a single origin so long as the [scope
url](#dfn-scope-url){#ref-for-dfn-scope-url① link-type="dfn"} of the
[service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration②
link-type="dfn"} differs. A [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration③
link-type="dfn"} of an identical [scope
url](#dfn-scope-url){#ref-for-dfn-scope-url② link-type="dfn"} when one
already exists in the user agent causes the existing [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration④
link-type="dfn"} to be replaced.

A [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration⑤
link-type="dfn"} has an associated [storage
key]{#service-worker-registration-storage-key .dfn .dfn-paneled
dfn-for="service worker registration" dfn-type="dfn" export=""} (a
[storage
key](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#storage-key){#ref-for-storage-key①
link-type="dfn"}).

A [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration⑥
link-type="dfn"} has an associated [scope url]{#dfn-scope-url .dfn
.dfn-paneled dfn-for="service worker registration" dfn-type="dfn"
export=""} (a
[URL](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url){#ref-for-concept-url③
link-type="dfn"}).

A [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration⑦
link-type="dfn"} has an associated [installing
worker]{#dfn-installing-worker .dfn .dfn-paneled
dfn-for="service worker registration" dfn-type="dfn" export=""} (a
[service worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker③⑤
link-type="dfn"} or null) whose [state](#dfn-state){#ref-for-dfn-state
link-type="dfn"} is \"`installing`\". It is initially set to null.

A [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration⑧
link-type="dfn"} has an associated [waiting worker]{#dfn-waiting-worker
.dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-for="service worker registration" dfn-type="dfn"
export=""} (a [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker③⑥
link-type="dfn"} or null) whose [state](#dfn-state){#ref-for-dfn-state①
link-type="dfn"} is \"`installed`\". It is initially set to null.

A [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration⑨
link-type="dfn"} has an associated [active worker]{#dfn-active-worker
.dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-for="service worker registration" dfn-type="dfn"
export=""} (a [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker③⑦
link-type="dfn"} or null) whose [state](#dfn-state){#ref-for-dfn-state②
link-type="dfn"} is either \"`activating`\" or \"`activated`\". It is
initially set to null.

A [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration①⓪
link-type="dfn"} has an associated [last update check
time]{#dfn-last-update-check-time .dfn .dfn-paneled
dfn-for="service worker registration" dfn-type="dfn" export=""}. It is
initially set to null.

A [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration①①
link-type="dfn"} is said to be
[stale]{#service-worker-registration-stale .dfn .dfn-paneled
dfn-for="service worker registration" dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""} if the
registration's [last update check
time](#dfn-last-update-check-time){#ref-for-dfn-last-update-check-time
link-type="dfn"} is non-null and the time difference in seconds
calculated by the current time minus the registration's [last update
check
time](#dfn-last-update-check-time){#ref-for-dfn-last-update-check-time①
link-type="dfn"} is greater than 86400.

A [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration①②
link-type="dfn"} has an associated [update via cache
mode]{#dfn-update-via-cache .dfn .dfn-paneled
dfn-for="service worker registration" dfn-type="dfn" export=""}, which
is \"`imports`\", \"`all`\", or \"`none`\". It is initially set to
\"`imports`\".

A [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration①③
link-type="dfn"} has one or more [task
queues]{#dfn-service-worker-registration-task-queue .dfn .dfn-paneled
dfn-for="service worker registration" dfn-type="dfn" export=""} that
back up the
[tasks](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-task){#ref-for-concept-task
link-type="dfn"} from its [active
worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker①
link-type="dfn"}'s [event
loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-loop){#ref-for-event-loop①
link-type="dfn"}'s corresponding [task
queues](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#task-queue){#ref-for-task-queue
link-type="dfn"}. (The target task sources for this back up operation
are the [handle fetch task
source](#dfn-handle-fetch-task-source){#ref-for-dfn-handle-fetch-task-source
link-type="dfn"} and the [handle functional event task
source](#dfn-handle-functional-event-task-source){#ref-for-dfn-handle-functional-event-task-source
link-type="dfn"}.) The user agent dumps the [active
worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker②
link-type="dfn"}'s
[tasks](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-task){#ref-for-concept-task①
link-type="dfn"} to the [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration①④
link-type="dfn"}'s [task
queues](#dfn-service-worker-registration-task-queue){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration-task-queue
link-type="dfn"} when the [active
worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker③ link-type="dfn"}
is
[terminated](#terminate-service-worker){#ref-for-terminate-service-worker①
link-type="dfn"} and [re-queues those
tasks](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task){#ref-for-queue-a-task
link-type="dfn"} to the [active
worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker④
link-type="dfn"}'s [event
loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-loop){#ref-for-event-loop②
link-type="dfn"}'s corresponding [task
queues](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#task-queue){#ref-for-task-queue①
link-type="dfn"} when the [active
worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker⑤ link-type="dfn"}
spins off. Unlike the [task
queues](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#task-queue){#ref-for-task-queue②
link-type="dfn"} owned by [event
loops](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-loop){#ref-for-event-loop③
link-type="dfn"}, the [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration①⑤
link-type="dfn"}'s [task
queues](#dfn-service-worker-registration-task-queue){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration-task-queue①
link-type="dfn"} are not processed by any [event
loops](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-loop){#ref-for-event-loop④
link-type="dfn"} in and of itself.

A [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration①⑥
link-type="dfn"} has an associated
[[`NavigationPreloadManager`{.idl}](#navigationpreloadmanager){#ref-for-navigationpreloadmanager
link-type="idl"}]{#service-worker-registration-navigationpreloadmanager
.dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-for="service worker registration" dfn-type="dfn"
export=""} object.

A [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration①⑦
link-type="dfn"} has an associated [navigation preload enabled
flag]{#service-worker-registration-navigation-preload-enabled-flag .dfn
.dfn-paneled dfn-for="service worker registration" dfn-type="dfn"
export=""}. It is initially unset.

A [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration①⑧
link-type="dfn"} has an associated [navigation preload header
value]{#service-worker-registration-navigation-preload-header-value .dfn
.dfn-paneled dfn-for="service worker registration" dfn-type="dfn"
export=""}, which is a [byte
sequence](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#byte-sequence){#ref-for-byte-sequence
link-type="dfn"}. It is initially set to \``true`\`.

A [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration①⑨
link-type="dfn"} is said to be
[unregistered]{#dfn-service-worker-registration-unregistered .dfn
.dfn-paneled dfn-for="service worker registration" dfn-type="dfn"
export=""} if [registration
map](#dfn-scope-to-registration-map){#ref-for-dfn-scope-to-registration-map
link-type="dfn"}\[this [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration②⓪
link-type="dfn"}'s ([storage
key](#service-worker-registration-storage-key){#ref-for-service-worker-registration-storage-key
link-type="dfn"},
[serialized](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-serializer){#ref-for-concept-url-serializer
link-type="dfn"} [scope url](#dfn-scope-url){#ref-for-dfn-scope-url③
link-type="dfn"})\] is not this [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration②①
link-type="dfn"}.

::: section
#### [2.3.1. ]{.secno}[Lifetime]{.content}[](#service-worker-registration-lifetime){.self-link} {#service-worker-registration-lifetime .heading .settled level="2.3.1"}

A user agent *must* persistently keep a list of
[registered](#register){#ref-for-register link-type="dfn"} [service
worker
registrations](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration②②
link-type="dfn"} unless otherwise they are explicitly
[unregistered](#dfn-service-worker-registration-unregistered){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration-unregistered
link-type="dfn"}. A user agent has a [registration
map](#dfn-scope-to-registration-map){#ref-for-dfn-scope-to-registration-map①
link-type="dfn"} that stores the entries of the tuple of [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration②③
link-type="dfn"}'s ([storage
key](#service-worker-registration-storage-key){#ref-for-service-worker-registration-storage-key①
link-type="dfn"},
[serialized](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-serializer){#ref-for-concept-url-serializer①
link-type="dfn"} [scope url](#dfn-scope-url){#ref-for-dfn-scope-url④
link-type="dfn"}) and the corresponding [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration②④
link-type="dfn"}. The lifetime of [service worker
registrations](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration②⑤
link-type="dfn"} is beyond that of the
[`ServiceWorkerRegistration`{.idl}](#serviceworkerregistration){#ref-for-serviceworkerregistration
link-type="idl"} objects which represent them within the lifetime of
their corresponding [service worker
clients](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client②
link-type="dfn"}.
:::
::::

::: section
### [2.4. ]{.secno}[Service Worker Client]{.content}[](#service-worker-client-concept){.self-link} {#service-worker-client-concept .heading .settled level="2.4"}

A [service worker client]{#dfn-service-worker-client .dfn .dfn-paneled
dfn-for="" dfn-type="dfn" export=""} is an
[environment](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment){#ref-for-environment
link-type="dfn"}.

A [service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client③
link-type="dfn"} has an associated [discarded
flag]{#service-worker-client-discarded-flag .dfn .dfn-paneled
dfn-for="service worker client" dfn-type="dfn" export=""}. It is
initially unset.

Each [service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client④
link-type="dfn"} has the following [environment discarding
steps](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-discarding-steps){#ref-for-environment-discarding-steps
link-type="dfn"}:

1.  Set `client`{.variable}'s [discarded
    flag](#service-worker-client-discarded-flag){#ref-for-service-worker-client-discarded-flag
    link-type="dfn"}.

[Note:]{.marker} Implementations can discard clients whose [discarded
flag](#service-worker-client-discarded-flag){#ref-for-service-worker-client-discarded-flag①
link-type="dfn"} is set.

A [service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client⑤
link-type="dfn"} has an algorithm defined as the
[origin]{#service-worker-client-origin .dfn .dfn-paneled
dfn-for="service worker client" dfn-type="dfn" export=""} that returns
the [service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client⑥
link-type="dfn"}'s
[origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-origin){#ref-for-concept-settings-object-origin
link-type="dfn"} if the [service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client⑦
link-type="dfn"} is an [environment settings
object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object){#ref-for-environment-settings-object
link-type="dfn"}, and the [service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client⑧
link-type="dfn"}'s [creation
URL](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-creation-url){#ref-for-concept-environment-creation-url
link-type="dfn"}'s
[origin](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-origin){#ref-for-concept-url-origin
link-type="dfn"} otherwise.

A [window client]{#dfn-window-client .dfn .dfn-paneled
dfn-for="service worker client" dfn-type="dfn" export=""} is a [service
worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client⑨
link-type="dfn"} whose [global
object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-global){#ref-for-concept-settings-object-global
link-type="dfn"} is a
[`Window`{.idl}](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#window){#ref-for-window
link-type="idl"} object.

A [dedicated worker client]{#dfn-dedicatedworker-client .dfn
.dfn-paneled dfn-for="service worker client" dfn-type="dfn" export=""}
is a [service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client①⓪
link-type="dfn"} whose [global
object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-global){#ref-for-concept-settings-object-global①
link-type="dfn"} is a
[`DedicatedWorkerGlobalScope`{.idl}](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/workers.html#dedicatedworkerglobalscope){#ref-for-dedicatedworkerglobalscope
link-type="idl"} object.

A [shared worker client]{#dfn-sharedworker-client .dfn .dfn-paneled
dfn-for="service worker client" dfn-type="dfn" export=""} is a [service
worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client①①
link-type="dfn"} whose [global
object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-global){#ref-for-concept-settings-object-global②
link-type="dfn"} is a
[`SharedWorkerGlobalScope`{.idl}](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/workers.html#sharedworkerglobalscope){#ref-for-sharedworkerglobalscope
link-type="idl"} object.

A [worker client]{#dfn-worker-client .dfn .dfn-paneled
dfn-for="service worker client" dfn-type="dfn" export=""} is either a
[dedicated worker
client](#dfn-dedicatedworker-client){#ref-for-dfn-dedicatedworker-client
link-type="dfn"} or a [shared worker
client](#dfn-sharedworker-client){#ref-for-dfn-sharedworker-client
link-type="dfn"}.
:::

::::: section
### [2.5. ]{.secno}[Control and Use]{.content}[](#control-and-use){.self-link} {#control-and-use .heading .settled level="2.5"}

A [service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client①②
link-type="dfn"} has an [active service
worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker){#ref-for-concept-environment-active-service-worker
link-type="dfn"} that serves its own loading and its subresources. When
a [service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client①③
link-type="dfn"} has a non-null [active service
worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker){#ref-for-concept-environment-active-service-worker①
link-type="dfn"}, it is said to be [controlled]{#dfn-control .dfn
.dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" lt="controlling|controls|controlled"
noexport=""} by that [active service
worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker){#ref-for-concept-environment-active-service-worker②
link-type="dfn"}. When a [service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client①④
link-type="dfn"} is [controlled](#dfn-control){#ref-for-dfn-control
link-type="dfn"} by a [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker③⑧
link-type="dfn"}, it is said that the [service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client①⑤
link-type="dfn"} is [using]{#dfn-use .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn"
lt="using|uses|used" noexport=""} the [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker③⑨
link-type="dfn"}'s [containing service worker
registration](#dfn-containing-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-containing-service-worker-registration
link-type="dfn"}. A [service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client①⑥
link-type="dfn"}'s [active service
worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker){#ref-for-concept-environment-active-service-worker③
link-type="dfn"} is determined as explained in the following
subsections.

*The rest of the section is non-normative.*

[](#issue-f6a7ea80){.self-link} The behavior in this section is not
fully specified yet and will be specified in [HTML
Standard](https://html.spec.whatwg.org). The work is tracked by the
[issue](https://github.com/w3c/ServiceWorker/issues/765) and the [pull
request](https://github.com/whatwg/html/pull/2809).

::: section
#### [2.5.1. ]{.secno}[The window client case]{.content}[](#control-and-use-window-client){.self-link} {#control-and-use-window-client .heading .settled level="2.5.1"}

A [window client](#dfn-window-client){#ref-for-dfn-window-client
link-type="dfn"} is
[created](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/#set-up-a-window-environment-settings-object)
when a [browsing
context](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#browsing-context){#ref-for-browsing-context
link-type="dfn"} is
[created](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/#creating-a-new-browsing-context)
and when it
[navigates](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsing-the-web.html#navigate){#ref-for-navigate
link-type="dfn"}.

When a [window client](#dfn-window-client){#ref-for-dfn-window-client①
link-type="dfn"} is
[created](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/#set-up-a-window-environment-settings-object)
in the process of a [browsing
context](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#browsing-context){#ref-for-browsing-context①
link-type="dfn"}
[creation](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/#creating-a-new-browsing-context):

If the [browsing
context](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#browsing-context){#ref-for-browsing-context②
link-type="dfn"}'s initial [active
document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#nav-document){#ref-for-nav-document
link-type="dfn"}'s
[origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#concept-origin){#ref-for-concept-origin①
link-type="dfn"} is an [opaque
origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#concept-origin-opaque){#ref-for-concept-origin-opaque
link-type="dfn"}, the [window
client](#dfn-window-client){#ref-for-dfn-window-client②
link-type="dfn"}'s [active service
worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker){#ref-for-concept-environment-active-service-worker④
link-type="dfn"} is set to null. Otherwise, it is set to the creator
[document](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-document){#ref-for-concept-document
link-type="dfn"}'s [service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client①⑦
link-type="dfn"}'s [active service
worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker){#ref-for-concept-environment-active-service-worker⑤
link-type="dfn"}.

When a [window client](#dfn-window-client){#ref-for-dfn-window-client③
link-type="dfn"} is
[created](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/#set-up-a-window-environment-settings-object)
in the process of the [browsing
context](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#browsing-context){#ref-for-browsing-context③
link-type="dfn"}'s
[navigation](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsing-the-web.html#navigate){#ref-for-navigate①
link-type="dfn"}:

If the
[fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch){#ref-for-concept-fetch
link-type="dfn"} is routed through [HTTP
fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-http-fetch){#ref-for-concept-http-fetch
link-type="dfn"}, the [window
client](#dfn-window-client){#ref-for-dfn-window-client④
link-type="dfn"}'s [active service
worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker){#ref-for-concept-environment-active-service-worker⑥
link-type="dfn"} is set to the result of the [service worker
registration
matching](#match-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-match-service-worker-registration
link-type="dfn"}. Otherwise, if the created
[document](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-document){#ref-for-concept-document①
link-type="dfn"}'s
[origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#concept-origin){#ref-for-concept-origin②
link-type="dfn"} is an [opaque
origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#concept-origin-opaque){#ref-for-concept-origin-opaque①
link-type="dfn"} or not the
[same](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#same-origin){#ref-for-same-origin
link-type="dfn"} as its creator
[document](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-document){#ref-for-concept-document②
link-type="dfn"}'s
[origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#concept-origin){#ref-for-concept-origin③
link-type="dfn"}, the [window
client](#dfn-window-client){#ref-for-dfn-window-client⑤
link-type="dfn"}'s [active service
worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker){#ref-for-concept-environment-active-service-worker⑦
link-type="dfn"} is set to null. Otherwise, it is set to the creator
[document](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-document){#ref-for-concept-document③
link-type="dfn"}'s [service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client①⑧
link-type="dfn"}'s [active service
worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker){#ref-for-concept-environment-active-service-worker⑧
link-type="dfn"}.

[Note:]{.marker} For an initial replacement
[navigation](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsing-the-web.html#navigate){#ref-for-navigate②
link-type="dfn"}, the initial [window
client](#dfn-window-client){#ref-for-dfn-window-client⑥ link-type="dfn"}
that was
[created](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/#set-up-a-window-environment-settings-object)
when the [browsing
context](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#browsing-context){#ref-for-browsing-context④
link-type="dfn"} was
[created](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/#creating-a-new-browsing-context)
is reused, but the [active service
worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker){#ref-for-concept-environment-active-service-worker⑨
link-type="dfn"} is determined by the same behavior as above.

[Note:]{.marker}
[Sandboxed](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/iframe-embed-object.html#attr-iframe-sandbox){#ref-for-attr-iframe-sandbox
link-type="element-attr"}
[`iframe`](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/iframe-embed-object.html#the-iframe-element){#ref-for-the-iframe-element
link-type="element"}s without the sandboxing directives,
`allow-same-origin` and `allow-scripts`, result in having the [active
service
worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker){#ref-for-concept-environment-active-service-worker①⓪
link-type="dfn"} value of null as their
[origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#concept-origin){#ref-for-concept-origin④
link-type="dfn"} is an [opaque
origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#concept-origin-opaque){#ref-for-concept-origin-opaque②
link-type="dfn"}.
:::

::: section
#### [2.5.2. ]{.secno}[The worker client case]{.content}[](#control-and-use-worker-client){.self-link} {#control-and-use-worker-client .heading .settled level="2.5.2"}

A [worker client](#dfn-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-worker-client
link-type="dfn"} is
[created](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/#set-up-a-worker-environment-settings-object)
when the user agent [runs a
worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/workers.html#run-a-worker){#ref-for-run-a-worker
link-type="dfn"}.

When the [worker client](#dfn-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-worker-client①
link-type="dfn"} is created:

When the
[fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch){#ref-for-concept-fetch①
link-type="dfn"} is routed through [HTTP
fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-http-fetch){#ref-for-concept-http-fetch①
link-type="dfn"}, the [worker
client](#dfn-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-worker-client②
link-type="dfn"}'s [active service
worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker){#ref-for-concept-environment-active-service-worker①①
link-type="dfn"} is set to the result of the [service worker
registration
matching](#match-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-match-service-worker-registration①
link-type="dfn"}. Otherwise, if the [worker
client](#dfn-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-worker-client③
link-type="dfn"}'s
[origin](#service-worker-client-origin){#ref-for-service-worker-client-origin
link-type="dfn"} is an [opaque
origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#concept-origin-opaque){#ref-for-concept-origin-opaque③
link-type="dfn"}, or the
[request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request){#ref-for-concept-request
link-type="dfn"}'s
[URL](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-url){#ref-for-concept-request-url
link-type="dfn"} is a [blob
URL](https://w3c.github.io/FileAPI/#blob-url){#ref-for-blob-url
link-type="dfn"} and the [worker
client](#dfn-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-worker-client④
link-type="dfn"}'s
[origin](#service-worker-client-origin){#ref-for-service-worker-client-origin①
link-type="dfn"} is not the
[same](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#same-origin){#ref-for-same-origin①
link-type="dfn"} as the
[origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#concept-origin){#ref-for-concept-origin⑤
link-type="dfn"} of the last
[item](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-item){#ref-for-list-item③
link-type="dfn"} in the [worker
client](#dfn-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-worker-client⑤
link-type="dfn"}'s [global
object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object){#ref-for-global-object
link-type="dfn"}'s [owner
set](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/workers.html#concept-WorkerGlobalScope-owner-set){#ref-for-concept-WorkerGlobalScope-owner-set
link-type="dfn"}, the [worker
client](#dfn-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-worker-client⑥
link-type="dfn"}'s [active service
worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker){#ref-for-concept-environment-active-service-worker①②
link-type="dfn"} is set to null. Otherwise, it is set to the [active
service
worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker){#ref-for-concept-environment-active-service-worker①③
link-type="dfn"} of the [environment settings
object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object){#ref-for-environment-settings-object①
link-type="dfn"} of the last
[item](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-item){#ref-for-list-item④
link-type="dfn"} in the [worker
client](#dfn-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-worker-client⑦
link-type="dfn"}'s [global
object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object){#ref-for-global-object①
link-type="dfn"}'s [owner
set](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/workers.html#concept-WorkerGlobalScope-owner-set){#ref-for-concept-WorkerGlobalScope-owner-set①
link-type="dfn"}.
:::

[Note:]{.marker} [Window
clients](#dfn-window-client){#ref-for-dfn-window-client⑦
link-type="dfn"} and [worker
clients](#dfn-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-worker-client⑧
link-type="dfn"} with a [data:
URL](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc2397#section-2) result in
having the [active service
worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker){#ref-for-concept-environment-active-service-worker①④
link-type="dfn"} value of null as their
[origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#concept-origin){#ref-for-concept-origin⑥
link-type="dfn"} is an [opaque
origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#concept-origin-opaque){#ref-for-concept-origin-opaque④
link-type="dfn"}. [Window
clients](#dfn-window-client){#ref-for-dfn-window-client⑧
link-type="dfn"} and [worker
clients](#dfn-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-worker-client⑨
link-type="dfn"} with a [blob
URL](https://w3c.github.io/FileAPI/#blob-url){#ref-for-blob-url①
link-type="dfn"} can inherit the [active service
worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker){#ref-for-concept-environment-active-service-worker①⑤
link-type="dfn"} of their creator
[document](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-document){#ref-for-concept-document④
link-type="dfn"} or owner, but if the
[request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request){#ref-for-concept-request①
link-type="dfn"}'s
[origin](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-origin){#ref-for-concept-request-origin
link-type="dfn"} is not the
[same](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#same-origin){#ref-for-same-origin②
link-type="dfn"} as the
[origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#concept-origin){#ref-for-concept-origin⑦
link-type="dfn"} of their creator
[document](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-document){#ref-for-concept-document⑤
link-type="dfn"} or owner, the [active service
worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker){#ref-for-concept-environment-active-service-worker①⑥
link-type="dfn"} is set to null.
:::::

::: section
### [2.6. ]{.secno}[Task Sources]{.content}[](#task-sources){.self-link} {#task-sources .heading .settled level="2.6"}

The following additional [task
sources](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#task-source){#ref-for-task-source
link-type="dfn"} are used by [service
workers](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker④⓪
link-type="dfn"}.

The [handle fetch task source]{#dfn-handle-fetch-task-source .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" export=""}

:   This [task
    source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#task-source){#ref-for-task-source①
    link-type="dfn"} is used for
    [dispatching](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event-dispatch){#ref-for-concept-event-dispatch
    link-type="dfn"}
    [`fetch`{.idl}](#service-worker-global-scope-fetch-event){#ref-for-service-worker-global-scope-fetch-event①
    .idl-code link-type="event"} events to [service
    workers](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker④①
    link-type="dfn"}.

The [handle functional event task source]{#dfn-handle-functional-event-task-source .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" export=""}

:   This [task
    source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#task-source){#ref-for-task-source②
    link-type="dfn"} is used for features that
    [dispatch](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event-dispatch){#ref-for-concept-event-dispatch①
    link-type="dfn"} other [functional
    events](#dfn-functional-events){#ref-for-dfn-functional-events
    link-type="dfn"}, e.g.
    [`push`{.idl}](https://w3c.github.io/push-api/#pushevent-interface){#ref-for-pushevent-interface
    .idl-code link-type="event"} events, to [service
    workers](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker④②
    link-type="dfn"}.

    [Note:]{.marker} A user agent may use a separate task source for
    each functional event type in order to avoid a head-of-line blocking
    phenomenon for certain functional events.
:::

::: section
### [2.7. ]{.secno}[User Agent Shutdown]{.content}[](#user-agent-shutdown){.self-link} {#user-agent-shutdown .heading .settled level="2.7"}

A user agent *must* maintain the state of its stored [service worker
registrations](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration②⑥
link-type="dfn"} across restarts with the following rules:

- An [installing
  worker](#dfn-installing-worker){#ref-for-dfn-installing-worker①
  link-type="dfn"} does not persist but is discarded. If the [installing
  worker](#dfn-installing-worker){#ref-for-dfn-installing-worker②
  link-type="dfn"} was the only [service
  worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker④③
  link-type="dfn"} for the [service worker
  registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration②⑦
  link-type="dfn"}, the [service worker
  registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration②⑧
  link-type="dfn"} is discarded.

- A [waiting worker](#dfn-waiting-worker){#ref-for-dfn-waiting-worker①
  link-type="dfn"} promotes to an [active
  worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker⑥
  link-type="dfn"}.

To attain this, the user agent *must* invoke [Handle User Agent
Shutdown](#handle-user-agent-shutdown){#ref-for-handle-user-agent-shutdown
link-type="dfn"} when it terminates.
:::
::::::::::::::::

:::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: section
## [3. ]{.secno}[Client Context]{.content}[](#document-context){.self-link} {#document-context .heading .settled level="3"}

::: {#example-c7be5225 .example}
[](#example-c7be5225){.self-link} Bootstrapping with a service worker:

``` highlight
// scope defaults to the path the script sits in
// "/" in this example
navigator.serviceWorker.register("/serviceworker.js").then(registration => {
  console.log("success!");
  if (registration.installing) {
    registration.installing.postMessage("Howdy from your installing page.");
  }
}, err => {
  console.error("Installing the worker failed!", err);
});
```
:::

::::::::::: section
### [3.1. ]{.secno}[[`ServiceWorker`{.idl}](#serviceworker){#ref-for-serviceworker① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#serviceworker-interface){.self-link} {#serviceworker-interface .heading .settled level="3.1"}

``` {.def .highlight .idl}
[SecureContext, Exposed=(Window,Worker)]
interface ServiceWorker : EventTarget {
  readonly attribute USVString scriptURL;
  readonly attribute ServiceWorkerState state;
  undefined postMessage(any message, sequence<object> transfer);
  undefined postMessage(any message, optional StructuredSerializeOptions options = {});

  // event
  attribute EventHandler onstatechange;
};
ServiceWorker includes AbstractWorker;

enum ServiceWorkerState {
  "parsed",
  "installing",
  "installed",
  "activating",
  "activated",
  "redundant"
};
```

A [`ServiceWorker`{.idl}](#serviceworker){#ref-for-serviceworker③
link-type="idl"} object represents a [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker④④
link-type="dfn"}. Each
[`ServiceWorker`{.idl}](#serviceworker){#ref-for-serviceworker④
link-type="idl"} object is associated with a [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker④⑤
link-type="dfn"}. Multiple separate objects implementing the
[`ServiceWorker`{.idl}](#serviceworker){#ref-for-serviceworker⑤
link-type="idl"} interface across documents and workers can all be
associated with the same [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker④⑥
link-type="dfn"} simultaneously.

A [`ServiceWorker`{.idl}](#serviceworker){#ref-for-serviceworker⑥
link-type="idl"} object has an associated
[`ServiceWorkerState`{.idl}](#enumdef-serviceworkerstate){#ref-for-enumdef-serviceworkerstate①
link-type="idl"} object which is itself associated with [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker④⑦
link-type="dfn"}'s [state](#dfn-state){#ref-for-dfn-state③
link-type="dfn"}.

:::: section
#### [3.1.1. ]{.secno}[Getting [`ServiceWorker`{.idl}](#serviceworker){#ref-for-serviceworker⑦ link-type="idl"} instances]{.content}[](#service-worker-creation){.self-link} {#service-worker-creation .heading .settled level="3.1.1"}

An [environment settings
object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object){#ref-for-environment-settings-object②
link-type="dfn"} has a [service worker object
map]{#environment-settings-object-service-worker-object-map .dfn
.dfn-paneled dfn-for="environment settings object" dfn-type="dfn"
noexport=""}, a
[map](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ordered-map){#ref-for-ordered-map①
link-type="dfn"} where the
[keys](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-getting-the-keys){#ref-for-map-getting-the-keys
link-type="dfn"} are [service
workers](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker④⑧
link-type="dfn"} and the
[values](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-getting-the-values){#ref-for-map-getting-the-values
link-type="dfn"} are
[`ServiceWorker`{.idl}](#serviceworker){#ref-for-serviceworker⑧
link-type="idl"} objects.

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="service-worker-creation-algorithm"}
To [get the service worker object]{#get-the-service-worker-object .dfn
.dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn"
lt="get the service worker object|getting the service worker object"
noexport=""} representing `serviceWorker`{.variable} (a [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker④⑨
link-type="dfn"}) in `environment`{.variable} (an [environment settings
object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object){#ref-for-environment-settings-object③
link-type="dfn"}), run these steps:

1.  Let `objectMap`{.variable} be `environment`{.variable}'s [service
    worker object
    map](#environment-settings-object-service-worker-object-map){#ref-for-environment-settings-object-service-worker-object-map
    link-type="dfn"}.

2.  If `objectMap`{.variable}\[`serviceWorker`{.variable}\] does not
    [exist](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists){#ref-for-map-exists
    link-type="dfn"}, then:

    1.  Let `serviceWorkerObj`{.variable} be a new
        [`ServiceWorker`{.idl}](#serviceworker){#ref-for-serviceworker⑨
        link-type="idl"} in `environment`{.variable}'s
        [Realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object's-realm){#ref-for-environment-settings-object's-realm
        link-type="dfn"}, and associate it with
        `serviceWorker`{.variable}.

    2.  Set `serviceWorkerObj`{.variable}'s
        [`state`{.idl}](#dom-serviceworker-state){#ref-for-dom-serviceworker-state①
        link-type="idl"} to `serviceWorker`{.variable}'s
        [state](#dfn-state){#ref-for-dfn-state④ link-type="dfn"}.

    3.  Set `objectMap`{.variable}\[`serviceWorker`{.variable}\] to
        `serviceWorkerObj`{.variable}.

3.  Return `objectMap`{.variable}\[`serviceWorker`{.variable}\].
:::
::::

:::: section
#### [3.1.2. ]{.secno}[[`scriptURL`{.idl}](#dom-serviceworker-scripturl){#ref-for-dom-serviceworker-scripturl① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#service-worker-url){.self-link} {#service-worker-url .heading .settled level="3.1.2"}

The [`scriptURL`]{#dom-serviceworker-scripturl .dfn .dfn-paneled
.idl-code dfn-for="ServiceWorker" dfn-type="attribute" export=""} getter
steps are to return the [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑤⓪
link-type="dfn"}'s
[serialized](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-serializer){#ref-for-concept-url-serializer②
link-type="dfn"} [script url](#dfn-script-url){#ref-for-dfn-script-url
link-type="dfn"}.

::: {#example-afd15a33 .example}
[](#example-afd15a33){.self-link} For example, consider a document
created by a navigation to `https://example.com/app.html` which
[matches](#match-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-match-service-worker-registration②
link-type="dfn"} via the following registration call which has been
previously executed:

``` highlight
// Script on the page https://example.com/app.html
navigator.serviceWorker.register("/service_worker.js");
```

The value of `navigator.serviceWorker.controller.scriptURL` will be
\"`https://example.com/service_worker.js`\".
:::
::::

::: section
#### [3.1.3. ]{.secno}[[`state`{.idl}](#dom-serviceworker-state){#ref-for-dom-serviceworker-state② link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#service-worker-state){.self-link} {#service-worker-state .heading .settled level="3.1.3"}

The [`state`]{#dom-serviceworker-state .dfn .dfn-paneled .idl-code
dfn-for="ServiceWorker" dfn-type="attribute" export=""} attribute *must*
return the value (in
[`ServiceWorkerState`{.idl}](#enumdef-serviceworkerstate){#ref-for-enumdef-serviceworkerstate②
link-type="idl"} enumeration) to which it was last set.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="service-worker-postmessage"}
#### [3.1.4. ]{.secno}[[`postMessage(message, transfer)`{.idl}](#dom-serviceworker-postmessage){#ref-for-dom-serviceworker-postmessage① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#service-worker-postmessage){.self-link} {#service-worker-postmessage .heading .settled level="3.1.4"}

The
[`postMessage(``message`{.variable}`, ``transfer`{.variable}`)`]{#dom-serviceworker-postmessage
.dfn .dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="ServiceWorker" dfn-type="method"
export=""} method steps are:

1.  Let `options`{.variable} be «\[ \"transfer\" → `transfer`{.variable}
    \]».

2.  Invoke
    [`postMessage(message, options)`{.idl}](#dom-serviceworker-postmessage-message-options){#ref-for-dom-serviceworker-postmessage-message-options①
    link-type="idl"} with `message`{.variable} and `options`{.variable}
    as the arguments.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="service-worker-postmessage-options"}
#### [3.1.5. ]{.secno}[[`postMessage(message, options)`{.idl}](#dom-serviceworker-postmessage-message-options){#ref-for-dom-serviceworker-postmessage-message-options② link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#service-worker-postmessage-options){.self-link} {#service-worker-postmessage-options .heading .settled level="3.1.5"}

The
[`postMessage(``message`{.variable}`, ``options`{.variable}`)`]{#dom-serviceworker-postmessage-message-options
.dfn .dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="ServiceWorker" dfn-type="method"
export="" lt="postMessage(message, options)|postMessage(message)"}
method steps are:

1.  Let `serviceWorker`{.variable} be the [service
    worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑤①
    link-type="dfn"} represented by
    [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this
    link-type="dfn"}.

2.  Let `incumbentSettings`{.variable} be the [incumbent settings
    object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#incumbent-settings-object){#ref-for-incumbent-settings-object
    link-type="dfn"}.

3.  Let `incumbentGlobal`{.variable} be `incumbentSettings`{.variable}'s
    [global
    object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-global){#ref-for-concept-settings-object-global③
    link-type="dfn"}.

4.  Let `serializeWithTransferResult`{.variable} be
    [StructuredSerializeWithTransfer](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/structured-data.html#structuredserializewithtransfer){#ref-for-structuredserializewithtransfer
    link-type="abstract-op"}(`message`{.variable},
    `options`{.variable}\[\"[`transfer`{.idl}](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/web-messaging.html#dom-structuredserializeoptions-transfer){#ref-for-dom-structuredserializeoptions-transfer
    link-type="idl"}\"\]). Rethrow any exceptions.

5.  If the result of running the [Should Skip
    Event](#should-skip-event){#ref-for-should-skip-event
    link-type="dfn"} algorithm with \"message\" and
    `serviceWorker`{.variable} is true, then return.

6.  Run these substeps [in
    parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel){#ref-for-in-parallel
    link-type="dfn"}:

    1.  If the result of running the [Run Service
        Worker](#run-service-worker){#ref-for-run-service-worker
        link-type="dfn"} algorithm with `serviceWorker`{.variable} is
        *failure*, then return.

    2.  [Queue a
        task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task){#ref-for-queue-a-task①
        link-type="dfn"} on the [DOM manipulation task
        source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source){#ref-for-dom-manipulation-task-source
        link-type="dfn"} to run the following steps:

        1.  Let `source`{.variable} be determined by switching on the
            type of `incumbentGlobal`{.variable}:

            [`ServiceWorkerGlobalScope`{.idl}](#serviceworkerglobalscope){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope① link-type="idl"}
            :   The result of [getting the service worker
                object](#get-the-service-worker-object){#ref-for-get-the-service-worker-object
                link-type="dfn"} that represents
                `incumbentGlobal`{.variable}'s [service
                worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker
                link-type="dfn"} in the [relevant settings
                object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object){#ref-for-relevant-settings-object
                link-type="dfn"} of `serviceWorker`{.variable}'s [global
                object](#dfn-service-worker-global-object){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-global-object
                link-type="dfn"}.

            [`Window`{.idl}](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#window){#ref-for-window① link-type="idl"}
            :   a new
                [`WindowClient`{.idl}](#windowclient){#ref-for-windowclient
                link-type="idl"} object that represents
                `incumbentGlobal`{.variable}'s [relevant settings
                object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object){#ref-for-relevant-settings-object①
                link-type="dfn"}.

            Otherwise
            :   a new [`Client`{.idl}](#client){#ref-for-client
                link-type="idl"} object that represents
                `incumbentGlobal`{.variable}'s associated worker

        2.  Let `origin`{.variable} be the
            [serialization](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#ascii-serialisation-of-an-origin){#ref-for-ascii-serialisation-of-an-origin
            link-type="dfn"} of `incumbentSettings`{.variable}'s
            [origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-origin){#ref-for-concept-settings-object-origin①
            link-type="dfn"}.

        3.  Let `destination`{.variable} be the
            [`ServiceWorkerGlobalScope`{.idl}](#serviceworkerglobalscope){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope②
            link-type="idl"} object associated with
            `serviceWorker`{.variable}.

        4.  Let `deserializeRecord`{.variable} be
            [StructuredDeserializeWithTransfer](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/structured-data.html#structureddeserializewithtransfer){#ref-for-structureddeserializewithtransfer
            link-type="abstract-op"}(`serializeWithTransferResult`{.variable},
            `destination`{.variable}'s
            [Realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-global-object-realm){#ref-for-concept-global-object-realm
            link-type="dfn"}).

            If this throws an exception, let `e`{.variable} be the
            result of [creating an
            event](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event-create){#ref-for-concept-event-create
            link-type="dfn"} named
            [`messageerror`{.idl}](#eventdef-serviceworkerglobalscope-messageerror){#ref-for-eventdef-serviceworkerglobalscope-messageerror①
            .idl-code link-type="event"}, using
            [`ExtendableMessageEvent`{.idl}](#extendablemessageevent){#ref-for-extendablemessageevent
            link-type="idl"}, with the
            [`origin`{.idl}](#dom-extendablemessageevent-origin){#ref-for-dom-extendablemessageevent-origin
            link-type="idl"} attribute initialized to
            `origin`{.variable} and the
            [`source`{.idl}](#dom-extendablemessageevent-source){#ref-for-dom-extendablemessageevent-source
            link-type="idl"} attribute initialized to
            `source`{.variable}.

        5.  Else:

            1.  Let `messageClone`{.variable} be
                `deserializeRecord`{.variable}.\[\[Deserialized\]\].

            2.  Let `newPorts`{.variable} be a new [frozen
                array](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-frozen-array-type){#ref-for-dfn-frozen-array-type
                link-type="dfn"} consisting of all
                [`MessagePort`{.idl}](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/web-messaging.html#messageport){#ref-for-messageport
                link-type="idl"} objects in
                `deserializeRecord`{.variable}.\[\[TransferredValues\]\],
                if any, maintaining their relative order.

            3.  Let `e`{.variable} be the result of [creating an
                event](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event-create){#ref-for-concept-event-create①
                link-type="dfn"} named
                [`message`{.idl}](#eventdef-serviceworkerglobalscope-message){#ref-for-eventdef-serviceworkerglobalscope-message①
                .idl-code link-type="event"}, using
                [`ExtendableMessageEvent`{.idl}](#extendablemessageevent){#ref-for-extendablemessageevent①
                link-type="idl"}, with the
                [`origin`{.idl}](#dom-extendablemessageevent-origin){#ref-for-dom-extendablemessageevent-origin①
                link-type="idl"} attribute initialized to
                `origin`{.variable}, the
                [`source`{.idl}](#dom-extendablemessageevent-source){#ref-for-dom-extendablemessageevent-source①
                link-type="idl"} attribute initialized to
                `source`{.variable}, the
                [`data`{.idl}](#dom-extendablemessageevent-data){#ref-for-dom-extendablemessageevent-data
                link-type="idl"} attribute initialized to
                `messageClone`{.variable}, and the
                [`ports`{.idl}](#dom-extendablemessageevent-ports){#ref-for-dom-extendablemessageevent-ports
                link-type="idl"} attribute initialized to
                `newPorts`{.variable}.

        6.  [Dispatch](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event-dispatch){#ref-for-concept-event-dispatch②
            link-type="dfn"} `e`{.variable} at `destination`{.variable}.

        7.  Invoke [Update Service Worker Extended Events
            Set](#update-service-worker-extended-events-set){#ref-for-update-service-worker-extended-events-set
            link-type="dfn"} with `serviceWorker`{.variable} and
            `e`{.variable}.
:::

::: section

link-type="dfn"} (and its corresponding [event handler event

attributes](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-handler-idl-attributes){#ref-for-event-handler-idl-attributes
link-type="dfn"}, by all objects implementing
[`ServiceWorker`{.idl}](#serviceworker){#ref-for-serviceworker①⓪
link-type="idl"} interface:

[event
handler](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-handlers){#ref-for-event-handlers①
link-type="dfn"}
