type](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-handler-event-type){#ref-for-event-handler-event-type①
link-type="dfn"}

[`onstatechange`]{#dom-serviceworker-onstatechange .dfn .dfn-paneled
.idl-code dfn-for="ServiceWorker" dfn-type="attribute" export=""}

[`statechange`{.idl}](#eventdef-serviceworker-statechange){#ref-for-eventdef-serviceworker-statechange
link-type="idl"}
:::
:::::::::::

::::::::::::::: section
### [3.2. ]{.secno}[[`ServiceWorkerRegistration`{.idl}](#serviceworkerregistration){#ref-for-serviceworkerregistration① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#serviceworkerregistration-interface){.self-link} {#serviceworkerregistration-interface .heading .settled level="3.2"}

``` {.def .highlight .idl}
[SecureContext, Exposed=(Window,Worker)]
interface ServiceWorkerRegistration : EventTarget {
  readonly attribute ServiceWorker? installing;
  readonly attribute ServiceWorker? waiting;
  readonly attribute ServiceWorker? active;
  [SameObject] readonly attribute NavigationPreloadManager navigationPreload;

  readonly attribute USVString scope;
  readonly attribute ServiceWorkerUpdateViaCache updateViaCache;

  [NewObject] Promise<ServiceWorkerRegistration> update();
  [NewObject] Promise<boolean> unregister();

  // event
  attribute EventHandler onupdatefound;
};

enum ServiceWorkerUpdateViaCache {
  "imports",
  "all",
  "none"
};
```

A
[`ServiceWorkerRegistration`{.idl}](#serviceworkerregistration){#ref-for-serviceworkerregistration③
link-type="idl"} has a [service worker
registration]{#serviceworkerregistration-service-worker-registration
.dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-for="ServiceWorkerRegistration" dfn-type="dfn"
export=""} (a [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration②⑨
link-type="dfn"}).

:::: section
#### [3.2.1. ]{.secno}[Getting [`ServiceWorkerRegistration`{.idl}](#serviceworkerregistration){#ref-for-serviceworkerregistration④ link-type="idl"} instances]{.content}[](#service-worker-registration-creation){.self-link} {#service-worker-registration-creation .heading .settled level="3.2.1"}

An [environment settings
object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object){#ref-for-environment-settings-object④
link-type="dfn"} has a [service worker registration object
map]{#environment-settings-object-service-worker-registration-object-map
.dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-for="environment settings object" dfn-type="dfn"
noexport=""}, a
[map](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ordered-map){#ref-for-ordered-map②
link-type="dfn"} where the
[keys](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-getting-the-keys){#ref-for-map-getting-the-keys①
link-type="dfn"} are [service worker
registrations](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration③⓪
link-type="dfn"} and the
[values](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-getting-the-values){#ref-for-map-getting-the-values①
link-type="dfn"} are
[`ServiceWorkerRegistration`{.idl}](#serviceworkerregistration){#ref-for-serviceworkerregistration⑤
link-type="idl"} objects.

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="service-worker-registration-creation-algorithm"}
To [get the service worker registration
object]{#get-the-service-worker-registration-object .dfn .dfn-paneled
dfn-type="dfn"
lt="get the service worker registration object|getting the service worker registration object"
noexport=""} representing `registration`{.variable} (a [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration③①
link-type="dfn"}) in `environment`{.variable} (an [environment settings
object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object){#ref-for-environment-settings-object⑤
link-type="dfn"}), run these steps:

1.  Let `objectMap`{.variable} be `environment`{.variable}'s [service
    worker registration object
    map](#environment-settings-object-service-worker-registration-object-map){#ref-for-environment-settings-object-service-worker-registration-object-map
    link-type="dfn"}.

2.  If `objectMap`{.variable}\[`registration`{.variable}\] does not
    [exist](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists){#ref-for-map-exists①
    link-type="dfn"}, then:

    1.  Let `registrationObject`{.variable} be a new
        [`ServiceWorkerRegistration`{.idl}](#serviceworkerregistration){#ref-for-serviceworkerregistration⑥
        link-type="idl"} in `environment`{.variable}'s
        [Realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object's-realm){#ref-for-environment-settings-object's-realm①
        link-type="dfn"}.

    2.  Set `registrationObject`{.variable}'s [service worker
        registration](#serviceworkerregistration-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-serviceworkerregistration-service-worker-registration
        link-type="dfn"} to `registration`{.variable}.

    3.  Set `registrationObject`{.variable}'s
        [`installing`{.idl}](#dom-serviceworkerregistration-installing){#ref-for-dom-serviceworkerregistration-installing①
        link-type="idl"} attribute to null.

    4.  Set `registrationObject`{.variable}'s
        [`waiting`{.idl}](#dom-serviceworkerregistration-waiting){#ref-for-dom-serviceworkerregistration-waiting①
        link-type="idl"} attribute to null.

    5.  Set `registrationObject`{.variable}'s
        [`active`{.idl}](#dom-serviceworkerregistration-active){#ref-for-dom-serviceworkerregistration-active①
        link-type="idl"} attribute to null.

    6.  If `registration`{.variable}'s [installing
        worker](#dfn-installing-worker){#ref-for-dfn-installing-worker③
        link-type="dfn"} is not null, then set
        `registrationObject`{.variable}'s
        [`installing`{.idl}](#dom-serviceworkerregistration-installing){#ref-for-dom-serviceworkerregistration-installing②
        link-type="idl"} attribute to the result of [getting the service
        worker
        object](#get-the-service-worker-object){#ref-for-get-the-service-worker-object①
        link-type="dfn"} that represents `registration`{.variable}'s
        [installing
        worker](#dfn-installing-worker){#ref-for-dfn-installing-worker④
        link-type="dfn"} in `environment`{.variable}.

    7.  If `registration`{.variable}'s [waiting
        worker](#dfn-waiting-worker){#ref-for-dfn-waiting-worker②
        link-type="dfn"} is not null, then set
        `registrationObject`{.variable}'s
        [`waiting`{.idl}](#dom-serviceworkerregistration-waiting){#ref-for-dom-serviceworkerregistration-waiting②
        link-type="idl"} attribute to the result of [getting the service
        worker
        object](#get-the-service-worker-object){#ref-for-get-the-service-worker-object②
        link-type="dfn"} that represents `registration`{.variable}'s
        [waiting
        worker](#dfn-waiting-worker){#ref-for-dfn-waiting-worker③
        link-type="dfn"} in `environment`{.variable}.

    8.  If `registration`{.variable}'s [active
        worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker⑦
        link-type="dfn"} is not null, then set
        `registrationObject`{.variable}'s
        [`active`{.idl}](#dom-serviceworkerregistration-active){#ref-for-dom-serviceworkerregistration-active②
        link-type="idl"} attribute to the result of [getting the service
        worker
        object](#get-the-service-worker-object){#ref-for-get-the-service-worker-object③
        link-type="dfn"} that represents `registration`{.variable}'s
        [active worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker⑧
        link-type="dfn"} in `environment`{.variable}.

    9.  Set `objectMap`{.variable}\[`registration`{.variable}\] to
        `registrationObject`{.variable}.

3.  Return `objectMap`{.variable}\[`registration`{.variable}\].
:::
::::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="navigator-service-worker-installing"}
#### [3.2.2. ]{.secno}[[`installing`{.idl}](#dom-serviceworkerregistration-installing){#ref-for-dom-serviceworkerregistration-installing③ link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#navigator-service-worker-installing){.self-link} {#navigator-service-worker-installing .heading .settled level="3.2.2"}

dfn-type="attribute" export=""} attribute *must* return the value to
which it was last set.

link-type="dfn"}, there is only one
[`ServiceWorker`{.idl}](#serviceworker){#ref-for-serviceworker①④
link-type="idl"} object per associated [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑤②
link-type="dfn"}.
:::

[`waiting`]{#dom-serviceworkerregistration-waiting .dfn .dfn-paneled

export=""} attribute *must* return the value to which it was last set.

[Note:]{.marker} Within a
[Realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object's-realm){#ref-for-environment-settings-object's-realm③
link-type="dfn"}, there is only one
[`ServiceWorker`{.idl}](#serviceworker){#ref-for-serviceworker①⑤
link-type="idl"} object per associated [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑤③

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="navigator-service-worker-active"}
#### [3.2.4. ]{.secno}[[`active`{.idl}](#dom-serviceworkerregistration-active){#ref-for-dom-serviceworkerregistration-active③ link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#navigator-service-worker-active){.self-link} {#navigator-service-worker-active .heading .settled level="3.2.4"}

[`active`]{#dom-serviceworkerregistration-active .dfn .dfn-paneled
.idl-code dfn-for="ServiceWorkerRegistration" dfn-type="attribute"
export=""} attribute *must* return the value to which it was last set.

[Note:]{.marker} Within a
[Realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object's-realm){#ref-for-environment-settings-object's-realm④
link-type="dfn"}, there is only one
[`ServiceWorker`{.idl}](#serviceworker){#ref-for-serviceworker①⑥
link-type="idl"} object per associated [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑤④
link-type="dfn"}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="service-worker-registration-navigationpreload"}
#### [3.2.5. ]{.secno}[[`navigationPreload`{.idl}](#dom-serviceworkerregistration-navigationpreload){#ref-for-dom-serviceworkerregistration-navigationpreload① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#service-worker-registration-navigationpreload){.self-link} {#service-worker-registration-navigationpreload .heading .settled level="3.2.5"}

The
[`navigationPreload`]{#dom-serviceworkerregistration-navigationpreload
.dfn .dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="ServiceWorkerRegistration"
dfn-type="attribute" export=""} getter steps are to return the [service
worker
registration](#serviceworkerregistration-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-serviceworkerregistration-service-worker-registration①
link-type="dfn"}'s
[`NavigationPreloadManager`{.idl}](#navigationpreloadmanager){#ref-for-navigationpreloadmanager②
link-type="idl"} object.
:::

:::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="service-worker-registration-scope"}
#### [3.2.6. ]{.secno}[[`scope`{.idl}](#dom-serviceworkerregistration-scope){#ref-for-dom-serviceworkerregistration-scope① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#service-worker-registration-scope){.self-link} {#service-worker-registration-scope .heading .settled level="3.2.6"}

The [`scope`]{#dom-serviceworkerregistration-scope .dfn .dfn-paneled
.idl-code dfn-for="ServiceWorkerRegistration" dfn-type="attribute"
export=""} getter steps are to return the [service worker
registration](#serviceworkerregistration-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-serviceworkerregistration-service-worker-registration②
link-type="dfn"}'s
[serialized](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-serializer){#ref-for-concept-url-serializer③
link-type="dfn"} [scope url](#dfn-scope-url){#ref-for-dfn-scope-url⑤
link-type="dfn"}.

::: {#example-0146b4b0 .example}
[](#example-0146b4b0){.self-link} In the example in [§ 3.1.2
scriptURL](#service-worker-url), the value of `registration.scope`,
obtained from
`navigator.serviceWorker.ready.then(registration => console.log(registration.scope))`
for example, will be \"`https://example.com/`\".
:::
::::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="service-worker-registration-updateviacache"}
#### [3.2.7. ]{.secno}[[`updateViaCache`{.idl}](#dom-serviceworkerregistration-updateviacache){#ref-for-dom-serviceworkerregistration-updateviacache① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#service-worker-registration-updateviacache){.self-link} {#service-worker-registration-updateviacache .heading .settled level="3.2.7"}

The [`updateViaCache`]{#dom-serviceworkerregistration-updateviacache
.dfn .dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="ServiceWorkerRegistration"
dfn-type="attribute" export=""} getter steps are to return the [service
worker
registration](#serviceworkerregistration-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-serviceworkerregistration-service-worker-registration③
link-type="dfn"}'s [update via cache
mode](#dfn-update-via-cache){#ref-for-dfn-update-via-cache
link-type="dfn"}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="service-worker-registration-update"}
#### [3.2.8. ]{.secno}[[`update()`{.idl}](#dom-serviceworkerregistration-update){#ref-for-dom-serviceworkerregistration-update① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#service-worker-registration-update){.self-link} {#service-worker-registration-update .heading .settled level="3.2.8"}

The [`update()`]{#dom-serviceworkerregistration-update .dfn .dfn-paneled
.idl-code dfn-for="ServiceWorkerRegistration" dfn-type="method"
export=""} method steps are:

1.  Let `registration`{.variable} be the [service worker
    registration](#serviceworkerregistration-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-serviceworkerregistration-service-worker-registration④
    link-type="dfn"}.

2.  Let `newestWorker`{.variable} be the result of running [Get Newest
    Worker](#get-newest-worker){#ref-for-get-newest-worker
    link-type="dfn"} algorithm passing `registration`{.variable} as its
    argument.

3.  If `newestWorker`{.variable} is null, return [a promise rejected
    with](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-promise-rejected-with){#ref-for-a-promise-rejected-with
    link-type="dfn"} an
    \"[`InvalidStateError`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#invalidstateerror){#ref-for-invalidstateerror
    link-type="idl"}\"
    [`DOMException`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException){#ref-for-idl-DOMException
    link-type="idl"} and abort these steps.

4.  If [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this①
    link-type="dfn"}'s [relevant global
    object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-global){#ref-for-concept-relevant-global
    link-type="dfn"} `globalObject`{.variable} is a
    [`ServiceWorkerGlobalScope`{.idl}](#serviceworkerglobalscope){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope③
    link-type="idl"} object, and `globalObject`{.variable}'s associated
    [service
    worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker①
    link-type="dfn"}'s [state](#dfn-state){#ref-for-dfn-state⑤
    link-type="dfn"} is \"`installing`\", return [a promise rejected
    with](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-promise-rejected-with){#ref-for-a-promise-rejected-with①
    link-type="dfn"} an
    \"[`InvalidStateError`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#invalidstateerror){#ref-for-invalidstateerror①
    link-type="idl"}\"
    [`DOMException`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException){#ref-for-idl-DOMException①
    link-type="idl"} and abort these steps.

5.  Let `promise`{.variable} be a
    [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects){#ref-for-sec-promise-objects
    link-type="dfn"}.

6.  Let `job`{.variable} be the result of running [Create
    Job](#create-job){#ref-for-create-job link-type="dfn"} with
    *update*, `registration`{.variable}'s [storage
    key](#service-worker-registration-storage-key){#ref-for-service-worker-registration-storage-key②
    link-type="dfn"}, `registration`{.variable}'s [scope
    url](#dfn-scope-url){#ref-for-dfn-scope-url⑥ link-type="dfn"},
    `newestWorker`{.variable}'s [script
    url](#dfn-script-url){#ref-for-dfn-script-url① link-type="dfn"},
    `promise`{.variable}, and
    [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this②
    link-type="dfn"}'s [relevant settings
    object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object){#ref-for-relevant-settings-object②
    link-type="dfn"}.

7.  Set `job`{.variable}'s [worker
    type](#dfn-job-worker-type){#ref-for-dfn-job-worker-type
    link-type="dfn"} to `newestWorker`{.variable}'s
    [type](#dfn-type){#ref-for-dfn-type link-type="dfn"}.

8.  Invoke [Schedule Job](#schedule-job){#ref-for-schedule-job
    link-type="dfn"} with `job`{.variable}.

9.  Return `promise`{.variable}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="navigator-service-worker-unregister"}
#### [3.2.9. ]{.secno}[[`unregister()`{.idl}](#dom-serviceworkerregistration-unregister){#ref-for-dom-serviceworkerregistration-unregister① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#navigator-service-worker-unregister){.self-link} {#navigator-service-worker-unregister .heading .settled level="3.2.9"}

[Note:]{.marker} The
[`unregister()`{.idl}](#dom-serviceworkerregistration-unregister){#ref-for-dom-serviceworkerregistration-unregister②
link-type="idl"} method unregisters the [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration③②
link-type="dfn"}. It is important to note that the currently
[controlled](#dfn-control){#ref-for-dfn-control① link-type="dfn"}
[service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client①⑨
link-type="dfn"}'s [active service
worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker){#ref-for-concept-environment-active-service-worker①⑦
link-type="dfn"}'s [containing service worker
registration](#dfn-containing-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-containing-service-worker-registration①
link-type="dfn"} is effective until all the [service worker
clients](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client②⓪
link-type="dfn"} (including itself) using this [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration③③
link-type="dfn"} unload. That is, the
[`unregister()`{.idl}](#dom-serviceworkerregistration-unregister){#ref-for-dom-serviceworkerregistration-unregister③
link-type="idl"} method only affects subsequent
[navigations](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsing-the-web.html#navigate){#ref-for-navigate③
link-type="dfn"}.

The [`unregister()`]{#dom-serviceworkerregistration-unregister .dfn
.dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="ServiceWorkerRegistration"
dfn-type="method" export=""} method steps are:

1.  Let `registration`{.variable} be the [service worker
    registration](#serviceworkerregistration-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-serviceworkerregistration-service-worker-registration⑤
    link-type="dfn"}.

2.  Let `promise`{.variable} be [a new
    promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-new-promise){#ref-for-a-new-promise
    link-type="dfn"}.

3.  Let `job`{.variable} be the result of running [Create
    Job](#create-job){#ref-for-create-job① link-type="dfn"} with
    *unregister*, `registration`{.variable}'s [storage
    key](#service-worker-registration-storage-key){#ref-for-service-worker-registration-storage-key③
    link-type="dfn"}, `registration`{.variable}'s [scope
    url](#dfn-scope-url){#ref-for-dfn-scope-url⑦ link-type="dfn"}, null,
    `promise`{.variable}, and
    [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this③
    link-type="dfn"}'s [relevant settings
    object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object){#ref-for-relevant-settings-object③
    link-type="dfn"}.

4.  Invoke [Schedule Job](#schedule-job){#ref-for-schedule-job①
    link-type="dfn"} with `job`{.variable}.

5.  Return `promise`{.variable}.
:::

::: section
#### [3.2.10. ]{.secno}[Event handler]{.content}[](#service-worker-registration-event-handler){.self-link} {#service-worker-registration-event-handler .heading .settled level="3.2.10"}

The following is the [event
handler](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-handlers){#ref-for-event-handlers②
link-type="dfn"} (and its corresponding [event handler event
type](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-handler-event-type){#ref-for-event-handler-event-type②
link-type="dfn"}) that *must* be supported, as [event handler IDL
attributes](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-handler-idl-attributes){#ref-for-event-handler-idl-attributes①
link-type="dfn"}, by all objects implementing
[`ServiceWorkerRegistration`{.idl}](#serviceworkerregistration){#ref-for-serviceworkerregistration⑦
link-type="idl"} interface:

[event
handler](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-handlers){#ref-for-event-handlers③
link-type="dfn"}

[event handler event
type](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-handler-event-type){#ref-for-event-handler-event-type③
link-type="dfn"}

[`onupdatefound`]{#dom-serviceworkerregistration-onupdatefound .dfn
.dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="ServiceWorkerRegistration"
dfn-type="attribute" export=""}

[`updatefound`{.idl}](#service-worker-registration-updatefound-event){#ref-for-service-worker-registration-updatefound-event
.idl-code link-type="event"}
:::
:::::::::::::::

::: section
### [3.3. ]{.secno}[[`navigator.serviceWorker`{.idl}](#navigator-service-worker-attribute){#ref-for-navigator-service-worker-attribute link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#navigator-serviceworker){.self-link} {#navigator-serviceworker .heading .settled level="3.3"}

``` {.def .highlight .idl}
partial interface Navigator {
  [SecureContext, SameObject] readonly attribute ServiceWorkerContainer serviceWorker;
};

partial interface WorkerNavigator {
  [SecureContext, SameObject] readonly attribute ServiceWorkerContainer serviceWorker;
};
```

The [`serviceWorker`]{#navigator-service-worker-attribute .dfn
.dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="Navigator,WorkerNavigator"
dfn-type="attribute" export=""} getter steps are to return the
[`ServiceWorkerContainer`{.idl}](#serviceworkercontainer){#ref-for-serviceworkercontainer②
link-type="idl"} object that is associated with
[this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this④
link-type="dfn"}.
:::

:::::::::: section
### [3.4. ]{.secno}[[`ServiceWorkerContainer`{.idl}](#serviceworkercontainer){#ref-for-serviceworkercontainer③ link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#serviceworkercontainer-interface){.self-link} {#serviceworkercontainer-interface .heading .settled level="3.4"}

``` {.def .highlight .idl}
[SecureContext, Exposed=(Window,Worker)]
interface ServiceWorkerContainer : EventTarget {
  readonly attribute ServiceWorker? controller;
  readonly attribute Promise<ServiceWorkerRegistration> ready;

  [NewObject] Promise<ServiceWorkerRegistration> register((TrustedScriptURL or USVString) scriptURL, optional RegistrationOptions options = {});

  [NewObject] Promise<(ServiceWorkerRegistration or undefined)> getRegistration(optional USVString clientURL = "");
  [NewObject] Promise<FrozenArray<ServiceWorkerRegistration>> getRegistrations();

  undefined startMessages();


  // events
  attribute EventHandler oncontrollerchange;
  attribute EventHandler onmessage; // event.source of message events is ServiceWorker object
  attribute EventHandler onmessageerror;
};
```

``` {#registration-option-list-dictionary .def .highlight .idl}
dictionary RegistrationOptions {
  USVString scope;
  WorkerType type = "classic";
  ServiceWorkerUpdateViaCache updateViaCache = "imports";
};
```

The user agent *must* create a
[`ServiceWorkerContainer`{.idl}](#serviceworkercontainer){#ref-for-serviceworkercontainer④
link-type="idl"} object when a
[`Navigator`{.idl}](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/system-state.html#navigator){#ref-for-navigator①
link-type="idl"} object or a
[`WorkerNavigator`{.idl}](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/workers.html#workernavigator){#ref-for-workernavigator①
link-type="idl"} object is created and associate it with that object.

A
[`ServiceWorkerContainer`{.idl}](#serviceworkercontainer){#ref-for-serviceworkercontainer⑤
link-type="idl"} provides capabilities to register, unregister, and
update the [service worker
registrations](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration③④
link-type="dfn"}, and provides access to the state of the [service
worker
registrations](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration③⑤
link-type="dfn"} and their associated [service
workers](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑤⑤
link-type="dfn"}.

A
[`ServiceWorkerContainer`{.idl}](#serviceworkercontainer){#ref-for-serviceworkercontainer⑥
link-type="idl"} has an associated [service worker
client]{#serviceworkercontainer-service-worker-client .dfn .dfn-paneled
dfn-for="ServiceWorkerContainer" dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}, which is a
[service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client②①
link-type="dfn"} whose [global
object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-global){#ref-for-concept-settings-object-global④
link-type="dfn"} is associated with the
[`Navigator`{.idl}](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/system-state.html#navigator){#ref-for-navigator②
link-type="idl"} object or the
[`WorkerNavigator`{.idl}](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/workers.html#workernavigator){#ref-for-workernavigator②
link-type="idl"} object that the
[`ServiceWorkerContainer`{.idl}](#serviceworkercontainer){#ref-for-serviceworkercontainer⑦
link-type="idl"} is retrieved from.

A
[`ServiceWorkerContainer`{.idl}](#serviceworkercontainer){#ref-for-serviceworkercontainer⑧
link-type="idl"} object has an associated [ready
promise]{#serviceworkercontainer-ready-promise .dfn .dfn-paneled
dfn-for="ServiceWorkerContainer" dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""} (a
[promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects){#ref-for-sec-promise-objects①
link-type="dfn"} or null). It is initially null.

A
[`ServiceWorkerContainer`{.idl}](#serviceworkercontainer){#ref-for-serviceworkercontainer⑨
link-type="idl"} object has a [task
source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#task-source){#ref-for-task-source③
link-type="dfn"} called the [client message
queue]{#dfn-client-message-queue .dfn .dfn-paneled
dfn-for="ServiceWorkerContainer" dfn-type="dfn" export=""}, initially
empty. A [client message
queue](#dfn-client-message-queue){#ref-for-dfn-client-message-queue
link-type="dfn"} can be enabled or disabled, and is initially disabled.
When a
[`ServiceWorkerContainer`{.idl}](#serviceworkercontainer){#ref-for-serviceworkercontainer①⓪
link-type="idl"} object's [client message
queue](#dfn-client-message-queue){#ref-for-dfn-client-message-queue①
link-type="dfn"} is enabled, the [event
loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-loop){#ref-for-event-loop⑤
link-type="dfn"} *must* use it as one of its [task
sources](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#task-source){#ref-for-task-source④
link-type="dfn"}. When the
[`ServiceWorkerContainer`{.idl}](#serviceworkercontainer){#ref-for-serviceworkercontainer①①
link-type="idl"} object's [relevant global
object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-global){#ref-for-concept-relevant-global①
link-type="dfn"} is a
[`Window`{.idl}](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#window){#ref-for-window②
link-type="idl"} object, all
[tasks](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-task){#ref-for-concept-task②
link-type="dfn"}
[queued](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task){#ref-for-queue-a-task②
link-type="dfn"} on its [client message
queue](#dfn-client-message-queue){#ref-for-dfn-client-message-queue②
link-type="dfn"} *must* be associated with its [relevant settings
object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object){#ref-for-relevant-settings-object④
link-type="dfn"}'s [associated
document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#concept-document-window){#ref-for-concept-document-window
link-type="dfn"}.

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="navigator-service-worker-controller"}
#### [3.4.1. ]{.secno}[[`controller`{.idl}](#dom-serviceworkercontainer-controller){#ref-for-dom-serviceworkercontainer-controller① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#navigator-service-worker-controller){.self-link} {#navigator-service-worker-controller .heading .settled level="3.4.1"}

[`controller`]{#dom-serviceworkercontainer-controller .dfn .dfn-paneled
.idl-code dfn-for="ServiceWorkerContainer" dfn-type="attribute"
export=""} attribute *must* run these steps:

1.  Let `client`{.variable} be
    [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this⑤
    link-type="dfn"}'s [service worker
    client](#serviceworkercontainer-service-worker-client){#ref-for-serviceworkercontainer-service-worker-client
    link-type="dfn"}.

2.  If `client`{.variable}'s [active service
    worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker){#ref-for-concept-environment-active-service-worker①⑧
    link-type="dfn"} is null, then return null.

3.  Return the result of [getting the service worker
    object](#get-the-service-worker-object){#ref-for-get-the-service-worker-object④
    link-type="dfn"} that represents `client`{.variable}'s [active
    service
    worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker){#ref-for-concept-environment-active-service-worker①⑨
    link-type="dfn"} in
    [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this⑥
    link-type="dfn"}'s [relevant settings
    object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object){#ref-for-relevant-settings-object⑤
    link-type="dfn"}.

[Note:]{.marker}
[`navigator.serviceWorker.controller`{.idl}](#dom-serviceworkercontainer-controller){#ref-for-dom-serviceworkercontainer-controller②
link-type="idl"} returns `null` if the request is a force refresh
(shift+refresh).
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="navigator-service-worker-ready"}
#### [3.4.2. ]{.secno}[[`ready`{.idl}](#dom-serviceworkercontainer-ready){#ref-for-dom-serviceworkercontainer-ready① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#navigator-service-worker-ready){.self-link} {#navigator-service-worker-ready .heading .settled level="3.4.2"}

[`ready`]{#dom-serviceworkercontainer-ready .dfn .dfn-paneled .idl-code
dfn-for="ServiceWorkerContainer" dfn-type="attribute" export=""}
attribute *must* run these steps:

1.  If [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this⑦
    link-type="dfn"}'s [ready
    promise](#serviceworkercontainer-ready-promise){#ref-for-serviceworkercontainer-ready-promise
    link-type="dfn"} is null, then set
    [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this⑧
    link-type="dfn"}'s [ready
    promise](#serviceworkercontainer-ready-promise){#ref-for-serviceworkercontainer-ready-promise①
    link-type="dfn"} to [a new
    promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-new-promise){#ref-for-a-new-promise①
    link-type="dfn"}.

2.  Let `readyPromise`{.variable} be
    [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this⑨
    link-type="dfn"}'s [ready
    promise](#serviceworkercontainer-ready-promise){#ref-for-serviceworkercontainer-ready-promise②
    link-type="dfn"}.

3.  If `readyPromise`{.variable} is pending, run the following substeps
    [in
    parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel){#ref-for-in-parallel①
    link-type="dfn"}:

    1.  Let `client`{.variable} by
        [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this①⓪
        link-type="dfn"}'s [service worker
        client](#serviceworkercontainer-service-worker-client){#ref-for-serviceworkercontainer-service-worker-client①
        link-type="dfn"}.

    2.  Let `storage key`{.variable} be the result of running [obtain a
        storage
        key](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#obtain-a-storage-key){#ref-for-obtain-a-storage-key
        link-type="dfn"} given client.

    3.  Let `registration`{.variable} be the result of running [Match
        Service Worker
        Registration](#match-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-match-service-worker-registration③
        link-type="dfn"} given `storage key`{.variable} and
        `client`{.variable}'s [creation
        URL](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-creation-url){#ref-for-concept-environment-creation-url①
        link-type="dfn"}.

    4.  If `registration`{.variable} is not null, and
        `registration`{.variable}'s [active
        worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker⑨
        link-type="dfn"} is not null, [queue a
        task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task){#ref-for-queue-a-task③
        link-type="dfn"} on `readyPromise`{.variable}'s [relevant
        settings
        object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object){#ref-for-relevant-settings-object⑥
        link-type="dfn"}'s [responsible event
        loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop){#ref-for-responsible-event-loop
        link-type="dfn"}, using the [DOM manipulation task
        source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source){#ref-for-dom-manipulation-task-source①
        link-type="dfn"}, to resolve `readyPromise`{.variable} with the
        result of [getting the service worker registration
        object](#get-the-service-worker-registration-object){#ref-for-get-the-service-worker-registration-object
        link-type="dfn"} that represents `registration`{.variable} in
        `readyPromise`{.variable}'s [relevant settings
        object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object){#ref-for-relevant-settings-object⑦
        link-type="dfn"}.

4.  Return `readyPromise`{.variable}.

[Note:]{.marker} The returned [ready
promise](#serviceworkercontainer-ready-promise){#ref-for-serviceworkercontainer-ready-promise③
link-type="dfn"} will never reject. If it does not resolve in this
algorithm, it will eventually resolve when a matching [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration③⑥
link-type="dfn"} is registered and its [active
worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker①⓪
link-type="dfn"} is set. (See the relevant [Activate algorithm
step](#activate-resolve-ready-step).)
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="navigator-service-worker-register"}
#### [3.4.3. ]{.secno}[[`register(scriptURL, options)`{.idl}](#dom-serviceworkercontainer-register){#ref-for-dom-serviceworkercontainer-register① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#navigator-service-worker-register){.self-link} {#navigator-service-worker-register .heading .settled level="3.4.3"}

[Note:]{.marker} The
[`register(scriptURL, options)`{.idl}](#dom-serviceworkercontainer-register){#ref-for-dom-serviceworkercontainer-register②
link-type="idl"} method creates or updates a [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration③⑦
link-type="dfn"} for the given [scope
url](#dfn-scope-url){#ref-for-dfn-scope-url⑧ link-type="dfn"}. If
successful, a [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration③⑧
link-type="dfn"} ties the provided `scriptURL`{.variable} to a [scope
url](#dfn-scope-url){#ref-for-dfn-scope-url⑨ link-type="dfn"}, which is
subsequently used for [navigation
matching](#handle-fetch){#ref-for-handle-fetch① link-type="dfn"}.

The
[`register(``scriptURL`{.variable}`, ``options`{.variable}`)`]{#dom-serviceworkercontainer-register
.dfn .dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="ServiceWorkerContainer"
dfn-type="method" export=""
lt="register(scriptURL, options)|register(scriptURL)"} method steps are:

1.  Let `p`{.variable} be a
    [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects){#ref-for-sec-promise-objects②
    link-type="dfn"}.

2.  Set `scriptURL`{.variable} to the result of invoking [Get Trusted
    Type compliant
    string](https://w3c.github.io/trusted-types/dist/spec/#get-trusted-type-compliant-string){#ref-for-get-trusted-type-compliant-string
    link-type="dfn"} with
    [`TrustedScriptURL`{.idl}](https://w3c.github.io/trusted-types/dist/spec/#trustedscripturl){#ref-for-trustedscripturl①
    link-type="idl"},
    [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this①①
    link-type="dfn"}'s [relevant global
    object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-global){#ref-for-concept-relevant-global②
    link-type="dfn"}, `scriptURL`{.variable}, \"ServiceWorkerContainer
    register\", and \"script\".

3.  Let `client`{.variable} be
    [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this①②
    link-type="dfn"}'s [service worker
    client](#serviceworkercontainer-service-worker-client){#ref-for-serviceworkercontainer-service-worker-client②
    link-type="dfn"}.

4.  Let `scriptURL`{.variable} be the result of
    [parsing](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-parser){#ref-for-concept-url-parser
    link-type="dfn"} `scriptURL`{.variable} with
    [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this①③
    link-type="dfn"}'s [relevant settings
    object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object){#ref-for-relevant-settings-object⑧
    link-type="dfn"}'s [API base
    URL](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#api-base-url){#ref-for-api-base-url
    link-type="dfn"}.

5.  Let `scopeURL`{.variable} be null.

6.  If
    `options`{.variable}\[\"[`scope`{.idl}](#dom-registrationoptions-scope){#ref-for-dom-registrationoptions-scope
    link-type="idl"}\"\]
    [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists){#ref-for-map-exists②
    link-type="dfn"}, set `scopeURL`{.variable} to the result of
    [parsing](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-parser){#ref-for-concept-url-parser①
    link-type="dfn"}
    `options`{.variable}\[\"[`scope`{.idl}](#dom-registrationoptions-scope){#ref-for-dom-registrationoptions-scope①
    link-type="idl"}\"\] with
    [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this①④
    link-type="dfn"}'s [relevant settings
    object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object){#ref-for-relevant-settings-object⑨
    link-type="dfn"}'s [API base
    URL](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#api-base-url){#ref-for-api-base-url①
    link-type="dfn"}.

7.  Invoke [Start Register](#start-register){#ref-for-start-register
    link-type="dfn"} with `scopeURL`{.variable}, `scriptURL`{.variable},
    `p`{.variable}, `client`{.variable}, `client`{.variable}'s [creation
    URL](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-creation-url){#ref-for-concept-environment-creation-url②
    link-type="dfn"},
    `options`{.variable}\[\"[`type`{.idl}](#dom-registrationoptions-type){#ref-for-dom-registrationoptions-type
    link-type="idl"}\"\], and
    `options`{.variable}\[\"[`updateViaCache`{.idl}](#dom-registrationoptions-updateviacache){#ref-for-dom-registrationoptions-updateviacache
    link-type="idl"}\"\].

8.  Return `p`{.variable}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="navigator-service-worker-getRegistration"}
#### [3.4.4. ]{.secno}[[`getRegistration(clientURL)`{.idl}](#dom-serviceworkercontainer-getregistration){#ref-for-dom-serviceworkercontainer-getregistration① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#navigator-service-worker-getRegistration){.self-link} {#navigator-service-worker-getRegistration .heading .settled level="3.4.4"}

[`getRegistration(``clientURL`{.variable}`)`]{#dom-serviceworkercontainer-getregistration
.dfn .dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="ServiceWorkerContainer"
dfn-type="method" export=""
lt="getRegistration(clientURL)|getRegistration()"} method steps are:

1.  Let `client`{.variable} be
    [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this①⑤
    link-type="dfn"}'s [service worker
    client](#serviceworkercontainer-service-worker-client){#ref-for-serviceworkercontainer-service-worker-client③
    link-type="dfn"}.

2.  Let `storage key`{.variable} be the result of running [obtain a
    storage
    key](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#obtain-a-storage-key){#ref-for-obtain-a-storage-key①
    link-type="dfn"} given `client`{.variable}.

3.  Let `clientURL`{.variable} be the result of
    [parsing](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-parser){#ref-for-concept-url-parser②
    link-type="dfn"} `clientURL`{.variable} with
    [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this①⑥
    link-type="dfn"}'s [relevant settings
    object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object){#ref-for-relevant-settings-object①⓪
    link-type="dfn"}'s [API base
    URL](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#api-base-url){#ref-for-api-base-url②
    link-type="dfn"}.

4.  If `clientURL`{.variable} is failure, return a
    [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects){#ref-for-sec-promise-objects③
    link-type="dfn"} rejected with a `TypeError`.

5.  Set `clientURL`{.variable}'s
    [fragment](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-fragment){#ref-for-concept-url-fragment
    link-type="dfn"} to null.

6.  If the
    [origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-origin){#ref-for-concept-settings-object-origin②
    link-type="dfn"} of `clientURL`{.variable} is not
    `client`{.variable}'s
    [origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-origin){#ref-for-concept-settings-object-origin③
    link-type="dfn"}, return a `promise`{.variable} rejected with a
    \"[`SecurityError`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#securityerror){#ref-for-securityerror
    link-type="idl"}\"
    [`DOMException`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException){#ref-for-idl-DOMException②
    link-type="idl"}.

7.  Let `promise`{.variable} be a new
    [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects){#ref-for-sec-promise-objects④
    link-type="dfn"}.

8.  Run the following substeps [in
    parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel){#ref-for-in-parallel②
    link-type="dfn"}:

    1.  Let `registration`{.variable} be the result of running [Match
        Service Worker
        Registration](#match-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-match-service-worker-registration④
        link-type="dfn"} given `storage key`{.variable} and
        `clientURL`{.variable}.

    2.  If `registration`{.variable} is null, resolve
        `promise`{.variable} with undefined and abort these steps.

    3.  Resolve `promise`{.variable} with the result of [getting the
        service worker registration
        object](#get-the-service-worker-registration-object){#ref-for-get-the-service-worker-registration-object①
        link-type="dfn"} that represents `registration`{.variable} in
        `promise`{.variable}'s [relevant settings
        object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object){#ref-for-relevant-settings-object①①
        link-type="dfn"}.

9.  Return `promise`{.variable}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="navigator-service-worker-getRegistrations"}
#### [3.4.5. ]{.secno}[[`getRegistrations()`{.idl}](#dom-serviceworkercontainer-getregistrations){#ref-for-dom-serviceworkercontainer-getregistrations① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#navigator-service-worker-getRegistrations){.self-link} {#navigator-service-worker-getRegistrations .heading .settled level="3.4.5"}

[`getRegistrations()`]{#dom-serviceworkercontainer-getregistrations .dfn
.dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="ServiceWorkerContainer"
dfn-type="method" export=""} method steps are:

1.  Let `client`{.variable} be
    [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this①⑦
    link-type="dfn"}'s [service worker
    client](#serviceworkercontainer-service-worker-client){#ref-for-serviceworkercontainer-service-worker-client④
    link-type="dfn"}.

2.  Let `client storage key`{.variable} be the result of running [obtain
    a storage
    key](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#obtain-a-storage-key){#ref-for-obtain-a-storage-key②
    link-type="dfn"} given `client`{.variable}.

3.  Let `promise`{.variable} be [a new
    promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-new-promise){#ref-for-a-new-promise②
    link-type="dfn"}.

4.  Run the following steps [in
    parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel){#ref-for-in-parallel③
    link-type="dfn"}:

    1.  Let `registrations`{.variable} be a new
        [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list){#ref-for-list②
        link-type="dfn"}.

    2.  [For
        each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate){#ref-for-list-iterate
        link-type="dfn"} (`storage key`{.variable}, `scope`{.variable})
        → `registration`{.variable} of [registration
        map](#dfn-scope-to-registration-map){#ref-for-dfn-scope-to-registration-map②
        link-type="dfn"}:

        1.  If `storage key`{.variable}
            [equals](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#storage-key-equal){#ref-for-storage-key-equal
            link-type="dfn"} `client storage key`{.variable}, then
            [append](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-append){#ref-for-list-append
            link-type="dfn"} `registration`{.variable} to
            `registrations`{.variable}.

    3.  [Queue a
        task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task){#ref-for-queue-a-task④
        link-type="dfn"} on `promise`{.variable}'s [relevant settings
        object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object){#ref-for-relevant-settings-object①②
        link-type="dfn"}'s [responsible event
        loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop){#ref-for-responsible-event-loop①
        link-type="dfn"}, using the [DOM manipulation task
        source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source){#ref-for-dom-manipulation-task-source②
        link-type="dfn"}, to run the following steps:

        1.  Let `registrationObjects`{.variable} be a new
            [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list){#ref-for-list③
            link-type="dfn"}.

        2.  [For
            each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate){#ref-for-list-iterate①
            link-type="dfn"} `registration`{.variable} of
            `registrations`{.variable}:

            1.  Let `registrationObj`{.variable} be the result of
                [getting the service worker registration
                object](#get-the-service-worker-registration-object){#ref-for-get-the-service-worker-registration-object②
                link-type="dfn"} that represents
                `registration`{.variable} in `promise`{.variable}'s
                [relevant settings
                object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object){#ref-for-relevant-settings-object①③
                link-type="dfn"}.

            2.  [Append](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-append){#ref-for-list-append①
                link-type="dfn"} `registrationObj`{.variable} to
                `registrationObjects`{.variable}.

        3.  Resolve `promise`{.variable} with [a new frozen array
            of](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-create-frozen-array){#ref-for-dfn-create-frozen-array
            link-type="dfn"} `registrationObjects`{.variable} in
            `promise`{.variable}'s [relevant
            Realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-realm){#ref-for-concept-relevant-realm
            link-type="dfn"}.

5.  Return `promise`{.variable}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="navigator-service-worker-startMessages"}
#### [3.4.6. ]{.secno}[[`startMessages()`{.idl}](#dom-serviceworkercontainer-startmessages){#ref-for-dom-serviceworkercontainer-startmessages① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#navigator-service-worker-startMessages){.self-link} {#navigator-service-worker-startMessages .heading .settled level="3.4.6"}

The [`startMessages()`]{#dom-serviceworkercontainer-startmessages .dfn
.dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="ServiceWorkerContainer"
dfn-type="method" export=""} method steps are to enable
[this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this①⑧
link-type="dfn"}'s [client message
queue](#dfn-client-message-queue){#ref-for-dfn-client-message-queue③
link-type="dfn"} if it is not enabled.
:::

::: section
#### [3.4.7. ]{.secno}[Event handlers]{.content}[](#service-worker-container-event-handlers){.self-link} {#service-worker-container-event-handlers .heading .settled level="3.4.7"}

The following are the [event
handlers](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-handlers){#ref-for-event-handlers④
link-type="dfn"} (and their corresponding [event handler event
types](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-handler-event-type){#ref-for-event-handler-event-type④
link-type="dfn"}) that *must* be supported, as [event handler IDL
attributes](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-handler-idl-attributes){#ref-for-event-handler-idl-attributes②
link-type="dfn"}, by all objects implementing the
[`ServiceWorkerContainer`{.idl}](#serviceworkercontainer){#ref-for-serviceworkercontainer①②
link-type="idl"} interface:

[event
handler](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-handlers){#ref-for-event-handlers⑤
link-type="dfn"}

[event handler event
type](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-handler-event-type){#ref-for-event-handler-event-type⑤
link-type="dfn"}

[`oncontrollerchange`]{#dom-serviceworkercontainer-oncontrollerchange
.dfn .dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="ServiceWorkerContainer"
dfn-type="attribute" export=""}

[`controllerchange`{.idl}](#service-worker-container-controllerchange-event){#ref-for-service-worker-container-controllerchange-event
.idl-code link-type="event"}

[`onmessage`]{#dom-serviceworkercontainer-onmessage .dfn .dfn-paneled
.idl-code dfn-for="ServiceWorkerContainer" dfn-type="attribute"
export=""}

[`message`{.idl}](#service-worker-container-message-event){#ref-for-service-worker-container-message-event
.idl-code link-type="event"}

[`onmessageerror`]{#dom-serviceworkercontainer-onmessageerror .dfn
.dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="ServiceWorkerContainer"
dfn-type="attribute" export=""}

[`messageerror`{.idl}](#service-worker-container-messageerror-event){#ref-for-service-worker-container-messageerror-event
.idl-code link-type="event"}

The first time the
[`onmessage`{.idl}](#dom-serviceworkercontainer-onmessage){#ref-for-dom-serviceworkercontainer-onmessage①
link-type="idl"} setter steps are performed, enable
[this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this①⑨
link-type="dfn"}'s [client message
queue](#dfn-client-message-queue){#ref-for-dfn-client-message-queue④
link-type="dfn"}.
:::
::::::::::

::: section
### [3.5. ]{.secno}[Events]{.content}[](#document-context-events){.self-link} {#document-context-events .heading .settled level="3.5"}

The following event is dispatched on
[`ServiceWorker`{.idl}](#serviceworker){#ref-for-serviceworker①⑧
link-type="idl"} object:

Event name

Interface

Dispatched when...

[`statechange`]{#eventdef-serviceworker-statechange .dfn .dfn-paneled
.idl-code dfn-for="ServiceWorker" dfn-type="event" export=""}

[`Event`{.idl}](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#event){#ref-for-event
link-type="idl"}

The
[`state`{.idl}](#dom-serviceworker-state){#ref-for-dom-serviceworker-state③
link-type="idl"} attribute of the
[`ServiceWorker`{.idl}](#serviceworker){#ref-for-serviceworker①⑨
link-type="idl"} object is changed.

The following event is dispatched on
[`ServiceWorkerRegistration`{.idl}](#serviceworkerregistration){#ref-for-serviceworkerregistration①②
link-type="idl"} object:

Event name

Interface

Dispatched when...

[`updatefound`]{#service-worker-registration-updatefound-event .dfn
.dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="ServiceWorkerRegistration"
dfn-type="event" export=""}

[`Event`{.idl}](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#event){#ref-for-event①
link-type="idl"}

The [service worker
registration](#serviceworkerregistration-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-serviceworkerregistration-service-worker-registration⑥
link-type="dfn"}'s [installing
worker](#dfn-installing-worker){#ref-for-dfn-installing-worker⑤
link-type="dfn"} changes. (See step 8 of the
[Install](#install){#ref-for-install link-type="dfn"} algorithm.)

The following events are dispatched on
[`ServiceWorkerContainer`{.idl}](#serviceworkercontainer){#ref-for-serviceworkercontainer①③
link-type="idl"} object:

Event name

Interface

Dispatched when...

[`controllerchange`]{#service-worker-container-controllerchange-event
.dfn .dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="ServiceWorkerContainer"
dfn-type="event" export=""}

[`Event`{.idl}](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#event){#ref-for-event②
link-type="idl"}

The [service worker
client](#serviceworkercontainer-service-worker-client){#ref-for-serviceworkercontainer-service-worker-client⑤
link-type="dfn"}'s [active service
worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker){#ref-for-concept-environment-active-service-worker②⓪
link-type="dfn"} changes. (See step 9.2 of the
[Activate](#activate){#ref-for-activate link-type="dfn"} algorithm. The
[skip waiting
flag](#dfn-skip-waiting-flag){#ref-for-dfn-skip-waiting-flag
link-type="dfn"} of a [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑤⑥
link-type="dfn"} causes [activation](#activate){#ref-for-activate①
link-type="dfn"} of the [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration③⑨
link-type="dfn"} to occur while [service worker
clients](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client②②
link-type="dfn"} are [using](#dfn-use){#ref-for-dfn-use link-type="dfn"}
the [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration④⓪
link-type="dfn"},
[`navigator.serviceWorker.controller`{.idl}](#dom-serviceworkercontainer-controller){#ref-for-dom-serviceworkercontainer-controller③
link-type="idl"} immediately reflects the [active
worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker①①
link-type="dfn"} as the [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑤⑦
link-type="dfn"} that [controls](#dfn-control){#ref-for-dfn-control②
link-type="dfn"} the [service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client②③
link-type="dfn"}.)

[`message`]{#service-worker-container-message-event .dfn .dfn-paneled
.idl-code dfn-for="ServiceWorkerContainer" dfn-type="event" export=""}

[`Event`{.idl}](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#event){#ref-for-event③
link-type="idl"}

The [service worker
client](#serviceworkercontainer-service-worker-client){#ref-for-serviceworkercontainer-service-worker-client⑥
link-type="dfn"} receives a message from a [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑤⑧
link-type="dfn"}. See
[`postMessage(message, options)`{.idl}](#dom-client-postmessage-message-options){#ref-for-dom-client-postmessage-message-options
link-type="idl"}.

[`messageerror`]{#service-worker-container-messageerror-event .dfn
.dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="ServiceWorkerContainer" dfn-type="event"
export=""}

[`Event`{.idl}](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#event){#ref-for-event④
link-type="idl"}

The [service worker
client](#serviceworkercontainer-service-worker-client){#ref-for-serviceworkercontainer-service-worker-client⑦
link-type="dfn"} is sent a message that cannot be deserialized from a
[service worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑤⑨
link-type="dfn"}. See
[`postMessage(message, options)`{.idl}](#dom-client-postmessage-message-options){#ref-for-dom-client-postmessage-message-options①
link-type="idl"}.
:::
::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::

::::::: section
### [3.6. ]{.secno}[[`NavigationPreloadManager`{.idl}](#navigationpreloadmanager){#ref-for-navigationpreloadmanager③ link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#navigation-preload-manager){.self-link} {#navigation-preload-manager .heading .settled level="3.6"}

``` {.def .highlight .idl}
[SecureContext, Exposed=(Window,Worker)]
interface NavigationPreloadManager {
  Promise<undefined> enable();
  Promise<undefined> disable();
  Promise<undefined> setHeaderValue(ByteString value);
  Promise<NavigationPreloadState> getState();
};

dictionary NavigationPreloadState {
  boolean enabled = false;
  ByteString headerValue;
};
```

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="navigation-preload-manager-enable"}
#### [3.6.1. ]{.secno}[[`enable()`{.idl}](#dom-navigationpreloadmanager-enable){#ref-for-dom-navigationpreloadmanager-enable① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#navigation-preload-manager-enable){.self-link} {#navigation-preload-manager-enable .heading .settled level="3.6.1"}

The [`enable()`]{#dom-navigationpreloadmanager-enable .dfn .dfn-paneled
.idl-code dfn-for="NavigationPreloadManager" dfn-type="method"
export=""} method steps are:

1.  Let `promise`{.variable} be [a new
    promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-new-promise){#ref-for-a-new-promise③
    link-type="dfn"}.

2.  Run the following steps [in
    parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel){#ref-for-in-parallel④
    link-type="dfn"}:

    1.  Let `registration`{.variable} be
        [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this②⓪
        link-type="dfn"}'s associated [service worker
        registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration④①
        link-type="dfn"}.

    2.  If `registration`{.variable}'s [active
        worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker①②
        link-type="dfn"} is null,
        [reject](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#reject){#ref-for-reject
        link-type="dfn"} `promise`{.variable} with an
        \"[`InvalidStateError`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#invalidstateerror){#ref-for-invalidstateerror②
        link-type="idl"}\"
        [`DOMException`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException){#ref-for-idl-DOMException③
        link-type="idl"}, and abort these steps.

    3.  Set `registration`{.variable}'s [navigation preload enabled
        flag](#service-worker-registration-navigation-preload-enabled-flag){#ref-for-service-worker-registration-navigation-preload-enabled-flag
        link-type="dfn"}.

    4.  Resolve `promise`{.variable} with undefined.

3.  Return `promise`{.variable}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="navigation-preload-manager-disable"}
#### [3.6.2. ]{.secno}[[`disable()`{.idl}](#dom-navigationpreloadmanager-disable){#ref-for-dom-navigationpreloadmanager-disable① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#navigation-preload-manager-disable){.self-link} {#navigation-preload-manager-disable .heading .settled level="3.6.2"}

The [`disable()`]{#dom-navigationpreloadmanager-disable .dfn
.dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="NavigationPreloadManager"
dfn-type="method" export=""} method steps are:

1.  Let `promise`{.variable} be [a new
    promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-new-promise){#ref-for-a-new-promise④
    link-type="dfn"}.

2.  Run the following steps [in
    parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel){#ref-for-in-parallel⑤
    link-type="dfn"}:

    1.  Let `registration`{.variable} be
        [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this②①
        link-type="dfn"}'s associated [service worker
        registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration④②
        link-type="dfn"}.

    2.  If `registration`{.variable}'s [active
        worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker①③
        link-type="dfn"} is null,
        [reject](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#reject){#ref-for-reject①
        link-type="dfn"} `promise`{.variable} with an
        \"[`InvalidStateError`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#invalidstateerror){#ref-for-invalidstateerror③
        link-type="idl"}\"
        [`DOMException`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException){#ref-for-idl-DOMException④
        link-type="idl"}, and abort these steps.

    3.  Unset `registration`{.variable}'s [navigation preload enabled
        flag](#service-worker-registration-navigation-preload-enabled-flag){#ref-for-service-worker-registration-navigation-preload-enabled-flag①
        link-type="dfn"}.

    4.  Resolve `promise`{.variable} with undefined.

3.  Return `promise`{.variable}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="navigation-preload-manager-setheadervalue"}
#### [3.6.3. ]{.secno}[[`setHeaderValue(value)`{.idl}](#dom-navigationpreloadmanager-setheadervalue){#ref-for-dom-navigationpreloadmanager-setheadervalue① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#navigation-preload-manager-setheadervalue){.self-link} {#navigation-preload-manager-setheadervalue .heading .settled level="3.6.3"}

The
[`setHeaderValue(``value`{.variable}`)`]{#dom-navigationpreloadmanager-setheadervalue
.dfn .dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="NavigationPreloadManager"
dfn-type="method" export=""} method steps are:

1.  Let `promise`{.variable} be [a new
    promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-new-promise){#ref-for-a-new-promise⑤
    link-type="dfn"}.

2.  Run the following steps [in
    parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel){#ref-for-in-parallel⑥
    link-type="dfn"}:

    1.  Let `registration`{.variable} be
        [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this②②
        link-type="dfn"}'s associated [service worker
        registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration④③
        link-type="dfn"}.

    2.  If `registration`{.variable}'s [active
        worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker①④
        link-type="dfn"} is null,
        [reject](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#reject){#ref-for-reject②
        link-type="dfn"} `promise`{.variable} with an
        \"[`InvalidStateError`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#invalidstateerror){#ref-for-invalidstateerror④
        link-type="idl"}\"
        [`DOMException`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException){#ref-for-idl-DOMException⑤
        link-type="idl"}, and abort these steps.

    3.  Set `registration`{.variable}'s [navigation preload header
        value](#service-worker-registration-navigation-preload-header-value){#ref-for-service-worker-registration-navigation-preload-header-value
        link-type="dfn"} to `value`{.variable}.

    4.  Resolve `promise`{.variable} with undefined.

3.  Return `promise`{.variable}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="navigation-preload-manager-getstate"}
#### [3.6.4. ]{.secno}[[`getState()`{.idl}](#dom-navigationpreloadmanager-getstate){#ref-for-dom-navigationpreloadmanager-getstate① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#navigation-preload-manager-getstate){.self-link} {#navigation-preload-manager-getstate .heading .settled level="3.6.4"}

The [`getState()`]{#dom-navigationpreloadmanager-getstate .dfn
.dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="NavigationPreloadManager"
dfn-type="method" export=""} method steps are:

1.  Let `promise`{.variable} be [a new
    promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-new-promise){#ref-for-a-new-promise⑥
    link-type="dfn"}.

2.  Run the following steps [in
    parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel){#ref-for-in-parallel⑦
    link-type="dfn"}:

    1.  Let `registration`{.variable} be
        [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this②③
        link-type="dfn"}'s associated [service worker
        registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration④④
        link-type="dfn"}.

    2.  Let `state`{.variable} be a new
        [`NavigationPreloadState`{.idl}](#dictdef-navigationpreloadstate){#ref-for-dictdef-navigationpreloadstate①
        link-type="idl"} dictionary.

    3.  If `registration`{.variable}'s [navigation preload enabled
        flag](#service-worker-registration-navigation-preload-enabled-flag){#ref-for-service-worker-registration-navigation-preload-enabled-flag②
        link-type="dfn"} is set, set
        `state`{.variable}\[\"[`enabled`{.idl}](#dom-navigationpreloadstate-enabled){#ref-for-dom-navigationpreloadstate-enabled
        link-type="idl"}\"\] to true.

    4.  Set
        `state`{.variable}\[\"[`headerValue`{.idl}](#dom-navigationpreloadstate-headervalue){#ref-for-dom-navigationpreloadstate-headervalue
        link-type="idl"}\"\] to `registration`{.variable}'s [navigation
        preload header
        value](#service-worker-registration-navigation-preload-header-value){#ref-for-service-worker-registration-navigation-preload-header-value①
        link-type="dfn"}.

    5.  Resolve `promise`{.variable} with `state`{.variable}.

3.  Return `promise`{.variable}.
:::
:::::::

::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: section
## [4. ]{.secno}[Execution Context]{.content}[](#execution-context){.self-link} {#execution-context .heading .settled level="4"}

::: {#example-b1dcfb45 .example}
[](#example-b1dcfb45){.self-link} Serving Cached Resources:

``` highlight
// caching.js
self.addEventListener("install", event => {
  event.waitUntil(
    // Open a cache of resources.
    caches.open("shell-v1").then(cache => {
      // Begins the process of fetching them. Succeeds only once all
      // resources have been stored. Even just one failing resource
      // causes the entire operation to fail.
      return cache.addAll([
        "/app.html",
        "/assets/v1/base.css",
        "/assets/v1/app.js",
        "/assets/v1/logo.png",
        "/assets/v1/intro_video.webm"
      ]);
    })
  );
});

self.addEventListener("fetch", event => {
  // No "fetch" events are dispatched to the service worker until it
  // successfully installs and activates.

  // All operations on caches are async, including matching URLs, so we use
  // promises heavily. e.respondWith() even takes promises to enable this:
  event.respondWith(
    caches.match(e.request).then(response => {
      return response || fetch(e.request);
    }).catch(() => {
      return caches.match("/fallback.html");
    })
  );
});
```
:::

:::::::: section
### [4.1. ]{.secno}[[`ServiceWorkerGlobalScope`{.idl}](#serviceworkerglobalscope){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope④ link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#serviceworkerglobalscope-interface){.self-link} {#serviceworkerglobalscope-interface .heading .settled level="4.1"}

``` {.def .highlight .idl}
[Global=(Worker,ServiceWorker), Exposed=ServiceWorker, SecureContext]
interface ServiceWorkerGlobalScope : WorkerGlobalScope {
  [SameObject] readonly attribute Clients clients;
  [SameObject] readonly attribute ServiceWorkerRegistration registration;
  [SameObject] readonly attribute ServiceWorker serviceWorker;

  [NewObject] Promise<undefined> skipWaiting();

  attribute EventHandler oninstall;
  attribute EventHandler onactivate;
  attribute EventHandler onfetch;

  attribute EventHandler onmessage;
  attribute EventHandler onmessageerror;
};
```

A
[`ServiceWorkerGlobalScope`{.idl}](#serviceworkerglobalscope){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope⑤
link-type="idl"} object represents the global execution context of a
[service worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑥⓪
link-type="dfn"}.

A
[`ServiceWorkerGlobalScope`{.idl}](#serviceworkerglobalscope){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope⑥
link-type="idl"} object has an associated [service
worker]{#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker .dfn .dfn-paneled
dfn-for="ServiceWorkerGlobalScope" dfn-type="dfn" export=""} (a [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑥①
link-type="dfn"}).

A
[`ServiceWorkerGlobalScope`{.idl}](#serviceworkerglobalscope){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope⑦
link-type="idl"} object has an associated [force bypass cache for import
scripts
flag]{#serviceworkerglobalscope-force-bypass-cache-for-import-scripts-flag
.dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-for="ServiceWorkerGlobalScope" dfn-type="dfn"
noexport=""}. It is initially unset.

A
[`ServiceWorkerGlobalScope`{.idl}](#serviceworkerglobalscope){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope⑧
link-type="idl"} object has an associated [race response
map]{#serviceworkerglobalscope-race-response-map .dfn .dfn-paneled
dfn-for="ServiceWorkerGlobalScope" dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""} which is
an [ordered
map](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ordered-map){#ref-for-ordered-map③
link-type="dfn"} where the
[keys](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-getting-the-keys){#ref-for-map-getting-the-keys②
link-type="dfn"} are
[requests](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request){#ref-for-concept-request②
link-type="dfn"} and the
[values](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-getting-the-values){#ref-for-map-getting-the-values②
link-type="dfn"} are [race
response](#dfn-race-response){#ref-for-dfn-race-response
link-type="dfn"}.

A [race response]{#dfn-race-response .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn"
noexport=""} is a
[struct](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct){#ref-for-struct①
link-type="dfn"} used to contain the network response when
[`"race-network-and-fetch-handler"`{.idl}](#dom-routersourceenum-race-network-and-fetch-handler){#ref-for-dom-routersourceenum-race-network-and-fetch-handler
link-type="idl"} performs. It has a [value]{#race-response-value .dfn
.dfn-paneled dfn-for="race response" dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}, which
is a
[response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response){#ref-for-concept-response①
link-type="dfn"}, \"`pending`\", or null.

[Note:]{.marker}
[`ServiceWorkerGlobalScope`{.idl}](#serviceworkerglobalscope){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope⑨
link-type="idl"} object provides generic, event-driven, time-limited
script execution contexts that run at an origin. Once successfully
[registered](#register){#ref-for-register① link-type="dfn"}, a [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑥②
link-type="dfn"} is started, kept alive and killed by their relationship
to events, not [service worker
clients](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client②④
link-type="dfn"}. Any type of synchronous requests must not be initiated
inside of a [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑥③
link-type="dfn"}.

::: section
#### [4.1.1. ]{.secno}[[`clients`{.idl}](#dom-serviceworkerglobalscope-clients){#ref-for-dom-serviceworkerglobalscope-clients① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#service-worker-global-scope-clients){.self-link} {#service-worker-global-scope-clients .heading .settled level="4.1.1"}

The [`clients`]{#dom-serviceworkerglobalscope-clients .dfn .dfn-paneled
.idl-code dfn-for="ServiceWorkerGlobalScope" dfn-type="attribute"
export=""} getter steps are to return the
[`Clients`{.idl}](#clients){#ref-for-clients① link-type="idl"} object
that is associated with
[this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this②④
link-type="dfn"}.
:::

::: section
#### [4.1.2. ]{.secno}[[`registration`{.idl}](#dom-serviceworkerglobalscope-registration){#ref-for-dom-serviceworkerglobalscope-registration① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#service-worker-global-scope-registration){.self-link} {#service-worker-global-scope-registration .heading .settled level="4.1.2"}

The [`registration`]{#dom-serviceworkerglobalscope-registration .dfn
.dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="ServiceWorkerGlobalScope"
dfn-type="attribute" export=""} getter steps are to return the result of
[getting the service worker registration
object](#get-the-service-worker-registration-object){#ref-for-get-the-service-worker-registration-object③
link-type="dfn"} representing
[this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this②⑤
link-type="dfn"}'s [service
worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker②
link-type="dfn"}'s [containing service worker
registration](#dfn-containing-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-containing-service-worker-registration②
link-type="dfn"} in
[this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this②⑥
link-type="dfn"}'s [relevant settings
object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object){#ref-for-relevant-settings-object①④
link-type="dfn"}.
:::

::: section
#### [4.1.3. ]{.secno}[[`serviceWorker`{.idl}](#dom-serviceworkerglobalscope-serviceworker){#ref-for-dom-serviceworkerglobalscope-serviceworker① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#service-worker-global-scope-serviceworker){.self-link} {#service-worker-global-scope-serviceworker .heading .settled level="4.1.3"}

The [`serviceWorker`]{#dom-serviceworkerglobalscope-serviceworker .dfn
.dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="ServiceWorkerGlobalScope"
dfn-type="attribute" export=""} getter steps are to return the result of
[getting the service worker
object](#get-the-service-worker-object){#ref-for-get-the-service-worker-object⑤
link-type="dfn"} that represents
[this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this②⑦
link-type="dfn"}'s [service
worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker③
link-type="dfn"} in
[this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this②⑧
link-type="dfn"}'s [relevant settings
object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object){#ref-for-relevant-settings-object①⑤
link-type="dfn"}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="service-worker-global-scope-skipwaiting"}
#### [4.1.4. ]{.secno}[[`skipWaiting()`{.idl}](#dom-serviceworkerglobalscope-skipwaiting){#ref-for-dom-serviceworkerglobalscope-skipwaiting① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#service-worker-global-scope-skipwaiting){.self-link} {#service-worker-global-scope-skipwaiting .heading .settled level="4.1.4"}

[Note:]{.marker} The
[`skipWaiting()`{.idl}](#dom-serviceworkerglobalscope-skipwaiting){#ref-for-dom-serviceworkerglobalscope-skipwaiting②
link-type="idl"} method allows this [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑥④
link-type="dfn"} to progress from the
[registration](#dfn-containing-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-containing-service-worker-registration③
link-type="dfn"}'s
[waiting](#dfn-waiting-worker){#ref-for-dfn-waiting-worker④
link-type="dfn"} position to
[active](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker①⑤
link-type="dfn"} even while [service worker
clients](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client②⑤
link-type="dfn"} are [using](#dfn-use){#ref-for-dfn-use①
link-type="dfn"} the
[registration](#dfn-containing-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-containing-service-worker-registration④
link-type="dfn"}.

The [`skipWaiting()`]{#dom-serviceworkerglobalscope-skipwaiting .dfn
.dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="ServiceWorkerGlobalScope"
dfn-type="method" export=""} method steps are:

1.  Let `promise`{.variable} be a new
    [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects){#ref-for-sec-promise-objects⑤
    link-type="dfn"}.

2.  Run the following substeps [in
    parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel){#ref-for-in-parallel⑧
    link-type="dfn"}:

    1.  Set [service
        worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker④
        link-type="dfn"}'s [skip waiting
        flag](#dfn-skip-waiting-flag){#ref-for-dfn-skip-waiting-flag①
        link-type="dfn"}.

    2.  Invoke [Try Activate](#try-activate){#ref-for-try-activate
        link-type="dfn"} with [service
        worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker⑤
        link-type="dfn"}'s [containing service worker
        registration](#dfn-containing-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-containing-service-worker-registration⑤
        link-type="dfn"}.

    3.  Resolve `promise`{.variable} with undefined.

3.  Return `promise`{.variable}.
:::

::: section
#### [4.1.5. ]{.secno}[Event handlers]{.content}[](#service-worker-global-scope-event-handlers){.self-link} {#service-worker-global-scope-event-handlers .heading .settled level="4.1.5"}

The following are the [event
handlers](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-handlers){#ref-for-event-handlers⑥
link-type="dfn"} (and their corresponding [event handler event
types](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-handler-event-type){#ref-for-event-handler-event-type⑥
link-type="dfn"}) that *must* be supported, as [event handler IDL
attributes](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-handler-idl-attributes){#ref-for-event-handler-idl-attributes③
link-type="dfn"}, by all objects implementing the
[`ServiceWorkerGlobalScope`{.idl}](#serviceworkerglobalscope){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope①⓪
link-type="idl"} interface:

[event
handler](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-handlers){#ref-for-event-handlers⑦
link-type="dfn"}

[event handler event
type](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-handler-event-type){#ref-for-event-handler-event-type⑦
link-type="dfn"}

[`oninstall`]{#dom-serviceworkerglobalscope-oninstall .dfn .dfn-paneled
.idl-code dfn-for="ServiceWorkerGlobalScope" dfn-type="attribute"
export=""}

[`install`{.idl}](#service-worker-global-scope-install-event){#ref-for-service-worker-global-scope-install-event①
.idl-code link-type="event"}

[`onactivate`]{#dom-serviceworkerglobalscope-onactivate .dfn
.dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="ServiceWorkerGlobalScope"
dfn-type="attribute" export=""}

[`activate`{.idl}](#service-worker-global-scope-activate-event){#ref-for-service-worker-global-scope-activate-event①
.idl-code link-type="event"}

[`onfetch`]{#dom-serviceworkerglobalscope-onfetch .dfn .dfn-paneled
.idl-code dfn-for="ServiceWorkerGlobalScope" dfn-type="attribute"
export=""}

[`fetch`{.idl}](#service-worker-global-scope-fetch-event){#ref-for-service-worker-global-scope-fetch-event②
.idl-code link-type="event"}

[`onmessage`]{#dom-serviceworkerglobalscope-onmessage .dfn .dfn-paneled
.idl-code dfn-for="ServiceWorkerGlobalScope" dfn-type="attribute"
export=""}

[`message`{.idl}](#eventdef-serviceworkerglobalscope-message){#ref-for-eventdef-serviceworkerglobalscope-message②
.idl-code link-type="event"}

[`onmessageerror`]{#dom-serviceworkerglobalscope-onmessageerror .dfn
.dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="ServiceWorkerGlobalScope"
dfn-type="attribute" export=""}

[`messageerror`{.idl}](#eventdef-serviceworkerglobalscope-messageerror){#ref-for-eventdef-serviceworkerglobalscope-messageerror②
.idl-code link-type="event"}
:::
::::::::

:::::::::::::: section
### [4.2. ]{.secno}[[`Client`{.idl}](#client){#ref-for-client① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#client-interface){.self-link} {#client-interface .heading .settled level="4.2"}

``` {.def .highlight .idl}
[Exposed=ServiceWorker]
interface Client {
  readonly attribute USVString url;
  readonly attribute FrameType frameType;
  readonly attribute DOMString id;
  readonly attribute ClientType type;
  undefined postMessage(any message, sequence<object> transfer);
```
