``` {.def .highlight .idl}
  undefined postMessage(any message, optional StructuredSerializeOptions options = {});
};

[Exposed=ServiceWorker]
interface WindowClient : Client {
  readonly attribute VisibilityState visibilityState;
  readonly attribute boolean focused;
  [SameObject] readonly attribute FrozenArray<USVString> ancestorOrigins;
  [NewObject] Promise<WindowClient> focus();
  [NewObject] Promise<WindowClient?> navigate(USVString url);
};

enum FrameType {
  "auxiliary",
  "top-level",
  "nested",
  "none"
};
```

A [`Client`{.idl}](#client){#ref-for-client③ link-type="idl"} object has
an associated [service worker
client]{#dfn-service-worker-client-service-worker-client .dfn
.dfn-paneled dfn-for="Client" dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""} (a [service
worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client②⑥
link-type="dfn"}).

A [`Client`{.idl}](#client){#ref-for-client④ link-type="idl"} object has
an associated [frame type]{#dfn-service-worker-client-frame-type .dfn
.dfn-paneled dfn-for="Client" dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}, which is one
of \"`auxiliary`\", \"`top-level`\", \"`nested`\", and \"`none`\".
Unless stated otherwise it is \"`none`\".

A [`WindowClient`{.idl}](#windowclient){#ref-for-windowclient③
link-type="idl"} object has an associated [browsing
context]{#dfn-service-worker-client-browsing-context .dfn .dfn-paneled
dfn-for="WindowClient" dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}, which is its
[service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client-service-worker-client
link-type="dfn"}'s [global
object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-global){#ref-for-concept-settings-object-global⑤
link-type="dfn"}'s [browsing
context](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#browsing-context){#ref-for-browsing-context⑤
link-type="dfn"}.

A [`WindowClient`{.idl}](#windowclient){#ref-for-windowclient④
link-type="idl"} object has an associated [visibility
state]{#dfn-service-worker-client-visibilitystate .dfn .dfn-paneled
dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}, which is one of
[`visibilityState`{.idl}](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/interaction.html#page-visibility){#ref-for-page-visibility①
link-type="idl" refhint-key="d7e375d3"} attribute value.

A [`WindowClient`{.idl}](#windowclient){#ref-for-windowclient⑤
link-type="idl"} object has an associated [focus
state]{#dfn-service-worker-client-focusstate .dfn .dfn-paneled
dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}, which is either true or false (initially
false).

A [`WindowClient`{.idl}](#windowclient){#ref-for-windowclient⑥
link-type="idl"} object has an associated [ancestor origins
array]{#windowclient-ancestor-origins-array .dfn .dfn-paneled
dfn-for="WindowClient" dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}.

::: section
#### [4.2.1. ]{.secno}[[`url`{.idl}](#dom-client-url){#ref-for-dom-client-url① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#client-url){.self-link} {#client-url .heading .settled level="4.2.1"}

The [`url`]{#dom-client-url .dfn .dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="Client"
dfn-type="attribute" export=""} getter steps are to return
[this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this②⑨
link-type="dfn"}'s associated [service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client-service-worker-client①
link-type="dfn"}'s
[serialized](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-serializer){#ref-for-concept-url-serializer④
link-type="dfn"} [creation
URL](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-creation-url){#ref-for-concept-environment-creation-url③
link-type="dfn"}.
:::

::: section
#### [4.2.2. ]{.secno}[[`frameType`{.idl}](#dom-client-frametype){#ref-for-dom-client-frametype① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#client-frametype){.self-link} {#client-frametype .heading .settled level="4.2.2"}

The [`frameType`]{#dom-client-frametype .dfn .dfn-paneled .idl-code
dfn-for="Client" dfn-type="attribute" export=""} getter steps are to
return [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this③⓪
link-type="dfn"}'s [frame
type](#dfn-service-worker-client-frame-type){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client-frame-type
link-type="dfn"}.
:::

::: section
#### [4.2.3. ]{.secno}[[`id`{.idl}](#dom-client-id){#ref-for-dom-client-id① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#client-id){.self-link} {#client-id .heading .settled level="4.2.3"}

The [`id`]{#dom-client-id .dfn .dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="Client"
dfn-type="attribute" export=""} getter steps are to return
[this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this③①
link-type="dfn"}'s associated [service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client-service-worker-client②
link-type="dfn"}'s
[id](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-id){#ref-for-concept-environment-id
link-type="dfn"}.
:::

::: section
#### [4.2.4. ]{.secno}[[`type`{.idl}](#dom-client-type){#ref-for-dom-client-type① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#client-type){.self-link} {#client-type .heading .settled level="4.2.4"}

The [`type`]{#dom-client-type .dfn .dfn-paneled .idl-code
dfn-for="Client" dfn-type="attribute" export=""} getter steps are:

1.  Let `client`{.variable} be
    [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this③②
    link-type="dfn"}'s [service worker
    client](#dfn-service-worker-client-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client-service-worker-client③
    link-type="dfn"}.

2.  If `client`{.variable} is an [environment settings
    object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object){#ref-for-environment-settings-object⑥
    link-type="dfn"}, then:

    1.  If `client`{.variable} is a [window
        client](#dfn-window-client){#ref-for-dfn-window-client⑨
        link-type="dfn"}, return
        [`"window"`{.idl}](#dom-clienttype-window){#ref-for-dom-clienttype-window
        link-type="idl"}.

    2.  Else if `client`{.variable} is a [dedicated worker
        client](#dfn-dedicatedworker-client){#ref-for-dfn-dedicatedworker-client①
        link-type="dfn"}, return
        [`"worker"`{.idl}](#dom-clienttype-worker){#ref-for-dom-clienttype-worker
        link-type="idl"}.

    3.  Else if `client`{.variable} is a [shared worker
        client](#dfn-sharedworker-client){#ref-for-dfn-sharedworker-client①
        link-type="dfn"}, return
        [`"sharedworker"`{.idl}](#dom-clienttype-sharedworker){#ref-for-dom-clienttype-sharedworker
        link-type="idl"}.

3.  Else:

    1.  Return
        [`"window"`{.idl}](#dom-clienttype-window){#ref-for-dom-clienttype-window①
        link-type="idl"}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="client-postmessage"}
#### [4.2.5. ]{.secno}[[`postMessage(message, transfer)`{.idl}](#dom-client-postmessage){#ref-for-dom-client-postmessage① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#client-postmessage){.self-link} {#client-postmessage .heading .settled level="4.2.5"}

The
[`postMessage(``message`{.variable}`, ``transfer`{.variable}`)`]{#dom-client-postmessage
.dfn .dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="Client" dfn-type="method"
export=""} method steps are:

1.  Let `options`{.variable} be «\[ \"transfer\" → `transfer`{.variable}
    \]».

2.  Invoke
    [`postMessage(message, options)`{.idl}](#dom-client-postmessage-message-options){#ref-for-dom-client-postmessage-message-options③
    link-type="idl"} with `message`{.variable} and `options`{.variable}
    as the arguments.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="client-postmessage-options"}
#### [4.2.6. ]{.secno}[[`postMessage(message, options)`{.idl}](#dom-client-postmessage-message-options){#ref-for-dom-client-postmessage-message-options④ link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#client-postmessage-options){.self-link} {#client-postmessage-options .heading .settled level="4.2.6"}

The
[`postMessage(``message`{.variable}`, ``options`{.variable}`)`]{#dom-client-postmessage-message-options
.dfn .dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="Client" dfn-type="method" export=""
lt="postMessage(message, options)|postMessage(message)"} method steps
are:

1.  Let `contextObject`{.variable} be
    [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this③③
    link-type="dfn"}.

2.  Let `sourceSettings`{.variable} be the `contextObject`{.variable}'s
    [relevant settings
    object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object){#ref-for-relevant-settings-object①⑥
    link-type="dfn"}.

3.  Let `serializeWithTransferResult`{.variable} be
    [StructuredSerializeWithTransfer](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/structured-data.html#structuredserializewithtransfer){#ref-for-structuredserializewithtransfer①
    link-type="abstract-op"}(`message`{.variable},
    `options`{.variable}\[\"[`transfer`{.idl}](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/web-messaging.html#dom-structuredserializeoptions-transfer){#ref-for-dom-structuredserializeoptions-transfer①
    link-type="idl"}\"\]). Rethrow any exceptions.

4.  Run the following steps [in
    parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel){#ref-for-in-parallel⑨
    link-type="dfn"}:

    1.  Let `targetClient`{.variable} be null.

    2.  For each [service worker
        client](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client②⑦
        link-type="dfn"} `client`{.variable}:

        1.  If `client`{.variable} is the `contextObject`{.variable}'s
            [service worker
            client](#dfn-service-worker-client-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client-service-worker-client④
            link-type="dfn"}, set `targetClient`{.variable} to
            `client`{.variable}, and
            [break](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-break){#ref-for-iteration-break
            link-type="dfn"}.

    3.  If `targetClient`{.variable} is null, return.

    4.  Let `destination`{.variable} be the
        [`ServiceWorkerContainer`{.idl}](#serviceworkercontainer){#ref-for-serviceworkercontainer①④
        link-type="idl"} object whose associated [service worker
        client](#serviceworkercontainer-service-worker-client){#ref-for-serviceworkercontainer-service-worker-client⑧
        link-type="dfn"} is `targetClient`{.variable}.

    5.  Add a
        [task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-task){#ref-for-concept-task③
        link-type="dfn"} that runs the following steps to
        `destination`{.variable}'s [client message
        queue](#dfn-client-message-queue){#ref-for-dfn-client-message-queue⑤
        link-type="dfn"}:

        1.  Let `origin`{.variable} be the
            [serialization](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#ascii-serialisation-of-an-origin){#ref-for-ascii-serialisation-of-an-origin①
            link-type="dfn"} of `sourceSettings`{.variable}'s
            [origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-origin){#ref-for-concept-settings-object-origin④
            link-type="dfn"}.

        2.  Let `source`{.variable} be the result of [getting the
            service worker
            object](#get-the-service-worker-object){#ref-for-get-the-service-worker-object⑥
            link-type="dfn"} that represents
            `contextObject`{.variable}'s [relevant global
            object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-global){#ref-for-concept-relevant-global③
            link-type="dfn"}'s [service
            worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker⑥
            link-type="dfn"} in `targetClient`{.variable}.

        3.  Let `deserializeRecord`{.variable} be
            [StructuredDeserializeWithTransfer](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/structured-data.html#structureddeserializewithtransfer){#ref-for-structureddeserializewithtransfer①
            link-type="abstract-op"}(`serializeWithTransferResult`{.variable},
            `destination`{.variable}'s [relevant
            Realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-realm){#ref-for-concept-relevant-realm①
            link-type="dfn"}).

            If this throws an exception, catch it, [fire an
            event](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event-fire){#ref-for-concept-event-fire
            link-type="dfn"} named
            [`messageerror`{.idl}](#service-worker-container-messageerror-event){#ref-for-service-worker-container-messageerror-event①
            .idl-code link-type="event"} at `destination`{.variable},
            using
            [`MessageEvent`{.idl}](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/comms.html#messageevent){#ref-for-messageevent
            link-type="idl"}, with the
            [`origin`{.idl}](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/comms.html#dom-messageevent-origin){#ref-for-dom-messageevent-origin
            link-type="idl"} attribute initialized to
            `origin`{.variable} and the
            [`source`{.idl}](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/comms.html#dom-messageevent-source){#ref-for-dom-messageevent-source
            link-type="idl"} attribute initialized to
            `source`{.variable}, and then abort these steps.

        4.  Let `messageClone`{.variable} be
            `deserializeRecord`{.variable}.\[\[Deserialized\]\].

        5.  Let `newPorts`{.variable} be a new [frozen
            array](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-frozen-array-type){#ref-for-dfn-frozen-array-type①
            link-type="dfn"} consisting of all
            [`MessagePort`{.idl}](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/web-messaging.html#messageport){#ref-for-messageport①
            link-type="idl"} objects in
            `deserializeRecord`{.variable}.\[\[TransferredValues\]\], if
            any.

        6.  [Dispatch an
            event](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event-dispatch){#ref-for-concept-event-dispatch③
            link-type="dfn"} named
            [`message`{.idl}](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/indices.html#event-message){#ref-for-event-message
            .idl-code link-type="event"} at `destination`{.variable},
            using
            [`MessageEvent`{.idl}](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/comms.html#messageevent){#ref-for-messageevent①
            link-type="idl"}, with the
            [`origin`{.idl}](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/comms.html#dom-messageevent-origin){#ref-for-dom-messageevent-origin①
            link-type="idl"} attribute initialized to
            `origin`{.variable}, the
            [`source`{.idl}](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/comms.html#dom-messageevent-source){#ref-for-dom-messageevent-source①
            link-type="idl"} attribute initialized to
            `source`{.variable}, the
            [`data`{.idl}](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/comms.html#dom-messageevent-data){#ref-for-dom-messageevent-data
            link-type="idl"} attribute initialized to
            `messageClone`{.variable}, and the
            [`ports`{.idl}](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/comms.html#dom-messageevent-ports){#ref-for-dom-messageevent-ports
            link-type="idl"} attribute initialized to
            `newPorts`{.variable}.
:::

::: section
#### [4.2.7. ]{.secno}[[`visibilityState`{.idl}](#dom-windowclient-visibilitystate){#ref-for-dom-windowclient-visibilitystate① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#client-visibilitystate){.self-link} {#client-visibilitystate .heading .settled level="4.2.7"}

The [`visibilityState`]{#dom-windowclient-visibilitystate .dfn
.dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="WindowClient" dfn-type="attribute"
export=""} getter steps are to return
[this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this③④
link-type="dfn"}'s [visibility
state](#dfn-service-worker-client-visibilitystate){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client-visibilitystate
link-type="dfn"}.
:::

::: section
#### [4.2.8. ]{.secno}[[`focused`{.idl}](#dom-windowclient-focused){#ref-for-dom-windowclient-focused① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#client-focused){.self-link} {#client-focused .heading .settled level="4.2.8"}

The [`focused`]{#dom-windowclient-focused .dfn .dfn-paneled .idl-code
dfn-for="WindowClient" dfn-type="attribute" export=""} getter steps are
to return [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this③⑤
link-type="dfn"}'s [focus
state](#dfn-service-worker-client-focusstate){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client-focusstate
link-type="dfn"}.
:::

::: section
#### [4.2.9. ]{.secno}[[`ancestorOrigins`{.idl}](#dom-windowclient-ancestororigins){#ref-for-dom-windowclient-ancestororigins① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#client-ancestororigins){.self-link} {#client-ancestororigins .heading .settled level="4.2.9"}

The [`ancestorOrigins`]{#dom-windowclient-ancestororigins .dfn
.dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="WindowClient" dfn-type="attribute"
export=""} getter steps are to return
[this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this③⑥
link-type="dfn"}'s associated [ancestor origins
array](#windowclient-ancestor-origins-array){#ref-for-windowclient-ancestor-origins-array
link-type="dfn"}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="client-focus"}
#### [4.2.10. ]{.secno}[[`focus()`{.idl}](#dom-windowclient-focus){#ref-for-dom-windowclient-focus① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#client-focus){.self-link} {#client-focus .heading .settled level="4.2.10"}

The [`focus()`]{#dom-windowclient-focus .dfn .dfn-paneled .idl-code
dfn-for="WindowClient" dfn-type="method" export=""} method steps are:

1.  If no
    [`Window`{.idl}](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#window){#ref-for-window③
    link-type="idl"} in this
    [origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#concept-origin){#ref-for-concept-origin⑧
    link-type="dfn"} has [transient
    activation](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/interaction.html#transient-activation){#ref-for-transient-activation
    link-type="dfn"}, return a
    [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects){#ref-for-sec-promise-objects⑥
    link-type="dfn"} rejected with an
    \"[`InvalidAccessError`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#invalidaccesserror){#ref-for-invalidaccesserror
    link-type="idl"}\"
    [`DOMException`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException){#ref-for-idl-DOMException⑥
    link-type="idl"}.

2.  Let `serviceWorkerEventLoop`{.variable} be the [surrounding
    agent](https://tc39.es/ecma262/multipage/executable-code-and-execution-contexts.html#surrounding-agent){#ref-for-surrounding-agent
    link-type="dfn"}'s [event
    loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-agent-event-loop){#ref-for-concept-agent-event-loop
    link-type="dfn"}.

3.  Let `promise`{.variable} be a new
    [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects){#ref-for-sec-promise-objects⑦
    link-type="dfn"}.

4.  [Queue a
    task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task){#ref-for-queue-a-task⑤
    link-type="dfn"} to run the following steps on
    [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this③⑦
    link-type="dfn"}'s associated [service worker
    client](#dfn-service-worker-client-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client-service-worker-client⑤
    link-type="dfn"}'s [responsible event
    loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop){#ref-for-responsible-event-loop②
    link-type="dfn"} using the [user interaction task
    source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#user-interaction-task-source){#ref-for-user-interaction-task-source
    link-type="dfn"}:

    1.  Run the [focusing
        steps](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/interaction.html#focusing-steps){#ref-for-focusing-steps
        link-type="dfn"} with
        [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this③⑧
        link-type="dfn"}'s [browsing
        context](#dfn-service-worker-client-browsing-context){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client-browsing-context
        link-type="dfn"}.

    2.  Let `frameType`{.variable} be the result of running [Get Frame
        Type](#get-frame-type){#ref-for-get-frame-type link-type="dfn"}
        with
        [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this③⑨
        link-type="dfn"}'s [browsing
        context](#dfn-service-worker-client-browsing-context){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client-browsing-context①
        link-type="dfn"}.

    3.  Let `visibilityState`{.variable} be
        [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this④⓪
        link-type="dfn"}'s [browsing
        context](#dfn-service-worker-client-browsing-context){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client-browsing-context②
        link-type="dfn"}'s [active
        document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#nav-document){#ref-for-nav-document①
        link-type="dfn"}'s
        [`visibilityState`{.idl}](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/interaction.html#page-visibility){#ref-for-page-visibility②
        link-type="idl" refhint-key="d7e375d3"} attribute value.

    4.  Let `focusState`{.variable} be the result of running the [has
        focus
        steps](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/interaction.html#has-focus-steps){#ref-for-has-focus-steps
        link-type="dfn"} with
        [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this④①
        link-type="dfn"}'s [browsing
        context](#dfn-service-worker-client-browsing-context){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client-browsing-context③
        link-type="dfn"}'s [active
        document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#nav-document){#ref-for-nav-document②
        link-type="dfn"}.

    5.  Let `ancestorOriginsList`{.variable} be
        [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this④②
        link-type="dfn"}'s [browsing
        context](#dfn-service-worker-client-browsing-context){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client-browsing-context④
        link-type="dfn"}'s [active
        document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#nav-document){#ref-for-nav-document③
        link-type="dfn"}'s [relevant global
        object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-global){#ref-for-concept-relevant-global④
        link-type="dfn"}'s
        [`Location`{.idl}](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#location){#ref-for-location
        link-type="idl"} object's [ancestor origins
        list](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#concept-location-ancestor-origins-list){#ref-for-concept-location-ancestor-origins-list
        link-type="dfn"}'s associated list.

    6.  [Queue a
        task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task){#ref-for-queue-a-task⑥
        link-type="dfn"} to run the following steps on
        `serviceWorkerEventLoop`{.variable} using the [DOM manipulation
        task
        source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source){#ref-for-dom-manipulation-task-source③
        link-type="dfn"}:

        1.  Let `windowClient`{.variable} be the result of running
            [Create Window
            Client](#create-window-client){#ref-for-create-window-client
            link-type="dfn"} with
            [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this④③
            link-type="dfn"}'s associated [service worker
            client](#dfn-service-worker-client-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client-service-worker-client⑥
            link-type="dfn"}, `frameType`{.variable},
            `visibilityState`{.variable}, `focusState`{.variable}, and
            `ancestorOriginsList`{.variable}.

        2.  If `windowClient`{.variable}'s [focus
            state](#dfn-service-worker-client-focusstate){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client-focusstate①
            link-type="dfn"} is true, resolve `promise`{.variable} with
            `windowClient`{.variable}.

        3.  Else, reject `promise`{.variable} with a `TypeError`.

5.  Return `promise`{.variable}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="client-navigate"}
#### [4.2.11. ]{.secno}[[`navigate(url)`{.idl}](#dom-windowclient-navigate){#ref-for-dom-windowclient-navigate① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#client-navigate){.self-link} {#client-navigate .heading .settled level="4.2.11"}

The [`navigate(url)`]{#dom-windowclient-navigate .dfn .dfn-paneled
.idl-code dfn-for="WindowClient" dfn-type="method" export=""} method
steps are:

1.  Let `url`{.variable} be the result of
    [parsing](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-parser){#ref-for-concept-url-parser③
    link-type="dfn"} `url`{.variable} with
    [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this④④
    link-type="dfn"}'s [relevant settings
    object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object){#ref-for-relevant-settings-object①⑦
    link-type="dfn"}'s [API base
    URL](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#api-base-url){#ref-for-api-base-url③
    link-type="dfn"}.

2.  If `url`{.variable} is failure, return a
    [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects){#ref-for-sec-promise-objects⑧
    link-type="dfn"} rejected with a `TypeError`.

3.  If `url`{.variable} is `about:blank`, return a
    [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects){#ref-for-sec-promise-objects⑨
    link-type="dfn"} rejected with a `TypeError`.

4.  If [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this④⑤
    link-type="dfn"}'s associated [service worker
    client](#dfn-service-worker-client-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client-service-worker-client⑦
    link-type="dfn"}'s [active service
    worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker){#ref-for-concept-environment-active-service-worker②①
    link-type="dfn"} is not
    [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this④⑥
    link-type="dfn"}'s [relevant global
    object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-global){#ref-for-concept-relevant-global⑤
    link-type="dfn"}'s [service
    worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker⑦
    link-type="dfn"}, return a
    [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects){#ref-for-sec-promise-objects①⓪
    link-type="dfn"} rejected with a `TypeError`.

5.  Let `serviceWorkerEventLoop`{.variable} be the [current global
    object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#current-global-object){#ref-for-current-global-object
    link-type="dfn"}'s [event
    loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-loop){#ref-for-event-loop⑥
    link-type="dfn"}.

6.  Let `promise`{.variable} be a new
    [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects){#ref-for-sec-promise-objects①①
    link-type="dfn"}.

7.  [Queue a
    task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task){#ref-for-queue-a-task⑦
    link-type="dfn"} to run the following steps on
    [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this④⑦
    link-type="dfn"}'s associated [service worker
    client](#dfn-service-worker-client-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client-service-worker-client⑧
    link-type="dfn"}'s [responsible event
    loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop){#ref-for-responsible-event-loop③
    link-type="dfn"} using the [user interaction task
    source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#user-interaction-task-source){#ref-for-user-interaction-task-source①
    link-type="dfn"}:

    1.  Let `browsingContext`{.variable} be
        [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this④⑧
        link-type="dfn"}'s [browsing
        context](#dfn-service-worker-client-browsing-context){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client-browsing-context⑤
        link-type="dfn"}.

    2.  If `browsingContext`{.variable}'s [associated
        document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#concept-document-window){#ref-for-concept-document-window①
        link-type="dfn"} is not [fully
        active](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#fully-active){#ref-for-fully-active
        link-type="dfn"}, [queue a
        task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task){#ref-for-queue-a-task⑧
        link-type="dfn"} to reject `promise`{.variable} with a
        `TypeError`, on `serviceWorkerEventLoop`{.variable} using the
        [DOM manipulation task
        source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source){#ref-for-dom-manipulation-task-source④
        link-type="dfn"}, and abort these steps.

    3.  *HandleNavigate*:
        [Navigate](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsing-the-web.html#navigate){#ref-for-navigate④
        link-type="dfn"} `browsingContext`{.variable} to
        `url`{.variable}, using `browsingContext`{.variable}'s
        [associated
        document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#concept-document-window){#ref-for-concept-document-window②
        link-type="dfn"}, with
        [exceptionsEnabled](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsing-the-web.html#exceptions-enabled){#ref-for-exceptions-enabled
        link-type="dfn"} true.

    4.  If the algorithm steps invoked in the step labeled
        *HandleNavigate*
        [throws](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw){#ref-for-dfn-throw
        link-type="dfn"} an exception, [queue a
        task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task){#ref-for-queue-a-task⑨
        link-type="dfn"} to reject `promise`{.variable} with the
        exception, on `serviceWorkerEventLoop`{.variable} using the [DOM
        manipulation task
        source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source){#ref-for-dom-manipulation-task-source⑤
        link-type="dfn"}, and abort these steps.

    5.  Let `frameType`{.variable} be the result of running [Get Frame
        Type](#get-frame-type){#ref-for-get-frame-type① link-type="dfn"}
        with `browsingContext`{.variable}.

    6.  Let `visibilityState`{.variable} be
        `browsingContext`{.variable}'s [active
        document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#nav-document){#ref-for-nav-document④
        link-type="dfn"}'s
        [`visibilityState`{.idl}](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/interaction.html#page-visibility){#ref-for-page-visibility③
        link-type="idl" refhint-key="d7e375d3"} attribute value.

    7.  Let `focusState`{.variable} be the result of running the [has
        focus
        steps](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/interaction.html#has-focus-steps){#ref-for-has-focus-steps①
        link-type="dfn"} with `browsingContext`{.variable}'s [active
        document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#nav-document){#ref-for-nav-document⑤
        link-type="dfn"}.

    8.  Let `ancestorOriginsList`{.variable} be
        `browsingContext`{.variable}'s [active
        document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#nav-document){#ref-for-nav-document⑥
        link-type="dfn"}'s [relevant global
        object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-global){#ref-for-concept-relevant-global⑥
        link-type="dfn"}'s
        [`Location`{.idl}](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#location){#ref-for-location①
        link-type="idl"} object's [ancestor origins
        list](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#concept-location-ancestor-origins-list){#ref-for-concept-location-ancestor-origins-list①
        link-type="dfn"}'s associated list.

    9.  [Queue a
        task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task){#ref-for-queue-a-task①⓪
        link-type="dfn"} to run the following steps on
        `serviceWorkerEventLoop`{.variable} using the [DOM manipulation
        task
        source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source){#ref-for-dom-manipulation-task-source⑥
        link-type="dfn"}:

        1.  If `browsingContext`{.variable}'s
            [`Window`{.idl}](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#window){#ref-for-window④
            link-type="idl"} object's [environment settings
            object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object){#ref-for-environment-settings-object⑦
            link-type="dfn"}'s [creation
            URL](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-creation-url){#ref-for-concept-environment-creation-url④
            link-type="dfn"}'s
            [origin](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-origin){#ref-for-concept-url-origin①
            link-type="dfn"} is not the
            [same](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#same-origin){#ref-for-same-origin③
            link-type="dfn"} as the [service
            worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker⑧
            link-type="dfn"}'s
            [origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-origin){#ref-for-concept-settings-object-origin⑤
            link-type="dfn"}, resolve `promise`{.variable} with null and
            abort these steps.

        2.  Let `windowClient`{.variable} be the result of running
            [Create Window
            Client](#create-window-client){#ref-for-create-window-client①
            link-type="dfn"} with
            [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this④⑨
            link-type="dfn"}'s [service worker
            client](#dfn-service-worker-client-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client-service-worker-client⑨
            link-type="dfn"}, `frameType`{.variable},
            `visibilityState`{.variable}, `focusState`{.variable}, and
            `ancestorOriginsList`{.variable}.

        3.  Resolve `promise`{.variable} with `windowClient`{.variable}.

8.  Return `promise`{.variable}.
:::
::::::::::::::

::::::: section
### [4.3. ]{.secno}[[`Clients`{.idl}](#clients){#ref-for-clients② link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#clients-interface){.self-link} {#clients-interface .heading .settled level="4.3"}

``` {.def .highlight .idl}
[Exposed=ServiceWorker]
interface Clients {
  // The objects returned will be new instances every time
  [NewObject] Promise<(Client or undefined)> get(DOMString id);
  [NewObject] Promise<FrozenArray<Client>> matchAll(optional ClientQueryOptions options = {});
  [NewObject] Promise<WindowClient?> openWindow(USVString url);
  [NewObject] Promise<undefined> claim();
};
```

``` {#serviceworker-client-query-options-dictionary .def .highlight .idl}
dictionary ClientQueryOptions {
  boolean includeUncontrolled = false;
  ClientType type = "window";
};
```

``` {#client-type-enum .def .highlight .idl}
enum ClientType {
  "window",
  "worker",
  "sharedworker",
  "all"
};
```

The user agent *must* create a
[`Clients`{.idl}](#clients){#ref-for-clients③ link-type="idl"} object
when a
[`ServiceWorkerGlobalScope`{.idl}](#serviceworkerglobalscope){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope①①
link-type="idl"} object is created and associate it with that object.

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="clients-get"}
#### [4.3.1. ]{.secno}[[`get(id)`{.idl}](#dom-clients-get){#ref-for-dom-clients-get① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#clients-get){.self-link} {#clients-get .heading .settled level="4.3.1"}

The [`get(``id`{.variable}`)`]{#dom-clients-get .dfn .dfn-paneled
.idl-code dfn-for="Clients" dfn-type="method" export=""} method steps
are:

1.  Let `promise`{.variable} be a new
    [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects){#ref-for-sec-promise-objects①②
    link-type="dfn"}.

2.  Run these substeps [in
    parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel){#ref-for-in-parallel①⓪
    link-type="dfn"}:

    1.  For each [service worker
        client](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client②⑧
        link-type="dfn"} `client`{.variable} where the result of running
        [obtain a storage
        key](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#obtain-a-storage-key){#ref-for-obtain-a-storage-key③
        link-type="dfn"} given `client`{.variable}
        [equals](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#storage-key-equal){#ref-for-storage-key-equal①
        link-type="dfn"} the associated [service
        worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker⑨
        link-type="dfn"}'s [containing service worker
        registration](#dfn-containing-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-containing-service-worker-registration⑥
        link-type="dfn"}'s [storage
        key](#service-worker-registration-storage-key){#ref-for-service-worker-registration-storage-key④
        link-type="dfn"}:

        1.  If `client`{.variable}'s
            [id](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-id){#ref-for-concept-environment-id①
            link-type="dfn"} is not `id`{.variable},
            [continue](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-continue){#ref-for-iteration-continue
            link-type="dfn"}.

        2.  Wait for either `client`{.variable}'s [execution ready
            flag](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-execution-ready-flag){#ref-for-concept-environment-execution-ready-flag
            link-type="dfn"} to be set or for `client`{.variable}'s
            [discarded
            flag](#service-worker-client-discarded-flag){#ref-for-service-worker-client-discarded-flag②
            link-type="dfn"} to be set.

        3.  If `client`{.variable}'s [execution ready
            flag](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-execution-ready-flag){#ref-for-concept-environment-execution-ready-flag①
            link-type="dfn"} is set, then invoke [Resolve Get Client
            Promise](#resolve-get-client-promise){#ref-for-resolve-get-client-promise
            link-type="dfn"} with `client`{.variable} and
            `promise`{.variable}, and abort these steps.

    2.  Resolve `promise`{.variable} with undefined.

3.  Return `promise`{.variable}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="clients-matchall"}
#### [4.3.2. ]{.secno}[[`matchAll(options)`{.idl}](#dom-clients-matchall){#ref-for-dom-clients-matchall① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#clients-matchall){.self-link} {#clients-matchall .heading .settled level="4.3.2"}

The [`matchAll(``options`{.variable}`)`]{#dom-clients-matchall .dfn
.dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="Clients" dfn-type="method" export=""
lt="matchAll(options)|matchAll()"} method steps are:

1.  Let `promise`{.variable} be [a new
    promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-new-promise){#ref-for-a-new-promise⑦
    link-type="dfn"}.

2.  Run the following steps [in
    parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel){#ref-for-in-parallel①①
    link-type="dfn"}:

    1.  Let `targetClients`{.variable} be a new
        [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list){#ref-for-list④
        link-type="dfn"}.

    2.  For each [service worker
        client](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client②⑨
        link-type="dfn"} `client`{.variable} where the result of running
        [obtain a storage
        key](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#obtain-a-storage-key){#ref-for-obtain-a-storage-key④
        link-type="dfn"} given `client`{.variable}
        [equals](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#storage-key-equal){#ref-for-storage-key-equal②
        link-type="dfn"} the associated [service
        worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker①⓪
        link-type="dfn"}'s [containing service worker
        registration](#dfn-containing-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-containing-service-worker-registration⑦
        link-type="dfn"}'s [storage
        key](#service-worker-registration-storage-key){#ref-for-service-worker-registration-storage-key⑤
        link-type="dfn"}:

        1.  If `client`{.variable}'s [execution ready
            flag](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-execution-ready-flag){#ref-for-concept-environment-execution-ready-flag②
            link-type="dfn"} is unset or `client`{.variable}'s
            [discarded
            flag](#service-worker-client-discarded-flag){#ref-for-service-worker-client-discarded-flag③
            link-type="dfn"} is set,
            [continue](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-continue){#ref-for-iteration-continue①
            link-type="dfn"}.

        2.  If `client`{.variable} is not a [secure
            context](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#secure-context){#ref-for-secure-context
            link-type="dfn"},
            [continue](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-continue){#ref-for-iteration-continue②
            link-type="dfn"}.

        3.  If
            `options`{.variable}\[\"[`includeUncontrolled`{.idl}](#dom-clientqueryoptions-includeuncontrolled){#ref-for-dom-clientqueryoptions-includeuncontrolled
            link-type="idl"}\"\] is false, and if `client`{.variable}'s
            [active service
            worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker){#ref-for-concept-environment-active-service-worker②②
            link-type="dfn"} is not the associated [service
            worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker①①
            link-type="dfn"},
            [continue](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-continue){#ref-for-iteration-continue③
            link-type="dfn"}.

        4.  Add `client`{.variable} to `targetClients`{.variable}.

    3.  Let `matchedWindowData`{.variable} be a new
        [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list){#ref-for-list⑤
        link-type="dfn"}.

    4.  Let `matchedClients`{.variable} be a new
        [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list){#ref-for-list⑥
        link-type="dfn"}.

    5.  For each [service worker
        client](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client③⓪
        link-type="dfn"} `client`{.variable} in
        `targetClients`{.variable}:

        1.  If
            `options`{.variable}\[\"[`type`{.idl}](#dom-clientqueryoptions-type){#ref-for-dom-clientqueryoptions-type
            link-type="idl"}\"\] is
            [`"window"`{.idl}](#dom-clienttype-window){#ref-for-dom-clienttype-window②
            link-type="idl"} or
            [`"all"`{.idl}](#dom-clienttype-all){#ref-for-dom-clienttype-all
            link-type="idl"}, and `client`{.variable} is not an
            [environment settings
            object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object){#ref-for-environment-settings-object⑧
            link-type="dfn"} or is a [window
            client](#dfn-window-client){#ref-for-dfn-window-client①⓪
            link-type="dfn"}, then:

            1.  Let `windowData`{.variable} be «\[ \"client\" →
                `client`{.variable}, \"ancestorOriginsList\" → a new
                [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list){#ref-for-list⑦
                link-type="dfn"} \]».

            2.  Let `browsingContext`{.variable} be null.

            3.  Let `isClientEnumerable`{.variable} be true.

            4.  If `client`{.variable} is an [environment settings
                object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object){#ref-for-environment-settings-object⑨
                link-type="dfn"}, set `browsingContext`{.variable} to
                `client`{.variable}'s [global
                object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-global){#ref-for-concept-settings-object-global⑥
                link-type="dfn"}'s [browsing
                context](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#browsing-context){#ref-for-browsing-context⑥
                link-type="dfn"}.

            5.  Else, set `browsingContext`{.variable} to
                `client`{.variable}'s [target browsing
                context](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-target-browsing-context){#ref-for-concept-environment-target-browsing-context
                link-type="dfn"}.

            6.  [Queue a
                task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task){#ref-for-queue-a-task①①
                link-type="dfn"} `task`{.variable} to run the following
                substeps on `browsingContext`{.variable}'s [event
                loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-loop){#ref-for-event-loop⑦
                link-type="dfn"} using the [user interaction task
                source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#user-interaction-task-source){#ref-for-user-interaction-task-source②
                link-type="dfn"}:

                1.  If `browsingContext`{.variable} has been
                    [discarded](https://wicg.github.io/page-lifecycle/#discarded){#ref-for-discarded
                    link-type="dfn"}, then set
                    `isClientEnumerable`{.variable} to false and abort
                    these steps.

                2.  If `client`{.variable} is a window client and
                    `client`{.variable}'s [associated
                    document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#concept-document-window){#ref-for-concept-document-window③
                    link-type="dfn"} is not
                    `browsingContext`{.variable}'s [active
                    document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#nav-document){#ref-for-nav-document⑦
                    link-type="dfn"}, then set
                    `isClientEnumerable`{.variable} to false and abort
                    these steps.

                3.  Set `windowData`{.variable}\[\"`frameType`\"\] to
                    the result of running [Get Frame
                    Type](#get-frame-type){#ref-for-get-frame-type②
                    link-type="dfn"} with `browsingContext`{.variable}.

                4.  Set `windowData`{.variable}\[\"`visibilityState`\"\]
                    to `browsingContext`{.variable}'s [active
                    document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#nav-document){#ref-for-nav-document⑧
                    link-type="dfn"}'s
                    [`visibilityState`{.idl}](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/interaction.html#page-visibility){#ref-for-page-visibility④
                    link-type="idl" refhint-key="d7e375d3"} attribute
                    value.

                5.  Set `windowData`{.variable}\[\"`focusState`\"\] to
                    the result of running the [has focus
                    steps](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/interaction.html#has-focus-steps){#ref-for-has-focus-steps②
                    link-type="dfn"} with `browsingContext`{.variable}'s
                    [active
                    document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#nav-document){#ref-for-nav-document⑨
                    link-type="dfn"} as the argument.

                6.  If `client`{.variable} is a [window
                    client](#dfn-window-client){#ref-for-dfn-window-client①①
                    link-type="dfn"}, then set
                    `windowData`{.variable}\[\"`ancestorOriginsList`\"\]
                    to `browsingContext`{.variable}'s [active
                    document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#nav-document){#ref-for-nav-document①⓪
                    link-type="dfn"}'s [relevant global
                    object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-global){#ref-for-concept-relevant-global⑦
                    link-type="dfn"}'s
                    [`Location`{.idl}](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#location){#ref-for-location②
                    link-type="idl"} object's [ancestor origins
                    list](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#concept-location-ancestor-origins-list){#ref-for-concept-location-ancestor-origins-list②
                    link-type="dfn"}'s associated list.

            7.  Wait for `task`{.variable} to have executed.

                [Note:]{.marker} Wait is a blocking wait, but
                implementers may run the iterations in parallel as long
                as the state is not broken.

            8.  If `isClientEnumerable`{.variable} is true, then:

                1.  Add `windowData`{.variable} to
                    `matchedWindowData`{.variable}.

        2.  Else if
            `options`{.variable}\[\"[`type`{.idl}](#dom-clientqueryoptions-type){#ref-for-dom-clientqueryoptions-type①
            link-type="idl"}\"\] is
            [`"worker"`{.idl}](#dom-clienttype-worker){#ref-for-dom-clienttype-worker①
            link-type="idl"} or
            [`"all"`{.idl}](#dom-clienttype-all){#ref-for-dom-clienttype-all①
            link-type="idl"} and `client`{.variable} is a [dedicated
            worker
            client](#dfn-dedicatedworker-client){#ref-for-dfn-dedicatedworker-client②
            link-type="dfn"}, or
            `options`{.variable}\[\"[`type`{.idl}](#dom-clientqueryoptions-type){#ref-for-dom-clientqueryoptions-type②
            link-type="idl"}\"\] is
            [`"sharedworker"`{.idl}](#dom-clienttype-sharedworker){#ref-for-dom-clienttype-sharedworker①
            link-type="idl"} or
            [`"all"`{.idl}](#dom-clienttype-all){#ref-for-dom-clienttype-all②
            link-type="idl"} and `client`{.variable} is a [shared worker
            client](#dfn-sharedworker-client){#ref-for-dfn-sharedworker-client②
            link-type="dfn"}, then:

            1.  Add `client`{.variable} to `matchedClients`{.variable}.

    6.  [Queue a
        task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task){#ref-for-queue-a-task①②
        link-type="dfn"} to run the following steps on
        `promise`{.variable}'s [relevant settings
        object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object){#ref-for-relevant-settings-object①⑧
        link-type="dfn"}'s [responsible event
        loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop){#ref-for-responsible-event-loop④
        link-type="dfn"} using the [DOM manipulation task
        source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source){#ref-for-dom-manipulation-task-source⑦
        link-type="dfn"}:

        1.  Let `clientObjects`{.variable} be a new
            [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list){#ref-for-list⑧
            link-type="dfn"}.

        2.  [For
            each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate){#ref-for-list-iterate②
            link-type="dfn"} `windowData`{.variable} in
            `matchedWindowData`{.variable}:

            1.  Let `windowClient`{.variable} be the result of running
                [Create Window
                Client](#create-window-client){#ref-for-create-window-client②
                link-type="dfn"} algorithm with
                `windowData`{.variable}\[\"`client`\"\],
                `windowData`{.variable}\[\"`frameType`\"\],
                `windowData`{.variable}\[\"`visibilityState`\"\],
                `windowData`{.variable}\[\"`focusState`\"\], and
                `windowData`{.variable}\[\"`ancestorOriginsList`\"\] as
                the arguments.

            2.  [Append](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-append){#ref-for-list-append②
                link-type="dfn"} `windowClient`{.variable} to
                `clientObjects`{.variable}.

        3.  [For
            each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate){#ref-for-list-iterate③
            link-type="dfn"} `client`{.variable} in
            `matchedClients`{.variable}:

            1.  Let `clientObject`{.variable} be the result of running
                [Create Client](#create-client){#ref-for-create-client
                link-type="dfn"} algorithm with `client`{.variable} as
                the argument.

            2.  [Append](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-append){#ref-for-list-append③
                link-type="dfn"} `clientObject`{.variable} to
                `clientObjects`{.variable}.

        4.  Sort `clientObjects`{.variable} such that:

            - [`WindowClient`{.idl}](#windowclient){#ref-for-windowclient⑧
              link-type="idl"} objects whose [browsing
              context](#dfn-service-worker-client-browsing-context){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client-browsing-context⑥
              link-type="dfn"} has been
              [focused](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/interaction.html#focusing-steps){#ref-for-focusing-steps①
              link-type="dfn"} are placed first, sorted in the most
              recently
              [focused](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/interaction.html#focusing-steps){#ref-for-focusing-steps②
              link-type="dfn"} order.

            - [`WindowClient`{.idl}](#windowclient){#ref-for-windowclient⑨
              link-type="idl"} objects whose [browsing
              context](#dfn-service-worker-client-browsing-context){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client-browsing-context⑦
              link-type="dfn"} has never been
              [focused](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/interaction.html#focusing-steps){#ref-for-focusing-steps③
              link-type="dfn"} are placed next, sorted in their [service
              worker
              client](#dfn-service-worker-client-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client-service-worker-client①⓪
              link-type="dfn"}'s creation order.

            - [`Client`{.idl}](#client){#ref-for-client⑦
              link-type="idl"} objects whose associated [service worker
              client](#dfn-service-worker-client-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client-service-worker-client①①
              link-type="dfn"} is a [worker
              client](#dfn-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-worker-client①⓪
              link-type="dfn"} are placed next, sorted in their [service
              worker
              client](#dfn-service-worker-client-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client-service-worker-client①②
              link-type="dfn"}'s creation order.

            [Note:]{.marker} [Window
            clients](#dfn-window-client){#ref-for-dfn-window-client①②
            link-type="dfn"} are always placed before [worker
            clients](#dfn-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-worker-client①①
            link-type="dfn"}.

        5.  Resolve `promise`{.variable} with [a new frozen array
            of](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-create-frozen-array){#ref-for-dfn-create-frozen-array①
            link-type="dfn"} `clientObjects`{.variable} in
            `promise`{.variable}'s [relevant
            Realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-realm){#ref-for-concept-relevant-realm②
            link-type="dfn"}.

3.  Return `promise`{.variable}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="clients-openwindow"}
#### [4.3.3. ]{.secno}[[`openWindow(url)`{.idl}](#dom-clients-openwindow){#ref-for-dom-clients-openwindow① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#clients-openwindow){.self-link} {#clients-openwindow .heading .settled level="4.3.3"}

The [`openWindow(``url`{.variable}`)`]{#dom-clients-openwindow .dfn
.dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="Clients" dfn-type="method" export=""}
method steps are:

1.  Let `url`{.variable} be the result of
    [parsing](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-parser){#ref-for-concept-url-parser④
    link-type="dfn"} `url`{.variable} with
    [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this⑤⓪
    link-type="dfn"}'s [relevant settings
    object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object){#ref-for-relevant-settings-object①⑨
    link-type="dfn"}'s [API base
    URL](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#api-base-url){#ref-for-api-base-url④
    link-type="dfn"}.

2.  If `url`{.variable} is failure, return a
    [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects){#ref-for-sec-promise-objects①③
    link-type="dfn"} rejected with a `TypeError`.

3.  If `url`{.variable} is `about:blank`, return a
    [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects){#ref-for-sec-promise-objects①④
    link-type="dfn"} rejected with a `TypeError`.

4.  If no
    [`Window`{.idl}](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#window){#ref-for-window⑤
    link-type="idl"} in this
    [origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#concept-origin){#ref-for-concept-origin⑨
    link-type="dfn"} has [transient
    activation](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/interaction.html#transient-activation){#ref-for-transient-activation①
    link-type="dfn"}, return a
    [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects){#ref-for-sec-promise-objects①⑤
    link-type="dfn"} rejected with an
    \"[`InvalidAccessError`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#invalidaccesserror){#ref-for-invalidaccesserror①
    link-type="idl"}\"
    [`DOMException`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException){#ref-for-idl-DOMException⑦
    link-type="idl"}.

5.  Let `serviceWorkerEventLoop`{.variable} be the [current global
    object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#current-global-object){#ref-for-current-global-object①
    link-type="dfn"}'s [event
    loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-loop){#ref-for-event-loop⑧
    link-type="dfn"}.

6.  Let `promise`{.variable} be a new
    [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects){#ref-for-sec-promise-objects①⑥
    link-type="dfn"}.

7.  Run these substeps [in
    parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel){#ref-for-in-parallel①②
    link-type="dfn"}:

    1.  Let `newContext`{.variable} be a new [top-level browsing
        context](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#top-level-browsing-context){#ref-for-top-level-browsing-context
        link-type="dfn"}.

    2.  [Queue a
        task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task){#ref-for-queue-a-task①③
        link-type="dfn"} to run the following steps on
        `newContext`{.variable}'s
        [`Window`{.idl}](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#window){#ref-for-window⑥
        link-type="idl"} object's [environment settings
        object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object){#ref-for-environment-settings-object①⓪
        link-type="dfn"}'s [responsible event
        loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop){#ref-for-responsible-event-loop⑤
        link-type="dfn"} using the [user interaction task
        source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#user-interaction-task-source){#ref-for-user-interaction-task-source③
        link-type="dfn"}:

        1.  *HandleNavigate*:
            [Navigate](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsing-the-web.html#navigate){#ref-for-navigate⑤
            link-type="dfn"} `newContext`{.variable} to `url`{.variable}
            with
            [exceptionsEnabled](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsing-the-web.html#exceptions-enabled){#ref-for-exceptions-enabled①
            link-type="dfn"} true, and
            *[historyHandling](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsing-the-web.html#navigation-hh){#ref-for-navigation-hh
            link-type="dfn"}* \"`replace`\".

        2.  If the algorithm steps invoked in the step labeled
            *HandleNavigate*
            [throws](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw){#ref-for-dfn-throw①
            link-type="dfn"} an exception, [queue a
            task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task){#ref-for-queue-a-task①④
            link-type="dfn"} to reject `promise`{.variable} with the
            exception, on `serviceWorkerEventLoop`{.variable} using the
            [DOM manipulation task
            source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source){#ref-for-dom-manipulation-task-source⑧
            link-type="dfn"}, and abort these steps.

        3.  Let `frameType`{.variable} be the result of running [Get
            Frame Type](#get-frame-type){#ref-for-get-frame-type③
            link-type="dfn"} with `newContext`{.variable}.

        4.  Let `visibilityState`{.variable} be
            `newContext`{.variable}'s [active
            document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#nav-document){#ref-for-nav-document①①
            link-type="dfn"}'s
            [`visibilityState`{.idl}](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/interaction.html#page-visibility){#ref-for-page-visibility⑤
            link-type="idl" refhint-key="d7e375d3"} attribute value.

        5.  Let `focusState`{.variable} be the result of running the
            [has focus
            steps](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/interaction.html#has-focus-steps){#ref-for-has-focus-steps③
            link-type="dfn"} with `newContext`{.variable}'s [active
            document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#nav-document){#ref-for-nav-document①②
            link-type="dfn"} as the argument.

        6.  Let `ancestorOriginsList`{.variable} be
            `newContext`{.variable}'s [active
            document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#nav-document){#ref-for-nav-document①③
            link-type="dfn"}'s [relevant global
            object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-global){#ref-for-concept-relevant-global⑧
            link-type="dfn"}'s
            [`Location`{.idl}](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#location){#ref-for-location③
            link-type="idl"} object's [ancestor origins
            list](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#concept-location-ancestor-origins-list){#ref-for-concept-location-ancestor-origins-list③
            link-type="dfn"}'s associated list.

        7.  [Queue a
            task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task){#ref-for-queue-a-task①⑤
            link-type="dfn"} to run the following steps on
            `serviceWorkerEventLoop`{.variable} using the [DOM
            manipulation task
            source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source){#ref-for-dom-manipulation-task-source⑨
            link-type="dfn"}:

            1.  If the result of running [obtain a storage
                key](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#obtain-a-storage-key){#ref-for-obtain-a-storage-key⑤
                link-type="dfn"} given `newContext`{.variable}'s
                [`Window`{.idl}](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#window){#ref-for-window⑦
                link-type="idl"} object's [environment settings
                object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object){#ref-for-environment-settings-object①①
                link-type="dfn"} is not
                [equal](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#storage-key-equal){#ref-for-storage-key-equal③
                link-type="dfn"} to the [service
                worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker①②
                link-type="dfn"}'s [containing service worker
                registration](#dfn-containing-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-containing-service-worker-registration⑧
                link-type="dfn"}'s [storage
                key](#service-worker-registration-storage-key){#ref-for-service-worker-registration-storage-key⑥
                link-type="dfn"}, then resolve `promise`{.variable} with
                null and abort these steps.

            2.  Let `client`{.variable} be the result of running [Create
                Window
                Client](#create-window-client){#ref-for-create-window-client③
                link-type="dfn"} with `newContext`{.variable}'s
                [`Window`{.idl}](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#window){#ref-for-window⑧
                link-type="idl"} object's [environment settings
                object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object){#ref-for-environment-settings-object①②
                link-type="dfn"}, `frameType`{.variable},
                `visibilityState`{.variable}, `focusState`{.variable},
                and `ancestorOriginsList`{.variable} as the arguments.

            3.  Resolve `promise`{.variable} with `client`{.variable}.

8.  Return `promise`{.variable}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="clients-claim"}
#### [4.3.4. ]{.secno}[[`claim()`{.idl}](#dom-clients-claim){#ref-for-dom-clients-claim① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#clients-claim){.self-link} {#clients-claim .heading .settled level="4.3.4"}

The [`claim()`]{#dom-clients-claim .dfn .dfn-paneled .idl-code
dfn-for="Clients" dfn-type="method" export=""} method steps are:

1.  If the [service
    worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker①③
    link-type="dfn"} is not an [active
    worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker①⑥
    link-type="dfn"}, return a
    [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects){#ref-for-sec-promise-objects①⑦
    link-type="dfn"} rejected with an
    \"[`InvalidStateError`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#invalidstateerror){#ref-for-invalidstateerror⑤
    link-type="idl"}\"
    [`DOMException`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException){#ref-for-idl-DOMException⑧
    link-type="idl"}.

2.  Let `promise`{.variable} be a new
    [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects){#ref-for-sec-promise-objects①⑧
    link-type="dfn"}.

3.  Run the following substeps [in
    parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel){#ref-for-in-parallel①③
    link-type="dfn"}:

    1.  For each [service worker
        client](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client③①
        link-type="dfn"} `client`{.variable} where the result of running
        [obtain a storage
        key](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#obtain-a-storage-key){#ref-for-obtain-a-storage-key⑥
        link-type="dfn"} given `client`{.variable}
        [equals](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#storage-key-equal){#ref-for-storage-key-equal④
        link-type="dfn"} the [service
        worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker①④
        link-type="dfn"}'s [containing service worker
        registration](#dfn-containing-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-containing-service-worker-registration⑨
        link-type="dfn"}'s [storage
        key](#service-worker-registration-storage-key){#ref-for-service-worker-registration-storage-key⑦
        link-type="dfn"}:

        1.  If `client`{.variable}'s [execution ready
            flag](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-execution-ready-flag){#ref-for-concept-environment-execution-ready-flag③
            link-type="dfn"} is unset or `client`{.variable}'s
            [discarded
            flag](#service-worker-client-discarded-flag){#ref-for-service-worker-client-discarded-flag④
            link-type="dfn"} is set,
            [continue](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-continue){#ref-for-iteration-continue④
            link-type="dfn"}.

        2.  If `client`{.variable} is not a [secure
            context](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#secure-context){#ref-for-secure-context①
            link-type="dfn"},
            [continue](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-continue){#ref-for-iteration-continue⑤
            link-type="dfn"}.

        3.  Let `storage key`{.variable} be the result of running
            [obtain a storage
            key](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#obtain-a-storage-key){#ref-for-obtain-a-storage-key⑦
            link-type="dfn"} given `client`{.variable}.

        4.  Let `registration`{.variable} be the result of running
            [Match Service Worker
            Registration](#match-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-match-service-worker-registration⑤
            link-type="dfn"} given `storage key`{.variable} and
            `client`{.variable}'s [creation
            URL](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-creation-url){#ref-for-concept-environment-creation-url⑤
            link-type="dfn"}.

        5.  If `registration`{.variable} is not the [service
            worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker①⑤
            link-type="dfn"}'s [containing service worker
            registration](#dfn-containing-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-containing-service-worker-registration①⓪
            link-type="dfn"},
            [continue](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-continue){#ref-for-iteration-continue⑥
            link-type="dfn"}.

            [Note:]{.marker} `registration`{.variable} will be null if
            the [service
            worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker①⑥
            link-type="dfn"}'s [containing service worker
            registration](#dfn-containing-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-containing-service-worker-registration①①
            link-type="dfn"} is
            [unregistered](#dfn-service-worker-registration-unregistered){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration-unregistered①
            link-type="dfn"}.

        6.  If `client`{.variable}'s [active service
            worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker){#ref-for-concept-environment-active-service-worker②③
            link-type="dfn"} is not the [service
            worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker①⑦
            link-type="dfn"}, then:

            1.  Invoke [Handle Service Worker Client
                Unload](#handle-service-worker-client-unload){#ref-for-handle-service-worker-client-unload
                link-type="dfn"} with `client`{.variable} as the
                argument.

            2.  Set `client`{.variable}'s [active service
                worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker){#ref-for-concept-environment-active-service-worker②④
                link-type="dfn"} to [service
                worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker①⑧
                link-type="dfn"}.

            3.  Invoke [Notify Controller
                Change](#notify-controller-change){#ref-for-notify-controller-change
                link-type="dfn"} algorithm with `client`{.variable} as
                the argument.

    2.  Resolve `promise`{.variable} with undefined.

4.  Return `promise`{.variable}.
:::
:::::::

::::: section
### [4.4. ]{.secno}[[`ExtendableEvent`{.idl}](#extendableevent){#ref-for-extendableevent① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#extendableevent-interface){.self-link} {#extendableevent-interface .heading .settled level="4.4"}

``` {.def .highlight .idl}
[Exposed=ServiceWorker]
interface ExtendableEvent : Event {
  constructor(DOMString type, optional ExtendableEventInit eventInitDict = {});
  undefined waitUntil(Promise<any> f);
};
```

``` {#extendable-event-init-dictionary .def .highlight .idl}
dictionary ExtendableEventInit : EventInit {
  // Defined for the forward compatibility across the derived events
};
```

An [`ExtendableEvent`{.idl}](#extendableevent){#ref-for-extendableevent②
link-type="idl"} object has an associated [extend lifetime
promises]{#extendableevent-extend-lifetime-promises .dfn .dfn-paneled
dfn-for="ExtendableEvent" dfn-type="dfn" export=""} (an array of
[promises](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects){#ref-for-sec-promise-objects①⑨
link-type="dfn"}). It is initially an empty array.

An [`ExtendableEvent`{.idl}](#extendableevent){#ref-for-extendableevent③
link-type="idl"} object has an associated [pending promises
count]{#extendableevent-pending-promises-count .dfn .dfn-paneled
dfn-for="ExtendableEvent" dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""} (the number of
pending promises in the [extend lifetime
promises](#extendableevent-extend-lifetime-promises){#ref-for-extendableevent-extend-lifetime-promises
link-type="dfn"}). It is initially set to zero.

An [`ExtendableEvent`{.idl}](#extendableevent){#ref-for-extendableevent④
link-type="idl"} object has an associated [timed out
flag]{#extendableevent-timed-out-flag .dfn .dfn-paneled
dfn-for="ExtendableEvent" dfn-type="dfn" export=""}. It is initially
unset, and is set after an optional user agent imposed delay if the
[pending promises
count](#extendableevent-pending-promises-count){#ref-for-extendableevent-pending-promises-count
link-type="dfn"} is greater than zero.

An [`ExtendableEvent`{.idl}](#extendableevent){#ref-for-extendableevent⑤
link-type="idl"} object is said to be [active]{#extendableevent-active
.dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-for="ExtendableEvent" dfn-type="dfn" export=""}
when its [timed out
flag](#extendableevent-timed-out-flag){#ref-for-extendableevent-timed-out-flag
link-type="dfn"} is unset and either its [pending promises
count](#extendableevent-pending-promises-count){#ref-for-extendableevent-pending-promises-count①
link-type="dfn"} is greater than zero or its [dispatch
flag](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#dispatch-flag){#ref-for-dispatch-flag
link-type="dfn"} is set.

[Service workers](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑥⑤
link-type="dfn"} have two [lifecycle
events](#dfn-lifecycle-events){#ref-for-dfn-lifecycle-events
link-type="dfn"},
[`install`{.idl}](#service-worker-global-scope-install-event){#ref-for-service-worker-global-scope-install-event②
.idl-code link-type="event"} and
[`activate`{.idl}](#service-worker-global-scope-activate-event){#ref-for-service-worker-global-scope-activate-event②
.idl-code link-type="event"}. [Service
workers](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑥⑥
link-type="dfn"} use the
[`ExtendableEvent`{.idl}](#extendableevent){#ref-for-extendableevent⑥
link-type="idl"} interface for
[`activate`{.idl}](#service-worker-global-scope-activate-event){#ref-for-service-worker-global-scope-activate-event③
.idl-code link-type="event"} event and
[`install`{.idl}](#service-worker-global-scope-install-event){#ref-for-service-worker-global-scope-install-event③
.idl-code link-type="event"} event.

[Service worker extensions](#extensibility) that [define event
handlers](#extension-to-service-worker-global-scope) *may* also use or
extend the
[`ExtendableEvent`{.idl}](#extendableevent){#ref-for-extendableevent⑦
link-type="idl"} interface.

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="wait-until-method"}
#### [4.4.1. ]{.secno}[[`event.waitUntil(f)`{.idl}](#dom-extendableevent-waituntil){#ref-for-dom-extendableevent-waituntil① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#wait-until-method){.self-link} {#wait-until-method .heading .settled level="4.4.1"}

[Note:]{.marker}
[`waitUntil()`{.idl}](#dom-extendableevent-waituntil){#ref-for-dom-extendableevent-waituntil②
link-type="idl"} method extends the lifetime of the event.

The [`waitUntil(``f`{.variable}`)`]{#dom-extendableevent-waituntil .dfn
.dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="ExtendableEvent" dfn-type="method"
export=""} method steps are to [add lifetime
promise](#extendableevent-add-lifetime-promise){#ref-for-extendableevent-add-lifetime-promise
link-type="dfn"} `f`{.variable} to
[this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this⑤①
link-type="dfn"}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="add-lifetime-promise"}
To [add lifetime promise]{#extendableevent-add-lifetime-promise .dfn
.dfn-paneled dfn-for="ExtendableEvent" dfn-type="dfn" export=""}
`promise`{.variable} (a
[promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects){#ref-for-sec-promise-objects②⓪
link-type="dfn"}) to `event`{.variable} (an
[`ExtendableEvent`{.idl}](#extendableevent){#ref-for-extendableevent⑧
link-type="idl"}), run these steps:

1.  If `event`{.variable}'s
    [`isTrusted`{.idl}](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-event-istrusted){#ref-for-dom-event-istrusted
    link-type="idl"} attribute is false,
    [throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw){#ref-for-dfn-throw②
    link-type="dfn"} an
    \"[`InvalidStateError`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#invalidstateerror){#ref-for-invalidstateerror⑥
    link-type="idl"}\"
    [`DOMException`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException){#ref-for-idl-DOMException⑨
    link-type="idl"}.

2.  If `event`{.variable} is not
    [active](#extendableevent-active){#ref-for-extendableevent-active
    link-type="dfn"},
    [throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw){#ref-for-dfn-throw③
    link-type="dfn"} an
    \"[`InvalidStateError`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#invalidstateerror){#ref-for-invalidstateerror⑦
    link-type="idl"}\"
    [`DOMException`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException){#ref-for-idl-DOMException①⓪
    link-type="idl"}.

    [Note:]{.marker} If no lifetime extension promise has been added in
    the task that called the event handlers, calling
    [`waitUntil()`{.idl}](#dom-extendableevent-waituntil){#ref-for-dom-extendableevent-waituntil③
    link-type="idl"} in subsequent asynchronous tasks will throw.

3.  Add `promise`{.variable} to `event`{.variable}'s [extend lifetime
    promises](#extendableevent-extend-lifetime-promises){#ref-for-extendableevent-extend-lifetime-promises①
    link-type="dfn"}.

4.  Increment `event`{.variable}'s [pending promises
    count](#extendableevent-pending-promises-count){#ref-for-extendableevent-pending-promises-count②
    link-type="dfn"} by one.

    [Note:]{.marker} The [pending promises
    count](#extendableevent-pending-promises-count){#ref-for-extendableevent-pending-promises-count③
    link-type="dfn"} is incremented even if the given promise has
    already been settled. The corresponding count decrement is done in
    the microtask queued by the reaction to the promise.

5.  Upon
    [fulfillment](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#upon-fulfillment){#ref-for-upon-fulfillment
    link-type="dfn"} or
    [rejection](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#upon-rejection){#ref-for-upon-rejection
    link-type="dfn"} of `promise`{.variable}, [queue a
