    microtask](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-microtask){#ref-for-queue-a-microtask
    link-type="dfn"} to run these substeps:

    1.  Decrement `event`{.variable}'s [pending promises
        count](#extendableevent-pending-promises-count){#ref-for-extendableevent-pending-promises-count④
        link-type="dfn"} by one.

    2.  If `event`{.variable}'s [pending promises
        count](#extendableevent-pending-promises-count){#ref-for-extendableevent-pending-promises-count⑤
        link-type="dfn"} is 0, then:

        1.  Let `registration`{.variable} be the [current global
            object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#current-global-object){#ref-for-current-global-object②
            link-type="dfn"}'s associated [service
            worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker①⑨
            link-type="dfn"}'s [containing service worker
            registration](#dfn-containing-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-containing-service-worker-registration①②
            link-type="dfn"}.

        2.  If `registration`{.variable} is
            [unregistered](#dfn-service-worker-registration-unregistered){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration-unregistered②
            link-type="dfn"}, invoke [Try Clear
            Registration](#try-clear-registration){#ref-for-try-clear-registration
            link-type="dfn"} with `registration`{.variable}.

        3.  If `registration`{.variable} is not null, invoke [Try
            Activate](#try-activate){#ref-for-try-activate①
            link-type="dfn"} with `registration`{.variable}.

The user agent *should not*
[terminate](#terminate-service-worker){#ref-for-terminate-service-worker②
link-type="dfn"} a [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑥⑦
link-type="dfn"} if [Service Worker Has No Pending
Events](#service-worker-has-no-pending-events){#ref-for-service-worker-has-no-pending-events
link-type="dfn"} returns false for that [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑥⑧
link-type="dfn"}.
:::

[Service workers](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑥⑨
link-type="dfn"} and [extensions](#extensibility) that [define event
handlers](#extension-to-service-worker-global-scope) *may* define their
own behaviors, allowing the [extend lifetime
promises](#extendableevent-extend-lifetime-promises){#ref-for-extendableevent-extend-lifetime-promises②
link-type="dfn"} to suggest operation length, and the rejected state of
any of the
[promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects){#ref-for-sec-promise-objects②①
link-type="dfn"} in [extend lifetime
promises](#extendableevent-extend-lifetime-promises){#ref-for-extendableevent-extend-lifetime-promises③
link-type="dfn"} to suggest operation failure.

[Note:]{.marker} [Service
workers](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑦⓪
link-type="dfn"} delay treating the [installing
worker](#dfn-installing-worker){#ref-for-dfn-installing-worker⑥
link-type="dfn"} as \"`installed`\" until all the
[promises](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects){#ref-for-sec-promise-objects②②
link-type="dfn"} in the
[`install`{.idl}](#service-worker-global-scope-install-event){#ref-for-service-worker-global-scope-install-event④
.idl-code link-type="event"} event's [extend lifetime
promises](#extendableevent-extend-lifetime-promises){#ref-for-extendableevent-extend-lifetime-promises④
link-type="dfn"} resolve successfully. (See the relevant [Install
algorithm step](#install-settle-step).) If any of the promises rejects,
the installation fails. This is primarily used to ensure that a [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑦①
link-type="dfn"} is not considered \"`installed`\" until all of the core
caches it depends on are populated. Likewise, [service
workers](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑦②
link-type="dfn"} delay treating the [active
worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker①⑦
link-type="dfn"} as \"`activated`\" until all the
[promises](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects){#ref-for-sec-promise-objects②③
link-type="dfn"} in the
[`activate`{.idl}](#service-worker-global-scope-activate-event){#ref-for-service-worker-global-scope-activate-event④
.idl-code link-type="event"} event's [extend lifetime
promises](#extendableevent-extend-lifetime-promises){#ref-for-extendableevent-extend-lifetime-promises⑤
link-type="dfn"} settle. (See the relevant [Activate algorithm
step](#activate-settle-step).) This is primarily used to ensure that any
[functional
events](#dfn-functional-events){#ref-for-dfn-functional-events①
link-type="dfn"} are not dispatched to the [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑦③
link-type="dfn"} until it upgrades database schemas and deletes the
outdated cache entries.
:::::

:::: section
### [4.5. ]{.secno}[[`InstallEvent`{.idl}](#installevent){#ref-for-installevent link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#installevent-interface){.self-link} {#installevent-interface .heading .settled level="4.5"}

``` {.def .highlight .idl}
[Exposed=ServiceWorker]
interface InstallEvent : ExtendableEvent {
  constructor(DOMString type, optional ExtendableEventInit eventInitDict = {});
  Promise<undefined> addRoutes((RouterRule or sequence<RouterRule>) rules);
};

dictionary RouterRule {
  required RouterCondition condition;
  required RouterSource source;
};

dictionary RouterCondition {
  URLPatternCompatible urlPattern;
  ByteString requestMethod;
  RequestMode requestMode;
  RequestDestination requestDestination;
  RunningStatus runningStatus;

  sequence<RouterCondition> _or;
  RouterCondition not;
};

typedef (RouterSourceDict or RouterSourceEnum) RouterSource;

dictionary RouterSourceDict {
  DOMString cacheName;
};

enum RunningStatus { "running", "not-running" };
enum RouterSourceEnum {
  "cache",
  "fetch-event",
  "network",
  "race-network-and-fetch-handler"
};
```

A [count router condition result]{#dfn-count-router-condition-result
.dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""} is a
[struct](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct){#ref-for-struct②
link-type="dfn"} that consists of:

- A [condition count]{#dfn-count-router-condition-result-condition-count
  .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-for="count router condition result"
  dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""} (a number).

- A [quota
  exceeded]{#dfn-dfn-count-router-condition-result-quota-exceeded .dfn
  .dfn-paneled dfn-for="count router condition result" dfn-type="dfn"
  noexport=""} (a boolean).

::: section
#### [4.5.1. ]{.secno}[[`event.addRoutes(rules)`{.idl}](#dom-installevent-addroutes){#ref-for-dom-installevent-addroutes① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#register-router-method){.self-link} {#register-router-method .heading .settled level="4.5.1"}

[Note:]{.marker}
[`addRoutes(rules)`{.idl}](#dom-installevent-addroutes){#ref-for-dom-installevent-addroutes②
link-type="idl"} registers rules for this service worker to offload
simple tasks that the fetch event handler ordinarily does.

The [`addRoutes(``rules`{.variable}`)`]{#dom-installevent-addroutes .dfn
.dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="InstallEvent" dfn-type="method"
export=""} method steps are:

1.  If `rules`{.variable} is a
    [`RouterRule`{.idl}](#dictdef-routerrule){#ref-for-dictdef-routerrule③
    link-type="idl"} dictionary, set `rules`{.variable} to «
    `rules`{.variable} ».

2.  Let `serviceWorker`{.variable} be the [current global
    object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#current-global-object){#ref-for-current-global-object③
    link-type="dfn"}'s associated [service
    worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker②⓪
    link-type="dfn"}.

3.  For each `rule`{.variable} of `rules`{.variable}:

    1.  If running the [Verify Router
        Condition](#verify-router-condition){#ref-for-verify-router-condition
        link-type="dfn"} algorithm with
        `rule`{.variable}\[\"[`condition`{.idl}](#dom-routerrule-condition){#ref-for-dom-routerrule-condition
        link-type="idl"}\"\] and `serviceWorker`{.variable} returns
        false, return [a promise rejected
        with](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-promise-rejected-with){#ref-for-a-promise-rejected-with②
        link-type="dfn"} a
        [`TypeError`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#exceptiondef-typeerror){#ref-for-exceptiondef-typeerror
        link-type="idl"}.

    2.  If
        `rule`{.variable}\[\"[`source`{.idl}](#dom-routerrule-source){#ref-for-dom-routerrule-source
        link-type="idl"}\"\] is either of
        \"[`fetch-event`{.idl}](#dom-routersourceenum-fetch-event){#ref-for-dom-routersourceenum-fetch-event
        link-type="idl"}\" or
        \"[`race-network-and-fetch-handler`{.idl}](#dom-routersourceenum-race-network-and-fetch-handler){#ref-for-dom-routersourceenum-race-network-and-fetch-handler①
        link-type="idl"}\", and `serviceWorker`{.variable}'s [set of
        event types to
        handle](#dfn-set-of-event-types-to-handle){#ref-for-dfn-set-of-event-types-to-handle
        link-type="dfn"} does not
        [contain](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-contain){#ref-for-list-contain
        link-type="dfn"}
        [`fetch`{.idl}](#service-worker-global-scope-fetch-event){#ref-for-service-worker-global-scope-fetch-event③
        .idl-code link-type="event"}, return [a promise rejected
        with](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-promise-rejected-with){#ref-for-a-promise-rejected-with③
        link-type="dfn"} a
        [`TypeError`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#exceptiondef-typeerror){#ref-for-exceptiondef-typeerror①
        link-type="idl"}.

4.  Let `lifetimePromise`{.variable} be a new
    [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects){#ref-for-sec-promise-objects②④
    link-type="dfn"}.

5.  [Add lifetime
    promise](#extendableevent-add-lifetime-promise){#ref-for-extendableevent-add-lifetime-promise①
    link-type="dfn"} `lifetimePromise`{.variable} to
    [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this⑤②
    link-type="dfn"}.

    [Note:]{.marker}
    [`event.addRoutes(rules)`{.idl}](#dom-installevent-addroutes){#ref-for-dom-installevent-addroutes③
    link-type="idl"} extends the lifetime of the event by default as if
    [`event.waitUntil(promise)`{.idl}](#dom-extendableevent-waituntil){#ref-for-dom-extendableevent-waituntil④
    link-type="idl"} is called.

6.  Let `promise`{.variable} be a new
    [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects){#ref-for-sec-promise-objects②⑤
    link-type="dfn"}.

7.  Upon
    [fulfillment](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#upon-fulfillment){#ref-for-upon-fulfillment①
    link-type="dfn"} or
    [rejection](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#upon-rejection){#ref-for-upon-rejection①
    link-type="dfn"} of `promise`{.variable}, resolve
    `lifetimePromise`{.variable} with undefined.

    [Note:]{.marker} this step is for making
    `lifetimePromise`{.variable} always fullfilled to avoid the install
    event failure.

8.  [Enqueue](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#queue-enqueue){#ref-for-queue-enqueue
    link-type="dfn"} the following steps to [\[\[service worker
    queue\]\]](#service-worker-service-worker-queue){#ref-for-service-worker-service-worker-queue
    link-type="dfn"}:

    1.  Let `allRules`{.variable} be a copy of
        `serviceWorker`{.variable}'s [list of router
        rules](#service-worker-list-of-router-rules){#ref-for-service-worker-list-of-router-rules
        link-type="dfn"}.

    2.  For each `rule`{.variable} of `rules`{.variable}:

        1.  Append `rule`{.variable} to `allRules`{.variable}.

    3.  If running the [Check Router Registration
        Limit](#check-router-registration-limit){#ref-for-check-router-registration-limit
        link-type="dfn"} with `allRules`{.variable} returns false,
        reject `promise`{.variable} with a
        [`TypeError`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#exceptiondef-typeerror){#ref-for-exceptiondef-typeerror②
        link-type="idl"}.

    4.  Set `serviceWorker`{.variable}'s [list of router
        rules](#service-worker-list-of-router-rules){#ref-for-service-worker-list-of-router-rules①
        link-type="dfn"} to `allRules`{.variable}.

    5.  Let `serviceWorkerEventLoop`{.variable} be the [current global
        object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#current-global-object){#ref-for-current-global-object④
        link-type="dfn"}'s [event
        loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-loop){#ref-for-event-loop⑨
        link-type="dfn"}.

    6.  [Queue a
        task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task){#ref-for-queue-a-task①⑥
        link-type="dfn"} to run the following steps on
        `serviceWorkerEventLoop`{.variable} using the [DOM manipulation
        task
        source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source){#ref-for-dom-manipulation-task-source①⓪
        link-type="dfn"}:

        1.  Resolve `promise`{.variable} with undefined.

9.  Return `promise`{.variable}.
:::
::::

:::::::::: section
### [4.6. ]{.secno}[[`FetchEvent`{.idl}](#fetchevent){#ref-for-fetchevent link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#fetchevent-interface){.self-link} {#fetchevent-interface .heading .settled level="4.6"}

``` {.def .highlight .idl}
[Exposed=ServiceWorker]
interface FetchEvent : ExtendableEvent {
  constructor(DOMString type, FetchEventInit eventInitDict);
  [SameObject] readonly attribute Request request;
  readonly attribute Promise<any> preloadResponse;
  readonly attribute DOMString clientId;
  readonly attribute DOMString resultingClientId;
  readonly attribute DOMString replacesClientId;
  readonly attribute Promise<undefined> handled;

  undefined respondWith(Promise<Response> r);
};
```

``` {#fetch-event-init-dictionary .def .highlight .idl}
dictionary FetchEventInit : ExtendableEventInit {
  required Request request;
  Promise<any> preloadResponse;
  DOMString clientId = "";
  DOMString resultingClientId = "";
  DOMString replacesClientId = "";
  Promise<undefined> handled;
};
```

[Service workers](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑦④
link-type="dfn"} have an essential [functional
event](#dfn-functional-events){#ref-for-dfn-functional-events②
link-type="dfn"}
[`fetch`{.idl}](#service-worker-global-scope-fetch-event){#ref-for-service-worker-global-scope-fetch-event④
.idl-code link-type="event"}. For
[`fetch`{.idl}](#service-worker-global-scope-fetch-event){#ref-for-service-worker-global-scope-fetch-event⑤
.idl-code link-type="event"} event, [service
workers](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑦⑤
link-type="dfn"} use the
[`FetchEvent`{.idl}](#fetchevent){#ref-for-fetchevent① link-type="idl"}
interface which extends the
[`ExtendableEvent`{.idl}](#extendableevent){#ref-for-extendableevent①①
link-type="idl"} interface.

Each event using [`FetchEvent`{.idl}](#fetchevent){#ref-for-fetchevent②
link-type="idl"} interface has an associated [potential
response]{#fetchevent-potential-response .dfn .dfn-paneled
dfn-for="FetchEvent" dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""} (a
[response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response){#ref-for-concept-response②
link-type="dfn"}), initially set to null, and the following associated
flags that are initially unset:

- [wait to respond flag]{#fetchevent-wait-to-respond-flag .dfn
  .dfn-paneled dfn-for="FetchEvent" dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}

- [respond-with entered flag]{#fetchevent-respond-with-entered-flag .dfn
  .dfn-paneled dfn-for="FetchEvent" dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}

- [respond-with error flag]{#fetchevent-respond-with-error-flag .dfn
  .dfn-paneled dfn-for="FetchEvent" dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}

::: section
#### [4.6.1. ]{.secno}[[`event.request`{.idl}](#dom-fetchevent-request){#ref-for-dom-fetchevent-request① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#fetch-event-request){.self-link} {#fetch-event-request .heading .settled level="4.6.1"}

[`request`]{#dom-fetchevent-request .dfn .dfn-paneled .idl-code
dfn-for="FetchEvent" dfn-type="attribute" export=""} attribute *must*
return the value it was initialized to.
:::

::: section
#### [4.6.2. ]{.secno}[[`event.preloadResponse`{.idl}](#dom-fetchevent-preloadresponse){#ref-for-dom-fetchevent-preloadresponse① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#fetch-event-preloadresponse){.self-link} {#fetch-event-preloadresponse .heading .settled level="4.6.2"}

[`preloadResponse`]{#dom-fetchevent-preloadresponse .dfn .dfn-paneled
.idl-code dfn-for="FetchEvent" dfn-type="attribute" export=""} attribute
*must* return the value it was initialized to. When an
[event](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event){#ref-for-concept-event②
link-type="dfn"} is created the attribute *must* be initialized to [a
promise resolved
with](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-promise-resolved-with){#ref-for-a-promise-resolved-with
link-type="dfn"} undefined.
:::

::: section
#### [4.6.3. ]{.secno}[[`event.clientId`{.idl}](#dom-fetchevent-clientid){#ref-for-dom-fetchevent-clientid① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#fetch-event-clientid){.self-link} {#fetch-event-clientid .heading .settled level="4.6.3"}

[`clientId`]{#dom-fetchevent-clientid .dfn .dfn-paneled .idl-code
dfn-for="FetchEvent" dfn-type="attribute" export=""} attribute *must*
return the value it was initialized to. When an
[event](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event){#ref-for-concept-event③
link-type="dfn"} is created the attribute *must* be initialized to the
empty string.
:::

::: section
#### [4.6.4. ]{.secno}[[`event.resultingClientId`{.idl}](#dom-fetchevent-resultingclientid){#ref-for-dom-fetchevent-resultingclientid① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#fetch-event-resultingclientid){.self-link} {#fetch-event-resultingclientid .heading .settled level="4.6.4"}

[`resultingClientId`]{#dom-fetchevent-resultingclientid .dfn
.dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="FetchEvent" dfn-type="attribute"
export=""} attribute *must* return the value it was initialized to. When
an
[event](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event){#ref-for-concept-event④
link-type="dfn"} is created the attribute *must* be initialized to the
empty string.
:::

::: section
#### [4.6.5. ]{.secno}[[`event.replacesClientId`{.idl}](#dom-fetchevent-replacesclientid){#ref-for-dom-fetchevent-replacesclientid① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#fetch-event-replacesClientId){.self-link} {#fetch-event-replacesClientId .heading .settled level="4.6.5"}

[`replacesClientId`]{#dom-fetchevent-replacesclientid .dfn .dfn-paneled
.idl-code dfn-for="FetchEvent" dfn-type="attribute" export=""} attribute
*must* return the value it was initialized to. When an
[event](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event){#ref-for-concept-event⑤
link-type="dfn"} is created the attribute *must* be initialized to the
empty string.
:::

::: section
#### [4.6.6. ]{.secno}[[`event.handled`{.idl}](#dom-fetchevent-handled){#ref-for-dom-fetchevent-handled① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#fetch-event-handled){.self-link} {#fetch-event-handled .heading .settled level="4.6.6"}

[`handled`]{#dom-fetchevent-handled .dfn .dfn-paneled .idl-code
dfn-for="FetchEvent" dfn-type="attribute" export=""} attribute *must*
return the value it was initialized to. When an
[event](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event){#ref-for-concept-event⑥
link-type="dfn"} is created the attribute *must* be initialized to a
pending
[promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects){#ref-for-sec-promise-objects②⑥
link-type="dfn"}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="fetch-event-respondwith"}
#### [4.6.7. ]{.secno}[[`event.respondWith(r)`{.idl}](#dom-fetchevent-respondwith){#ref-for-dom-fetchevent-respondwith① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#fetch-event-respondwith){.self-link} {#fetch-event-respondwith .heading .settled level="4.6.7"}

[Note:]{.marker} Developers can set the argument `r`{.variable} with
either a
[promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects){#ref-for-sec-promise-objects②⑦
link-type="dfn"} that resolves with a
[`Response`{.idl}](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response){#ref-for-response①
link-type="idl"} object or a
[`Response`{.idl}](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response){#ref-for-response②
link-type="idl"} object (which is automatically cast to a promise).
Otherwise, a [network
error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error){#ref-for-concept-network-error
link-type="dfn"} is returned to
[Fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch){#ref-for-concept-fetch②
link-type="dfn"}. Renderer-side security checks about tainting for
cross-origin content are tied to the types of [filtered
responses](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-filtered-response){#ref-for-concept-filtered-response
link-type="dfn"} defined in
[Fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch){#ref-for-concept-fetch③
link-type="dfn"}.

[`respondWith(``r`{.variable}`)`]{#dom-fetchevent-respondwith .dfn
.dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="FetchEvent" dfn-type="method" export=""}
method steps are:

1.  Let `event`{.variable} be
    [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this⑤③
    link-type="dfn"}.

2.  If `event`{.variable}'s [dispatch
    flag](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#dispatch-flag){#ref-for-dispatch-flag①
    link-type="dfn"} is unset,
    [throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw){#ref-for-dfn-throw④
    link-type="dfn"} an
    \"[`InvalidStateError`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#invalidstateerror){#ref-for-invalidstateerror⑧
    link-type="idl"}\"
    [`DOMException`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException){#ref-for-idl-DOMException①①
    link-type="idl"}.

3.  If `event`{.variable}'s [respond-with entered
    flag](#fetchevent-respond-with-entered-flag){#ref-for-fetchevent-respond-with-entered-flag
    link-type="dfn"} is set,
    [throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw){#ref-for-dfn-throw⑤
    link-type="dfn"} an
    \"[`InvalidStateError`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#invalidstateerror){#ref-for-invalidstateerror⑨
    link-type="idl"}\"
    [`DOMException`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException){#ref-for-idl-DOMException①②
    link-type="idl"}.

4.  [Add lifetime
    promise](#extendableevent-add-lifetime-promise){#ref-for-extendableevent-add-lifetime-promise②
    link-type="dfn"} `r`{.variable} to `event`{.variable}.

    [Note:]{.marker}
    [`event.respondWith(r)`{.idl}](#dom-fetchevent-respondwith){#ref-for-dom-fetchevent-respondwith②
    link-type="idl"} extends the lifetime of the event by default as if
    [`event.waitUntil(r)`{.idl}](#dom-extendableevent-waituntil){#ref-for-dom-extendableevent-waituntil⑤
    link-type="idl"} is called.

5.  Set `event`{.variable}'s [stop propagation
    flag](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#stop-propagation-flag){#ref-for-stop-propagation-flag
    link-type="dfn"} and [stop immediate propagation
    flag](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#stop-immediate-propagation-flag){#ref-for-stop-immediate-propagation-flag
    link-type="dfn"}.

6.  Set `event`{.variable}'s [respond-with entered
    flag](#fetchevent-respond-with-entered-flag){#ref-for-fetchevent-respond-with-entered-flag①
    link-type="dfn"}.

7.  Set `event`{.variable}'s [wait to respond
    flag](#fetchevent-wait-to-respond-flag){#ref-for-fetchevent-wait-to-respond-flag
    link-type="dfn"}.

8.  Let `targetRealm`{.variable} be `event`{.variable}'s [relevant
    Realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-realm){#ref-for-concept-relevant-realm③
    link-type="dfn"}.

9.  [Upon
    rejection](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#upon-rejection){#ref-for-upon-rejection②
    link-type="dfn"} of `r`{.variable}:

    1.  Set `event`{.variable}'s [respond-with error
        flag](#fetchevent-respond-with-error-flag){#ref-for-fetchevent-respond-with-error-flag
        link-type="dfn"}.

    2.  Unset `event`{.variable}'s [wait to respond
        flag](#fetchevent-wait-to-respond-flag){#ref-for-fetchevent-wait-to-respond-flag①
        link-type="dfn"}.

10. [Upon
    fulfillment](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#upon-fulfillment){#ref-for-upon-fulfillment②
    link-type="dfn"} of `r`{.variable} with `response`{.variable}:

    1.  If `response`{.variable} is not a
        [`Response`{.idl}](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response){#ref-for-response③
        link-type="idl"} object, then set the [respond-with error
        flag](#fetchevent-respond-with-error-flag){#ref-for-fetchevent-respond-with-error-flag①
        link-type="dfn"}.

        [Note:]{.marker} If the [respond-with error
        flag](#fetchevent-respond-with-error-flag){#ref-for-fetchevent-respond-with-error-flag②
        link-type="dfn"} is set, a [network
        error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error){#ref-for-concept-network-error①
        link-type="dfn"} is returned to
        [Fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch){#ref-for-concept-fetch④
        link-type="dfn"} through [Handle
        Fetch](#handle-fetch){#ref-for-handle-fetch② link-type="dfn"}
        algorithm. (See the step 21.1.) Otherwise, the value
        `response`{.variable} is returned to
        [Fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch){#ref-for-concept-fetch⑤
        link-type="dfn"} through [Handle
        Fetch](#handle-fetch){#ref-for-handle-fetch③ link-type="dfn"}
        algorithm. (See the step 22.1.)

    2.  Else:

        1.  Let `bytes`{.variable} be an empty byte sequence.

        2.  Let `end-of-body`{.variable} be false.

        3.  Let `done`{.variable} be false.

        4.  Let `potentialResponse`{.variable} be a copy of
            `response`{.variable}'s associated
            [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-response){#ref-for-concept-response-response
            link-type="dfn"}, except for its
            [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body){#ref-for-concept-response-body
            link-type="dfn"}.

        5.  If `response`{.variable}'s
            [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body){#ref-for-concept-response-body①
            link-type="dfn"} is non-null, run these substeps:

            1.  Let `reader`{.variable} be the result of [getting a
                reader](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-get-a-reader){#ref-for-readablestream-get-a-reader
                link-type="dfn"} from `response`{.variable}'s
                [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body){#ref-for-concept-response-body②
                link-type="dfn"}'s
                [stream](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-stream){#ref-for-concept-body-stream
                link-type="dfn"}.

            2.  Let `pullAlgorithm`{.variable} be an action that runs
                these steps:

                1.  Let `readRequest`{.variable} be a new [read
                    request](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#read-request){#ref-for-read-request
                    link-type="dfn"} with the following
                    [items](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct-item){#ref-for-struct-item①
                    link-type="dfn"}:

                    [chunk steps](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#read-request-chunk-steps){#ref-for-read-request-chunk-steps link-type="dfn"}, given `chunk`{.variable}

                    :   1.  Assert: `chunk`{.variable} is a
                            [`Uint8Array`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-Uint8Array){#ref-for-idl-Uint8Array
                            link-type="idl"}.

                        2.  Append the bytes represented by
                            `chunk`{.variable} to `bytes`{.variable}.

                        3.  Increment `potentialResponse`{.variable}'s
                            [body
                            info](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body-info){#ref-for-concept-response-body-info
                            link-type="dfn"}'s [encoded
                            size](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-timing-info-encoded-body-size){#ref-for-fetch-timing-info-encoded-body-size
                            link-type="dfn"} by `bytes`{.variable}'s
                            [byte
                            length](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#buffersource-byte-length){#ref-for-buffersource-byte-length
                            link-type="dfn"}.

                        4.  Increment `potentialResponse`{.variable}'s
                            [body
                            info](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body-info){#ref-for-concept-response-body-info①
                            link-type="dfn"}'s [decoded
                            size](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-timing-info-decoded-body-size){#ref-for-fetch-timing-info-decoded-body-size
                            link-type="dfn"} by `bytes`{.variable}'s
                            [byte
                            length](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#buffersource-byte-length){#ref-for-buffersource-byte-length①
                            link-type="dfn"}.

                        5.  Perform !
                            [DetachArrayBuffer](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-detacharraybuffer){#ref-for-sec-detacharraybuffer
                            link-type="dfn"}(`chunk`{.variable}.\[\[ViewedArrayBuffer\]\]).

                    [close steps](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#read-request-close-steps){#ref-for-read-request-close-steps link-type="dfn"}

                    :   1.  Set `end-of-body`{.variable} to true.

                    [error steps](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#read-request-error-steps){#ref-for-read-request-error-steps link-type="dfn"}

                    :   1.  [error](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-error){#ref-for-readablestream-error
                            link-type="dfn"} `newStream`{.variable} with
                            a
                            [`TypeError`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#exceptiondef-typeerror){#ref-for-exceptiondef-typeerror③
                            link-type="idl"}.

                2.  [Read a
                    chunk](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestreamdefaultreader-read-a-chunk){#ref-for-readablestreamdefaultreader-read-a-chunk
                    link-type="dfn"} from `reader`{.variable} given
                    `readRequest`{.variable}.

            3.  Let `cancelAlgorithm`{.variable} be an action that
                [cancels](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestreamdefaultreader-cancel){#ref-for-readablestreamdefaultreader-cancel
                link-type="dfn"} `reader`{.variable}.

            4.  Let `highWaterMark`{.variable} be a non-negative,
                non-NaN number, chosen by the user agent.

            5.  Let `sizeAlgorithm`{.variable} be an algorithm that
                accepts a
                [chunk](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#chunk){#ref-for-chunk
                link-type="dfn"} object and returns a non-negative,
                non-NaN, non-infinite number, chosen by the user agent.

            6.  Let `newStream`{.variable} be a new
                [`ReadableStream`{.idl}](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream){#ref-for-readablestream
                link-type="idl"}, [set
                up](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-set-up){#ref-for-readablestream-set-up
                link-type="dfn"} with the
                [pullAlgorithm](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-set-up-pullalgorithm){#ref-for-readablestream-set-up-pullalgorithm
                link-type="dfn"} `pullAlgorithm`{.variable}, the
                [cancelAlgorithm](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-set-up-cancelalgorithm){#ref-for-readablestream-set-up-cancelalgorithm
                link-type="dfn"} `cancelAlgorithm`{.variable}, the
                [highWaterMark](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-set-up-highwatermark){#ref-for-readablestream-set-up-highwatermark
                link-type="dfn"} `highWaterMark`{.variable}, and
                [sizeAlgorithm](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-set-up-sizealgorithm){#ref-for-readablestream-set-up-sizealgorithm
                link-type="dfn"} `sizeAlgorithm`{.variable}, in
                `targetRealm`{.variable}.

            7.  Set `potentialResponse`{.variable}'s
                [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body){#ref-for-concept-response-body③
                link-type="dfn"} to a new
                [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body){#ref-for-concept-body
                link-type="dfn"} whose
                [stream](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-stream){#ref-for-concept-body-stream①
                link-type="dfn"} is `newStream`{.variable}.

            8.  Run these subsubsteps repeatedly [in
                parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel){#ref-for-in-parallel①④
                link-type="dfn"} while `done`{.variable} is false:

                1.  If `newStream`{.variable} is
                    [errored](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-errored){#ref-for-readablestream-errored
                    link-type="dfn"}, then set `done`{.variable} to
                    true.

                2.  Otherwise, if `bytes`{.variable} is empty and
                    `end-of-body`{.variable} is true, then
                    [close](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-close){#ref-for-readablestream-close
                    link-type="dfn"} `newStream`{.variable} and set
                    `done`{.variable} to true.

                3.  Otherwise, if `bytes`{.variable} is not empty, run
                    these subsubsubsteps:

                    1.  Let `chunk`{.variable} be a subsequence of
                        `bytes`{.variable} starting from the beginning
                        of `bytes`{.variable}.

                    2.  Remove `chunk`{.variable} from
                        `bytes`{.variable}.

                    3.  Let `buffer`{.variable} be an
                        [`ArrayBuffer`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-ArrayBuffer){#ref-for-idl-ArrayBuffer
                        link-type="idl"} object created in
                        `targetRealm`{.variable} and containing
                        `chunk`{.variable}.

                    4.  [Enqueue](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-enqueue){#ref-for-readablestream-enqueue
                        link-type="dfn"} a
                        [`Uint8Array`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-Uint8Array){#ref-for-idl-Uint8Array①
                        link-type="idl"} object created in
                        `targetRealm`{.variable} and wrapping
                        `buffer`{.variable} to `newStream`{.variable}.

            [Note:]{.marker} These substeps are meant to produce the
            observable equivalent of \"piping\" `response`{.variable}'s
            [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body){#ref-for-concept-response-body④
            link-type="dfn"}'s
            [stream](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-stream){#ref-for-concept-body-stream②
            link-type="dfn"} into `potentialResponse`{.variable}.

            [Note:]{.marker} The data written by the service worker in
            chunks are not guaranteed to read in identical chunks by the
            client that receives the data. That is, the client will read
            the same data that was written, but it may be chunked
            differently by the browser.

        6.  Set `event`{.variable}'s [potential
            response](#fetchevent-potential-response){#ref-for-fetchevent-potential-response
            link-type="dfn"} to `potentialResponse`{.variable}.

    3.  Unset `event`{.variable}'s [wait to respond
        flag](#fetchevent-wait-to-respond-flag){#ref-for-fetchevent-wait-to-respond-flag②
        link-type="dfn"}.
:::
::::::::::

:::::::: section
### [4.7. ]{.secno}[[`ExtendableMessageEvent`{.idl}](#extendablemessageevent){#ref-for-extendablemessageevent② link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#extendablemessageevent-interface){.self-link} {#extendablemessageevent-interface .heading .settled level="4.7"}

``` {.def .highlight .idl}
[Exposed=ServiceWorker]
interface ExtendableMessageEvent : ExtendableEvent {
  constructor(DOMString type, optional ExtendableMessageEventInit eventInitDict = {});
  readonly attribute any data;
  readonly attribute USVString origin;
  readonly attribute DOMString lastEventId;
  [SameObject] readonly attribute (Client or ServiceWorker or MessagePort)? source;
  readonly attribute FrozenArray<MessagePort> ports;
};
```

``` {#extendablemessage-event-init-dictionary .def .highlight .idl}
dictionary ExtendableMessageEventInit : ExtendableEventInit {
  any data = null;
  USVString origin = "";
  DOMString lastEventId = "";
  (Client or ServiceWorker or MessagePort)? source = null;
  sequence<MessagePort> ports = [];
};
```

[Service workers](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑦⑥
link-type="dfn"} define the
[extendable](#dom-extendableevent-waituntil){#ref-for-dom-extendableevent-waituntil⑥
.idl-code link-type="method"}
[`message`{.idl}](#eventdef-serviceworkerglobalscope-message){#ref-for-eventdef-serviceworkerglobalscope-message③
.idl-code link-type="event"} event to allow extending the lifetime of
the event. For the
[`message`{.idl}](#eventdef-serviceworkerglobalscope-message){#ref-for-eventdef-serviceworkerglobalscope-message④
.idl-code link-type="event"} event, [service
workers](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑦⑦
link-type="dfn"} use the
[`ExtendableMessageEvent`{.idl}](#extendablemessageevent){#ref-for-extendablemessageevent③
link-type="idl"} interface which extends the
[`ExtendableEvent`{.idl}](#extendableevent){#ref-for-extendableevent①③
link-type="idl"} interface.

::: section
#### [4.7.1. ]{.secno}[[`event.data`{.idl}](#dom-extendablemessageevent-data){#ref-for-dom-extendablemessageevent-data② link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#extendablemessage-event-data){.self-link} {#extendablemessage-event-data .heading .settled level="4.7.1"}

The [`data`]{#dom-extendablemessageevent-data .dfn .dfn-paneled
.idl-code dfn-for="ExtendableMessageEvent" dfn-type="attribute"
export=""} attribute *must* return the value it was initialized to. When
the object is created, this attribute *must* be initialized to null. It
represents the message being sent.
:::

::: section
#### [4.7.2. ]{.secno}[[`event.origin`{.idl}](#dom-extendablemessageevent-origin){#ref-for-dom-extendablemessageevent-origin③ link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#extendablemessage-event-origin){.self-link} {#extendablemessage-event-origin .heading .settled level="4.7.2"}

The [`origin`]{#dom-extendablemessageevent-origin .dfn .dfn-paneled
.idl-code dfn-for="ExtendableMessageEvent" dfn-type="attribute"
export=""} attribute *must* return the value it was initialized to. When
the object is created, this attribute *must* be initialized to the empty
string. It represents the
[origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-origin){#ref-for-concept-settings-object-origin⑥
link-type="dfn"} of the [service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client③②
link-type="dfn"} that sent the message.
:::

::: section
#### [4.7.3. ]{.secno}[[`event.lastEventId`{.idl}](#dom-extendablemessageevent-lasteventid){#ref-for-dom-extendablemessageevent-lasteventid① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#extendablemessage-event-lasteventid){.self-link} {#extendablemessage-event-lasteventid .heading .settled level="4.7.3"}

The [`lastEventId`]{#dom-extendablemessageevent-lasteventid .dfn
.dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="ExtendableMessageEvent"
dfn-type="attribute" export=""} attribute *must* return the value it was
initialized to. When the object is created, this attribute *must* be
initialized to the empty string.
:::

::: section
#### [4.7.4. ]{.secno}[[`event.source`{.idl}](#dom-extendablemessageevent-source){#ref-for-dom-extendablemessageevent-source③ link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#extendablemessage-event-source){.self-link} {#extendablemessage-event-source .heading .settled level="4.7.4"}

The [`source`]{#dom-extendablemessageevent-source .dfn .dfn-paneled
.idl-code dfn-for="ExtendableMessageEvent" dfn-type="attribute"
export=""} attribute *must* return the value it was initialized to. When
the object is created, this attribute *must* be initialized to null. It
represents the [`Client`{.idl}](#client){#ref-for-client①⓪
link-type="idl"} object from which the message is sent.
:::

::: section
#### [4.7.5. ]{.secno}[[`event.ports`{.idl}](#dom-extendablemessageevent-ports){#ref-for-dom-extendablemessageevent-ports② link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#extendablemessage-event-ports){.self-link} {#extendablemessage-event-ports .heading .settled level="4.7.5"}

The [`ports`]{#dom-extendablemessageevent-ports .dfn .dfn-paneled
.idl-code dfn-for="ExtendableMessageEvent" dfn-type="attribute"
export=""} attribute *must* return the value it was initialized to. When
the object is created, this attribute *must* be initialized to the empty
array. It represents the
[`MessagePort`{.idl}](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/web-messaging.html#messageport){#ref-for-messageport⑥
link-type="idl"} array being sent.
:::
::::::::

::: section
### [4.8. ]{.secno}[Events]{.content}[](#execution-context-events){.self-link} {#execution-context-events .heading .settled level="4.8"}

The following events, called [service worker
events](#dfn-service-worker-events){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-events
link-type="dfn"}, are dispatched on
[`ServiceWorkerGlobalScope`{.idl}](#serviceworkerglobalscope){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope①②
link-type="idl"} object:

Event name

Interface

Category

Dispatched when...

[`install`]{#service-worker-global-scope-install-event .dfn .dfn-paneled
.idl-code dfn-for="ServiceWorkerGlobalScope" dfn-type="event" export=""}

[`InstallEvent`{.idl}](#installevent){#ref-for-installevent①
link-type="idl"}

[Lifecycle](#dfn-lifecycle-events){#ref-for-dfn-lifecycle-events①
link-type="dfn"}

The [service
worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker②①
link-type="dfn"}'s [containing service worker
registration](#dfn-containing-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-containing-service-worker-registration①③
link-type="dfn"}'s [installing
worker](#dfn-installing-worker){#ref-for-dfn-installing-worker⑦
link-type="dfn"} changes. (See step 11.2 of the
[Install](#install){#ref-for-install① link-type="dfn"} algorithm.)

[`activate`]{#service-worker-global-scope-activate-event .dfn
.dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="ServiceWorkerGlobalScope"
dfn-type="event" export=""}

[`ExtendableEvent`{.idl}](#extendableevent){#ref-for-extendableevent①④
link-type="idl"}

[Lifecycle](#dfn-lifecycle-events){#ref-for-dfn-lifecycle-events②
link-type="dfn"}

The [service
worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker②②
link-type="dfn"}'s [containing service worker
registration](#dfn-containing-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-containing-service-worker-registration①④
link-type="dfn"}'s [active
worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker①⑧
link-type="dfn"} changes. (See step 12.2 of the
[Activate](#activate){#ref-for-activate② link-type="dfn"} algorithm.)

[`fetch`]{#service-worker-global-scope-fetch-event .dfn .dfn-paneled
.idl-code dfn-for="ServiceWorkerGlobalScope" dfn-type="event" export=""}

[`FetchEvent`{.idl}](#fetchevent){#ref-for-fetchevent③ link-type="idl"}

[Functional](#dfn-functional-events){#ref-for-dfn-functional-events③
link-type="dfn"}

The [http
fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-http-fetch){#ref-for-concept-http-fetch②
link-type="dfn"} invokes [Handle
Fetch](#handle-fetch){#ref-for-handle-fetch④ link-type="dfn"} with
`request`{.variable}. As a result of performing [Handle
Fetch](#handle-fetch){#ref-for-handle-fetch⑤ link-type="dfn"}, the
[service
worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker②③
link-type="dfn"} returns a
[response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response){#ref-for-concept-response③
link-type="dfn"} to the [http
fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-http-fetch){#ref-for-concept-http-fetch③
link-type="dfn"}. The
[response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response){#ref-for-concept-response④
link-type="dfn"}, represented by a
[`Response`{.idl}](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response){#ref-for-response④
link-type="idl"} object, can be retrieved from a
[`Cache`{.idl}](#cache){#ref-for-cache link-type="idl"} object or
directly from network using
[`self.fetch(input, init)`{.idl}](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-global-fetch){#ref-for-dom-global-fetch
link-type="idl"} method. (A custom
[`Response`{.idl}](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response){#ref-for-response⑤
link-type="idl"} object can be another option.)

[push](https://w3c.github.io/push-api/#pushevent-interface){#c22e82f90}

[`PushEvent`](https://w3c.github.io/push-api/#dom-pushevent)

[Functional](#dfn-functional-events){#ref-for-dfn-functional-events④
link-type="dfn"}

(See [Firing a push
event](https://w3c.github.io/push-api/#receiving-a-push-message).)

[notificationclick](https://notifications.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-serviceworkerglobalscope-onnotificationclick)

[`NotificationEvent`](https://notifications.spec.whatwg.org/#notificationevent)

[Functional](#dfn-functional-events){#ref-for-dfn-functional-events⑤
link-type="dfn"}

(See [Activating a
notification.](https://notifications.spec.whatwg.org/#activating-a-notification))

[notificationclose](https://notifications.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-serviceworkerglobalscope-onnotificationclose)

[`NotificationEvent`](https://notifications.spec.whatwg.org/#notificationevent)

[Functional](#dfn-functional-events){#ref-for-dfn-functional-events⑥
link-type="dfn"}

(See [Closing a
notification.](https://notifications.spec.whatwg.org/#closing-a-notification))

[sync](https://wicg.github.io/background-sync/spec/#syncevent)

[`SyncEvent`](https://wicg.github.io/background-sync/spec/#syncevent)

[Functional](#dfn-functional-events){#ref-for-dfn-functional-events⑦
link-type="dfn"}

(See [Firing a sync
event](https://wicg.github.io/background-sync/spec/#fire-a-sync-event).)

[canmakepayment](https://w3c.github.io/payment-handler/#the-canmakepaymentevent)

[CanMakePaymentEvent](https://w3c.github.io/payment-handler/#dom-canmakepaymentevent)

[Functional](#dfn-functional-events){#ref-for-dfn-functional-events⑧
link-type="dfn"}

(See [Handling a
CanMakePaymentEvent](https://w3c.github.io/payment-handler/#dfn-handling-a-canmakepaymentevent).)

[paymentrequest](https://w3c.github.io/payment-handler/#the-paymentrequestevent)

link-type="dfn"}

PaymentRequestEvent](https://w3c.github.io/payment-handler/#dfn-handling-a-paymentrequestevent).)

[`message`]{#eventdef-serviceworkerglobalscope-message .dfn .dfn-paneled
.idl-code dfn-for="ServiceWorkerGlobalScope" dfn-type="event" export=""}

[`ExtendableMessageEvent`{.idl}](#extendablemessageevent){#ref-for-extendablemessageevent④
link-type="idl"}

Legacy

When it receives a message.

[`messageerror`]{#eventdef-serviceworkerglobalscope-messageerror .dfn
.dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="ServiceWorkerGlobalScope"
dfn-type="event" export=""}

:::
:::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::

::::::::::::::::::::: section
## [5. ]{.secno}[Caches]{.content}[](#cache-objects){.self-link} {#cache-objects .heading .settled level="5"}

To allow authors to fully manage their content caches for offline use,
the
[`Window`{.idl}](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#window){#ref-for-window⑨
link-type="idl"} and the
[`WorkerGlobalScope`{.idl}](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/workers.html#workerglobalscope){#ref-for-workerglobalscope①
link-type="idl"} provide the asynchronous caching methods that open and
manipulate [`Cache`{.idl}](#cache){#ref-for-cache① link-type="idl"}
objects. An
[origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-origin){#ref-for-concept-settings-object-origin⑦
link-type="dfn"} can have multiple, named
[`Cache`{.idl}](#cache){#ref-for-cache② link-type="idl"} objects, whose
contents are entirely under the control of scripts. Caches are not
shared across
[origins](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#concept-origin){#ref-for-concept-origin①⓪
link-type="dfn"}, and they are completely isolated from the browser's
HTTP cache.

::: section
### [5.1. ]{.secno}[Constructs]{.content}[](#cache-constructs){.self-link} {#cache-constructs .heading .settled level="5.1"}

A [request response list]{#dfn-request-response-list .dfn .dfn-paneled
dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""} is a
[list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list){#ref-for-list⑨
link-type="dfn"} of
[tuples](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#tuple){#ref-for-tuple
link-type="dfn"} consisting of a request (a
[request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request){#ref-for-concept-request③
link-type="dfn"}) and a response (a
[response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response){#ref-for-concept-response⑤
link-type="dfn"}).

The [relevant request response list]{#dfn-relevant-request-response-list
.dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""} is the instance that
[this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this⑤④
link-type="dfn"} represents.

A [name to cache map]{#dfn-name-to-cache-map .dfn .dfn-paneled
dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""} is an [ordered
map](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ordered-map){#ref-for-ordered-map④
link-type="dfn"} whose
[entry](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-entry){#ref-for-map-entry
link-type="dfn"} consists of a
[key](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-key){#ref-for-map-key
link-type="dfn"} (a string that represents the name of a [request
response
list](#dfn-request-response-list){#ref-for-dfn-request-response-list
link-type="dfn"}) and a
[value](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-value){#ref-for-map-value
link-type="dfn"} (a [request response
list](#dfn-request-response-list){#ref-for-dfn-request-response-list①
link-type="dfn"}).

The [relevant name to cache map]{#relevant-name-to-cache-map .dfn
.dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""} for a
[`CacheStorage`{.idl}](#cachestorage){#ref-for-cachestorage
link-type="idl"} object is the [name to cache
map](#dfn-name-to-cache-map){#ref-for-dfn-name-to-cache-map
link-type="dfn"} associated with the result of running [obtain a local
storage bottle
map](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#obtain-a-local-storage-bottle-map){#ref-for-obtain-a-local-storage-bottle-map
link-type="dfn"} with the object's [relevant settings
object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object){#ref-for-relevant-settings-object②⓪
link-type="dfn"} and \"`caches`\".
:::

::: section
### [5.2. ]{.secno}[Understanding Cache Lifetimes]{.content}[](#cache-lifetimes){.self-link} {#cache-lifetimes .heading .settled level="5.2"}

The [`Cache`{.idl}](#cache){#ref-for-cache③ link-type="idl"} instances
are not part of the browser's HTTP cache. The
[`Cache`{.idl}](#cache){#ref-for-cache④ link-type="idl"} objects are
exactly what authors have to manage themselves. The
[`Cache`{.idl}](#cache){#ref-for-cache⑤ link-type="idl"} objects do not
get updated unless authors explicitly request them to be. The
[`Cache`{.idl}](#cache){#ref-for-cache⑥ link-type="idl"} objects do not
expire unless authors delete the entries. The
[`Cache`{.idl}](#cache){#ref-for-cache⑦ link-type="idl"} objects do not
disappear just because the [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑦⑧
link-type="dfn"} script is updated. That is, caches are not updated
automatically. Updates must be manually managed. This implies that
authors should version their caches by name and make sure to use the
caches only from the version of the [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑦⑨
link-type="dfn"} that can safely operate on.
:::

:::: section
### [5.3. ]{.secno}[[`self.caches`{.idl}](#global-caches-attribute){#ref-for-global-caches-attribute link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#self-caches){.self-link} {#self-caches .heading .settled level="5.3"}

``` {.def .highlight .idl}
partial interface mixin WindowOrWorkerGlobalScope {
  [SecureContext, SameObject] readonly attribute CacheStorage caches;
};
```

::: section
#### [5.3.1. ]{.secno}[[`caches`{.idl}](#global-caches-attribute){#ref-for-global-caches-attribute② link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#global-caches){.self-link} {#global-caches .heading .settled level="5.3.1"}

[`caches`]{#global-caches-attribute .dfn .dfn-paneled .idl-code
dfn-for="WindowOrWorkerGlobalScope" dfn-type="attribute" export=""}
getter steps are to return
[this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this⑤⑤
link-type="dfn"}'s associated
[`CacheStorage`{.idl}](#cachestorage){#ref-for-cachestorage②
link-type="idl"} object.
:::
::::

:::::::::: section
### [5.4. ]{.secno}[[`Cache`{.idl}](#cache){#ref-for-cache⑧ link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#cache-interface){.self-link} {#cache-interface .heading .settled level="5.4"}

``` {.def .highlight .idl}
[SecureContext, Exposed=(Window,Worker)]
interface Cache {
  [NewObject] Promise<(Response or undefined)> match(RequestInfo request, optional CacheQueryOptions options = {});
  [NewObject] Promise<FrozenArray<Response>> matchAll(optional RequestInfo request, optional CacheQueryOptions options = {});
  [NewObject] Promise<undefined> add(RequestInfo request);
  [NewObject] Promise<undefined> addAll(sequence<RequestInfo> requests);
  [NewObject] Promise<undefined> put(RequestInfo request, Response response);
  [NewObject] Promise<boolean> delete(RequestInfo request, optional CacheQueryOptions options = {});
  [NewObject] Promise<FrozenArray<Request>> keys(optional RequestInfo request, optional CacheQueryOptions options = {});
};
```

``` {#cache-query-options-dictionary .def .highlight .idl}
dictionary CacheQueryOptions {
  boolean ignoreSearch = false;
  boolean ignoreMethod = false;
  boolean ignoreVary = false;
};
```

A [`Cache`{.idl}](#cache){#ref-for-cache⑨ link-type="idl"} object
represents a [request response
list](#dfn-request-response-list){#ref-for-dfn-request-response-list②
link-type="dfn"}. Multiple separate objects implementing the
[`Cache`{.idl}](#cache){#ref-for-cache①⓪ link-type="idl"} interface
across documents and workers can all be associated with the same
[request response
list](#dfn-request-response-list){#ref-for-dfn-request-response-list③
link-type="dfn"} simultaneously.

A [cache batch operation]{#dfn-cache-batch-operation .dfn .dfn-paneled
dfn-type="dfn" export=""} is a
[struct](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct){#ref-for-struct③
link-type="dfn"} that consists of:

- A [type]{#dfn-cache-batch-operation-type .dfn .dfn-paneled
  dfn-for="cache batch operation" dfn-type="dfn" export=""}
  (\"`delete`\" or \"`put`\").

- A [request]{#dfn-cache-batch-operation-request .dfn .dfn-paneled
  dfn-for="cache batch operation" dfn-type="dfn" export=""} (a
  [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request){#ref-for-concept-request④
  link-type="dfn"}).

- A [response]{#dfn-cache-batch-operation-response .dfn .dfn-paneled
  dfn-for="cache batch operation" dfn-type="dfn" export=""} (a
  [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response){#ref-for-concept-response⑥
  link-type="dfn"}).

- An [options]{#dfn-cache-batch-operation-options .dfn .dfn-paneled
  dfn-for="cache batch operation" dfn-type="dfn" export=""} (a
  [`CacheQueryOptions`{.idl}](#dictdef-cachequeryoptions){#ref-for-dictdef-cachequeryoptions④
  link-type="idl"}).

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="cache-match"}
#### [5.4.1. ]{.secno}[[`match(request, options)`{.idl}](#dom-cache-match){#ref-for-dom-cache-match① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#cache-match){.self-link} {#cache-match .heading .settled level="5.4.1"}

The
[`match(``request`{.variable}`, ``options`{.variable}`)`]{#dom-cache-match
.dfn .dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="Cache" dfn-type="method" export=""
lt="match(request, options)|match(request)"} method steps are:

1.  Let `promise`{.variable} be [a new
    promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-new-promise){#ref-for-a-new-promise⑧
    link-type="dfn"}.

2.  Run these substeps [in
    parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel){#ref-for-in-parallel①⑤
    link-type="dfn"}:

    1.  Let `p`{.variable} be the result of running the algorithm
        specified in
        [`matchAll(request, options)`{.idl}](#dom-cache-matchall){#ref-for-dom-cache-matchall①
        link-type="idl"} method with `request`{.variable} and
        `options`{.variable}.

    2.  Wait until `p`{.variable} settles.

    3.  If `p`{.variable} rejects with an exception, then:

        1.  Reject `promise`{.variable} with that exception.

    4.  Else if `p`{.variable} resolves with an array,
        `responses`{.variable}, then:

        1.  If `responses`{.variable} is an empty array, then:

            1.  Resolve `promise`{.variable} with undefined.

        2.  Else:

            1.  Resolve `promise`{.variable} with the first element of
                `responses`{.variable}.

3.  Return `promise`{.variable}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="cache-matchall"}
#### [5.4.2. ]{.secno}[[`matchAll(request, options)`{.idl}](#dom-cache-matchall){#ref-for-dom-cache-matchall② link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#cache-matchall){.self-link} {#cache-matchall .heading .settled level="5.4.2"}

The
[`matchAll(``request`{.variable}`, ``options`{.variable}`)`]{#dom-cache-matchall
.dfn .dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="Cache" dfn-type="method" export=""
lt="matchAll(request, options)|matchAll(request)|matchAll()"} method
steps are:

1.  Let `r`{.variable} be null.

2.  If the optional argument `request`{.variable} is not omitted, then:

    1.  If `request`{.variable} is a
        [`Request`{.idl}](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request){#ref-for-request③
        link-type="idl"} object, then:

        1.  Set `r`{.variable} to `request`{.variable}'s
            [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request){#ref-for-concept-request-request
            link-type="dfn"}.

        2.  If `r`{.variable}'s
            [method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-method){#ref-for-concept-request-method
            link-type="dfn"} is not \``GET`\` and
            `options`{.variable}.ignoreMethod is false, return [a
            promise resolved
            with](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-promise-resolved-with){#ref-for-a-promise-resolved-with①
            link-type="dfn"} an empty array.

    2.  Else if `request`{.variable} is a string, then:

        1.  Set `r`{.variable} to the associated
            [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request){#ref-for-concept-request-request①
            link-type="dfn"} of the result of invoking the initial value
            of
            [`Request`{.idl}](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request){#ref-for-request④
            link-type="idl"} as constructor with `request`{.variable} as
            its argument. If this
            [throws](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw){#ref-for-dfn-throw⑥
            link-type="dfn"} an exception, return [a promise rejected
            with](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-promise-rejected-with){#ref-for-a-promise-rejected-with④
            link-type="dfn"} that exception.

3.  Let `realm`{.variable} be
    [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this⑤⑥
    link-type="dfn"}'s [relevant
    realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-realm){#ref-for-concept-relevant-realm④
    link-type="dfn"}.

4.  Let `promise`{.variable} be [a new
    promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-new-promise){#ref-for-a-new-promise⑨
    link-type="dfn"}.

5.  Run these substeps [in
    parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel){#ref-for-in-parallel①⑥
    link-type="dfn"}:

    1.  Let `responses`{.variable} be an empty
        [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list){#ref-for-list①⓪
        link-type="dfn"}.

    2.  If the optional argument `request`{.variable} is omitted, then:

        1.  [For
            each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate){#ref-for-list-iterate④
            link-type="dfn"} `requestResponse`{.variable} of the
            [relevant request response
            list](#dfn-relevant-request-response-list){#ref-for-dfn-relevant-request-response-list
            link-type="dfn"}:

            1.  Add a copy of `requestResponse`{.variable}'s response to
                `responses`{.variable}.

    3.  Else:

        1.  Let `requestResponses`{.variable} be the result of running
            [Query Cache](#query-cache){#ref-for-query-cache
            link-type="dfn"} with `r`{.variable} and
            `options`{.variable}.

        2.  [For
            each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate){#ref-for-list-iterate⑤
            link-type="dfn"} `requestResponse`{.variable} of
            `requestResponses`{.variable}:

            1.  Add a copy of `requestResponse`{.variable}'s response to
                `responses`{.variable}.

    4.  [For
        each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate){#ref-for-list-iterate⑥
        link-type="dfn"} `response`{.variable} of
        `responses`{.variable}:

        1.  If `response`{.variable}'s
            [type](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-type){#ref-for-concept-response-type
            link-type="dfn"} is \"`opaque`\" and [cross-origin resource
            policy
            check](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#cross-origin-resource-policy-check){#ref-for-cross-origin-resource-policy-check
            link-type="dfn"} with `promise`{.variable}'s [relevant
            settings
            object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object){#ref-for-relevant-settings-object②①
            link-type="dfn"}'s
            [origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-origin){#ref-for-concept-settings-object-origin⑧
            link-type="dfn"}, `promise`{.variable}'s [relevant settings
            object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object){#ref-for-relevant-settings-object②②
            link-type="dfn"}, \"\", and `response`{.variable}'s
            [internal
            response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-internal-response){#ref-for-concept-internal-response
            link-type="dfn"} returns **blocked**, then reject
            `promise`{.variable} with a `TypeError` and abort these
            steps.

    5.  [Queue a
        task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task){#ref-for-queue-a-task①⑦
        link-type="dfn"}, on `promise`{.variable}'s [relevant settings
        object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object){#ref-for-relevant-settings-object②③
        link-type="dfn"}'s [responsible event
        loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop){#ref-for-responsible-event-loop⑥
        link-type="dfn"} using the [DOM manipulation task
        source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source){#ref-for-dom-manipulation-task-source①①
        link-type="dfn"}, to perform the following steps:

        1.  Let `responseList`{.variable} be a
            [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list){#ref-for-list①①
            link-type="dfn"}.

        2.  [For
            each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate){#ref-for-list-iterate⑦
            link-type="dfn"} `response`{.variable} of
            `responses`{.variable}:

            1.  Add a new
                [`Response`{.idl}](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response){#ref-for-response⑨
                link-type="idl"} object associated with
                `response`{.variable} and a new
                [`Headers`{.idl}](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#headers){#ref-for-headers
                link-type="idl"} object whose
                [guard](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-guard){#ref-for-concept-headers-guard
                link-type="dfn"} is \"`immutable`\" to
                `responseList`{.variable}.

        3.  Resolve `promise`{.variable} with a [frozen
            array](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-frozen-array-type){#ref-for-dfn-frozen-array-type②
            link-type="dfn"}
            [created](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-create-frozen-array){#ref-for-dfn-create-frozen-array②
            link-type="dfn"} from `responseList`{.variable}, in
            `realm`{.variable}.

6.  Return `promise`{.variable}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="cache-add"}
#### [5.4.3. ]{.secno}[[`add(request)`{.idl}](#dom-cache-add){#ref-for-dom-cache-add① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#cache-add){.self-link} {#cache-add .heading .settled level="5.4.3"}

The [`add(``request`{.variable}`)`]{#dom-cache-add .dfn .dfn-paneled
.idl-code dfn-for="Cache" dfn-type="method" export=""} method steps are:

1.  Let `requests`{.variable} be an array containing only
    `request`{.variable}.

2.  Let `responseArrayPromise`{.variable} be the result of running the
    algorithm specified in
    [`addAll(requests)`{.idl}](#dom-cache-addall){#ref-for-dom-cache-addall①
    link-type="idl"} passing `requests`{.variable} as the argument.

3.  Return the result of
    [reacting](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-perform-steps-once-promise-is-settled){#ref-for-dfn-perform-steps-once-promise-is-settled
    link-type="dfn"} to `responseArrayPromise`{.variable} with a
    fulfillment handler that returns undefined.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="cache-addAll"}
#### [5.4.4. ]{.secno}[[`addAll(requests)`{.idl}](#dom-cache-addall){#ref-for-dom-cache-addall② link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#cache-addAll){.self-link} {#cache-addAll .heading .settled level="5.4.4"}

The [`addAll(``requests`{.variable}`)`]{#dom-cache-addall .dfn
.dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="Cache" dfn-type="method" export=""}
method steps are:

1.  Let `responsePromises`{.variable} be an empty
    [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list){#ref-for-list①②
    link-type="dfn"}.

2.  Let `requestList`{.variable} be an empty
    [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list){#ref-for-list①③
    link-type="dfn"}.

3.  For each `request`{.variable} whose type is
    [`Request`{.idl}](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request){#ref-for-request⑤
    link-type="idl"} in `requests`{.variable}:

    1.  Let `r`{.variable} be `request`{.variable}'s
        [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request){#ref-for-concept-request-request②
        link-type="dfn"}.

    2.  If `r`{.variable}'s
        [url](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-url){#ref-for-concept-request-url①
        link-type="dfn"}'s
        [scheme](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-scheme){#ref-for-concept-url-scheme
        link-type="dfn"} is not one of \"`http`\" and \"`https`\", or
        `r`{.variable}'s
        [method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-method){#ref-for-concept-request-method①
        link-type="dfn"} is not \``GET`\`, return [a promise rejected
        with](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-promise-rejected-with){#ref-for-a-promise-rejected-with⑤
        link-type="dfn"} a `TypeError`.

4.  Let `fetchControllers`{.variable} be a
    [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list){#ref-for-list①④
    link-type="dfn"} of [fetch
    controllers](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-controller){#ref-for-fetch-controller
    link-type="dfn"}.

5.  For each `request`{.variable} in `requests`{.variable}:

    1.  Let `r`{.variable} be the associated
        [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request){#ref-for-concept-request-request③
        link-type="dfn"} of the result of invoking the initial value of
        [`Request`{.idl}](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request){#ref-for-request⑥
        link-type="idl"} as constructor with `request`{.variable} as its
        argument. If this
        [throws](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw){#ref-for-dfn-throw⑦
        link-type="dfn"} an exception, return [a promise rejected
        with](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-promise-rejected-with){#ref-for-a-promise-rejected-with⑥
        link-type="dfn"} that exception.

    2.  If `r`{.variable}'s
        [url](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-url){#ref-for-concept-request-url②
        link-type="dfn"}'s
        [scheme](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-scheme){#ref-for-concept-url-scheme①
        link-type="dfn"} is not one of \"`http`\" and \"`https`\", then:

        1.  [For
            each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate){#ref-for-list-iterate⑧
            link-type="dfn"} `fetchController`{.variable} of
            `fetchControllers`{.variable},
            [abort](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-controller-abort){#ref-for-fetch-controller-abort
            link-type="dfn"} `fetchController`{.variable}.

        2.  Return [a promise rejected
            with](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-promise-rejected-with){#ref-for-a-promise-rejected-with⑦
            link-type="dfn"} a `TypeError`.

    3.  If `r`{.variable}'s
        [client](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-client){#ref-for-concept-request-client
        link-type="dfn"}'s [global
        object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-global){#ref-for-concept-settings-object-global⑦
        link-type="dfn"} is a
        [`ServiceWorkerGlobalScope`{.idl}](#serviceworkerglobalscope){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope①③
        link-type="idl"} object, set `request`{.variable}'s
        [service-workers
        mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-service-workers-mode){#ref-for-request-service-workers-mode
        link-type="dfn"} to \"`none`\".

    4.  Set `r`{.variable}'s
        [initiator](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-initiator){#ref-for-concept-request-initiator
        link-type="dfn"} to \"`fetch`\" and
        [destination](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-destination){#ref-for-concept-request-destination
        link-type="dfn"} to \"`subresource`\".

    5.  Add `r`{.variable} to `requestList`{.variable}.

    6.  Let `responsePromise`{.variable} be [a new
        promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-new-promise){#ref-for-a-new-promise①⓪
        link-type="dfn"}.

    7.  Run the following substeps [in
        parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel){#ref-for-in-parallel①⑦
        link-type="dfn"}:

        - [Append](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-append){#ref-for-list-append④
          link-type="dfn"} the result of
          [fetching](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch){#ref-for-concept-fetch⑥
          link-type="dfn"} `r`{.variable}.

        - To
          [processResponse](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#process-response){#ref-for-process-response
          link-type="dfn"} for `response`{.variable}, run these
          substeps:

          1.  If `response`{.variable}'s
              [type](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-type){#ref-for-concept-response-type①
              link-type="dfn"} is \"`error`\", or
              `response`{.variable}'s
              [status](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-status){#ref-for-concept-response-status
              link-type="dfn"} is not an [ok
              status](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#ok-status){#ref-for-ok-status
              link-type="dfn"} or is `206`, reject
              `responsePromise`{.variable} with a `TypeError`.

          2.  Else if `response`{.variable}'s [header
              list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-header-list){#ref-for-concept-response-header-list
              link-type="dfn"} contains a
              [header](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header){#ref-for-concept-header
              link-type="dfn"}
              [named](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-name){#ref-for-concept-header-name
              link-type="dfn"} \``Vary`\`, then:

              1.  Let `fieldValues`{.variable} be the
                  [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list){#ref-for-list①⑤
                  link-type="dfn"} containing the elements corresponding
                  to the
                  [field-values](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc7230#section-3.2){#ref-for-section-3.2
                  link-type="dfn"} of the
                  [Vary](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc7231#section-7.1.4){#ref-for-section-7.1.4
                  link-type="dfn"} header.

              2.  [For
                  each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate){#ref-for-list-iterate⑨
                  link-type="dfn"} `fieldValue`{.variable} of
                  `fieldValues`{.variable}:

                  1.  If `fieldValue`{.variable} matches \"`*`\", then:

                      1.  Reject `responsePromise`{.variable} with a
                          `TypeError`.

                      2.  [For
                          each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate){#ref-for-list-iterate①⓪
                          link-type="dfn"} `fetchController`{.variable}
                          of `fetchControllers`{.variable},
                          [abort](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-controller-abort){#ref-for-fetch-controller-abort①
                          link-type="dfn"} `fetchController`{.variable}.

                      3.  Abort these steps.

        - To
          [processResponseEndOfBody](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-processresponseendofbody){#ref-for-fetch-processresponseendofbody
          link-type="dfn"} for `response`{.variable}, run these
          substeps:

          1.  If `response`{.variable}'s [aborted
              flag](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-aborted){#ref-for-concept-response-aborted
              link-type="dfn"} is set, reject
              `responsePromise`{.variable} with an
              \"[`AbortError`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#aborterror){#ref-for-aborterror
              link-type="idl"}\"
              [`DOMException`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException){#ref-for-idl-DOMException①③
              link-type="idl"} and abort these steps.

          2.  Resolve `responsePromise`{.variable} with
              `response`{.variable}.

          [Note:]{.marker} The cache commit is allowed when the
          response's body is fully received.

    8.  Add `responsePromise`{.variable} to
        `responsePromises`{.variable}.

6.  Let `p`{.variable} be the result of [getting a promise to wait for
    all](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#waiting-for-all-promise){#ref-for-waiting-for-all-promise
    link-type="dfn"} of `responsePromises`{.variable}.

7.  Return the result of
    [reacting](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-perform-steps-once-promise-is-settled){#ref-for-dfn-perform-steps-once-promise-is-settled①
    link-type="dfn"} to `p`{.variable} with a fulfillment handler that,
    when called with argument `responses`{.variable}, performs the
    following substeps:

    1.  Let `operations`{.variable} be an empty
        [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list){#ref-for-list①⑥
        link-type="dfn"}.

    2.  Let `index`{.variable} be zero.

    3.  For each `response`{.variable} in `responses`{.variable}:

        1.  Let `operation`{.variable} be a [cache batch
            operation](#dfn-cache-batch-operation){#ref-for-dfn-cache-batch-operation
            link-type="dfn"}.

        2.  Set `operation`{.variable}'s
            [type](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-type){#ref-for-dfn-cache-batch-operation-type
            link-type="dfn"} to \"`put`\".

        3.  Set `operation`{.variable}'s
            [request](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-request){#ref-for-dfn-cache-batch-operation-request
            link-type="dfn"} to
            `requestList`{.variable}\[`index`{.variable}\].

        4.  Set `operation`{.variable}'s
            [response](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-response){#ref-for-dfn-cache-batch-operation-response
            link-type="dfn"} to `response`{.variable}.

        5.  [Append](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-append){#ref-for-list-append⑤
            link-type="dfn"} `operation`{.variable} to
            `operations`{.variable}.

        6.  Increment `index`{.variable} by one.

    4.  Let `realm`{.variable} be
        [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this⑤⑦
        link-type="dfn"}'s [relevant
        realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-realm){#ref-for-concept-relevant-realm⑤
        link-type="dfn"}.

    5.  Let `cacheJobPromise`{.variable} be [a new
        promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-new-promise){#ref-for-a-new-promise①①
        link-type="dfn"}.

    6.  Run the following substeps [in
        parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel){#ref-for-in-parallel①⑧
        link-type="dfn"}:

        1.  Let `errorData`{.variable} be null.

        2.  Invoke [Batch Cache
            Operations](#batch-cache-operations){#ref-for-batch-cache-operations
            link-type="dfn"} with `operations`{.variable}. If this
            [throws](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw){#ref-for-dfn-throw⑧
            link-type="dfn"} an exception, set `errorData`{.variable} to
            the exception.

        3.  [Queue a
            task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task){#ref-for-queue-a-task①⑧
            link-type="dfn"}, on `cacheJobPromise`{.variable}'s
            [relevant settings
            object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object){#ref-for-relevant-settings-object②④
            link-type="dfn"}'s [responsible event
            loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop){#ref-for-responsible-event-loop⑦
            link-type="dfn"} using the [DOM manipulation task
            source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source){#ref-for-dom-manipulation-task-source①②
            link-type="dfn"}, to perform the following substeps:

            1.  If `errorData`{.variable} is null, resolve
                `cacheJobPromise`{.variable} with undefined.

            2.  Else, reject `cacheJobPromise`{.variable} with a
                [new](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-create-exception){#ref-for-dfn-create-exception
                link-type="dfn"}
                [exception](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-exception){#ref-for-dfn-exception
                link-type="dfn"} with `errorData`{.variable}, in
                `realm`{.variable}.

    7.  Return `cacheJobPromise`{.variable}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="cache-put"}
#### [5.4.5. ]{.secno}[[`put(request, response)`{.idl}](#dom-cache-put){#ref-for-dom-cache-put① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#cache-put){.self-link} {#cache-put .heading .settled level="5.4.5"}

The
