[`put(``request`{.variable}`, ``response`{.variable}`)`]{#dom-cache-put
.dfn .dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="Cache" dfn-type="method" export=""}
method steps are:

1.  Let `innerRequest`{.variable} be null.

2.  If `request`{.variable} is a
    [`Request`{.idl}](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request){#ref-for-request⑦
    link-type="idl"} object, then set `innerRequest`{.variable} to
    `request`{.variable}'s
    [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request){#ref-for-concept-request-request④
    link-type="dfn"}.

3.  Else:

    1.  Let `requestObj`{.variable} be the result of invoking
        [`Request`{.idl}](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request){#ref-for-request⑧
        link-type="idl"}'s constructor with `request`{.variable} as its
        argument. If this
        [throws](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw){#ref-for-dfn-throw⑨
        link-type="dfn"} an `exception`{.variable}, return [a promise
        rejected
        with](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-promise-rejected-with){#ref-for-a-promise-rejected-with⑧
        link-type="dfn"} `exception`{.variable}.

    2.  Set `innerRequest`{.variable} to `requestObj`{.variable}'s
        [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request){#ref-for-concept-request-request⑤
        link-type="dfn"}.

4.  If `innerRequest`{.variable}'s
    [url](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-url){#ref-for-concept-request-url③
    link-type="dfn"}'s
    [scheme](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-scheme){#ref-for-concept-url-scheme②
    link-type="dfn"} is not one of \"`http`\" and \"`https`\", or
    `innerRequest`{.variable}'s
    [method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-method){#ref-for-concept-request-method②
    link-type="dfn"} is not \``GET`\`, return [a promise rejected
    with](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-promise-rejected-with){#ref-for-a-promise-rejected-with⑨
    link-type="dfn"} a `TypeError`.

5.  Let `innerResponse`{.variable} be `response`{.variable}'s
    [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-response){#ref-for-concept-response-response①
    link-type="dfn"}.

6.  If `innerResponse`{.variable}'s
    [status](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-status){#ref-for-concept-response-status①
    link-type="dfn"} is `206`, return [a promise rejected
    with](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-promise-rejected-with){#ref-for-a-promise-rejected-with①⓪
    link-type="dfn"} a `TypeError`.

7.  If `innerResponse`{.variable}'s [header
    list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-header-list){#ref-for-concept-response-header-list①
    link-type="dfn"} contains a
    [header](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header){#ref-for-concept-header①
    link-type="dfn"}
    [named](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-name){#ref-for-concept-header-name①
    link-type="dfn"} \``Vary`\`, then:

    1.  Let `fieldValues`{.variable} be the
        [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list){#ref-for-list①⑦
        link-type="dfn"} containing the
        [items](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-item){#ref-for-list-item⑤
        link-type="dfn"} corresponding to the
        [Vary](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc7231#section-7.1.4){#ref-for-section-7.1.4①
        link-type="dfn"} header's
        [field-values](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc7230#section-3.2){#ref-for-section-3.2①
        link-type="dfn"}.

    2.  [For
        each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate){#ref-for-list-iterate①①
        link-type="dfn"} `fieldValue`{.variable} in
        `fieldValues`{.variable}:

        1.  If `fieldValue`{.variable} matches \"`*`\", return [a
            promise rejected
            with](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-promise-rejected-with){#ref-for-a-promise-rejected-with①①
            link-type="dfn"} a `TypeError`.

8.  If `innerResponse`{.variable}'s
    [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body){#ref-for-concept-response-body⑤
    link-type="dfn"} is
    [disturbed](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#is-readable-stream-disturbed){#ref-for-is-readable-stream-disturbed
    link-type="dfn"} or
    [locked](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-locked){#ref-for-readablestream-locked
    link-type="dfn"}, return [a promise rejected
    with](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-promise-rejected-with){#ref-for-a-promise-rejected-with①②
    link-type="dfn"} a `TypeError`.

9.  Let `clonedResponse`{.variable} be a
    [clone](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-clone){#ref-for-concept-response-clone
    link-type="dfn"} of `innerResponse`{.variable}.

10. Let `bodyReadPromise`{.variable} be [a promise resolved
    with](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-promise-resolved-with){#ref-for-a-promise-resolved-with②
    link-type="dfn"} undefined.

11. If `innerResponse`{.variable}'s
    [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body){#ref-for-concept-response-body⑥
    link-type="dfn"} is non-null, run these substeps:

    1.  Let `stream`{.variable} be `innerResponse`{.variable}'s
        [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body){#ref-for-concept-response-body⑦
        link-type="dfn"}'s
        [stream](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-stream){#ref-for-concept-body-stream③
        link-type="dfn"}.

    2.  Let `reader`{.variable} be the result of [getting a
        reader](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-get-a-reader){#ref-for-readablestream-get-a-reader①
        link-type="dfn"} for `stream`{.variable}.

    3.  Set `bodyReadPromise`{.variable} to the result of [reading all
        bytes](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestreamdefaultreader-read-all-bytes){#ref-for-readablestreamdefaultreader-read-all-bytes
        link-type="dfn"} from `reader`{.variable}.

    [Note:]{.marker} This ensures that `innerResponse`{.variable}'s
    [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body){#ref-for-concept-response-body⑧
    link-type="dfn"} is
    [locked](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-locked){#ref-for-readablestream-locked①
    link-type="dfn"}, and we have a full buffered copy of the body in
    `clonedResponse`{.variable}. An implementation could optimize by
    streaming directly to disk rather than memory.

12. Let `operations`{.variable} be an empty
    [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list){#ref-for-list①⑧
    link-type="dfn"}.

13. Let `operation`{.variable} be a [cache batch
    operation](#dfn-cache-batch-operation){#ref-for-dfn-cache-batch-operation①
    link-type="dfn"}.

14. Set `operation`{.variable}'s
    [type](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-type){#ref-for-dfn-cache-batch-operation-type①
    link-type="dfn"} to \"`put`\".

15. Set `operation`{.variable}'s
    [request](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-request){#ref-for-dfn-cache-batch-operation-request①
    link-type="dfn"} to `innerRequest`{.variable}.

16. Set `operation`{.variable}'s
    [response](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-response){#ref-for-dfn-cache-batch-operation-response①
    link-type="dfn"} to `clonedResponse`{.variable}.

17. [Append](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-append){#ref-for-list-append⑥
    link-type="dfn"} `operation`{.variable} to `operations`{.variable}.

18. Let `realm`{.variable} be
    [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this⑤⑧
    link-type="dfn"}'s [relevant
    realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-realm){#ref-for-concept-relevant-realm⑥
    link-type="dfn"}.

19. Return the result of the
    [fulfillment](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#upon-fulfillment){#ref-for-upon-fulfillment③
    link-type="dfn"} of `bodyReadPromise`{.variable}:

    1.  Let `cacheJobPromise`{.variable} be [a new
        promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-new-promise){#ref-for-a-new-promise①②
        link-type="dfn"}.

    2.  Return `cacheJobPromise`{.variable} and run these steps [in
        parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel){#ref-for-in-parallel①⑨
        link-type="dfn"}:

        1.  Let `errorData`{.variable} be null.

        2.  Invoke [Batch Cache
            Operations](#batch-cache-operations){#ref-for-batch-cache-operations①
            link-type="dfn"} with `operations`{.variable}. If this
            [throws](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw){#ref-for-dfn-throw①⓪
            link-type="dfn"} an exception, set `errorData`{.variable} to
            the exception.

        3.  [Queue a
            task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task){#ref-for-queue-a-task①⑨
            link-type="dfn"}, on `cacheJobPromise`{.variable}'s
            [relevant settings
            object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object){#ref-for-relevant-settings-object②⑤
            link-type="dfn"}'s [responsible event
            loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop){#ref-for-responsible-event-loop⑧
            link-type="dfn"} using the [DOM manipulation task
            source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source){#ref-for-dom-manipulation-task-source①③
            link-type="dfn"}, to perform the following substeps:

            1.  If `errorData`{.variable} is null, resolve
                `cacheJobPromise`{.variable} with undefined.

            2.  Else, reject `cacheJobPromise`{.variable} with a
                [new](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-create-exception){#ref-for-dfn-create-exception①
                link-type="dfn"}
                [exception](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-exception){#ref-for-dfn-exception①
                link-type="dfn"} with `errorData`{.variable}, in
                `realm`{.variable}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="cache-delete"}
#### [5.4.6. ]{.secno}[[`delete(request, options)`{.idl}](#dom-cache-delete){#ref-for-dom-cache-delete① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#cache-delete){.self-link} {#cache-delete .heading .settled level="5.4.6"}

The
[`delete(``request`{.variable}`, ``options`{.variable}`)`]{#dom-cache-delete
.dfn .dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="Cache" dfn-type="method" export=""
lt="delete(request, options)|delete(request)"} method steps are:

1.  Let `r`{.variable} be null.

2.  If `request`{.variable} is a
    [`Request`{.idl}](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request){#ref-for-request⑨
    link-type="idl"} object, then:

    1.  Set `r`{.variable} to `request`{.variable}'s
        [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request){#ref-for-concept-request-request⑥
        link-type="dfn"}.

    2.  If `r`{.variable}'s
        [method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-method){#ref-for-concept-request-method③
        link-type="dfn"} is not \``GET`\` and
        `options`{.variable}.ignoreMethod is false, return [a promise
        resolved
        with](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-promise-resolved-with){#ref-for-a-promise-resolved-with③
        link-type="dfn"} false.

3.  Else if `request`{.variable} is a string, then:

    1.  Set `r`{.variable} to the associated
        [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request){#ref-for-concept-request-request⑦
        link-type="dfn"} of the result of invoking the initial value of
        [`Request`{.idl}](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request){#ref-for-request①⓪
        link-type="idl"} as constructor with `request`{.variable} as its
        argument. If this
        [throws](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw){#ref-for-dfn-throw①①
        link-type="dfn"} an exception, return [a promise rejected
        with](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-promise-rejected-with){#ref-for-a-promise-rejected-with①③
        link-type="dfn"} that exception.

4.  Let `operations`{.variable} be an empty
    [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list){#ref-for-list①⑨
    link-type="dfn"}.

5.  Let `operation`{.variable} be a [cache batch
    operation](#dfn-cache-batch-operation){#ref-for-dfn-cache-batch-operation②
    link-type="dfn"}.

6.  Set `operation`{.variable}'s
    [type](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-type){#ref-for-dfn-cache-batch-operation-type②
    link-type="dfn"} to \"`delete`\".

7.  Set `operation`{.variable}'s
    [request](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-request){#ref-for-dfn-cache-batch-operation-request②
    link-type="dfn"} to `r`{.variable}.

8.  Set `operation`{.variable}'s
    [options](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-options){#ref-for-dfn-cache-batch-operation-options
    link-type="dfn"} to `options`{.variable}.

9.  [Append](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-append){#ref-for-list-append⑦
    link-type="dfn"} `operation`{.variable} to `operations`{.variable}.

10. Let `realm`{.variable} be
    [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this⑤⑨
    link-type="dfn"}'s [relevant
    realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-realm){#ref-for-concept-relevant-realm⑦
    link-type="dfn"}.

11. Let `cacheJobPromise`{.variable} be [a new
    promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-new-promise){#ref-for-a-new-promise①③
    link-type="dfn"}.

12. Run the following substeps [in
    parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel){#ref-for-in-parallel②⓪
    link-type="dfn"}:

    1.  Let `errorData`{.variable} be null.

    2.  Let `requestResponses`{.variable} be the result of running
        [Batch Cache
        Operations](#batch-cache-operations){#ref-for-batch-cache-operations②
        link-type="dfn"} with `operations`{.variable}. If this
        [throws](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw){#ref-for-dfn-throw①②
        link-type="dfn"} an exception, set `errorData`{.variable} to the
        exception.

    3.  [Queue a
        task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task){#ref-for-queue-a-task②⓪
        link-type="dfn"}, on `cacheJobPromise`{.variable}'s [relevant
        settings
        object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object){#ref-for-relevant-settings-object②⑥
        link-type="dfn"}'s [responsible event
        loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop){#ref-for-responsible-event-loop⑨
        link-type="dfn"} using the [DOM manipulation task
        source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source){#ref-for-dom-manipulation-task-source①④
        link-type="dfn"}, to perform the following substeps:

        1.  If `errorData`{.variable} is null, then:

            1.  If `requestResponses`{.variable} [is not
                empty](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-is-empty){#ref-for-list-is-empty
                link-type="dfn"}, resolve `cacheJobPromise`{.variable}
                with true.

            2.  Else, resolve `cacheJobPromise`{.variable} with false.

        2.  Else, reject `cacheJobPromise`{.variable} with a
            [new](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-create-exception){#ref-for-dfn-create-exception②
            link-type="dfn"}
            [exception](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-exception){#ref-for-dfn-exception②
            link-type="dfn"} with `errorData`{.variable}, in
            `realm`{.variable}.

13. Return `cacheJobPromise`{.variable}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="cache-keys"}
#### [5.4.7. ]{.secno}[[`keys(request, options)`{.idl}](#dom-cache-keys){#ref-for-dom-cache-keys① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#cache-keys){.self-link} {#cache-keys .heading .settled level="5.4.7"}

The
[`keys(``request`{.variable}`, ``options`{.variable}`)`]{#dom-cache-keys
.dfn .dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="Cache" dfn-type="method" export=""
lt="keys(request, options)|keys(request)|keys()"} method steps are:

1.  Let `r`{.variable} be null.

2.  If the optional argument `request`{.variable} is not omitted, then:

    1.  If `request`{.variable} is a
        [`Request`{.idl}](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request){#ref-for-request①①
        link-type="idl"} object, then:

        1.  Set `r`{.variable} to `request`{.variable}'s
            [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request){#ref-for-concept-request-request⑧
            link-type="dfn"}.

        2.  If `r`{.variable}'s
            [method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-method){#ref-for-concept-request-method④
            link-type="dfn"} is not \``GET`\` and
            `options`{.variable}.ignoreMethod is false, return [a
            promise resolved
            with](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-promise-resolved-with){#ref-for-a-promise-resolved-with④
            link-type="dfn"} an empty array.

    2.  Else if `request`{.variable} is a string, then:

        1.  Set `r`{.variable} to the associated
            [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request){#ref-for-concept-request-request⑨
            link-type="dfn"} of the result of invoking the initial value
            of
            [`Request`{.idl}](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request){#ref-for-request①②
            link-type="idl"} as constructor with `request`{.variable} as
            its argument. If this
            [throws](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw){#ref-for-dfn-throw①③
            link-type="dfn"} an exception, return [a promise rejected
            with](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-promise-rejected-with){#ref-for-a-promise-rejected-with①④
            link-type="dfn"} that exception.

3.  Let `realm`{.variable} be
    [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this){#ref-for-this⑥⓪
    link-type="dfn"}'s [relevant
    realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-realm){#ref-for-concept-relevant-realm⑧
    link-type="dfn"}.

4.  Let `promise`{.variable} be [a new
    promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-new-promise){#ref-for-a-new-promise①④
    link-type="dfn"}.

5.  Run these substeps [in
    parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel){#ref-for-in-parallel②①
    link-type="dfn"}:

    1.  Let `requests`{.variable} be an empty
        [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list){#ref-for-list②⓪
        link-type="dfn"}.

    2.  If the optional argument `request`{.variable} is omitted, then:

        1.  [For
            each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate){#ref-for-list-iterate①②
            link-type="dfn"} `requestResponse`{.variable} of the
            [relevant request response
            list](#dfn-relevant-request-response-list){#ref-for-dfn-relevant-request-response-list①
            link-type="dfn"}:

            1.  Add `requestResponse`{.variable}'s request to
                `requests`{.variable}.

    3.  Else:

        1.  Let `requestResponses`{.variable} be the result of running
            [Query Cache](#query-cache){#ref-for-query-cache①
            link-type="dfn"} with `r`{.variable} and
            `options`{.variable}.

        2.  [For
            each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate){#ref-for-list-iterate①③
            link-type="dfn"} `requestResponse`{.variable} of
            `requestResponses`{.variable}:

            1.  Add `requestResponse`{.variable}'s request to
                `requests`{.variable}.

    4.  [Queue a
        task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task){#ref-for-queue-a-task②①
        link-type="dfn"}, on `promise`{.variable}'s [relevant settings
        object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object){#ref-for-relevant-settings-object②⑦
        link-type="dfn"}'s [responsible event
        loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop){#ref-for-responsible-event-loop①⓪
        link-type="dfn"} using the [DOM manipulation task
        source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source){#ref-for-dom-manipulation-task-source①⑤
        link-type="dfn"}, to perform the following steps:

        1.  Let `requestList`{.variable} be a
            [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list){#ref-for-list②①
            link-type="dfn"}.

        2.  [For
            each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate){#ref-for-list-iterate①④
            link-type="dfn"} `request`{.variable} of
            `requests`{.variable}:

            1.  Add a new
                [`Request`{.idl}](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request){#ref-for-request①③
                link-type="idl"} object associated with
                `request`{.variable} and a new associated
                [`Headers`{.idl}](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#headers){#ref-for-headers①
                link-type="idl"} object whose
                [guard](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-guard){#ref-for-concept-headers-guard①
                link-type="dfn"} is \"`immutable`\" to
                `requestList`{.variable}.

        3.  Resolve `promise`{.variable} with a [frozen
            array](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-frozen-array-type){#ref-for-dfn-frozen-array-type③
            link-type="dfn"}
            [created](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-create-frozen-array){#ref-for-dfn-create-frozen-array③
            link-type="dfn"} from `requestList`{.variable}, in
            `realm`{.variable}.

6.  Return `promise`{.variable}.
:::
::::::::::

:::::::: section
### [5.5. ]{.secno}[[`CacheStorage`{.idl}](#cachestorage){#ref-for-cachestorage③ link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#cachestorage-interface){.self-link} {#cachestorage-interface .heading .settled level="5.5"}

``` {.def .highlight .idl}
[SecureContext, Exposed=(Window,Worker)]
interface CacheStorage {
  [NewObject] Promise<(Response or undefined)> match(RequestInfo request, optional MultiCacheQueryOptions options = {});
  [NewObject] Promise<boolean> has(DOMString cacheName);
  [NewObject] Promise<Cache> open(DOMString cacheName);
  [NewObject] Promise<boolean> delete(DOMString cacheName);
  [NewObject] Promise<sequence<DOMString>> keys();
};

dictionary MultiCacheQueryOptions : CacheQueryOptions {
  DOMString cacheName;
};
```

[Note:]{.marker}
[`CacheStorage`{.idl}](#cachestorage){#ref-for-cachestorage④
link-type="idl"} interface is designed to largely conform to [ECMAScript
6 Map
objects](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-map-objects){#ref-for-sec-map-objects
link-type="dfn"} but entirely async, and with additional convenience
methods. The methods, `clear`, `forEach`, `entries` and `values`, are
intentionally excluded from the scope of the first version resorting to
the ongoing discussion about the async iteration by TC39.

The user agent *must* create a
[`CacheStorage`{.idl}](#cachestorage){#ref-for-cachestorage⑤
link-type="idl"} object when a
[`Window`{.idl}](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#window){#ref-for-window①⓪
link-type="idl"} object or a
[`WorkerGlobalScope`{.idl}](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/workers.html#workerglobalscope){#ref-for-workerglobalscope②
link-type="idl"} object is created.

A [`CacheStorage`{.idl}](#cachestorage){#ref-for-cachestorage⑥
link-type="idl"} object represents the [name to cache
map](#dfn-name-to-cache-map){#ref-for-dfn-name-to-cache-map①
link-type="dfn"} associated with the result of running [obtain a local
storage bottle
map](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#obtain-a-local-storage-bottle-map){#ref-for-obtain-a-local-storage-bottle-map①
link-type="dfn"} with its [relevant settings
object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object){#ref-for-relevant-settings-object②⑧
link-type="dfn"} and \"`caches`\". Multiple separate objects
implementing the
[`CacheStorage`{.idl}](#cachestorage){#ref-for-cachestorage⑦
link-type="idl"} interface across documents and workers can all be
associated with the same [name to cache
map](#dfn-name-to-cache-map){#ref-for-dfn-name-to-cache-map②
link-type="dfn"} simultaneously.

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="cache-storage-match"}
#### [5.5.1. ]{.secno}[[`match(request, options)`{.idl}](#dom-cachestorage-match){#ref-for-dom-cachestorage-match① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#cache-storage-match){.self-link} {#cache-storage-match .heading .settled level="5.5.1"}

The
[`match(``request`{.variable}`, ``options`{.variable}`)`]{#dom-cachestorage-match
.dfn .dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="CacheStorage" dfn-type="method"
export="" lt="match(request, options)|match(request)"} method steps are:

1.  If
    `options`{.variable}\[\"[`cacheName`{.idl}](#dom-multicachequeryoptions-cachename){#ref-for-dom-multicachequeryoptions-cachename
    link-type="idl"}\"\]
    [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists){#ref-for-map-exists③
    link-type="dfn"}, then:

    1.  Return [a new
        promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-new-promise){#ref-for-a-new-promise①⑤
        link-type="dfn"} `promise`{.variable} and run the following
        substeps [in
        parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel){#ref-for-in-parallel②②
        link-type="dfn"}:

        1.  [For
            each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-iterate){#ref-for-map-iterate
            link-type="dfn"} `cacheName`{.variable} → `cache`{.variable}
            of the [relevant name to cache
            map](#relevant-name-to-cache-map){#ref-for-relevant-name-to-cache-map
            link-type="dfn"}:

            1.  If
                `options`{.variable}\[\"[`cacheName`{.idl}](#dom-multicachequeryoptions-cachename){#ref-for-dom-multicachequeryoptions-cachename①
                link-type="idl"}\"\] matches `cacheName`{.variable},
                then:

                1.  Resolve `promise`{.variable} with the result of
                    running the algorithm specified in
                    [`match(request, options)`{.idl}](#dom-cache-match){#ref-for-dom-cache-match②
                    link-type="idl"} method of
                    [`Cache`{.idl}](#cache){#ref-for-cache①②
                    link-type="idl"} interface with `request`{.variable}
                    and `options`{.variable} (providing
                    `cache`{.variable} as thisArgument to the
                    `\[[Call]]` internal method of
                    [`match(request, options)`{.idl}](#dom-cache-match){#ref-for-dom-cache-match③
                    link-type="idl"}.)

                2.  Abort these steps.

        2.  Resolve `promise`{.variable} with undefined.

2.  Else:

    1.  Let `promise`{.variable} be [a promise resolved
        with](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-promise-resolved-with){#ref-for-a-promise-resolved-with⑤
        link-type="dfn"} undefined.

    2.  [For
        each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-iterate){#ref-for-map-iterate①
        link-type="dfn"} `cacheName`{.variable} → `cache`{.variable} of
        the [relevant name to cache
        map](#relevant-name-to-cache-map){#ref-for-relevant-name-to-cache-map①
        link-type="dfn"}:

        1.  Set `promise`{.variable} to the result of
            [reacting](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-perform-steps-once-promise-is-settled){#ref-for-dfn-perform-steps-once-promise-is-settled②
            link-type="dfn"} to itself with a fulfillment handler that,
            when called with argument `response`{.variable}, performs
            the following substeps:

            1.  If `response`{.variable} is not undefined, return
                `response`{.variable}.

            2.  Return the result of running the algorithm specified in
                [`match(request, options)`{.idl}](#dom-cache-match){#ref-for-dom-cache-match④
                link-type="idl"} method of
                [`Cache`{.idl}](#cache){#ref-for-cache①③
                link-type="idl"} interface with `request`{.variable} and
                `options`{.variable} as the arguments (providing
                `cache`{.variable} as thisArgument to the `\[[Call]]`
                internal method of
                [`match(request, options)`{.idl}](#dom-cache-match){#ref-for-dom-cache-match⑤
                link-type="idl"}.)

    3.  Return `promise`{.variable}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="cache-storage-has"}
#### [5.5.2. ]{.secno}[[`has(cacheName)`{.idl}](#dom-cachestorage-has){#ref-for-dom-cachestorage-has① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#cache-storage-has){.self-link} {#cache-storage-has .heading .settled level="5.5.2"}

The [`has(``cacheName`{.variable}`)`]{#dom-cachestorage-has .dfn
.dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="CacheStorage" dfn-type="method"
export=""} method steps are:

1.  Let `promise`{.variable} be [a new
    promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-new-promise){#ref-for-a-new-promise①⑥
    link-type="dfn"}.

2.  Run the following substeps [in
    parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel){#ref-for-in-parallel②③
    link-type="dfn"}:

    1.  [For
        each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-iterate){#ref-for-map-iterate②
        link-type="dfn"} `key`{.variable} → `value`{.variable} of the
        [relevant name to cache
        map](#relevant-name-to-cache-map){#ref-for-relevant-name-to-cache-map②
        link-type="dfn"}:

        1.  If `cacheName`{.variable} matches `key`{.variable}, resolve
            `promise`{.variable} with true and abort these steps.

    2.  Resolve `promise`{.variable} with false.

3.  Return `promise`{.variable}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="cache-storage-open"}
#### [5.5.3. ]{.secno}[[`open(cacheName)`{.idl}](#dom-cachestorage-open){#ref-for-dom-cachestorage-open① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#cache-storage-open){.self-link} {#cache-storage-open .heading .settled level="5.5.3"}

The [`open(``cacheName`{.variable}`)`]{#dom-cachestorage-open .dfn
.dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="CacheStorage" dfn-type="method"
export=""} method steps are:

1.  Let `promise`{.variable} be [a new
    promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-new-promise){#ref-for-a-new-promise①⑦
    link-type="dfn"}.

2.  Run the following substeps [in
    parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel){#ref-for-in-parallel②④
    link-type="dfn"}:

    1.  [For
        each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-iterate){#ref-for-map-iterate③
        link-type="dfn"} `key`{.variable} → `value`{.variable} of the
        [relevant name to cache
        map](#relevant-name-to-cache-map){#ref-for-relevant-name-to-cache-map③
        link-type="dfn"}:

        1.  If `cacheName`{.variable} matches `key`{.variable}, then:

            1.  Resolve `promise`{.variable} with a new
                [`Cache`{.idl}](#cache){#ref-for-cache①④
                link-type="idl"} object that represents
                `value`{.variable}.

            2.  Abort these steps.

    2.  Let `cache`{.variable} be a new [request response
        list](#dfn-request-response-list){#ref-for-dfn-request-response-list④
        link-type="dfn"}.

    3.  [Set](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-set){#ref-for-map-set
        link-type="dfn"} the [relevant name to cache
        map](#relevant-name-to-cache-map){#ref-for-relevant-name-to-cache-map④
        link-type="dfn"}\[`cacheName`{.variable}\] to
        `cache`{.variable}. If this cache write operation failed due to
        exceeding the granted quota limit, reject `promise`{.variable}
        with a
        [`QuotaExceededError`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#quotaexceedederror){#ref-for-quotaexceedederror
        link-type="idl"} and abort these steps.

    4.  Resolve `promise`{.variable} with a new
        [`Cache`{.idl}](#cache){#ref-for-cache①⑤ link-type="idl"} object
        that represents `cache`{.variable}.

3.  Return `promise`{.variable}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="cache-storage-delete"}
#### [5.5.4. ]{.secno}[[`delete(cacheName)`{.idl}](#dom-cachestorage-delete){#ref-for-dom-cachestorage-delete① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#cache-storage-delete){.self-link} {#cache-storage-delete .heading .settled level="5.5.4"}

The [`delete(``cacheName`{.variable}`)`]{#dom-cachestorage-delete .dfn
.dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="CacheStorage" dfn-type="method"
export=""} method steps are:

1.  Let `promise`{.variable} be the result of running the algorithm
    specified in
    [`has(cacheName)`{.idl}](#dom-cachestorage-has){#ref-for-dom-cachestorage-has②
    link-type="idl"} method with `cacheName`{.variable}.

2.  Return the result of
    [reacting](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-perform-steps-once-promise-is-settled){#ref-for-dfn-perform-steps-once-promise-is-settled③
    link-type="dfn"} to `promise`{.variable} with a fulfillment handler
    that, when called with argument `cacheExists`{.variable}, performs
    the following substeps:

    1.  If `cacheExists`{.variable} is false, then:

        1.  Return false.

    2.  Let `cacheJobPromise`{.variable} be [a new
        promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-new-promise){#ref-for-a-new-promise①⑧
        link-type="dfn"}.

    3.  Run the following substeps [in
        parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel){#ref-for-in-parallel②⑤
        link-type="dfn"}:

        1.  [Remove](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-remove){#ref-for-map-remove
            link-type="dfn"} the [relevant name to cache
            map](#relevant-name-to-cache-map){#ref-for-relevant-name-to-cache-map⑤
            link-type="dfn"}\[`cacheName`{.variable}\].

        2.  Resolve `cacheJobPromise`{.variable} with true.

        [Note:]{.marker} After this step, the existing DOM objects (i.e.
        the currently referenced Cache, Request, and Response objects)
        should remain functional.

    4.  Return `cacheJobPromise`{.variable}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="cache-storage-keys"}
#### [5.5.5. ]{.secno}[[`keys()`{.idl}](#dom-cachestorage-keys){#ref-for-dom-cachestorage-keys① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#cache-storage-keys){.self-link} {#cache-storage-keys .heading .settled level="5.5.5"}

The [`keys()`]{#dom-cachestorage-keys .dfn .dfn-paneled .idl-code
dfn-for="CacheStorage" dfn-type="method" export=""} method steps are:

1.  Let `promise`{.variable} be [a new
    promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-new-promise){#ref-for-a-new-promise①⑨
    link-type="dfn"}.

2.  Run the following substeps [in
    parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel){#ref-for-in-parallel②⑥
    link-type="dfn"}:

    1.  Let `cacheKeys`{.variable} be the result of [getting the
        keys](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-getting-the-keys){#ref-for-map-getting-the-keys③
        link-type="dfn"} of the [relevant name to cache
        map](#relevant-name-to-cache-map){#ref-for-relevant-name-to-cache-map⑥
        link-type="dfn"}.

        [Note:]{.marker} The
        [items](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-item){#ref-for-list-item⑥
        link-type="dfn"} in the result [ordered
        set](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ordered-set){#ref-for-ordered-set⑥
        link-type="dfn"} are in the order that their corresponding entry
        was added to the [name to cache
        map](#dfn-name-to-cache-map){#ref-for-dfn-name-to-cache-map③
        link-type="dfn"}.

    2.  Resolve `promise`{.variable} with `cacheKeys`{.variable}.

3.  Return `promise`{.variable}.
:::
::::::::
:::::::::::::::::::::

::::::::::::: section
## [6. ]{.secno}[Security Considerations]{.content}[](#security-considerations){.self-link} {#security-considerations .heading .settled level="6"}

::: section
### [6.1. ]{.secno}[Secure Context]{.content}[](#secure-context){.self-link} {#secure-context .heading .settled level="6.1"}

[Service workers](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑧⓪
link-type="dfn"} *must* execute in [secure
contexts](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#secure-context){#ref-for-secure-context②
link-type="dfn"}. [Service worker
clients](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client③③
link-type="dfn"} *must* also be [secure
contexts](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#secure-context){#ref-for-secure-context③
link-type="dfn"} to register a [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration④⑤
link-type="dfn"}, to get access to the [service worker
registrations](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration④⑥
link-type="dfn"} and the [service
workers](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑧①
link-type="dfn"}, to do messaging with the [service
workers](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑧②
link-type="dfn"}, and to be manipulated by the [service
workers](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑧③
link-type="dfn"}.

[Note:]{.marker} This effectively means that [service
workers](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑧④
link-type="dfn"} and their [service worker
clients](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client③④
link-type="dfn"} need to be hosted over HTTPS. A user agent can allow
`localhost` (see [the
requirements](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-secure-contexts/#localhost)),
`127.0.0.0/8`, and `::1/128` for development purposes. The primary
reason for this restriction is to protect users from the [risks
associated with insecure
contexts](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-secure-contexts/#threat-risks).
:::

::: section
### [6.2. ]{.secno}[Content Security Policy]{.content}[](#content-security-policy){.self-link} {#content-security-policy .heading .settled level="6.2"}

Whenever a user agent invokes the [Run Service
Worker](#run-service-worker){#ref-for-run-service-worker①
link-type="dfn"} algorithm with a [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑧⑤
link-type="dfn"} `serviceWorker`{.variable}:

- If `serviceWorker`{.variable}'s [script
  resource](#dfn-script-resource){#ref-for-dfn-script-resource②
  link-type="dfn"} was delivered with a `Content-Security-Policy` HTTP
  header containing the value `policy`{.variable}, the user agent *must*
  [enforce](https://wicg.github.io/csp-next/scripting-policy.html#scripting-policy-pair-enforced){#ref-for-scripting-policy-pair-enforced
  link-type="dfn"} `policy`{.variable} for `serviceWorker`{.variable}.

- If `serviceWorker`{.variable}'s [script
  resource](#dfn-script-resource){#ref-for-dfn-script-resource③
  link-type="dfn"} was delivered with a
  `Content-Security-Policy-Report-Only` HTTP header containing the value
  `policy`{.variable}, the user agent *must*
  [monitor](https://w3c.github.io/mediacapture-screen-share/#dfn-monitor){#ref-for-dfn-monitor
  link-type="dfn"} `policy`{.variable} for `serviceWorker`{.variable}.

The primary reason for this restriction is to mitigate a broad class of
content injection vulnerabilities, such as cross-site scripting (XSS).
:::

::::: section
### [6.3. ]{.secno}[Origin Relativity]{.content}[](#origin-relativity){.self-link} {#origin-relativity .heading .settled level="6.3"}

::: section
#### [6.3.1. ]{.secno}[Origin restriction]{.content}[](#origin-restriction){.self-link} {#origin-restriction .heading .settled level="6.3.1"}

*This section is non-normative.*

A [service worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑧⑥
link-type="dfn"} executes in the registering [service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client③⑤
link-type="dfn"}'s
[origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-origin){#ref-for-concept-settings-object-origin⑨
link-type="dfn"}. One of the advanced concerns that major applications
would encounter is whether they can be hosted from a CDN. By definition,
these are servers in other places, often on other
[origins](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#concept-origin){#ref-for-concept-origin①①
link-type="dfn"}. Therefore, [service
workers](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑧⑦
link-type="dfn"} cannot be hosted on CDNs. But they can include
resources via
[importScripts()](#importscripts-method){#ref-for-importscripts-method
link-type="functionish"}. The reason for this restriction is that
[service workers](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑧⑧
link-type="dfn"} create the opportunity for a bad actor to turn a bad
day into a bad eternity.
:::

::: section
#### [6.3.2. ]{.secno}[[`importScripts(urls)`{.idl}](#importscripts-method){#ref-for-importscripts-method① link-type="idl"}]{.content}[](#importscripts){.self-link} {#importscripts .heading .settled level="6.3.2"}

When the [`importScripts(``urls`{.variable}`)`]{#importscripts-method
.dfn .dfn-paneled .idl-code dfn-for="ServiceWorkerGlobalScope"
dfn-type="method" export=""} method is called on a
[`ServiceWorkerGlobalScope`{.idl}](#serviceworkerglobalscope){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope①④
link-type="idl"} object, the user agent *must* [import scripts into
worker global
scope](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/workers.html#import-scripts-into-worker-global-scope){#ref-for-import-scripts-into-worker-global-scope
link-type="dfn"}, given this
[`ServiceWorkerGlobalScope`{.idl}](#serviceworkerglobalscope){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope①⑤
link-type="idl"} object and `urls`{.variable}, and with the following
[perform the fetch
hook](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#fetching-scripts-perform-fetch){#ref-for-fetching-scripts-perform-fetch
link-type="dfn"} steps, given the
[request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request){#ref-for-concept-request⑤
link-type="dfn"} `request`{.variable}:

1.  Let `serviceWorker`{.variable} be `request`{.variable}'s
    [client](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-client){#ref-for-concept-request-client①
    link-type="dfn"}'s [global
    object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-global){#ref-for-concept-settings-object-global⑧
    link-type="dfn"}'s [service
    worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker②④
    link-type="dfn"}.

2.  Let `map`{.variable} be `serviceWorker`{.variable}'s [script
    resource
    map](#dfn-script-resource-map){#ref-for-dfn-script-resource-map
    link-type="dfn"}.

3.  Let `url`{.variable} be `request`{.variable}'s
    [url](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-url){#ref-for-concept-request-url④
    link-type="dfn"}.

4.  If `serviceWorker`{.variable}'s
    [state](#dfn-state){#ref-for-dfn-state⑥ link-type="dfn"} is not
    \"`parsed`\" or \"`installing`\":

    1.  Return `map`{.variable}\[`url`{.variable}\] if it
        [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists){#ref-for-map-exists④
        link-type="dfn"} and a [network
        error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error){#ref-for-concept-network-error②
        link-type="dfn"} otherwise.

5.  If `map`{.variable}\[`url`{.variable}\]
    [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists){#ref-for-map-exists⑤
    link-type="dfn"}:

    1.  [Append](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#set-append){#ref-for-set-append
        link-type="dfn"} `url`{.variable} to
        `serviceWorker`{.variable}'s [set of used
        scripts](#dfn-set-of-used-scripts){#ref-for-dfn-set-of-used-scripts①
        link-type="dfn"}.

    2.  Return `map`{.variable}\[`url`{.variable}\].

6.  Let `registration`{.variable} be `serviceWorker`{.variable}'s
    [containing service worker
    registration](#dfn-containing-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-containing-service-worker-registration①⑤
    link-type="dfn"}.

7.  Set `request`{.variable}'s [service-workers
    mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-service-workers-mode){#ref-for-request-service-workers-mode①
    link-type="dfn"} to \"`none`\".

8.  Set `request`{.variable}'s [cache
    mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-cache-mode){#ref-for-concept-request-cache-mode
    link-type="dfn"} to \"`no-cache`\" if any of the following are true:

    - `registration`{.variable}'s [update via cache
      mode](#dfn-update-via-cache){#ref-for-dfn-update-via-cache①
      link-type="dfn"} is \"`none`\".

    - The [current global
      object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#current-global-object){#ref-for-current-global-object⑤
      link-type="dfn"}'s [force bypass cache for import scripts
      flag](#serviceworkerglobalscope-force-bypass-cache-for-import-scripts-flag){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope-force-bypass-cache-for-import-scripts-flag
      link-type="dfn"} is set.

    - `registration`{.variable} is
      [stale](#service-worker-registration-stale){#ref-for-service-worker-registration-stale
      link-type="dfn"}.

9.  Let `response`{.variable} be the result of
    [fetching](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch){#ref-for-concept-fetch⑦
    link-type="dfn"} `request`{.variable}.

10. If `response`{.variable}'s [cache
    state](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-cache-state){#ref-for-concept-response-cache-state
    link-type="dfn"} is not \"`local`\", set `registration`{.variable}'s
    [last update check
    time](#dfn-last-update-check-time){#ref-for-dfn-last-update-check-time②
    link-type="dfn"} to the current time.

11. If `response`{.variable}'s [unsafe
    response](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/urls-and-fetching.html#unsafe-response){#ref-for-unsafe-response
    link-type="dfn"} is a [bad import script
    response](#dfn-bad-import-script-response){#ref-for-dfn-bad-import-script-response
    link-type="dfn"}, then return a [network
    error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error){#ref-for-concept-network-error③
    link-type="dfn"}.

12. [Set](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-set){#ref-for-map-set①
    link-type="dfn"} `map`{.variable}\[`url`{.variable}\] to
    `response`{.variable}.

13. [Append](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#set-append){#ref-for-set-append①
    link-type="dfn"} `url`{.variable} to `serviceWorker`{.variable}'s
    [set of used
    scripts](#dfn-set-of-used-scripts){#ref-for-dfn-set-of-used-scripts②
    link-type="dfn"}.

14. Set `serviceWorker`{.variable}'s [classic scripts imported
    flag](#dfn-classic-scripts-imported-flag){#ref-for-dfn-classic-scripts-imported-flag
    link-type="dfn"}.

15. Return `response`{.variable}.
:::
:::::

::: section
### [6.4. ]{.secno}[Cross-Origin Resources and CORS]{.content}[](#cross-origin-resources){.self-link} {#cross-origin-resources .heading .settled level="6.4"}

*This section is non-normative.*

Applications tend to cache items that come from a CDN or other
[origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-origin){#ref-for-concept-settings-object-origin①⓪
link-type="dfn"}. It is possible to request many of them directly using
`<script>`, `<img>`, `<video>` and `<link>` elements. It would be hugely
limiting if this sort of runtime collaboration broke when offline.
Similarly, it is possible to
[fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch){#ref-for-concept-fetch⑧
link-type="dfn"} many sorts of
off-[origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-origin){#ref-for-concept-settings-object-origin①①
link-type="dfn"} resources when appropriate CORS headers are set.
[Service workers](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑧⑨
link-type="dfn"} enable this by allowing
[`Caches`{.idl}](#cache){#ref-for-cache①⑥ link-type="idl"} to
[fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch){#ref-for-concept-fetch⑨
link-type="dfn"} and cache off-origin items. Some restrictions apply,
however. First, unlike same-origin resources which are managed in the
[`Cache`{.idl}](#cache){#ref-for-cache①⑦ link-type="idl"} as
[`Response`{.idl}](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response){#ref-for-response①①
link-type="idl"} objects whose corresponding
[responses](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-response){#ref-for-concept-response-response②
link-type="dfn"} are [basic filtered
response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-filtered-response-basic){#ref-for-concept-filtered-response-basic
link-type="dfn"}, the objects stored are
[`Response`{.idl}](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response){#ref-for-response①②
link-type="idl"} objects whose corresponding
[responses](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-response){#ref-for-concept-response-response③
link-type="dfn"} are either [CORS filtered
responses](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-filtered-response-cors){#ref-for-concept-filtered-response-cors
link-type="dfn"} or [opaque filtered
responses](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-filtered-response-opaque){#ref-for-concept-filtered-response-opaque
link-type="dfn"}. They can be passed to
[`event.respondWith(r)`{.idl}](#dom-fetchevent-respondwith){#ref-for-dom-fetchevent-respondwith③
link-type="idl"} method in the same manner as the
[`Response`{.idl}](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response){#ref-for-response①③
link-type="idl"} objects whose corresponding
[responses](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-response){#ref-for-concept-response-response④
link-type="dfn"} are [basic filtered
responses](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-filtered-response-basic){#ref-for-concept-filtered-response-basic①
link-type="dfn"}, but cannot be meaningfully created programmatically.
These limitations are necessary to preserve the security invariants of
the platform. Allowing [`Caches`{.idl}](#cache){#ref-for-cache①⑧
link-type="idl"} to store them allows applications to avoid
re-architecting in most cases.
:::

::: section
### [6.5. ]{.secno}[Path restriction]{.content}[](#path-restriction){.self-link} {#path-restriction .heading .settled level="6.5"}

*This section is non-normative.*

In addition to the [origin restriction](#origin-restriction), service
workers are restricted by the
[path](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-path){#ref-for-concept-url-path
link-type="dfn"} of the service worker script. For example, a service
worker script at `https://www.example.com/~bob/sw.js` can be registered
for the [scope url](#dfn-scope-url){#ref-for-dfn-scope-url①⓪
link-type="dfn"} `https://www.example.com/~bob/` but not for the scope
`https://www.example.com/` or `https://www.example.com/~alice/`. This
provides some protection for sites that host multiple-user content in
separated directories on the same origin. However, the path restriction
is not considered a hard security boundary, as only origins are. Sites
are encouraged to use different origins to securely isolate segments of
the site if appropriate.

Servers can remove the path restriction by setting a
[Service-Worker-Allowed](#service-worker-allowed){#ref-for-service-worker-allowed
link-type="dfn"} header on the service worker script.
:::

::: section
### [6.6. ]{.secno}[Service worker script request]{.content}[](#script-request){.self-link} {#script-request .heading .settled level="6.6"}

*This section is non-normative.*

To further defend against malicious registration of a service worker on
a site, this specification requires that:

- The [Service-Worker](#service-worker){#ref-for-service-worker
  link-type="dfn"} header is present on service worker script requests,
  and

- Service worker scripts are served with a [JavaScript MIME
  type](https://mimesniff.spec.whatwg.org/#javascript-mime-type){#ref-for-javascript-mime-type
  link-type="dfn"}.
:::

::: section
### [6.7. ]{.secno}[Implementer Concerns]{.content}[](#implementer-concerns){.self-link} {#implementer-concerns .heading .settled level="6.7"}

*This section is non-normative.*

The implementers are encouraged to note:

- Plug-ins should not load via [service
  workers](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑨⓪
  link-type="dfn"}. As plug-ins may get their security origins from
  their own urls, the embedding [service
  worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑨①
  link-type="dfn"} cannot handle it. For this reason, the [Handle
  Fetch](#handle-fetch){#ref-for-handle-fetch⑥ link-type="dfn"}
  algorithm makes `<embed>` and `<object>` requests immediately fallback
  to the network without dispatching
  [`fetch`{.idl}](#service-worker-global-scope-fetch-event){#ref-for-service-worker-global-scope-fetch-event⑥
  .idl-code link-type="event"} event.

- Some of the legacy networking stack code may need to be carefully
  audited to understand the ramifications of interactions with [service
  workers](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑨②
  link-type="dfn"}.
:::

::: section
### [6.8. ]{.secno}[Privacy]{.content}[](#privacy){.self-link} {#privacy .heading .settled level="6.8"}

[Service workers](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑨③
link-type="dfn"} introduce new persistent storage features including
[registration
map](#dfn-scope-to-registration-map){#ref-for-dfn-scope-to-registration-map③
link-type="dfn"} (for [service worker
registrations](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration④⑦
link-type="dfn"} and their [service
workers](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑨④
link-type="dfn"}), [request response
list](#dfn-request-response-list){#ref-for-dfn-request-response-list⑤
link-type="dfn"} and [name to cache
map](#dfn-name-to-cache-map){#ref-for-dfn-name-to-cache-map④
link-type="dfn"} (for caches), and [script resource
map](#dfn-script-resource-map){#ref-for-dfn-script-resource-map①
link-type="dfn"} (for script resources). In order to protect users from
any potential [unsanctioned
tracking](#biblio-unsanctioned-tracking "Unsanctioned Web Tracking"){link-type="biblio"}
threat, these persistent storages *should* be cleared when users intend
to clear them and *should* maintain and interoperate with existing user
controls e.g. purging all existing persistent storages.
:::
:::::::::::::

::::::: section
## [7. ]{.secno}[Extensibility]{.content}[](#extensibility){.self-link} {#extensibility .heading .settled level="7"}

Service Workers specification is extensible from other specifications.

::: section
### [7.1. ]{.secno}[Define API bound to Service Worker Registration]{.content}[](#extension-to-service-worker-registration){.self-link} {#extension-to-service-worker-registration .heading .settled level="7.1"}

Specifications *may* define an API tied to a [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration④⑧
link-type="dfn"} by using [partial
interface](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-partial-interface){#ref-for-dfn-partial-interface
link-type="dfn"} definition to the
[`ServiceWorkerRegistration`{.idl}](#serviceworkerregistration){#ref-for-serviceworkerregistration①④
link-type="idl"} interface where it *may* define the specification
specific attributes and methods:

``` {#example-e75a31ac .def .example .highlight .idl no-idl=""}
partial interface ServiceWorkerRegistration {
  // e.g. define an API namespace
  readonly attribute APISpaceType APISpace;
  // e.g. define a method
  Promise<T> methodName(/* list of arguments */);
};
```
:::

::: section
### [7.2. ]{.secno}[Define Functional Event]{.content}[](#extension-to-extendable-event){.self-link} {#extension-to-extendable-event .heading .settled level="7.2"}

Specifications *may* define a [functional
event](#dfn-functional-events){#ref-for-dfn-functional-events①⓪
link-type="dfn"} by extending
[`ExtendableEvent`{.idl}](#extendableevent){#ref-for-extendableevent①⑤
link-type="idl"} interface:

``` {#example-85e5c3c4 .def .example .highlight .idl no-idl=""}
// e.g. define FunctionalEvent interface
interface FunctionalEvent : ExtendableEvent {
  // add a functional event’s own attributes and methods
};
```
:::

::: section
### [7.3. ]{.secno}[Define Event Handler]{.content}[](#extension-to-service-worker-global-scope){.self-link} {#extension-to-service-worker-global-scope .heading .settled level="7.3"}

Specifications *may* define an event handler attribute for the
corresponding [functional
event](#dfn-functional-events){#ref-for-dfn-functional-events①①
link-type="dfn"} using [partial
interface](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-partial-interface){#ref-for-dfn-partial-interface①
link-type="dfn"} definition to the
[`ServiceWorkerGlobalScope`{.idl}](#serviceworkerglobalscope){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope①⑥
link-type="idl"} interface:

``` {#example-80342678 .def .example .highlight .idl no-idl=""}
partial interface ServiceWorkerGlobalScope {
  attribute EventHandler onfunctionalevent;
};
```
:::

::: section
### [7.4. ]{.secno}[Firing Functional Events]{.content}[](#firing-functional-events){.self-link} {#firing-functional-events .heading .settled level="7.4"}

To request a [functional
event](#dfn-functional-events){#ref-for-dfn-functional-events①②
link-type="dfn"} dispatch to the [active
worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker①⑨
link-type="dfn"} of a [service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration④⑨
link-type="dfn"}, specifications *should* invoke [Fire Functional
Event](#fire-functional-event){#ref-for-fire-functional-event
link-type="dfn"}.
:::
:::::::

:::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: section
## [Appendix A: Algorithms]{.content}[](#algorithms){.self-link} {#algorithms .heading .settled}

The following definitions are the user agent's internal data structures
used throughout the specification.

A [registration map]{#dfn-scope-to-registration-map .dfn .dfn-paneled
dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""} is an [ordered
map](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ordered-map){#ref-for-ordered-map⑤
link-type="dfn"} where the keys are ([storage
key](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#storage-key){#ref-for-storage-key②
link-type="dfn"},
[serialized](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-serializer){#ref-for-concept-url-serializer⑤
link-type="dfn"} [scope urls](#dfn-scope-url){#ref-for-dfn-scope-url①①
link-type="dfn"}) and the values are [service worker
registrations](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration⑤⓪
link-type="dfn"}.

A [job]{#dfn-job .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""} is an
abstraction of one of register, update, and unregister request for a
[service worker
registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration⑤①
link-type="dfn"}.

::: {}
A [job](#dfn-job){#ref-for-dfn-job link-type="dfn"} has a [job
type]{#dfn-job-type .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-for="job" dfn-type="dfn"
noexport=""}, which is one of *register*, *update*, and *unregister*.

A [job](#dfn-job){#ref-for-dfn-job① link-type="dfn"} has a [storage
key]{#job-storage-key .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-for="job" dfn-type="dfn"
noexport=""} (a [storage
key](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#storage-key){#ref-for-storage-key③
link-type="dfn"}).

A [job](#dfn-job){#ref-for-dfn-job② link-type="dfn"} has a [scope
url]{#dfn-job-scope-url .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-for="job" dfn-type="dfn"
noexport=""} (a
[URL](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url){#ref-for-concept-url④
link-type="dfn"}).

A [job](#dfn-job){#ref-for-dfn-job③ link-type="dfn"} has a [script
url]{#dfn-job-script-url .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-for="job" dfn-type="dfn"
noexport=""} (a
[URL](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url){#ref-for-concept-url⑤
link-type="dfn"}).

A [job](#dfn-job){#ref-for-dfn-job④ link-type="dfn"} has a [worker
type]{#dfn-job-worker-type .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-for="job"
dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""} (\"`classic`\" or \"`module`\").

A [job](#dfn-job){#ref-for-dfn-job⑤ link-type="dfn"} has an [update via
cache mode]{#dfn-job-update-via-cache-mode .dfn .dfn-paneled
dfn-for="job" dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}, which is \"`imports`\",
\"`all`\", or \"`none`\".

A [job](#dfn-job){#ref-for-dfn-job⑥ link-type="dfn"} has a
[client]{#dfn-job-client .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-for="job" dfn-type="dfn"
noexport=""} (a [service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client③⑥
link-type="dfn"}). It is initially null.

A [job](#dfn-job){#ref-for-dfn-job⑦ link-type="dfn"} has a
[referrer]{#job-referrer .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-for="job" dfn-type="dfn"
noexport=""} (a
[URL](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url){#ref-for-concept-url⑥
link-type="dfn"} or null).

A [job](#dfn-job){#ref-for-dfn-job⑧ link-type="dfn"} has a [job
promise]{#dfn-job-promise .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-for="job" dfn-type="dfn"
noexport=""} (a
[promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects){#ref-for-sec-promise-objects②⑧
link-type="dfn"}). It is initially null.

A [job](#dfn-job){#ref-for-dfn-job⑨ link-type="dfn"} has a [containing
job queue]{#dfn-containing-job-queue .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-for="job"
dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""} (a [job
queue](#dfn-job-queue){#ref-for-dfn-job-queue link-type="dfn"} or null).
It is initially null.

A [job](#dfn-job){#ref-for-dfn-job①⓪ link-type="dfn"} has a [list of
equivalent jobs]{#dfn-job-list-of-equivalent-jobs .dfn .dfn-paneled
dfn-for="job" dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""} (a list of
[jobs](#dfn-job){#ref-for-dfn-job①① link-type="dfn"}). It is initially
the empty list.

A [job](#dfn-job){#ref-for-dfn-job①② link-type="dfn"} has a [force
bypass cache flag]{#dfn-job-force-bypass-cache-flag .dfn .dfn-paneled
dfn-for="job" dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}. It is initially unset.
:::

Two [jobs](#dfn-job){#ref-for-dfn-job①③ link-type="dfn"} are
[equivalent]{#dfn-job-equivalent .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn"
noexport=""} when their [job type](#dfn-job-type){#ref-for-dfn-job-type
link-type="dfn"} is the same and:

- For *register* and *update* [jobs](#dfn-job){#ref-for-dfn-job①④
  link-type="dfn"}, their [scope
  url](#dfn-job-scope-url){#ref-for-dfn-job-scope-url link-type="dfn"},
  [script url](#dfn-job-script-url){#ref-for-dfn-job-script-url
  link-type="dfn"}, [worker
  type](#dfn-job-worker-type){#ref-for-dfn-job-worker-type①
  link-type="dfn"}, and [update via cache
  mode](#dfn-job-update-via-cache-mode){#ref-for-dfn-job-update-via-cache-mode
  link-type="dfn"} are the same.

- For *unregister* [jobs](#dfn-job){#ref-for-dfn-job①⑤ link-type="dfn"},
  their [scope url](#dfn-job-scope-url){#ref-for-dfn-job-scope-url①
  link-type="dfn"} is the same.

A [job queue]{#dfn-job-queue .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn"
noexport=""} is a thread safe
[queue](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#queue){#ref-for-queue
link-type="dfn"} used to synchronize the set of concurrent
[jobs](#dfn-job){#ref-for-dfn-job①⑥ link-type="dfn"}. The [job
queue](#dfn-job-queue){#ref-for-dfn-job-queue① link-type="dfn"} contains
[jobs](#dfn-job){#ref-for-dfn-job①⑦ link-type="dfn"} as its
[items](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-item){#ref-for-list-item⑦
link-type="dfn"}. A [job queue](#dfn-job-queue){#ref-for-dfn-job-queue②
link-type="dfn"} is initially empty.

A [scope to job queue map]{#dfn-scope-to-job-queue-map .dfn .dfn-paneled
dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""} is an [ordered
map](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ordered-map){#ref-for-ordered-map⑥
link-type="dfn"} where the keys are [scope
urls](#dfn-scope-url){#ref-for-dfn-scope-url①② link-type="dfn"},
[serialized](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-serializer){#ref-for-concept-url-serializer⑥
link-type="dfn"}, and the values are [job
queues](#dfn-job-queue){#ref-for-dfn-job-queue③ link-type="dfn"}.

A [bad import script response]{#dfn-bad-import-script-response .dfn
.dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""} is a
[response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response){#ref-for-concept-response⑦
link-type="dfn"} for which any of the following conditions are met:

- `response`{.variable}'s
  [type](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-type){#ref-for-concept-response-type②
  link-type="dfn"} is \"`error`\"

- `response`{.variable}'s
  [status](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-status){#ref-for-concept-response-status②
  link-type="dfn"} is not an [ok
  status](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#ok-status){#ref-for-ok-status①
  link-type="dfn"}

- The result of [extracting a MIME
  type](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-extract-mime-type){#ref-for-concept-header-extract-mime-type
  link-type="dfn"} from `response`{.variable}'s [header
  list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-header-list){#ref-for-concept-response-header-list②
  link-type="dfn"} is not a [JavaScript MIME
  type](https://mimesniff.spec.whatwg.org/#javascript-mime-type){#ref-for-javascript-mime-type①
  link-type="dfn"}

  [Note:]{.marker} Keep this definition in sync with [fetch a classic
  worker-imported
  script](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#fetch-a-classic-worker-imported-script){#ref-for-fetch-a-classic-worker-imported-script
  link-type="dfn"}.

A [race result]{#dfn-race-result .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn"
noexport=""} is a
[tuple](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#tuple){#ref-for-tuple①
link-type="dfn"} of a [routed
response](#race-result-routed-response){#ref-for-race-result-routed-response
link-type="dfn"} and [used
route](#race-result-used-route){#ref-for-race-result-used-route
link-type="dfn"}.

A [race result](#dfn-race-result){#ref-for-dfn-race-result
link-type="dfn"} has an associated [routed
response]{#race-result-routed-response .dfn .dfn-paneled
dfn-for="race result" dfn-type="dfn" export=""} (a
[response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response){#ref-for-concept-response⑧
link-type="dfn"}).

A [race result](#dfn-race-result){#ref-for-dfn-race-result①
link-type="dfn"} has an associated [used route]{#race-result-used-route
.dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-for="race result" dfn-type="dfn" export=""} (a
[`RouterSourceEnum`{.idl}](#enumdef-routersourceenum){#ref-for-enumdef-routersourceenum①
link-type="idl"}).

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="Create Job"}
### [[Create Job]{#create-job .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}]{.content}[](#create-job-algorithm){.self-link} {#create-job-algorithm .heading .settled}

Input

:   `jobType`{.variable}, a [job
    type](#dfn-job-type){#ref-for-dfn-job-type① link-type="dfn"}

:   `storage key`{.variable}, a [storage
    key](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#storage-key){#ref-for-storage-key④
    link-type="dfn"}

:   `scopeURL`{.variable}, a
    [URL](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url){#ref-for-concept-url⑦
    link-type="dfn"}

:   `scriptURL`{.variable}, a
    [URL](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url){#ref-for-concept-url⑧
    link-type="dfn"}

:   `promise`{.variable}, a
    [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects){#ref-for-sec-promise-objects②⑨
    link-type="dfn"}

:   `client`{.variable}, a [service worker
    client](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client③⑦
    link-type="dfn"}

Output

:   `job`{.variable}, a [job](#dfn-job){#ref-for-dfn-job①⑧
    link-type="dfn"}

1.  Let `job`{.variable} be a new [job](#dfn-job){#ref-for-dfn-job①⑨
    link-type="dfn"}.

2.  Set `job`{.variable}'s [job
    type](#dfn-job-type){#ref-for-dfn-job-type② link-type="dfn"} to
    `jobType`{.variable}.

3.  Set `job`{.variable}'s [storage
    key](#job-storage-key){#ref-for-job-storage-key link-type="dfn"} to
    `storage key`{.variable}.

4.  Set `job`{.variable}'s [scope
    url](#dfn-job-scope-url){#ref-for-dfn-job-scope-url②
    link-type="dfn"} to `scopeURL`{.variable}.

5.  Set `job`{.variable}'s [script
    url](#dfn-job-script-url){#ref-for-dfn-job-script-url①
    link-type="dfn"} to `scriptURL`{.variable}.

6.  Set `job`{.variable}'s [job
    promise](#dfn-job-promise){#ref-for-dfn-job-promise link-type="dfn"}
    to `promise`{.variable}.

7.  Set `job`{.variable}'s
    [client](#dfn-job-client){#ref-for-dfn-job-client link-type="dfn"}
    to `client`{.variable}.

8.  If `client`{.variable} is not null, set `job`{.variable}'s
    [referrer](#job-referrer){#ref-for-job-referrer link-type="dfn"} to
    `client`{.variable}'s [creation
    URL](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-creation-url){#ref-for-concept-environment-creation-url⑥
    link-type="dfn"}.

9.  Return `job`{.variable}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="Schedule Job"}
### [[Schedule Job]{#schedule-job .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}]{.content}[](#schedule-job-algorithm){.self-link} {#schedule-job-algorithm .heading .settled}

Input

:   `job`{.variable}, a [job](#dfn-job){#ref-for-dfn-job②⓪
    link-type="dfn"}

Output

:   none

1.  Let `jobQueue`{.variable} be null.

2.  Let `jobScope`{.variable} be `job`{.variable}'s [scope
    url](#dfn-job-scope-url){#ref-for-dfn-job-scope-url③
    link-type="dfn"},
    [serialized](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-serializer){#ref-for-concept-url-serializer⑦
    link-type="dfn"}.

3.  If [scope to job queue
    map](#dfn-scope-to-job-queue-map){#ref-for-dfn-scope-to-job-queue-map
    link-type="dfn"}\[`jobScope`{.variable}\] does not
    [exist](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists){#ref-for-map-exists⑥
    link-type="dfn"},
    [set](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-set){#ref-for-map-set②
    link-type="dfn"} [scope to job queue
    map](#dfn-scope-to-job-queue-map){#ref-for-dfn-scope-to-job-queue-map①
    link-type="dfn"}\[`jobScope`{.variable}\] to a new [job
    queue](#dfn-job-queue){#ref-for-dfn-job-queue④ link-type="dfn"}.

4.  Set `jobQueue`{.variable} to [scope to job queue
    map](#dfn-scope-to-job-queue-map){#ref-for-dfn-scope-to-job-queue-map②
    link-type="dfn"}\[`jobScope`{.variable}\].

5.  If `jobQueue`{.variable} is empty, then:

    1.  Set `job`{.variable}'s [containing job
        queue](#dfn-containing-job-queue){#ref-for-dfn-containing-job-queue
        link-type="dfn"} to `jobQueue`{.variable}, and
        [enqueue](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#queue-enqueue){#ref-for-queue-enqueue①
        link-type="dfn"} `job`{.variable} to `jobQueue`{.variable}.

    2.  Invoke [Run Job](#run-job){#ref-for-run-job link-type="dfn"}
        with `jobQueue`{.variable}.

6.  Else:

    1.  Let `lastJob`{.variable} be the element at the back of
        `jobQueue`{.variable}.

    2.  If `job`{.variable} is
        [equivalent](#dfn-job-equivalent){#ref-for-dfn-job-equivalent
        link-type="dfn"} to `lastJob`{.variable} and
        `lastJob`{.variable}'s [job
        promise](#dfn-job-promise){#ref-for-dfn-job-promise①
        link-type="dfn"} has not settled, append `job`{.variable} to
        `lastJob`{.variable}'s [list of equivalent
        jobs](#dfn-job-list-of-equivalent-jobs){#ref-for-dfn-job-list-of-equivalent-jobs
        link-type="dfn"}.

    3.  Else, set `job`{.variable}'s [containing job
        queue](#dfn-containing-job-queue){#ref-for-dfn-containing-job-queue①
        link-type="dfn"} to `jobQueue`{.variable}, and
        [enqueue](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#queue-enqueue){#ref-for-queue-enqueue②
        link-type="dfn"} `job`{.variable} to `jobQueue`{.variable}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="Run Job"}
### [[Run Job]{#run-job .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}]{.content}[](#run-job-algorithm){.self-link} {#run-job-algorithm .heading .settled}

Input

:   jobQueue, a [job queue](#dfn-job-queue){#ref-for-dfn-job-queue⑤
    link-type="dfn"}

Output

:   none

1.  Assert: `jobQueue`{.variable} [is not
    empty](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-is-empty){#ref-for-list-is-empty①
    link-type="dfn"}.

2.  [Queue a
    task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task){#ref-for-queue-a-task②②
    link-type="dfn"} to run these steps:

    1.  Let `job`{.variable} be the first
        [item](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-item){#ref-for-list-item⑧
        link-type="dfn"} in `jobQueue`{.variable}.

    2.  If `job`{.variable}'s [job
        type](#dfn-job-type){#ref-for-dfn-job-type③ link-type="dfn"} is
        *register*, run [Register](#register){#ref-for-register②
        link-type="dfn"} with `job`{.variable} [in
        parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel){#ref-for-in-parallel②⑦
        link-type="dfn"}.

    3.  Else if `job`{.variable}'s [job
        type](#dfn-job-type){#ref-for-dfn-job-type④ link-type="dfn"} is
        *update*, run [Update](#update){#ref-for-update link-type="dfn"}
        with `job`{.variable} [in
        parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel){#ref-for-in-parallel②⑧
        link-type="dfn"}.

        [Note:]{.marker} For a register job and an update job, the user
        agent delays queuing a task for running the job until after a
        [`DOMContentLoaded`{.idl}](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/indices.html#event-domcontentloaded){#ref-for-event-domcontentloaded
        link-type="idl"} event has been dispatched to the document that
        initiated the job.

    4.  Else if `job`{.variable}'s [job
        type](#dfn-job-type){#ref-for-dfn-job-type⑤ link-type="dfn"} is
        *unregister*, run [Unregister](#unregister){#ref-for-unregister
        link-type="dfn"} with `job`{.variable} [in
        parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel){#ref-for-in-parallel②⑨
        link-type="dfn"}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="Finish Job"}
### [[Finish Job]{#finish-job .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}]{.content}[](#finish-job-algorithm){.self-link} {#finish-job-algorithm .heading .settled}

Input

:   `job`{.variable}, a [job](#dfn-job){#ref-for-dfn-job②①
    link-type="dfn"}

Output

:   none

1.  Let `jobQueue`{.variable} be `job`{.variable}'s [containing job
    queue](#dfn-containing-job-queue){#ref-for-dfn-containing-job-queue②
    link-type="dfn"}.

2.  Assert: the first
    [item](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-item){#ref-for-list-item⑨
    link-type="dfn"} in `jobQueue`{.variable} is `job`{.variable}.

3.  [Dequeue](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#queue-dequeue){#ref-for-queue-dequeue
    link-type="dfn"} from `jobQueue`{.variable}.

4.  If `jobQueue`{.variable} [is not
    empty](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-is-empty){#ref-for-list-is-empty②
    link-type="dfn"}, invoke [Run Job](#run-job){#ref-for-run-job①
    link-type="dfn"} with `jobQueue`{.variable}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="Resolve Job Promise"}
### [[Resolve Job Promise]{#resolve-job-promise .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}]{.content}[](#resolve-job-promise-algorithm){.self-link} {#resolve-job-promise-algorithm .heading .settled}

Input

:   `job`{.variable}, a [job](#dfn-job){#ref-for-dfn-job②②
    link-type="dfn"}

:   `value`{.variable}, any

Output

:   none

1.  If `job`{.variable}'s
    [client](#dfn-job-client){#ref-for-dfn-job-client① link-type="dfn"}
    is not null, [queue a
    task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task){#ref-for-queue-a-task②③
    link-type="dfn"}, on `job`{.variable}'s
    [client](#dfn-job-client){#ref-for-dfn-job-client②
    link-type="dfn"}'s [responsible event
    loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop){#ref-for-responsible-event-loop①①
    link-type="dfn"} using the [DOM manipulation task
    source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source){#ref-for-dom-manipulation-task-source①⑥
    link-type="dfn"}, to run the following substeps:

    1.  Let `convertedValue`{.variable} be null.

    2.  If `job`{.variable}'s [job
        type](#dfn-job-type){#ref-for-dfn-job-type⑥ link-type="dfn"} is
        either *register* or *update*, set `convertedValue`{.variable}
        to the result of [getting the service worker registration
        object](#get-the-service-worker-registration-object){#ref-for-get-the-service-worker-registration-object④
        link-type="dfn"} that represents `value`{.variable} in
        `job`{.variable}'s
        [client](#dfn-job-client){#ref-for-dfn-job-client③
        link-type="dfn"}.

    3.  Else, set `convertedValue`{.variable} to `value`{.variable}, in
        `job`{.variable}'s
        [client](#dfn-job-client){#ref-for-dfn-job-client④
        link-type="dfn"}'s
        [Realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object's-realm){#ref-for-environment-settings-object's-realm⑤
        link-type="dfn"}.

    4.  Resolve `job`{.variable}'s [job
        promise](#dfn-job-promise){#ref-for-dfn-job-promise②
        link-type="dfn"} with `convertedValue`{.variable}.

2.  For each `equivalentJob`{.variable} in `job`{.variable}'s [list of
    equivalent
    jobs](#dfn-job-list-of-equivalent-jobs){#ref-for-dfn-job-list-of-equivalent-jobs①
    link-type="dfn"}:

    1.  If `equivalentJob`{.variable}'s
        [client](#dfn-job-client){#ref-for-dfn-job-client⑤
        link-type="dfn"} is null, continue to the next iteration of the
        loop.

    2.  [Queue a
        task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task){#ref-for-queue-a-task②④
        link-type="dfn"}, on `equivalentJob`{.variable}'s
        [client](#dfn-job-client){#ref-for-dfn-job-client⑥
        link-type="dfn"}'s [responsible event
        loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop){#ref-for-responsible-event-loop①②
        link-type="dfn"} using the [DOM manipulation task
        source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source){#ref-for-dom-manipulation-task-source①⑦
        link-type="dfn"}, to run the following substeps:

        1.  Let `convertedValue`{.variable} be null.

        2.  If `equivalentJob`{.variable}'s [job
            type](#dfn-job-type){#ref-for-dfn-job-type⑦ link-type="dfn"}
            is either *register* or *update*, set
            `convertedValue`{.variable} to the result of [getting the
            service worker registration
            object](#get-the-service-worker-registration-object){#ref-for-get-the-service-worker-registration-object⑤
            link-type="dfn"} that represents `value`{.variable} in
            `equivalentJob`{.variable}'s
            [client](#dfn-job-client){#ref-for-dfn-job-client⑦
            link-type="dfn"}.

        3.  Else, set `convertedValue`{.variable} to `value`{.variable},
            in `equivalentJob`{.variable}'s
            [client](#dfn-job-client){#ref-for-dfn-job-client⑧
            link-type="dfn"}'s
            [Realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object's-realm){#ref-for-environment-settings-object's-realm⑥
            link-type="dfn"}.

        4.  Resolve `equivalentJob`{.variable}'s [job
            promise](#dfn-job-promise){#ref-for-dfn-job-promise③
            link-type="dfn"} with `convertedValue`{.variable}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="Reject Job Promise"}
### [[Reject Job Promise]{#reject-job-promise .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}]{.content}[](#reject-job-promise-algorithm){.self-link} {#reject-job-promise-algorithm .heading .settled}

Input

:   `job`{.variable}, a [job](#dfn-job){#ref-for-dfn-job②③
    link-type="dfn"}

:   `errorData`{.variable}, the information necessary to [create an
    exception](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-create-exception){#ref-for-dfn-create-exception③
    link-type="dfn"}

Output

:   none

1.  If `job`{.variable}'s
    [client](#dfn-job-client){#ref-for-dfn-job-client⑨ link-type="dfn"}
    is not null, [queue a
    task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task){#ref-for-queue-a-task②⑤
    link-type="dfn"}, on `job`{.variable}'s
    [client](#dfn-job-client){#ref-for-dfn-job-client①⓪
    link-type="dfn"}'s [responsible event
    loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop){#ref-for-responsible-event-loop①③
    link-type="dfn"} using the [DOM manipulation task
