    source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source){#ref-for-dom-manipulation-task-source①⑧
    link-type="dfn"}, to reject `job`{.variable}'s [job
    promise](#dfn-job-promise){#ref-for-dfn-job-promise④
    link-type="dfn"} with a
    [new](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-create-exception){#ref-for-dfn-create-exception④
    link-type="dfn"}
    [exception](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-exception){#ref-for-dfn-exception③
    link-type="dfn"} with `errorData`{.variable}, in `job`{.variable}'s
    [client](#dfn-job-client){#ref-for-dfn-job-client①①
    link-type="dfn"}'s
    [Realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object's-realm){#ref-for-environment-settings-object's-realm⑦
    link-type="dfn"}.

2.  For each `equivalentJob`{.variable} in `job`{.variable}'s [list of
    equivalent
    jobs](#dfn-job-list-of-equivalent-jobs){#ref-for-dfn-job-list-of-equivalent-jobs②
    link-type="dfn"}:

    1.  If `equivalentJob`{.variable}'s
        [client](#dfn-job-client){#ref-for-dfn-job-client①②
        link-type="dfn"} is null,
        [continue](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-continue){#ref-for-iteration-continue⑦
        link-type="dfn"}.

    2.  [Queue a
        task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task){#ref-for-queue-a-task②⑥
        link-type="dfn"}, on `equivalentJob`{.variable}'s
        [client](#dfn-job-client){#ref-for-dfn-job-client①③
        link-type="dfn"}'s [responsible event
        loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop){#ref-for-responsible-event-loop①④
        link-type="dfn"} using the [DOM manipulation task
        source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source){#ref-for-dom-manipulation-task-source①⑨
        link-type="dfn"}, to reject `equivalentJob`{.variable}'s [job
        promise](#dfn-job-promise){#ref-for-dfn-job-promise⑤
        link-type="dfn"} with a
        [new](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-create-exception){#ref-for-dfn-create-exception⑤
        link-type="dfn"}
        [exception](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-exception){#ref-for-dfn-exception④
        link-type="dfn"} with `errorData`{.variable}, in
        `equivalentJob`{.variable}'s
        [client](#dfn-job-client){#ref-for-dfn-job-client①④
        link-type="dfn"}'s
        [Realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object's-realm){#ref-for-environment-settings-object's-realm⑧
        link-type="dfn"}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="Start Register"}
### [[Start Register]{#start-register .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}]{.content}[](#start-register-algorithm){.self-link} {#start-register-algorithm .heading .settled}

Input

:   `scopeURL`{.variable}, a
    [URL](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url){#ref-for-concept-url⑨
    link-type="dfn"} or failure or null

:   `scriptURL`{.variable}, a
    [URL](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url){#ref-for-concept-url①⓪
    link-type="dfn"} or failure

:   `promise`{.variable}, a
    [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects){#ref-for-sec-promise-objects③⓪
    link-type="dfn"}

:   `client`{.variable}, a [service worker
    client](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client③⑧
    link-type="dfn"}

:   `referrer`{.variable}, a
    [URL](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url){#ref-for-concept-url①①
    link-type="dfn"}

:   `workerType`{.variable}, a [worker
    type](#dfn-job-worker-type){#ref-for-dfn-job-worker-type②
    link-type="dfn"}

:   `updateViaCache`{.variable}, an [update via cache
    mode](#dfn-update-via-cache){#ref-for-dfn-update-via-cache②
    link-type="dfn"}

Output

:   none

1.  If `scriptURL`{.variable} is failure, reject `promise`{.variable}
    with a `TypeError` and abort these steps.

2.  Set `scriptURL`{.variable}'s
    [fragment](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-fragment){#ref-for-concept-url-fragment①
    link-type="dfn"} to null.

    [Note:]{.marker} The user agent does not store the
    [fragment](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-fragment){#ref-for-concept-url-fragment②
    link-type="dfn"} of the script's url. This means that the
    [fragment](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-fragment){#ref-for-concept-url-fragment③
    link-type="dfn"} does not have an effect on identifying [service
    workers](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑨⑤
    link-type="dfn"}.

3.  If `scriptURL`{.variable}'s
    [scheme](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-scheme){#ref-for-concept-url-scheme③
    link-type="dfn"} is not one of \"`http`\" and \"`https`\", reject
    `promise`{.variable} with a `TypeError` and abort these steps.

4.  If any of the strings in `scriptURL`{.variable}'s
    [path](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-path){#ref-for-concept-url-path①
    link-type="dfn"} contains either [ASCII
    case-insensitive](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-case-insensitive){#ref-for-ascii-case-insensitive
    link-type="dfn"} \"`%2f`\" or [ASCII
    case-insensitive](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-case-insensitive){#ref-for-ascii-case-insensitive①
    link-type="dfn"} \"`%5c`\", reject `promise`{.variable} with a
    `TypeError` and abort these steps.

5.  If `scopeURL`{.variable} is null, set `scopeURL`{.variable} to the
    result of
    [parsing](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-parser){#ref-for-concept-url-parser⑤
    link-type="dfn"} the string \"`./`\" with `scriptURL`{.variable}.

    [Note:]{.marker} The scope url for the registration is set to the
    location of the service worker script by default.

6.  If `scopeURL`{.variable} is failure, reject `promise`{.variable}
    with a `TypeError` and abort these steps.

7.  Set `scopeURL`{.variable}'s
    [fragment](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-fragment){#ref-for-concept-url-fragment④
    link-type="dfn"} to null.

    [Note:]{.marker} The user agent does not store the
    [fragment](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-fragment){#ref-for-concept-url-fragment⑤
    link-type="dfn"} of the scope url. This means that the
    [fragment](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-fragment){#ref-for-concept-url-fragment⑥
    link-type="dfn"} does not have an effect on identifying [service
    worker
    registrations](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration⑤②
    link-type="dfn"}.

8.  If `scopeURL`{.variable}'s
    [scheme](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-scheme){#ref-for-concept-url-scheme④
    link-type="dfn"} is not one of \"`http`\" and \"`https`\", reject
    `promise`{.variable} with a `TypeError` and abort these steps.

9.  If any of the strings in `scopeURL`{.variable}'s
    [path](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-path){#ref-for-concept-url-path②
    link-type="dfn"} contains either [ASCII
    case-insensitive](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-case-insensitive){#ref-for-ascii-case-insensitive②
    link-type="dfn"} \"`%2f`\" or [ASCII
    case-insensitive](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-case-insensitive){#ref-for-ascii-case-insensitive③
    link-type="dfn"} \"`%5c`\", reject `promise`{.variable} with a
    `TypeError` and abort these steps.

10. Let `storage key`{.variable} be the result of running [obtain a
    storage
    key](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#obtain-a-storage-key){#ref-for-obtain-a-storage-key⑧
    link-type="dfn"} given `client`{.variable}.

11. Let `job`{.variable} be the result of running [Create
    Job](#create-job){#ref-for-create-job② link-type="dfn"} with
    *register*, `storage key`{.variable}, `scopeURL`{.variable},
    `scriptURL`{.variable}, `promise`{.variable}, and
    `client`{.variable}.

12. Set `job`{.variable}'s [worker
    type](#dfn-job-worker-type){#ref-for-dfn-job-worker-type③
    link-type="dfn"} to `workerType`{.variable}.

13. Set `job`{.variable}'s [update via cache
    mode](#dfn-job-update-via-cache-mode){#ref-for-dfn-job-update-via-cache-mode①
    link-type="dfn"} to `updateViaCache`{.variable}.

14. Set `job`{.variable}'s
    [referrer](#job-referrer){#ref-for-job-referrer① link-type="dfn"} to
    `referrer`{.variable}.

15. Invoke [Schedule Job](#schedule-job){#ref-for-schedule-job②
    link-type="dfn"} with `job`{.variable}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="Register"}
### [[Register]{#register .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}]{.content}[](#register-algorithm){.self-link} {#register-algorithm .heading .settled}

Input

:   `job`{.variable}, a [job](#dfn-job){#ref-for-dfn-job②④
    link-type="dfn"}

Output

:   none

1.  If the result of running [potentially trustworthy
    origin](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-secure-contexts/#potentially-trustworthy-origin){#ref-for-potentially-trustworthy-origin
    link-type="dfn"} with the
    [origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-origin){#ref-for-concept-settings-object-origin①②
    link-type="dfn"} of `job`{.variable}'s [script
    url](#dfn-job-script-url){#ref-for-dfn-job-script-url②
    link-type="dfn"} as the argument is `Not Trusted`, then:

    1.  Invoke [Reject Job
        Promise](#reject-job-promise){#ref-for-reject-job-promise
        link-type="dfn"} with `job`{.variable} and
        \"[`SecurityError`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#securityerror){#ref-for-securityerror①
        link-type="idl"}\"
        [`DOMException`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException){#ref-for-idl-DOMException①④
        link-type="idl"}.

    2.  Invoke [Finish Job](#finish-job){#ref-for-finish-job
        link-type="dfn"} with `job`{.variable} and abort these steps.

2.  If `job`{.variable}'s [script
    url](#dfn-job-script-url){#ref-for-dfn-job-script-url③
    link-type="dfn"}'s
    [origin](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-origin){#ref-for-concept-url-origin②
    link-type="dfn"} and `job`{.variable}'s
    [referrer](#job-referrer){#ref-for-job-referrer② link-type="dfn"}'s
    [origin](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-origin){#ref-for-concept-url-origin③
    link-type="dfn"} are not [same
    origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#same-origin){#ref-for-same-origin④
    link-type="dfn"}, then:

    1.  Invoke [Reject Job
        Promise](#reject-job-promise){#ref-for-reject-job-promise①
        link-type="dfn"} with `job`{.variable} and
        \"[`SecurityError`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#securityerror){#ref-for-securityerror②
        link-type="idl"}\"
        [`DOMException`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException){#ref-for-idl-DOMException①⑤
        link-type="idl"}.

    2.  Invoke [Finish Job](#finish-job){#ref-for-finish-job①
        link-type="dfn"} with `job`{.variable} and abort these steps.

3.  If `job`{.variable}'s [scope
    url](#dfn-job-scope-url){#ref-for-dfn-job-scope-url④
    link-type="dfn"}'s
    [origin](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-origin){#ref-for-concept-url-origin④
    link-type="dfn"} and `job`{.variable}'s
    [referrer](#job-referrer){#ref-for-job-referrer③ link-type="dfn"}'s
    [origin](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-origin){#ref-for-concept-url-origin⑤
    link-type="dfn"} are not [same
    origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#same-origin){#ref-for-same-origin⑤
    link-type="dfn"}, then:

    1.  Invoke [Reject Job
        Promise](#reject-job-promise){#ref-for-reject-job-promise②
        link-type="dfn"} with `job`{.variable} and
        \"[`SecurityError`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#securityerror){#ref-for-securityerror③
        link-type="idl"}\"
        [`DOMException`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException){#ref-for-idl-DOMException①⑥
        link-type="idl"}.

    2.  Invoke [Finish Job](#finish-job){#ref-for-finish-job②
        link-type="dfn"} with `job`{.variable} and abort these steps.

4.  Let `registration`{.variable} be the result of running [Get
    Registration](#get-registration){#ref-for-get-registration
    link-type="dfn"} given `job`{.variable}'s [storage
    key](#job-storage-key){#ref-for-job-storage-key① link-type="dfn"}
    and `job`{.variable}'s [scope
    url](#dfn-job-scope-url){#ref-for-dfn-job-scope-url⑤
    link-type="dfn"}.

5.  If `registration`{.variable} is not null, then:

    1.  Let `newestWorker`{.variable} be the result of running the [Get
        Newest Worker](#get-newest-worker){#ref-for-get-newest-worker①
        link-type="dfn"} algorithm passing `registration`{.variable} as
        the argument.

    2.  If `newestWorker`{.variable} is not null, `job`{.variable}'s
        [script url](#dfn-job-script-url){#ref-for-dfn-job-script-url④
        link-type="dfn"}
        [equals](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-equals){#ref-for-concept-url-equals
        link-type="dfn"} `newestWorker`{.variable}'s [script
        url](#dfn-script-url){#ref-for-dfn-script-url② link-type="dfn"},
        `job`{.variable}'s [worker
        type](#dfn-job-worker-type){#ref-for-dfn-job-worker-type④
        link-type="dfn"} equals `newestWorker`{.variable}'s
        [type](#dfn-type){#ref-for-dfn-type① link-type="dfn"}, and
        `job`{.variable}'s [update via cache
        mode](#dfn-job-update-via-cache-mode){#ref-for-dfn-job-update-via-cache-mode②
        link-type="dfn"}'s value equals `registration`{.variable}'s
        [update via cache
        mode](#dfn-update-via-cache){#ref-for-dfn-update-via-cache③
        link-type="dfn"}, then:

        1.  Invoke [Resolve Job
            Promise](#resolve-job-promise){#ref-for-resolve-job-promise
            link-type="dfn"} with `job`{.variable} and
            `registration`{.variable}.

        2.  Invoke [Finish Job](#finish-job){#ref-for-finish-job③
            link-type="dfn"} with `job`{.variable} and abort these
            steps.

6.  Else:

    1.  Invoke [Set
        Registration](#set-registration){#ref-for-set-registration
        link-type="dfn"} algorithm with `job`{.variable}'s [storage
        key](#job-storage-key){#ref-for-job-storage-key②
        link-type="dfn"}, `job`{.variable}'s [scope
        url](#dfn-job-scope-url){#ref-for-dfn-job-scope-url⑥
        link-type="dfn"}, and `job`{.variable}'s [update via cache
        mode](#dfn-job-update-via-cache-mode){#ref-for-dfn-job-update-via-cache-mode③
        link-type="dfn"}.

7.  Invoke [Update](#update){#ref-for-update① link-type="dfn"} algorithm
    passing `job`{.variable} as the argument.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="Update"}
### [[Update]{#update .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}]{.content}[](#update-algorithm){.self-link} {#update-algorithm .heading .settled}

Input

:   `job`{.variable}, a [job](#dfn-job){#ref-for-dfn-job②⑤
    link-type="dfn"}

Output

:   none

1.  Let `registration`{.variable} be the result of running [Get
    Registration](#get-registration){#ref-for-get-registration①
    link-type="dfn"} given `job`{.variable}'s [storage
    key](#job-storage-key){#ref-for-job-storage-key③ link-type="dfn"}
    and `job`{.variable}'s [scope
    url](#dfn-job-scope-url){#ref-for-dfn-job-scope-url⑦
    link-type="dfn"}.

2.  If `registration`{.variable} is null, then:

    1.  Invoke [Reject Job
        Promise](#reject-job-promise){#ref-for-reject-job-promise③
        link-type="dfn"} with `job`{.variable} and `TypeError`.

    2.  Invoke [Finish Job](#finish-job){#ref-for-finish-job④
        link-type="dfn"} with `job`{.variable} and abort these steps.

3.  Let `newestWorker`{.variable} be the result of running [Get Newest
    Worker](#get-newest-worker){#ref-for-get-newest-worker②
    link-type="dfn"} algorithm passing `registration`{.variable} as the
    argument.

4.  If `job`{.variable}'s [job
    type](#dfn-job-type){#ref-for-dfn-job-type⑧ link-type="dfn"} is
    *update*, and `newestWorker`{.variable} is not null and its [script
    url](#dfn-script-url){#ref-for-dfn-script-url③ link-type="dfn"} does
    not
    [equal](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-equals){#ref-for-concept-url-equals①
    link-type="dfn"} `job`{.variable}'s [script
    url](#dfn-job-script-url){#ref-for-dfn-job-script-url⑤
    link-type="dfn"}, then:

    1.  Invoke [Reject Job
        Promise](#reject-job-promise){#ref-for-reject-job-promise④
        link-type="dfn"} with `job`{.variable} and `TypeError`.

    2.  Invoke [Finish Job](#finish-job){#ref-for-finish-job⑤
        link-type="dfn"} with `job`{.variable} and abort these steps.

5.  Let `hasUpdatedResources`{.variable} be false.

6.  Let `updatedResourceMap`{.variable} be an [ordered
    map](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ordered-map){#ref-for-ordered-map⑦
    link-type="dfn"} where the
    [keys](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-getting-the-keys){#ref-for-map-getting-the-keys④
    link-type="dfn"} are
    [URLs](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url){#ref-for-concept-url①②
    link-type="dfn"} and the
    [values](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-getting-the-values){#ref-for-map-getting-the-values③
    link-type="dfn"} are
    [responses](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response){#ref-for-concept-response⑨
    link-type="dfn"}.

7.  Switching on `job`{.variable}'s [worker
    type](#dfn-job-worker-type){#ref-for-dfn-job-worker-type⑤
    link-type="dfn"}, run these substeps with the following options:

    \"`classic`\"

    :   [Fetch a classic worker
        script](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#fetch-a-classic-worker-script){#ref-for-fetch-a-classic-worker-script
        link-type="dfn"} given `job`{.variable}'s
        [serialized](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-serializer){#ref-for-concept-url-serializer⑧
        link-type="dfn"} [script
        url](#dfn-job-script-url){#ref-for-dfn-job-script-url⑥
        link-type="dfn"}, `job`{.variable}'s
        [client](#dfn-job-client){#ref-for-dfn-job-client①⑤
        link-type="dfn"}, \"`serviceworker`\", and the to-be-created
        [environment settings
        object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object){#ref-for-environment-settings-object①③
        link-type="dfn"} for this service worker.

    \"`module`\"

    :   [Fetch a module worker script
        graph](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#fetch-a-module-worker-script-tree){#ref-for-fetch-a-module-worker-script-tree
        link-type="dfn"} given `job`{.variable}'s
        [serialized](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-serializer){#ref-for-concept-url-serializer⑨
        link-type="dfn"} [script
        url](#dfn-job-script-url){#ref-for-dfn-job-script-url⑦
        link-type="dfn"}, `job`{.variable}'s
        [client](#dfn-job-client){#ref-for-dfn-job-client①⑥
        link-type="dfn"}, \"`serviceworker`\", \"`omit`\", and the
        to-be-created [environment settings
        object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object){#ref-for-environment-settings-object①④
        link-type="dfn"} for this service worker.

    [](#issue-02e2fe82){.self-link} Using the to-be-created [environment
    settings
    object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object){#ref-for-environment-settings-object①⑤
    link-type="dfn"} rather than a concrete [environment settings
    object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object){#ref-for-environment-settings-object①⑥
    link-type="dfn"}. This is used due to the unique processing model of
    service workers compared to the processing model of other [web
    workers](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/workers.html#workers){#ref-for-workers①
    link-type="dfn"}. The script fetching algorithms of HTML standard
    originally designed for other [web
    workers](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/workers.html#workers){#ref-for-workers②
    link-type="dfn"} require an [environment settings
    object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object){#ref-for-environment-settings-object①⑦
    link-type="dfn"} of the execution environment, but service workers
    fetch a script separately in the [Update](#update){#ref-for-update②
    link-type="dfn"} algorithm before the script later runs multiple
    times through the [Run Service
    Worker](#run-service-worker){#ref-for-run-service-worker②
    link-type="dfn"} algorithm.

    [](#issue-9d9e65b5){.self-link} The [fetch a classic worker
    script](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#fetch-a-classic-worker-script){#ref-for-fetch-a-classic-worker-script①
    link-type="dfn"} algorithm and the [fetch a module worker script
    graph](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#fetch-a-module-worker-script-tree){#ref-for-fetch-a-module-worker-script-tree①
    link-type="dfn"} algorithm in HTML take `job`{.variable}'s
    [client](#dfn-job-client){#ref-for-dfn-job-client①⑦ link-type="dfn"}
    as an argument. `job`{.variable}'s
    [client](#dfn-job-client){#ref-for-dfn-job-client①⑧ link-type="dfn"}
    is null when passed from the [Soft
    Update](#soft-update){#ref-for-soft-update link-type="dfn"}
    algorithm.

    To [perform the fetch
    hook](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#fetching-scripts-perform-fetch){#ref-for-fetching-scripts-perform-fetch①
    link-type="dfn"} given `request`{.variable}, run the following
    steps:

    1.  Append \``Service-Worker`\`/\``script`\` to
        `request`{.variable}'s [header
        list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-header-list){#ref-for-concept-request-header-list
        link-type="dfn"}.

        [Note:]{.marker} See the definition of the Service-Worker header
        in Appendix B: Extended HTTP headers.

    2.  Set `request`{.variable}'s [cache
        mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-cache-mode){#ref-for-concept-request-cache-mode①
        link-type="dfn"} to \"`no-cache`\" if any of the following are
        true:

        - `registration`{.variable}'s [update via cache
          mode](#dfn-update-via-cache){#ref-for-dfn-update-via-cache④
          link-type="dfn"} is not \"`all`\".

        - `job`{.variable}'s [force bypass cache
          flag](#dfn-job-force-bypass-cache-flag){#ref-for-dfn-job-force-bypass-cache-flag
          link-type="dfn"} is set.

        - `newestWorker`{.variable} is not null and
          `registration`{.variable} is
          [stale](#service-worker-registration-stale){#ref-for-service-worker-registration-stale①
          link-type="dfn"}.

        [Note:]{.marker} Even if the cache mode is not set to
        \"`no-cache`\", the user agent obeys Cache-Control header's
        max-age value in the network layer to determine if it should
        bypass the browser cache.

    3.  Set `request`{.variable}'s [service-workers
        mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-service-workers-mode){#ref-for-request-service-workers-mode②
        link-type="dfn"} to \"`none`\".

    4.  If the
        [isTopLevel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#fetching-scripts-is-top-level){#ref-for-fetching-scripts-is-top-level
        link-type="dfn"} flag is unset, then return the result of
        [fetching](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch){#ref-for-concept-fetch①⓪
        link-type="dfn"} `request`{.variable}.

    5.  Set `request`{.variable}'s [redirect
        mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-redirect-mode){#ref-for-concept-request-redirect-mode
        link-type="dfn"} to \"`error`\".

    6.  [Fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch){#ref-for-concept-fetch①①
        link-type="dfn"} `request`{.variable}, and asynchronously wait
        to run the remaining steps as part of fetch's
        [processResponse](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#process-response){#ref-for-process-response①
        link-type="dfn"} for the
        [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response){#ref-for-concept-response①⓪
        link-type="dfn"} `response`{.variable}.

    7.  [Extract a MIME
        type](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-extract-mime-type){#ref-for-concept-header-extract-mime-type①
        link-type="dfn"} from the `response`{.variable}'s [header
        list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-header-list){#ref-for-concept-response-header-list③
        link-type="dfn"}. If this MIME type (ignoring parameters) is not
        a [JavaScript MIME
        type](https://mimesniff.spec.whatwg.org/#javascript-mime-type){#ref-for-javascript-mime-type②
        link-type="dfn"}, then:

        1.  Invoke [Reject Job
            Promise](#reject-job-promise){#ref-for-reject-job-promise⑤
            link-type="dfn"} with `job`{.variable} and
            \"[`SecurityError`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#securityerror){#ref-for-securityerror④
            link-type="idl"}\"
            [`DOMException`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException){#ref-for-idl-DOMException①⑦
            link-type="idl"}.

        2.  Asynchronously complete these steps with a [network
            error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error){#ref-for-concept-network-error④
            link-type="dfn"}.

    8.  Let `serviceWorkerAllowed`{.variable} be the result of
        [extracting header list
        values](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#extract-header-list-values){#ref-for-extract-header-list-values
        link-type="dfn"} given \``Service-Worker-Allowed`\` and
        `response`{.variable}'s [header
        list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-header-list){#ref-for-concept-response-header-list④
        link-type="dfn"}.

        [Note:]{.marker} See the definition of the
        [Service-Worker-Allowed](#service-worker-allowed){#ref-for-service-worker-allowed①
        link-type="dfn"} header in Appendix B: Extended HTTP headers.

    9.  Set `policyContainer`{.variable} to the result of [creating a
        policy container from a fetch
        response](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#creating-a-policy-container-from-a-fetch-response){#ref-for-creating-a-policy-container-from-a-fetch-response
        link-type="dfn"} given `response`{.variable}.

    10. If `serviceWorkerAllowed`{.variable} is failure, then:

        1.  Asynchronously complete these steps with a [network
            error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error){#ref-for-concept-network-error⑤
            link-type="dfn"}.

    11. Let `scopeURL`{.variable} be `registration`{.variable}'s [scope
        url](#dfn-scope-url){#ref-for-dfn-scope-url①③ link-type="dfn"}.

    12. Let `maxScopeString`{.variable} be null.

    13. If `serviceWorkerAllowed`{.variable} is null, then:

        1.  Let `resolvedScope`{.variable} be the result of
            [parsing](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-parser){#ref-for-concept-url-parser⑥
            link-type="dfn"} \"`./`\" using `job`{.variable}'s [script
            url](#dfn-job-script-url){#ref-for-dfn-job-script-url⑧
            link-type="dfn"} as the [base
            URL](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-script-base-url){#ref-for-concept-script-base-url
            link-type="dfn"}.

        2.  Set `maxScopeString`{.variable} to \"`/`\", followed by the
            strings in `resolvedScope`{.variable}'s
            [path](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-path){#ref-for-concept-url-path③
            link-type="dfn"} (including empty strings), separated from
            each other by \"`/`\".

            [Note:]{.marker} The final item in
            `resolvedScope`{.variable}'s
            [path](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-path){#ref-for-concept-url-path④
            link-type="dfn"} will always be an empty string, so
            `maxScopeString`{.variable} will have a trailing \"`/`\".

    14. Else:

        1.  Let `maxScope`{.variable} be the result of
            [parsing](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-parser){#ref-for-concept-url-parser⑦
            link-type="dfn"} `serviceWorkerAllowed`{.variable} using
            `job`{.variable}'s [script
            url](#dfn-job-script-url){#ref-for-dfn-job-script-url⑨
            link-type="dfn"} as the [base
            URL](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-script-base-url){#ref-for-concept-script-base-url①
            link-type="dfn"}.

        2.  If `maxScope`{.variable}'s
            [origin](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-origin){#ref-for-concept-url-origin⑥
            link-type="dfn"} is `job`{.variable}'s [script
            url](#dfn-job-script-url){#ref-for-dfn-job-script-url①⓪
            link-type="dfn"}'s
            [origin](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-origin){#ref-for-concept-url-origin⑦
            link-type="dfn"}, then:

            1.  Set `maxScopeString`{.variable} to \"`/`\", followed by
                the strings in `maxScope`{.variable}'s
                [path](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-path){#ref-for-concept-url-path⑤
                link-type="dfn"} (including empty strings), separated
                from each other by \"`/`\".

    15. Let `scopeString`{.variable} be \"`/`\", followed by the strings
        in `scopeURL`{.variable}'s
        [path](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-path){#ref-for-concept-url-path⑥
        link-type="dfn"} (including empty strings), separated from each
        other by \"`/`\".

    16. If `maxScopeString`{.variable} is null or
        `scopeString`{.variable} does not start with
        `maxScopeString`{.variable}, then:

        1.  Invoke [Reject Job
            Promise](#reject-job-promise){#ref-for-reject-job-promise⑥
            link-type="dfn"} with `job`{.variable} and
            \"[`SecurityError`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#securityerror){#ref-for-securityerror⑤
            link-type="idl"}\"
            [`DOMException`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException){#ref-for-idl-DOMException①⑧
            link-type="idl"}.

        2.  Asynchronously complete these steps with a [network
            error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error){#ref-for-concept-network-error⑥
            link-type="dfn"}.

    17. Let `url`{.variable} be `request`{.variable}'s
        [url](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-url){#ref-for-concept-request-url⑤
        link-type="dfn"}.

    18. Set `updatedResourceMap`{.variable}\[`url`{.variable}\] to
        `response`{.variable}.

    19. If `response`{.variable}'s [cache
        state](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-cache-state){#ref-for-concept-response-cache-state①
        link-type="dfn"} is not \"`local`\", set
        `registration`{.variable}'s [last update check
        time](#dfn-last-update-check-time){#ref-for-dfn-last-update-check-time③
        link-type="dfn"} to the current time.

    20. Set `hasUpdatedResources`{.variable} to true if any of the
        following are true:

        - `newestWorker`{.variable} is null.

        - `newestWorker`{.variable}'s [script
          url](#dfn-script-url){#ref-for-dfn-script-url④
          link-type="dfn"} is not `url`{.variable} or
          `newestWorker`{.variable}'s
          [type](#dfn-type){#ref-for-dfn-type② link-type="dfn"} is not
          `job`{.variable}'s [worker
          type](#dfn-job-worker-type){#ref-for-dfn-job-worker-type⑥
          link-type="dfn"}.

        - `newestWorker`{.variable}'s [script resource
          map](#dfn-script-resource-map){#ref-for-dfn-script-resource-map②
          link-type="dfn"}\[`url`{.variable}\]\'s
          [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body){#ref-for-concept-response-body⑨
          link-type="dfn"} is not byte-for-byte identical with
          `response`{.variable}'s
          [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body){#ref-for-concept-response-body①⓪
          link-type="dfn"}.

    21. If `hasUpdatedResources`{.variable} is false and
        `newestWorker`{.variable}'s [classic scripts imported
        flag](#dfn-classic-scripts-imported-flag){#ref-for-dfn-classic-scripts-imported-flag①
        link-type="dfn"} is set, then:

        [Note:]{.marker} The following checks to see if an imported
        script has been updated, since the main script has not changed.

        1.  [For
            each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-iterate){#ref-for-map-iterate④
            link-type="dfn"} `importUrl`{.variable} →
            `storedResponse`{.variable} of `newestWorker`{.variable}'s
            [script resource
            map](#dfn-script-resource-map){#ref-for-dfn-script-resource-map③
            link-type="dfn"}:

            1.  If `importUrl`{.variable} is `url`{.variable}, then
                continue.

            2.  Let `importRequest`{.variable} be a new
                [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request){#ref-for-concept-request⑥
                link-type="dfn"} whose
                [url](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-url){#ref-for-concept-request-url⑥
                link-type="dfn"} is `importUrl`{.variable},
                [client](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-client){#ref-for-concept-request-client②
                link-type="dfn"} is `job`{.variable}'s
                [client](#dfn-job-client){#ref-for-dfn-job-client①⑨
                link-type="dfn"},
                [destination](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-destination){#ref-for-concept-request-destination①
                link-type="dfn"} is \"`script`\", [parser
                metadata](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-parser-metadata){#ref-for-concept-request-parser-metadata
                link-type="dfn"} is \"`not parser-inserted`\", and whose
                [use-URL-credentials
                flag](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-use-url-credentials-flag){#ref-for-concept-request-use-url-credentials-flag
                link-type="dfn"} is set.

            3.  Set `importRequest`{.variable}'s [cache
                mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-cache-mode){#ref-for-concept-request-cache-mode②
                link-type="dfn"} to \"`no-cache`\" if any of the
                following are true:

                - `registration`{.variable}'s [update via cache
                  mode](#dfn-update-via-cache){#ref-for-dfn-update-via-cache⑤
                  link-type="dfn"} is \"`none`\".

                - `job`{.variable}'s [force bypass cache
                  flag](#dfn-job-force-bypass-cache-flag){#ref-for-dfn-job-force-bypass-cache-flag①
                  link-type="dfn"} is set.

                - `registration`{.variable} is
                  [stale](#service-worker-registration-stale){#ref-for-service-worker-registration-stale②
                  link-type="dfn"}.

            4.  Let `fetchedResponse`{.variable} be the result of
                [fetching](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch){#ref-for-concept-fetch①②
                link-type="dfn"} `importRequest`{.variable}.

            5.  Set
                `updatedResourceMap`{.variable}\[`importRequest`{.variable}'s
                [url](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-url){#ref-for-concept-request-url⑦
                link-type="dfn"}\] to `fetchedResponse`{.variable}.

            6.  Set `fetchedResponse`{.variable} to
                `fetchedResponse`{.variable}'s [unsafe
                response](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/urls-and-fetching.html#unsafe-response){#ref-for-unsafe-response①
                link-type="dfn"}.

            7.  If `fetchedResponse`{.variable}'s [cache
                state](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-cache-state){#ref-for-concept-response-cache-state②
                link-type="dfn"} is not \"`local`\", set
                `registration`{.variable}'s [last update check
                time](#dfn-last-update-check-time){#ref-for-dfn-last-update-check-time④
                link-type="dfn"} to the current time.

            8.  If `fetchedResponse`{.variable} is a [bad import script
                response](#dfn-bad-import-script-response){#ref-for-dfn-bad-import-script-response①
                link-type="dfn"}, continue.

                [Note:]{.marker} Bad responses for
                [importScripts()](#importscripts-method){#ref-for-importscripts-method②
                link-type="functionish"} are ignored for the purpose of
                the byte-to-byte check. Only good responses for the
                incumbent worker and good responses for the potential
                update worker are considered. See [issue
                #1374](https://github.com/w3c/ServiceWorker/issues/1374)
                for some rationale.

            9.  If `fetchedResponse`{.variable}'s
                [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body){#ref-for-concept-response-body①①
                link-type="dfn"} is not byte-for-byte identical with
                `storedResponse`{.variable}'s [unsafe
                response](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/urls-and-fetching.html#unsafe-response){#ref-for-unsafe-response②
                link-type="dfn"}'s
                [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body){#ref-for-concept-response-body①②
                link-type="dfn"}, set `hasUpdatedResources`{.variable}
                to true.

                [Note:]{.marker} The control does not break the loop in
                this step to continue with all the imported scripts to
                populate the cache.

    22. Asynchronously complete these steps with `response`{.variable}.

    When the algorithm asynchronously completes, continue the rest of
    these steps, with `script`{.variable} being the asynchronous
    completion value.

8.  If `script`{.variable} is null or [Is Async
    Module](#is-async-module){#ref-for-is-async-module link-type="dfn"}
    with `script`{.variable}'s
    [record](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-script-record){#ref-for-concept-script-record
    link-type="dfn"}, `script`{.variable}'s [base
    URL](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-script-base-url){#ref-for-concept-script-base-url②
    link-type="dfn"}, and « » is true, then:

    1.  Invoke [Reject Job
        Promise](#reject-job-promise){#ref-for-reject-job-promise⑦
        link-type="dfn"} with `job`{.variable} and `TypeError`.

        [Note:]{.marker} This will do nothing if [Reject Job
        Promise](#reject-job-promise){#ref-for-reject-job-promise⑧
        link-type="dfn"} was previously invoked with
        \"[`SecurityError`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#securityerror){#ref-for-securityerror⑥
        link-type="idl"}\"
        [`DOMException`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException){#ref-for-idl-DOMException①⑨
        link-type="idl"}.

    2.  If `newestWorker`{.variable} is null, then
        [remove](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-remove){#ref-for-map-remove①
        link-type="dfn"} [registration
        map](#dfn-scope-to-registration-map){#ref-for-dfn-scope-to-registration-map④
        link-type="dfn"}\[(`registration`{.variable}'s [storage
        key](#service-worker-registration-storage-key){#ref-for-service-worker-registration-storage-key⑧
        link-type="dfn"},
        [serialized](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-serializer){#ref-for-concept-url-serializer①⓪
        link-type="dfn"} `scopeURL`{.variable})\].

    3.  Invoke [Finish Job](#finish-job){#ref-for-finish-job⑥
        link-type="dfn"} with `job`{.variable} and abort these steps.

9.  If `hasUpdatedResources`{.variable} is false, then:

    1.  Set `registration`{.variable}'s [update via cache
        mode](#dfn-update-via-cache){#ref-for-dfn-update-via-cache⑥
        link-type="dfn"} to `job`{.variable}'s [update via cache
        mode](#dfn-job-update-via-cache-mode){#ref-for-dfn-job-update-via-cache-mode④
        link-type="dfn"}.

    2.  Invoke [Resolve Job
        Promise](#resolve-job-promise){#ref-for-resolve-job-promise①
        link-type="dfn"} with `job`{.variable} and
        `registration`{.variable}.

    3.  Invoke [Finish Job](#finish-job){#ref-for-finish-job⑦
        link-type="dfn"} with `job`{.variable} and abort these steps.

10. Let `worker`{.variable} be a new [service
    worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑨⑥
    link-type="dfn"}.

11. Set `worker`{.variable}'s [script
    url](#dfn-script-url){#ref-for-dfn-script-url⑤ link-type="dfn"} to
    `job`{.variable}'s [script
    url](#dfn-job-script-url){#ref-for-dfn-job-script-url①①
    link-type="dfn"}, `worker`{.variable}'s [script
    resource](#dfn-script-resource){#ref-for-dfn-script-resource④
    link-type="dfn"} to `script`{.variable}, `worker`{.variable}'s
    [type](#dfn-type){#ref-for-dfn-type③ link-type="dfn"} to
    `job`{.variable}'s [worker
    type](#dfn-job-worker-type){#ref-for-dfn-job-worker-type⑦
    link-type="dfn"}, and `worker`{.variable}'s [script resource
    map](#dfn-script-resource-map){#ref-for-dfn-script-resource-map④
    link-type="dfn"} to `updatedResourceMap`{.variable}.

12. Append `url`{.variable} to `worker`{.variable}'s [set of used
    scripts](#dfn-set-of-used-scripts){#ref-for-dfn-set-of-used-scripts③
    link-type="dfn"}.

13. Set `worker`{.variable}'s [script
    resource](#dfn-script-resource){#ref-for-dfn-script-resource⑤
    link-type="dfn"}'s [policy
    container](#dfn-policy-container){#ref-for-dfn-policy-container
    link-type="dfn"} to `policyContainer`{.variable}.

14. Let `forceBypassCache`{.variable} be true if `job`{.variable}'s
    [force bypass cache
    flag](#dfn-job-force-bypass-cache-flag){#ref-for-dfn-job-force-bypass-cache-flag②
    link-type="dfn"} is set, and false otherwise.

15. Let `runResult`{.variable} be the result of running the [Run Service
    Worker](#run-service-worker){#ref-for-run-service-worker③
    link-type="dfn"} algorithm with `worker`{.variable} and
    `forceBypassCache`{.variable}.

16. If `runResult`{.variable} is *failure* or an [abrupt
    completion](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-completion-record-specification-type){#ref-for-sec-completion-record-specification-type①
    link-type="dfn" refhint-key="03b441c0"}, then:

    1.  Invoke [Reject Job
        Promise](#reject-job-promise){#ref-for-reject-job-promise⑨
        link-type="dfn"} with `job`{.variable} and `TypeError`.

    2.  If `newestWorker`{.variable} is null, then
        [remove](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-remove){#ref-for-map-remove②
        link-type="dfn"} [registration
        map](#dfn-scope-to-registration-map){#ref-for-dfn-scope-to-registration-map⑤
        link-type="dfn"}\[(`registration`{.variable}'s [storage
        key](#service-worker-registration-storage-key){#ref-for-service-worker-registration-storage-key⑨
        link-type="dfn"},
        [serialized](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-serializer){#ref-for-concept-url-serializer①①
        link-type="dfn"} `scopeURL`{.variable})\].

    3.  Invoke [Finish Job](#finish-job){#ref-for-finish-job⑧
        link-type="dfn"} with `job`{.variable}.

17. Else, invoke [Install](#install){#ref-for-install② link-type="dfn"}
    algorithm with `job`{.variable}, `worker`{.variable}, and
    `registration`{.variable} as its arguments.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="Soft Update"}
### [[Soft Update]{#soft-update .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}]{.content}[](#soft-update-algorithm){.self-link} {#soft-update-algorithm .heading .settled}

The user agent *may* call this as often as it likes to check for
updates.

Input

:   `registration`{.variable}, a [service worker
    registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration⑤③
    link-type="dfn"}

:   `forceBypassCache`{.variable}, an optional boolean, false by default

    [Note:]{.marker} Implementers may use `forceBypassCache`{.variable}
    to aid debugging (e.g. invocations from developer tools), and other
    specifications that extend service workers may also use the flag on
    their own needs.

Output

:   None

1.  Let `newestWorker`{.variable} be the result of running [Get Newest
    Worker](#get-newest-worker){#ref-for-get-newest-worker③
    link-type="dfn"} algorithm passing `registration`{.variable} as its
    argument.

2.  If `newestWorker`{.variable} is null, abort these steps.

3.  Let `job`{.variable} be the result of running [Create
    Job](#create-job){#ref-for-create-job③ link-type="dfn"} with
    *update*, `registration`{.variable}'s [storage
    key](#service-worker-registration-storage-key){#ref-for-service-worker-registration-storage-key①⓪
    link-type="dfn"}, `registration`{.variable}'s [scope
    url](#dfn-scope-url){#ref-for-dfn-scope-url①④ link-type="dfn"},
    `newestWorker`{.variable}'s [script
    url](#dfn-script-url){#ref-for-dfn-script-url⑥ link-type="dfn"},
    null, and null.

4.  Set `job`{.variable}'s [worker
    type](#dfn-job-worker-type){#ref-for-dfn-job-worker-type⑧
    link-type="dfn"} to `newestWorker`{.variable}'s
    [type](#dfn-type){#ref-for-dfn-type④ link-type="dfn"}.

5.  Set `job`{.variable}'s [force bypass cache
    flag](#dfn-job-force-bypass-cache-flag){#ref-for-dfn-job-force-bypass-cache-flag③
    link-type="dfn"} if `forceBypassCache`{.variable} is true.

6.  Invoke [Schedule Job](#schedule-job){#ref-for-schedule-job③
    link-type="dfn"} with `job`{.variable}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="Install"}
### [[Install]{#install .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}]{.content}[](#installation-algorithm){.self-link} {#installation-algorithm .heading .settled}

Input

:   `job`{.variable}, a [job](#dfn-job){#ref-for-dfn-job②⑥
    link-type="dfn"}

:   `worker`{.variable}, a [service
    worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑨⑦
    link-type="dfn"}

:   `registration`{.variable}, a [service worker
    registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration⑤④
    link-type="dfn"}

Output

:   none

1.  Let `installFailed`{.variable} be false.

2.  Let `newestWorker`{.variable} be the result of running [Get Newest
    Worker](#get-newest-worker){#ref-for-get-newest-worker④
    link-type="dfn"} algorithm passing `registration`{.variable} as its
    argument.

3.  Set `registration`{.variable}'s [update via cache
    mode](#dfn-update-via-cache){#ref-for-dfn-update-via-cache⑦
    link-type="dfn"} to `job`{.variable}'s [update via cache
    mode](#dfn-job-update-via-cache-mode){#ref-for-dfn-job-update-via-cache-mode⑤
    link-type="dfn"}.

4.  Run the [Update Registration
    State](#update-registration-state){#ref-for-update-registration-state
    link-type="dfn"} algorithm passing `registration`{.variable},
    \"`installing`\" and `worker`{.variable} as the arguments.

5.  Run the [Update Worker
    State](#update-worker-state){#ref-for-update-worker-state
    link-type="dfn"} algorithm passing `registration`{.variable}'s
    [installing
    worker](#dfn-installing-worker){#ref-for-dfn-installing-worker⑧
    link-type="dfn"} and \"`installing`\" as the arguments.

6.  Assert: `job`{.variable}'s [job
    promise](#dfn-job-promise){#ref-for-dfn-job-promise⑥
    link-type="dfn"} is not null.

7.  Invoke [Resolve Job
    Promise](#resolve-job-promise){#ref-for-resolve-job-promise②
    link-type="dfn"} with `job`{.variable} and
    `registration`{.variable}.

8.  Let `settingsObjects`{.variable} be all [environment settings
    objects](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object){#ref-for-environment-settings-object①⑧
    link-type="dfn"} whose
    [origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-origin){#ref-for-concept-settings-object-origin①③
    link-type="dfn"} is `registration`{.variable}'s [scope
    url](#dfn-scope-url){#ref-for-dfn-scope-url①⑤ link-type="dfn"}'s
    [origin](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-origin){#ref-for-concept-url-origin⑧
    link-type="dfn"}.

9.  For each `settingsObject`{.variable} of
    `settingsObjects`{.variable}, [queue a
    task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task){#ref-for-queue-a-task②⑦
    link-type="dfn"} on `settingsObject`{.variable}'s [responsible event
    loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop){#ref-for-responsible-event-loop①⑤
    link-type="dfn"} in the [DOM manipulation task
    source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source){#ref-for-dom-manipulation-task-source②⓪
    link-type="dfn"} to run the following steps:

    1.  Let `registrationObjects`{.variable} be every
        [`ServiceWorkerRegistration`{.idl}](#serviceworkerregistration){#ref-for-serviceworkerregistration①⑤
        link-type="idl"} object in `settingsObject`{.variable}'s
        [realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object's-realm){#ref-for-environment-settings-object's-realm⑨
        link-type="dfn"}, whose [service worker
        registration](#serviceworkerregistration-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-serviceworkerregistration-service-worker-registration⑦
        link-type="dfn"} is `registration`{.variable}.

    2.  For each `registrationObject`{.variable} of
        `registrationObjects`{.variable}, [fire an
        event](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event-fire){#ref-for-concept-event-fire①
        link-type="dfn"} on `registrationObject`{.variable} named
        `updatefound`.

10. Let `installingWorker`{.variable} be `registration`{.variable}'s
    [installing
    worker](#dfn-installing-worker){#ref-for-dfn-installing-worker⑨
    link-type="dfn"}.

11. If the result of running the [Should Skip
    Event](#should-skip-event){#ref-for-should-skip-event①
    link-type="dfn"} algorithm with `installingWorker`{.variable} and
    \"install\" is false, then:

    1.  Let `forceBypassCache`{.variable} be true if `job`{.variable}'s
        [force bypass cache
        flag](#dfn-job-force-bypass-cache-flag){#ref-for-dfn-job-force-bypass-cache-flag④
        link-type="dfn"} is set, and false otherwise.

    2.  If the result of running the [Run Service
        Worker](#run-service-worker){#ref-for-run-service-worker④
        link-type="dfn"} algorithm with `installingWorker`{.variable}
        and `forceBypassCache`{.variable} is *failure*, then:

        1.  Set `installFailed`{.variable} to true.

    3.  Else:

        1.  [Queue a
            task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task){#ref-for-queue-a-task②⑧
            link-type="dfn"} `task`{.variable} on
            `installingWorker`{.variable}'s [event
            loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-loop){#ref-for-event-loop①⓪
            link-type="dfn"} using the [DOM manipulation task
            source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source){#ref-for-dom-manipulation-task-source②①
            link-type="dfn"} to run the following steps:

            1.  Let `e`{.variable} be the result of [creating an
                event](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event-create){#ref-for-concept-event-create②
                link-type="dfn"} with
                [`InstallEvent`{.idl}](#installevent){#ref-for-installevent②
                link-type="idl"}.

            2.  Initialize `e`{.variable}'s
                [`type`{.idl}](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-event-type){#ref-for-dom-event-type
                link-type="idl"} attribute to
                [`install`{.idl}](#service-worker-global-scope-install-event){#ref-for-service-worker-global-scope-install-event⑤
                .idl-code link-type="event"}.

            3.  [Dispatch](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event-dispatch){#ref-for-concept-event-dispatch④
                link-type="dfn"} `e`{.variable} at
                `installingWorker`{.variable}'s [global
                object](#dfn-service-worker-global-object){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-global-object①
                link-type="dfn"}.

            4.  *WaitForAsynchronousExtensions*: Run the following
                substeps [in
                parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel){#ref-for-in-parallel③⓪
                link-type="dfn"}:

                1.  [Wait until `e`{.variable} is not
                    [active](#extendableevent-active){#ref-for-extendableevent-active①
                    link-type="dfn"}.]{#install-settle-step}

                2.  If `e`{.variable}'s [timed out
                    flag](#extendableevent-timed-out-flag){#ref-for-extendableevent-timed-out-flag①
                    link-type="dfn"} is set, set
                    `installFailed`{.variable} to true.

                3.  Let `p`{.variable} be the result of [getting a
                    promise to wait for
                    all](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#waiting-for-all-promise){#ref-for-waiting-for-all-promise①
                    link-type="dfn"} of `e`{.variable}'s [extend
                    lifetime
                    promises](#extendableevent-extend-lifetime-promises){#ref-for-extendableevent-extend-lifetime-promises⑥
                    link-type="dfn"}.

                4.  [Upon
                    rejection](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#upon-rejection){#ref-for-upon-rejection③
                    link-type="dfn"} of `p`{.variable}, set
                    `installFailed`{.variable} to true.

            If `task`{.variable} is discarded, set
            `installFailed`{.variable} to true.

        2.  Wait for `task`{.variable} to have executed or been
            discarded.

        3.  Wait for the step labeled *WaitForAsynchronousExtensions* to
            complete.

12. If `installFailed`{.variable} is true, then:

    1.  Run the [Update Worker
        State](#update-worker-state){#ref-for-update-worker-state①
        link-type="dfn"} algorithm passing `registration`{.variable}'s
        [installing
        worker](#dfn-installing-worker){#ref-for-dfn-installing-worker①⓪
        link-type="dfn"} and \"`redundant`\" as the arguments.

    2.  Run the [Update Registration
        State](#update-registration-state){#ref-for-update-registration-state①
        link-type="dfn"} algorithm passing `registration`{.variable},
        \"`installing`\" and null as the arguments.

    3.  If `newestWorker`{.variable} is null, then
        [remove](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-remove){#ref-for-map-remove③
        link-type="dfn"} [registration
        map](#dfn-scope-to-registration-map){#ref-for-dfn-scope-to-registration-map⑥
        link-type="dfn"}\[(`registration`{.variable}'s [storage
        key](#service-worker-registration-storage-key){#ref-for-service-worker-registration-storage-key①①
        link-type="dfn"},
        [serialized](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-serializer){#ref-for-concept-url-serializer①②
        link-type="dfn"} `registration`{.variable}'s [scope
        url](#dfn-scope-url){#ref-for-dfn-scope-url①⑥
        link-type="dfn"})\].

    4.  Invoke [Finish Job](#finish-job){#ref-for-finish-job⑨
        link-type="dfn"} with `job`{.variable} and abort these steps.

13. Let `map`{.variable} be `registration`{.variable}'s [installing
    worker](#dfn-installing-worker){#ref-for-dfn-installing-worker①①
    link-type="dfn"}'s [script resource
    map](#dfn-script-resource-map){#ref-for-dfn-script-resource-map⑤
    link-type="dfn"}.

14. Let `usedSet`{.variable} be `registration`{.variable}'s [installing
    worker](#dfn-installing-worker){#ref-for-dfn-installing-worker①②
    link-type="dfn"}'s [set of used
    scripts](#dfn-set-of-used-scripts){#ref-for-dfn-set-of-used-scripts④
    link-type="dfn"}.

15. [For
    each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-iterate){#ref-for-map-iterate⑤
    link-type="dfn"} `url`{.variable} of `map`{.variable}:

    1.  If `usedSet`{.variable} does not
        [contain](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-contain){#ref-for-list-contain①
        link-type="dfn"} `url`{.variable}, then
        [remove](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-remove){#ref-for-map-remove④
        link-type="dfn"} `map`{.variable}\[`url`{.variable}\].

16. If `registration`{.variable}'s [waiting
    worker](#dfn-waiting-worker){#ref-for-dfn-waiting-worker⑤
    link-type="dfn"} is not null, then:

    1.  [Terminate](#terminate-service-worker){#ref-for-terminate-service-worker③
        link-type="dfn"} `registration`{.variable}'s [waiting
        worker](#dfn-waiting-worker){#ref-for-dfn-waiting-worker⑥
        link-type="dfn"}.

    2.  Run the [Update Worker
        State](#update-worker-state){#ref-for-update-worker-state②
        link-type="dfn"} algorithm passing `registration`{.variable}'s
        [waiting
        worker](#dfn-waiting-worker){#ref-for-dfn-waiting-worker⑦
        link-type="dfn"} and \"`redundant`\" as the arguments.

17. Run the [Update Registration
    State](#update-registration-state){#ref-for-update-registration-state②
    link-type="dfn"} algorithm passing `registration`{.variable},
    \"`waiting`\" and `registration`{.variable}'s [installing
    worker](#dfn-installing-worker){#ref-for-dfn-installing-worker①③
    link-type="dfn"} as the arguments.

18. Run the [Update Registration
    State](#update-registration-state){#ref-for-update-registration-state③
    link-type="dfn"} algorithm passing `registration`{.variable},
    \"`installing`\" and null as the arguments.

19. Run the [Update Worker
    State](#update-worker-state){#ref-for-update-worker-state③
    link-type="dfn"} algorithm passing `registration`{.variable}'s
    [waiting worker](#dfn-waiting-worker){#ref-for-dfn-waiting-worker⑧
    link-type="dfn"} and \"`installed`\" as the arguments.

20. Invoke [Finish Job](#finish-job){#ref-for-finish-job①⓪
    link-type="dfn"} with `job`{.variable}.

21. Wait for all the
    [tasks](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-task){#ref-for-concept-task④
    link-type="dfn"}
    [queued](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task){#ref-for-queue-a-task②⑨
    link-type="dfn"} by [Update Worker
    State](#update-worker-state){#ref-for-update-worker-state④
    link-type="dfn"} invoked in this algorithm to have executed.

22. Invoke [Try Activate](#try-activate){#ref-for-try-activate②
    link-type="dfn"} with `registration`{.variable}.

    [Note:]{.marker} If [Try
    Activate](#try-activate){#ref-for-try-activate③ link-type="dfn"}
    does not trigger [Activate](#activate){#ref-for-activate③
    link-type="dfn"} here, [Activate](#activate){#ref-for-activate④
    link-type="dfn"} is tried again when the last client controlled by
    the existing [active
    worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker②⓪
    link-type="dfn"} is
    [unloaded](#handle-service-worker-client-unload){#ref-for-handle-service-worker-client-unload①
    link-type="dfn"},
    [`skipWaiting()`{.idl}](#dom-serviceworkerglobalscope-skipwaiting){#ref-for-dom-serviceworkerglobalscope-skipwaiting③
    link-type="idl"} is asynchronously called, or the [extend lifetime
    promises](#extendableevent-extend-lifetime-promises){#ref-for-extendableevent-extend-lifetime-promises⑦
    link-type="dfn"} for the existing [active
    worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker②①
    link-type="dfn"} settle.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="Activate"}
### [[Activate]{#activate .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}]{.content}[](#activation-algorithm){.self-link} {#activation-algorithm .heading .settled}

Input

:   `registration`{.variable}, a [service worker
    registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration⑤⑤
    link-type="dfn"}

Output

:   None

1.  If `registration`{.variable}'s [waiting
    worker](#dfn-waiting-worker){#ref-for-dfn-waiting-worker⑨
    link-type="dfn"} is null, abort these steps.

2.  If `registration`{.variable}'s [active
    worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker②②
    link-type="dfn"} is not null, then:

    1.  [Terminate](#terminate-service-worker){#ref-for-terminate-service-worker④
        link-type="dfn"} `registration`{.variable}'s [active
        worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker②③
        link-type="dfn"}.

    2.  Run the [Update Worker
        State](#update-worker-state){#ref-for-update-worker-state⑤
        link-type="dfn"} algorithm passing `registration`{.variable}'s
        [active worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker②④
        link-type="dfn"} and \"`redundant`\" as the arguments.

3.  Run the [Update Registration
    State](#update-registration-state){#ref-for-update-registration-state④
    link-type="dfn"} algorithm passing `registration`{.variable},
    \"`active`\" and `registration`{.variable}'s [waiting
    worker](#dfn-waiting-worker){#ref-for-dfn-waiting-worker①⓪
    link-type="dfn"} as the arguments.

4.  Run the [Update Registration
    State](#update-registration-state){#ref-for-update-registration-state⑤
    link-type="dfn"} algorithm passing `registration`{.variable},
    \"`waiting`\" and null as the arguments.

5.  Run the [Update Worker
    State](#update-worker-state){#ref-for-update-worker-state⑥
    link-type="dfn"} algorithm passing `registration`{.variable}'s
    [active worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker②⑤
    link-type="dfn"} and \"`activating`\" as the arguments.

    [Note:]{.marker} Once an active worker is activating, neither a
    runtime script error nor a force termination of the active worker
    prevents the active worker from getting activated.

    [Note:]{.marker} Make sure to design activation handlers to do
    non-essential work (like cleanup). This is because activation
    handlers may not all run to completion, especially in the case of
    browser termination during activation. A Service Worker should be
    designed to function properly, even if the activation handlers do
    not all complete successfully.

6.  Let `matchedClients`{.variable} be a
    [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list){#ref-for-list②②
    link-type="dfn"} of [service worker
    clients](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client③⑨
    link-type="dfn"} whose [creation
    URL](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-creation-url){#ref-for-concept-environment-creation-url⑦
    link-type="dfn"}
    [matches](#match-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-match-service-worker-registration⑥
    link-type="dfn"} `registration`{.variable}'s [storage
    key](#service-worker-registration-storage-key){#ref-for-service-worker-registration-storage-key①②
    link-type="dfn"} and `registration`{.variable}'s [scope
    url](#dfn-scope-url){#ref-for-dfn-scope-url①⑦ link-type="dfn"}.

7.  [For
    each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate){#ref-for-list-iterate①⑤
    link-type="dfn"} `client`{.variable} of `matchedClients`{.variable},
    [queue a
    task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task){#ref-for-queue-a-task③⓪
    link-type="dfn"} on `client`{.variable}'s [responsible event
    loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop){#ref-for-responsible-event-loop①⑥
    link-type="dfn"}, using the [DOM manipulation task
    source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source){#ref-for-dom-manipulation-task-source②②
    link-type="dfn"}, to run the following substeps:

    1.  Let `readyPromise`{.variable} be `client`{.variable}'s [global
        object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-global){#ref-for-concept-settings-object-global⑨
        link-type="dfn"}'s
        [`ServiceWorkerContainer`{.idl}](#serviceworkercontainer){#ref-for-serviceworkercontainer①⑤
        link-type="idl"} object's [ready
        promise](#serviceworkercontainer-ready-promise){#ref-for-serviceworkercontainer-ready-promise④
        link-type="dfn"}.

    2.  If `readyPromise`{.variable} is null, then
        [continue](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-continue){#ref-for-iteration-continue⑧
        link-type="dfn"}.

    3.  [If `readyPromise`{.variable} is pending, resolve
        `readyPromise`{.variable} with the the result of [getting the
        service worker registration
        object](#get-the-service-worker-registration-object){#ref-for-get-the-service-worker-registration-object⑥
        link-type="dfn"} that represents `registration`{.variable} in
        `readyPromise`{.variable}'s [relevant settings
        object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object){#ref-for-relevant-settings-object②⑨
        link-type="dfn"}]{#activate-resolve-ready-step}.

8.  For each [service worker
    client](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client④⓪
    link-type="dfn"} `client`{.variable} who is
    [using](#dfn-use){#ref-for-dfn-use② link-type="dfn"}
    `registration`{.variable}:

    1.  Set `client`{.variable}'s [active
        worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker②⑥
        link-type="dfn"} to `registration`{.variable}'s [active
        worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker②⑦
        link-type="dfn"}.

    2.  Invoke [Notify Controller
        Change](#notify-controller-change){#ref-for-notify-controller-change①
        link-type="dfn"} algorithm with `client`{.variable} as the
        argument.

9.  Let `activeWorker`{.variable} be `registration`{.variable}'s [active
    worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker②⑧
    link-type="dfn"}.

10. If the result of running the [Should Skip
    Event](#should-skip-event){#ref-for-should-skip-event②
    link-type="dfn"} algorithm with `activeWorker`{.variable} and
    \"activate\" is false, then:

    1.  If the result of running the [Run Service
        Worker](#run-service-worker){#ref-for-run-service-worker⑤
        link-type="dfn"} algorithm with `activeWorker`{.variable} is not
        *failure*, then:

        1.  [Queue a
            task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task){#ref-for-queue-a-task③①
            link-type="dfn"} `task`{.variable} on
            `activeWorker`{.variable}'s [event
            loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-loop){#ref-for-event-loop①①
            link-type="dfn"} using the [DOM manipulation task
            source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source){#ref-for-dom-manipulation-task-source②③
            link-type="dfn"} to run the following steps:

            1.  Let `e`{.variable} be the result of [creating an
                event](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event-create){#ref-for-concept-event-create③
                link-type="dfn"} with
                [`ExtendableEvent`{.idl}](#extendableevent){#ref-for-extendableevent①⑥
                link-type="idl"}.

            2.  Initialize `e`{.variable}'s
                [`type`{.idl}](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-event-type){#ref-for-dom-event-type①
                link-type="idl"} attribute to
                [`activate`{.idl}](#service-worker-global-scope-activate-event){#ref-for-service-worker-global-scope-activate-event⑤
                .idl-code link-type="event"}.

            3.  [Dispatch](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event-dispatch){#ref-for-concept-event-dispatch⑤
                link-type="dfn"} `e`{.variable} at
                `activeWorker`{.variable}'s [global
                object](#dfn-service-worker-global-object){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-global-object②
                link-type="dfn"}.

            4.  [*WaitForAsynchronousExtensions*: Wait, [in
                parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel){#ref-for-in-parallel③①
                link-type="dfn"}, until `e`{.variable} is not
                [active](#extendableevent-active){#ref-for-extendableevent-active②
                link-type="dfn"}.]{#activate-settle-step}

        2.  Wait for `task`{.variable} to have executed or been
            discarded.

        3.  Wait for the step labeled *WaitForAsynchronousExtensions* to
            complete.

11. Run the [Update Worker
    State](#update-worker-state){#ref-for-update-worker-state⑦
    link-type="dfn"} algorithm passing `registration`{.variable}'s
    [active worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker②⑨
    link-type="dfn"} and \"`activated`\" as the arguments.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="Try Activate"}
### [[Try Activate]{#try-activate .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}]{.content}[](#try-activate-algorithm){.self-link} {#try-activate-algorithm .heading .settled}

Input

:   `registration`{.variable}, a [service worker
    registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration⑤⑥
    link-type="dfn"}

Output

:   None

1.  If `registration`{.variable}'s [waiting
    worker](#dfn-waiting-worker){#ref-for-dfn-waiting-worker①①
    link-type="dfn"} is null, return.

2.  If `registration`{.variable}'s [active
    worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker③⓪
    link-type="dfn"} is not null and `registration`{.variable}'s [active
    worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker③①
    link-type="dfn"}'s [state](#dfn-state){#ref-for-dfn-state⑦
    link-type="dfn"} is \"`activating`\", return.

    [Note:]{.marker} If the existing active worker is still in
    activating state, the activation of the waiting worker is delayed.

3.  Invoke [Activate](#activate){#ref-for-activate⑤ link-type="dfn"}
    with `registration`{.variable} if either of the following is true:

    - `registration`{.variable}'s [active
      worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker③②
      link-type="dfn"} is null.

    - The result of running [Service Worker Has No Pending
      Events](#service-worker-has-no-pending-events){#ref-for-service-worker-has-no-pending-events①
      link-type="dfn"} with `registration`{.variable}'s [active
      worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker③③
      link-type="dfn"} is true, and no [service worker
      client](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client④①
      link-type="dfn"} is [using](#dfn-use){#ref-for-dfn-use③
      link-type="dfn"} `registration`{.variable} or
      `registration`{.variable}'s [waiting
      worker](#dfn-waiting-worker){#ref-for-dfn-waiting-worker①②
      link-type="dfn"}'s [skip waiting
      flag](#dfn-skip-waiting-flag){#ref-for-dfn-skip-waiting-flag②
      link-type="dfn"} is set.
:::

:::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="Setup ServiceWorkerGlobalScope"}
### [[Setup ServiceWorkerGlobalScope]{#setup-serviceworkerglobalscope .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}]{.content}[](#setup-serviceworkerglobalscope-algorithm){.self-link} {#setup-serviceworkerglobalscope-algorithm .heading .settled}

Input

:   `serviceWorker`{.variable}, a [service
    worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑨⑧
    link-type="dfn"}

Output

:   a
    [`ServiceWorkerGlobalScope`{.idl}](#serviceworkerglobalscope){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope①⑦
    link-type="idl"} object or null

[Note:]{.marker} This algorithm returns a
[`ServiceWorkerGlobalScope`{.idl}](#serviceworkerglobalscope){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope①⑧
link-type="idl"} usable for a CSP check, or null. If
`serviceWorker`{.variable} has an active
[`ServiceWorkerGlobalScope`{.idl}](#serviceworkerglobalscope){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope①⑨
link-type="idl"}, then it is returned. Otherwise, the object will be
newly created.

::: {.note role="note"}
This algorithm does the minimal setup for the service worker that is
necessary to create something usable for security checks like CSP and
COEP. This specification ensures that this algorithm is called before
any such checks are performed.

In specifications, such security checks require creating a
[`ServiceWorkerGlobalScope`{.idl}](#serviceworkerglobalscope){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope②⓪
link-type="idl"}, a [relevant settings
object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object){#ref-for-relevant-settings-object③⓪
link-type="dfn"}, a
[realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-global-object-realm){#ref-for-concept-global-object-realm①
link-type="dfn"}, and an
[agent](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#agent){#ref-for-agent link-type="dfn"}.
In implementations, the amount of work required might be much less.
Therefore, implementations could do less work in their equivalent of
this algorithm, and more work in [Run Service
Worker](#run-service-worker){#ref-for-run-service-worker⑥
link-type="dfn"}, as long as the results are observably equivalent. (And
in particular, as long as all security checks have the same result.)
:::

1.  Let `unsafeCreationTime`{.variable} be the [unsafe shared current
    time](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dfn-unsafe-shared-current-time){#ref-for-dfn-unsafe-shared-current-time
    link-type="dfn"}.

2.  If `serviceWorker`{.variable} is
    [running](#service-worker-running){#ref-for-service-worker-running
    link-type="dfn"}, then return `serviceWorker`{.variable}'s [global
    object](#dfn-service-worker-global-object){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-global-object③
    link-type="dfn"}.

3.  If `serviceWorker`{.variable}'s
    [state](#dfn-state){#ref-for-dfn-state⑧ link-type="dfn"} is
    \"`redundant`\", then return null.

4.  If `serviceWorker`{.variable}'s [global
    object](#dfn-service-worker-global-object){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-global-object④
    link-type="dfn"} is not null, then return
    `serviceWorker`{.variable}'s [global
    object](#dfn-service-worker-global-object){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-global-object⑤
    link-type="dfn"}.

5.  Assert: `serviceWorker`{.variable}'s [start
    status](#service-worker-start-status){#ref-for-service-worker-start-status
    link-type="dfn"} is null.

6.  Let `setupFailed`{.variable} be false.

7.  Let `globalObject`{.variable} be null.

8.  Let `agent`{.variable} be the result of [obtaining a service worker
    agent](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#obtain-a-service-worker-agent){#ref-for-obtain-a-service-worker-agent
    link-type="dfn"}, and run the following steps in that context:

    1.  Let `realmExecutionContext`{.variable} be the result of
        [creating a new
        realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#creating-a-new-javascript-realm){#ref-for-creating-a-new-javascript-realm
        link-type="dfn"} given `agent`{.variable} and the following
        customizations:

        - For the global object, create a new
          [`ServiceWorkerGlobalScope`{.idl}](#serviceworkerglobalscope){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope②①
          link-type="idl"} object. Let `workerGlobalScope`{.variable} be
          the created object.

    2.  Let `settingsObject`{.variable} be a new [environment settings
        object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object){#ref-for-environment-settings-object①⑨
        link-type="dfn"} whose algorithms are defined as follows:

        The [realm execution context](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#realm-execution-context){#ref-for-realm-execution-context link-type="dfn"}

        :   Return `realmExecutionContext`{.variable}.

        The [module map](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-module-map){#ref-for-concept-settings-object-module-map link-type="dfn"}

        :   Return `workerGlobalScope`{.variable}'s [module
            map](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/workers.html#concept-workerglobalscope-module-map){#ref-for-concept-workerglobalscope-module-map
            link-type="dfn"}.

        The [API base URL](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#api-base-url){#ref-for-api-base-url⑤ link-type="dfn"}

        :   Return `serviceWorker`{.variable}'s [script
            url](#dfn-script-url){#ref-for-dfn-script-url⑦
            link-type="dfn"}.

        The [origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-origin){#ref-for-concept-settings-object-origin①④ link-type="dfn"}

        :   Return its registering [service worker
            client](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client④②
            link-type="dfn"}'s
            [origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-origin){#ref-for-concept-settings-object-origin①⑤
            link-type="dfn"}.

        The [has cross-site ancestor](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-has-cross-site-ancestor){#ref-for-concept-settings-object-has-cross-site-ancestor link-type="dfn"}

        :   Return its registering [service worker
            client](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client④③
            link-type="dfn"}'s [has cross-site
            ancestor](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-has-cross-site-ancestor){#ref-for-concept-settings-object-has-cross-site-ancestor①
            link-type="dfn"}.

        The [policy container](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-policy-container){#ref-for-concept-settings-object-policy-container link-type="dfn"}

        :   Return `workerGlobalScope`{.variable}'s [policy
            container](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/workers.html#concept-workerglobalscope-policy-container){#ref-for-concept-workerglobalscope-policy-container
            link-type="dfn"}.

        The [time origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-time-origin){#ref-for-concept-settings-object-time-origin link-type="dfn"}

        :   Return the result of
            [coarsening](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dfn-coarsen-time){#ref-for-dfn-coarsen-time
            link-type="dfn"} `unsafeCreationTime`{.variable} given
            `workerGlobalScope`{.variable}'s [cross-origin isolated
            capability](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/workers.html#concept-workerglobalscope-cross-origin-isolated-capability){#ref-for-concept-workerglobalscope-cross-origin-isolated-capability
            link-type="dfn"}.

    3.  Set `settingsObject`{.variable}'s
        [id](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-id){#ref-for-concept-environment-id②
        link-type="dfn"} to a new unique opaque string, [creation
        URL](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-creation-url){#ref-for-concept-environment-creation-url⑧
        link-type="dfn"} to `serviceWorker`{.variable}'s [script
        url](#dfn-script-url){#ref-for-dfn-script-url⑧ link-type="dfn"},
        [top-level creation
        URL](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-top-level-creation-url){#ref-for-concept-environment-top-level-creation-url
        link-type="dfn"} to null, [top-level
