        origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-top-level-origin){#ref-for-concept-environment-top-level-origin
        link-type="dfn"} to an
        [implementation-defined](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#implementation-defined){#ref-for-implementation-defined
        link-type="dfn"} value, [target browsing
        context](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-target-browsing-context){#ref-for-concept-environment-target-browsing-context①
        link-type="dfn"} to null, and [active service
        worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker){#ref-for-concept-environment-active-service-worker②⑤
        link-type="dfn"} to null.

    4.  Set `workerGlobalScope`{.variable}'s
        [url](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/workers.html#concept-workerglobalscope-url){#ref-for-concept-workerglobalscope-url
        link-type="dfn"} to `serviceWorker`{.variable}'s [script
        url](#dfn-script-url){#ref-for-dfn-script-url⑨ link-type="dfn"}.

    5.  Set `workerGlobalScope`{.variable}'s [policy
        container](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/workers.html#concept-workerglobalscope-policy-container){#ref-for-concept-workerglobalscope-policy-container①
        link-type="dfn"} to `serviceWorker`{.variable}'s [script
        resource](#dfn-script-resource){#ref-for-dfn-script-resource⑥
        link-type="dfn"}'s [policy
        container](#dfn-policy-container){#ref-for-dfn-policy-container①
        link-type="dfn"}.

    6.  Set `workerGlobalScope`{.variable}'s
        [type](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/workers.html#concept-workerglobalscope-type){#ref-for-concept-workerglobalscope-type
        link-type="dfn"} to `serviceWorker`{.variable}'s
        [type](#dfn-type){#ref-for-dfn-type⑤ link-type="dfn"}.

    7.  Create a new
        [`WorkerLocation`{.idl}](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/workers.html#workerlocation){#ref-for-workerlocation
        link-type="idl"} object and associate it with
        `workerGlobalScope`{.variable}.

    8.  If the [run CSP initialization for a global
        object](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-csp/#run-global-object-csp-initialization){#ref-for-run-global-object-csp-initialization
        link-type="dfn"} algorithm returns \"`Blocked`\" when executed
        upon `workerGlobalScope`{.variable}, set
        `setupFailed`{.variable} to true and abort these steps.

    9.  Set `globalObject`{.variable} to `workerGlobalScope`{.variable}.

9.  Wait for `globalObject`{.variable} is not null, or for
    `setupFailed`{.variable} to be true.

10. If `setupFailed`{.variable} is true, then return null.

11. Return `globalObject`{.variable}.
::::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="Run Service Worker"}
### [[Run Service Worker]{#run-service-worker .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}]{.content}[](#run-service-worker-algorithm){.self-link} {#run-service-worker-algorithm .heading .settled}

Input

:   `serviceWorker`{.variable}, a [service
    worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker⑨⑨
    link-type="dfn"}

:   `forceBypassCache`{.variable}, an optional boolean, false by default

Output

:   a
    [Completion](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-completion-record-specification-type){#ref-for-sec-completion-record-specification-type②
    link-type="dfn"} or *failure*

[Note:]{.marker} This algorithm blocks until the service worker is
[running](#service-worker-running){#ref-for-service-worker-running①
link-type="dfn"} or fails to start.

1.  If `serviceWorker`{.variable} is
    [running](#service-worker-running){#ref-for-service-worker-running②
    link-type="dfn"}, then return `serviceWorker`{.variable}'s [start
    status](#service-worker-start-status){#ref-for-service-worker-start-status①
    link-type="dfn"}.

2.  If `serviceWorker`{.variable}'s
    [state](#dfn-state){#ref-for-dfn-state⑨ link-type="dfn"} is
    \"`redundant`\", then return *failure*.

3.  Assert: `serviceWorker`{.variable}'s [start
    status](#service-worker-start-status){#ref-for-service-worker-start-status②
    link-type="dfn"} is null.

4.  Let `script`{.variable} be `serviceWorker`{.variable}'s [script
    resource](#dfn-script-resource){#ref-for-dfn-script-resource⑦
    link-type="dfn"}.

5.  Assert: `script`{.variable} is not null.

6.  Let `startFailed`{.variable} be false.

7.  Let `workerGlobalScope`{.variable} be `serviceWorker`{.variable}'s
    [global
    object](#dfn-service-worker-global-object){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-global-object⑥
    link-type="dfn"}.

8.  If `workerGlobalScope`{.variable} is null:

    1.  Set `workerGlobalScope`{.variable} to be the result of running
        the [Setup
        ServiceWorkerGlobalScope](#setup-serviceworkerglobalscope){#ref-for-setup-serviceworkerglobalscope
        link-type="dfn"} algorithm with `serviceWorker`{.variable}.

    2.  If `workerGlobalScope`{.variable} is null, then return
        *failure*.

    3.  Set `serviceWorker`{.variable}'s [global
        object](#dfn-service-worker-global-object){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-global-object⑦
        link-type="dfn"} to `workerGlobalScope`{.variable}.

9.  Obtain agent for `workerGlobalScope`{.variable}'s [realm execution
    context](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#realm-execution-context){#ref-for-realm-execution-context①
    link-type="dfn"}, and run the following steps in that context:

    1.  Set `workerGlobalScope`{.variable}'s [force bypass cache for
        import scripts
        flag](#serviceworkerglobalscope-force-bypass-cache-for-import-scripts-flag){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope-force-bypass-cache-for-import-scripts-flag①
        link-type="dfn"} if `forceBypassCache`{.variable} is true.

    2.  If `serviceWorker`{.variable} is an [active
        worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker③④
        link-type="dfn"}, and there are any
        [tasks](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-task){#ref-for-concept-task⑤
        link-type="dfn"} queued in `serviceWorker`{.variable}'s
        [containing service worker
        registration](#dfn-containing-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-containing-service-worker-registration①⑥
        link-type="dfn"}'s [task
        queues](#dfn-service-worker-registration-task-queue){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration-task-queue②
        link-type="dfn"},
        [queue](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task){#ref-for-queue-a-task③②
        link-type="dfn"} them to `serviceWorker`{.variable}'s [event
        loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-loop){#ref-for-event-loop①②
        link-type="dfn"}'s [task
        queues](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#task-queue){#ref-for-task-queue③
        link-type="dfn"} in the same order using their original [task
        sources](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#task-source){#ref-for-task-source⑤
        link-type="dfn"}.

    3.  Let `evaluationStatus`{.variable} be null.

    4.  If `script`{.variable} is a [classic
        script](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#classic-script){#ref-for-classic-script
        link-type="dfn"}, then:

        1.  Set `evaluationStatus`{.variable} to the result of [running
            the classic
            script](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#run-a-classic-script){#ref-for-run-a-classic-script
            link-type="dfn"} `script`{.variable}.

        2.  If `evaluationStatus`{.variable}.\[\[Value\]\] is empty,
            this means the script was not evaluated. Set
            `startFailed`{.variable} to true and abort these steps.

    5.  Otherwise, if `script`{.variable} is a [module
        script](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#module-script){#ref-for-module-script
        link-type="dfn"}, then:

        1.  Let `evaluationPromise`{.variable} be the result of [running
            the module
            script](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#run-a-module-script){#ref-for-run-a-module-script
            link-type="dfn"} `script`{.variable}, with report errors set
            to false.

        2.  Assert: `evaluationPromise`{.variable}.\[\[PromiseState\]\]
            is not \"pending\".

        3.  If `evaluationPromise`{.variable}.\[\[PromiseState\]\] is
            \"rejected\":

            1.  Set `evaluationStatus`{.variable} to
                [ThrowCompletion](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-throwcompletion){#ref-for-sec-throwcompletion
                link-type="abstract-op"}(`evaluationPromise`{.variable}.\[\[PromiseResult\]\]).

        4.  Otherwise:

            1.  Set `evaluationStatus`{.variable} to
                [NormalCompletion](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-normalcompletion){#ref-for-sec-normalcompletion
                link-type="abstract-op"}(undefined).

    6.  If the script was aborted by the [Terminate Service
        Worker](#terminate-service-worker){#ref-for-terminate-service-worker⑤
        link-type="dfn"} algorithm, set `startFailed`{.variable} to true
        and abort these steps.

    7.  Set `serviceWorker`{.variable}'s [start
        status](#service-worker-start-status){#ref-for-service-worker-start-status③
        link-type="dfn"} to `evaluationStatus`{.variable}.

    8.  If `script`{.variable}'s [has ever been evaluated
        flag](#dfn-has-ever-been-evaluated-flag){#ref-for-dfn-has-ever-been-evaluated-flag
        link-type="dfn"} is unset, then:

        1.  For each `eventType`{.variable} of
            `settingsObject`{.variable}'s [global
            object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-global){#ref-for-concept-settings-object-global①⓪
            link-type="dfn"}'s associated list of [event
            listeners](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event-listener){#ref-for-concept-event-listener①
            link-type="dfn"}\' event types:

            1.  [Append](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#set-append){#ref-for-set-append②
                link-type="dfn"} `eventType`{.variable} to
                `workerGlobalScope`{.variable}'s associated [service
                worker](#serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope-service-worker②⑤
                link-type="dfn"}'s [set of event types to
                handle](#dfn-set-of-event-types-to-handle){#ref-for-dfn-set-of-event-types-to-handle①
                link-type="dfn"}.

            [Note:]{.marker} If the global object's associated list of
            event listeners does not have any event listener added at
            this moment, the service worker's [set of event types to
            handle](#dfn-set-of-event-types-to-handle){#ref-for-dfn-set-of-event-types-to-handle②
            link-type="dfn"} remains an empty set.

        2.  Set `script`{.variable}'s [has ever been evaluated
            flag](#dfn-has-ever-been-evaluated-flag){#ref-for-dfn-has-ever-been-evaluated-flag①
            link-type="dfn"}.

        3.  Unset the `serviceWorker`{.variable}'s [all fetch listeners
            are empty
            flag](#service-worker-all-fetch-listeners-are-empty-flag){#ref-for-service-worker-all-fetch-listeners-are-empty-flag
            link-type="dfn"}.

        4.  The user agent may, if the [All Fetch Listeners Are
            Empty](#all-fetch-listeners-are-empty){#ref-for-all-fetch-listeners-are-empty
            link-type="dfn"} algorithm with
            `workerGlobalScope`{.variable} returns true, set
            `serviceWorker`{.variable}'s [all fetch listeners are empty
            flag](#service-worker-all-fetch-listeners-are-empty-flag){#ref-for-service-worker-all-fetch-listeners-are-empty-flag①
            link-type="dfn"}.

    9.  Run the [responsible event
        loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop){#ref-for-responsible-event-loop①⑦
        link-type="dfn"} specified by `settingsObject`{.variable} until
        it is destroyed.

    10. [Clear](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-clear){#ref-for-map-clear
        link-type="dfn"} `workerGlobalScope`{.variable}'s [map of active
        timers](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/timers-and-user-prompts.html#map-of-active-timers){#ref-for-map-of-active-timers
        link-type="dfn"}.

10. Wait for `serviceWorker`{.variable} to be
    [running](#service-worker-running){#ref-for-service-worker-running③
    link-type="dfn"}, or for `startFailed`{.variable} to be true.

11. If `startFailed`{.variable} is true, then return *failure*.

12. Return `serviceWorker`{.variable}'s [start
    status](#service-worker-start-status){#ref-for-service-worker-start-status④
    link-type="dfn"}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="All Fetch Listeners Are Empty"}
### [[All Fetch Listeners Are Empty]{#all-fetch-listeners-are-empty .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}]{.content}[](#all-fetch-listeners-are-empty-algorithm){.self-link} {#all-fetch-listeners-are-empty-algorithm .heading .settled}

Input

:   `workerGlobalScope`{.variable}, a [global
    object](#dfn-service-worker-global-object){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-global-object⑧
    link-type="dfn"}.

Output

:   a boolean

1.  If `workerGlobalScope`{.variable}'s [set of event types to
    handle](#dfn-set-of-event-types-to-handle){#ref-for-dfn-set-of-event-types-to-handle③
    link-type="dfn"} does not
    [contain](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-contain){#ref-for-list-contain②
    link-type="dfn"} `fetch`, then return true.

2.  Let `eventHandler`{.variable} be `workerGlobalScope`{.variable}'s
    [event handler
    map](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-handler-map){#ref-for-event-handler-map
    link-type="dfn"}\[\"onfetch\"\]\'s value.

3.  Let `eventListenerCallbacks`{.variable} be the result of calling
    [legacy-obtain service worker fetch event listener
    callbacks](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#legacy-obtain-service-worker-fetch-event-listener-callbacks){#ref-for-legacy-obtain-service-worker-fetch-event-listener-callbacks
    link-type="dfn"} given `workerGlobalScope`{.variable}.

4.  [For
    each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate){#ref-for-list-iterate①⑥
    link-type="dfn"} `eventListenerCallback`{.variable} of
    `eventListenerCallbacks`{.variable}:

    1.  Let `callback`{.variable} be null.

    2.  If `eventHandler`{.variable} is not null and
        `eventListenerCallback`{.variable} equals
        `eventHandler`{.variable}'s
        [listener](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-handler-listener){#ref-for-event-handler-listener
        link-type="dfn"}'s
        [callback](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#event-listener-callback){#ref-for-event-listener-callback
        link-type="dfn"}, then set `callback`{.variable} to the result
        of [converting to an ECMAScript
        value](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-convert-idl-to-javascript-value){#ref-for-dfn-convert-idl-to-javascript-value
        link-type="dfn"} `eventHandler`{.variable}'s
        [value](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-handler-value){#ref-for-event-handler-value
        link-type="dfn"}.

    3.  Otherwise, set `callback`{.variable} to the result of
        [converting to an ECMAScript
        value](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-convert-idl-to-javascript-value){#ref-for-dfn-convert-idl-to-javascript-value①
        link-type="dfn"} `eventListenerCallback`{.variable}.

    4.  If
        [IsCallable](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-iscallable){#ref-for-sec-iscallable
        link-type="abstract-op"}(`callback`{.variable}) is false, then
        return false.

        [Note:]{.marker}
        [Callback](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#event-listener-callback){#ref-for-event-listener-callback①
        link-type="dfn"} objects that use
        [`handleEvent(event)`{.idl}](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-eventlistener-handleevent){#ref-for-dom-eventlistener-handleevent
        link-type="idl"} are assumed to be non-empty. This avoids
        calling the
        [`handleEvent(event)`{.idl}](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-eventlistener-handleevent){#ref-for-dom-eventlistener-handleevent①
        link-type="idl"} getters, which could modify the event listeners
        during this check.

    5.  If `callback`{.variable}'s [function
        body](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#prod-FunctionBody){#ref-for-prod-FunctionBody
        link-type="dfn"} is not empty (i.e. either a
        [statement](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations){#ref-for-sec-ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations
        link-type="dfn"} or
        [declaration](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations){#ref-for-sec-ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations①
        link-type="dfn" refhint-key="b5a40239"} exist), then return
        false.

    [Note:]{.marker} This detects \"`fetch`\" listeners like `() => {}`.
    Some sites have a fetch event listener with empty body to make them
    recognized by Chromium as a progressive web application (PWA).

5.  Return true.

[Note:]{.marker} User agents are encouraged to show a warning indicating
that empty \"`fetch`\" listeners are unnecessary, and may have a
negative performance impact.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="Terminate Service Worker"}
### [[Terminate Service Worker]{#terminate-service-worker .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}]{.content}[](#terminate-service-worker-algorithm){.self-link} {#terminate-service-worker-algorithm .heading .settled}

Input

:   `serviceWorker`{.variable}, a [service
    worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker①⓪⓪
    link-type="dfn"}

Output

:   None

1.  Run the following steps [in
    parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel){#ref-for-in-parallel③②
    link-type="dfn"} with `serviceWorker`{.variable}'s main loop:

    1.  Let `serviceWorkerGlobalScope`{.variable} be
        `serviceWorker`{.variable}'s [global
        object](#dfn-service-worker-global-object){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-global-object⑨
        link-type="dfn"}.

    2.  Set `serviceWorkerGlobalScope`{.variable}'s
        [closing](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/workers.html#dom-workerglobalscope-closing){#ref-for-dom-workerglobalscope-closing
        link-type="dfn"} flag to true.

    3.  [Remove](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-remove){#ref-for-list-remove
        link-type="dfn"} all the
        [items](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-item){#ref-for-list-item①⓪
        link-type="dfn"} from `serviceWorker`{.variable}'s [set of
        extended
        events](#dfn-set-of-extended-events){#ref-for-dfn-set-of-extended-events
        link-type="dfn"}.

    4.  If there are any
        [tasks](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-task){#ref-for-concept-task⑥
        link-type="dfn"}, whose [task
        source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#task-source){#ref-for-task-source⑥
        link-type="dfn"} is either the [handle fetch task
        source](#dfn-handle-fetch-task-source){#ref-for-dfn-handle-fetch-task-source①
        link-type="dfn"} or the [handle functional event task
        source](#dfn-handle-functional-event-task-source){#ref-for-dfn-handle-functional-event-task-source①
        link-type="dfn"}, queued in
        `serviceWorkerGlobalScope`{.variable}'s [event
        loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-loop){#ref-for-event-loop①③
        link-type="dfn"}'s [task
        queues](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#task-queue){#ref-for-task-queue④
        link-type="dfn"},
        [queue](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task){#ref-for-queue-a-task③③
        link-type="dfn"} them to `serviceWorker`{.variable}'s
        [containing service worker
        registration](#dfn-containing-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-containing-service-worker-registration①⑦
        link-type="dfn"}'s corresponding [task
        queues](#dfn-service-worker-registration-task-queue){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration-task-queue③
        link-type="dfn"} in the same order using their original [task
        sources](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#task-source){#ref-for-task-source⑦
        link-type="dfn"}, and discard all the
        [tasks](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-task){#ref-for-concept-task⑦
        link-type="dfn"} (including
        [tasks](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-task){#ref-for-concept-task⑧
        link-type="dfn"} whose [task
        source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#task-source){#ref-for-task-source⑧
        link-type="dfn"} is neither the [handle fetch task
        source](#dfn-handle-fetch-task-source){#ref-for-dfn-handle-fetch-task-source②
        link-type="dfn"} nor the [handle functional event task
        source](#dfn-handle-functional-event-task-source){#ref-for-dfn-handle-functional-event-task-source②
        link-type="dfn"}) from `serviceWorkerGlobalScope`{.variable}'s
        [event
        loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-loop){#ref-for-event-loop①④
        link-type="dfn"}'s [task
        queues](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#task-queue){#ref-for-task-queue⑤
        link-type="dfn"} without processing them.

        [Note:]{.marker} This effectively means that the fetch events
        and the other functional events such as push events are backed
        up by the registration's task queues while the other tasks
        including message events are discarded.

    5.  [Abort the
        script](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#abort-a-running-script){#ref-for-abort-a-running-script
        link-type="dfn"} currently running in
        `serviceWorker`{.variable}.

    6.  Set `serviceWorker`{.variable}'s [start
        status](#service-worker-start-status){#ref-for-service-worker-start-status⑤
        link-type="dfn"} to null.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="Handle Fetch"}
### [[Handle Fetch]{#handle-fetch .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" export=""}]{.content}[](#on-fetch-request-algorithm){.self-link} {#on-fetch-request-algorithm .heading .settled}

The [Handle Fetch](#handle-fetch){#ref-for-handle-fetch⑦
link-type="dfn"} algorithm is the entry point for the
[fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch){#ref-for-concept-fetch①③
link-type="dfn"} handling handed to the [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker①⓪①
link-type="dfn"} context.

Input

:   `request`{.variable}, a
    [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request){#ref-for-concept-request⑦
    link-type="dfn"}

:   `fetchController`{.variable}, a [fetch
    controller](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-controller){#ref-for-fetch-controller①
    link-type="dfn"}

:   `useHighResPerformanceTimers`{.variable}, a boolean

Output

:   a
    [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response){#ref-for-concept-response①①
    link-type="dfn"} or [service worker timing
    info](#service-worker-timing-info){#ref-for-service-worker-timing-info
    link-type="dfn"}

1.  Let `registration`{.variable} be null.

2.  Let `client`{.variable} be `request`{.variable}'s
    [client](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-client){#ref-for-concept-request-client③
    link-type="dfn"}.

3.  Let `reservedClient`{.variable} be `request`{.variable}'s [reserved
    client](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-reserved-client){#ref-for-concept-request-reserved-client
    link-type="dfn"}.

4.  Let `preloadResponse`{.variable} be a new
    [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects){#ref-for-sec-promise-objects③①
    link-type="dfn"}.

5.  Let `workerRealm`{.variable} be null.

6.  Let `timingInfo`{.variable} be a new [service worker timing
    info](#service-worker-timing-info){#ref-for-service-worker-timing-info①
    link-type="dfn"}.

7.  Assert: `request`{.variable}'s
    [destination](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-destination){#ref-for-concept-request-destination②
    link-type="dfn"} is not \"`serviceworker`\".

8.  If `request`{.variable}'s
    [destination](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-destination){#ref-for-concept-request-destination③
    link-type="dfn"} is either \"`embed`\" or \"`object`\", then:

    1.  Return null.

9.  Else if `request`{.variable} is a [non-subresource
    request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#non-subresource-request){#ref-for-non-subresource-request
    link-type="dfn"}, then:

    1.  If `reservedClient`{.variable} is not null and is an
        [environment settings
        object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object){#ref-for-environment-settings-object②⓪
        link-type="dfn"}, then:

        1.  If `reservedClient`{.variable} is not a [secure
            context](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#secure-context){#ref-for-secure-context④
            link-type="dfn"}, return null.

    2.  Else:

        1.  If `request`{.variable}'s
            [url](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-url){#ref-for-concept-request-url⑧
            link-type="dfn"} is not a [potentially trustworthy
            URL](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-secure-contexts/#potentially-trustworthy-url){#ref-for-potentially-trustworthy-url
            link-type="dfn"}, return null.

    3.  If `request`{.variable} is a [navigation
        request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#navigation-request){#ref-for-navigation-request
        link-type="dfn"} and the
        [navigation](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsing-the-web.html#navigate){#ref-for-navigate⑥
        link-type="dfn"} triggering it was initiated with a shift+reload
        or equivalent, return null.

    4.  [Assert](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-algorithm-conventions){#ref-for-sec-algorithm-conventions
        link-type="dfn"} `reservedClient`{.variable} is not null.

    5.  Let `storage key`{.variable} be the result of running [obtain a
        storage
        key](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#obtain-a-storage-key){#ref-for-obtain-a-storage-key⑨
        link-type="dfn"} given `reservedClient`{.variable}.

    6.  Set `registration`{.variable} to the result of running [Match
        Service Worker
        Registration](#match-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-match-service-worker-registration⑦
        link-type="dfn"} given `storage key`{.variable} and
        `request`{.variable}'s
        [url](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-url){#ref-for-concept-request-url⑨
        link-type="dfn"}.

    7.  If `registration`{.variable} is null or
        `registration`{.variable}'s [active
        worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker③⑤
        link-type="dfn"} is null, return null.

    8.  If `request`{.variable}'s
        [destination](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-destination){#ref-for-concept-request-destination④
        link-type="dfn"} is not
        [`"report"`{.idl}](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-requestdestination-report){#ref-for-dom-requestdestination-report
        link-type="idl"}, set `reservedClient`{.variable}'s [active
        service
        worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker){#ref-for-concept-environment-active-service-worker②⑥
        link-type="dfn"} to `registration`{.variable}'s [active
        worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker③⑥
        link-type="dfn"}.

    [Note:]{.marker} From this point, the [service worker
    client](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client④④
    link-type="dfn"} starts to [use](#dfn-use){#ref-for-dfn-use④
    link-type="dfn"} its [active service
    worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker){#ref-for-concept-environment-active-service-worker②⑦
    link-type="dfn"}'s [containing service worker
    registration](#dfn-containing-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-containing-service-worker-registration①⑧
    link-type="dfn"}.

10. Else if `request`{.variable} is a [subresource
    request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#subresource-request){#ref-for-subresource-request
    link-type="dfn"}, then:

    1.  If `client`{.variable}'s [active service
        worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker){#ref-for-concept-environment-active-service-worker②⑧
        link-type="dfn"} is non-null, set `registration`{.variable} to
        `client`{.variable}'s [active service
        worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker){#ref-for-concept-environment-active-service-worker②⑨
        link-type="dfn"}'s [containing service worker
        registration](#dfn-containing-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-containing-service-worker-registration①⑨
        link-type="dfn"}.

    2.  Else, return null.

11. Let `activeWorker`{.variable} be `registration`{.variable}'s [active
    worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker③⑦
    link-type="dfn"}.

12. Let `shouldSoftUpdate`{.variable} be true if any of the following
    are true, and false otherwise:

    - `request`{.variable} is a [non-subresource
      request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#non-subresource-request){#ref-for-non-subresource-request①
      link-type="dfn"}.

    - `request`{.variable} is a [subresource
      request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#subresource-request){#ref-for-subresource-request①
      link-type="dfn"} and `registration`{.variable} is
      [stale](#service-worker-registration-stale){#ref-for-service-worker-registration-stale③
      link-type="dfn"}.

13. If `activeWorker`{.variable}'s [list of router
    rules](#service-worker-list-of-router-rules){#ref-for-service-worker-list-of-router-rules②
    link-type="dfn"} [is not
    empty](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-is-empty){#ref-for-list-is-empty③
    link-type="dfn"}:

    1.  Set `timingInfo`{.variable}'s [worker router evaluation
        start](#service-worker-timing-info-worker-router-evaluation-start){#ref-for-service-worker-timing-info-worker-router-evaluation-start
        link-type="dfn"} to the [coarsened shared current
        time](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dfn-coarsened-shared-current-time){#ref-for-dfn-coarsened-shared-current-time
        link-type="dfn"} given `useHighResPerformanceTimers`{.variable}.

    2.  Let `source`{.variable} be the result of running the [Get Router
        Source](#get-router-source){#ref-for-get-router-source
        link-type="dfn"} algorithm with `registration`{.variable}'s
        [active worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker③⑧
        link-type="dfn"} and `request`{.variable}.

    3.  If `source`{.variable} is non-null, then:

        1.  Set `timingInfo`{.variable}'s [worker matched router
            source](#service-worker-timing-info-worker-matched-router-source){#ref-for-service-worker-timing-info-worker-matched-router-source
            link-type="dfn"} be set to `source`{.variable}, and [worker
            final router
            source](#service-worker-timing-info-worker-final-router-source){#ref-for-service-worker-timing-info-worker-final-router-source
            link-type="dfn"} be set to
            [`"network"`{.idl}](#dom-routersourceenum-network){#ref-for-dom-routersourceenum-network
            link-type="idl"}.

        2.  If `source`{.variable} is
            [`"network"`{.idl}](#dom-routersourceenum-network){#ref-for-dom-routersourceenum-network①
            link-type="idl"}:

            1.  If `shouldSoftUpdate`{.variable} is true, then [in
                parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel){#ref-for-in-parallel③③
                link-type="dfn"} run the [Soft
                Update](#soft-update){#ref-for-soft-update①
                link-type="dfn"} algorithm with
                `registration`{.variable}.

            2.  Return `timingInfo`{.variable}.

        3.  Else if `source`{.variable} is
            [`"cache"`{.idl}](#dom-routersourceenum-cache){#ref-for-dom-routersourceenum-cache
            link-type="idl"}, or
            `source`{.variable}\[\"[`cacheName`{.idl}](#dom-routersourcedict-cachename){#ref-for-dom-routersourcedict-cachename
            link-type="idl"}\"\]
            [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists){#ref-for-map-exists⑦
            link-type="dfn"}, then:

            1.  If `shouldSoftUpdate`{.variable} is true, then [in
                parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel){#ref-for-in-parallel③④
                link-type="dfn"} run the [Soft
                Update](#soft-update){#ref-for-soft-update②
                link-type="dfn"} algorithm with
                `registration`{.variable}.

            2.  Set `timingInfo`{.variable}'s [worker cache lookup
                start](#service-worker-timing-info-worker-cache-lookup-start){#ref-for-service-worker-timing-info-worker-cache-lookup-start
                link-type="dfn"} to the [coarsened shared current
                time](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dfn-coarsened-shared-current-time){#ref-for-dfn-coarsened-shared-current-time①
                link-type="dfn"} given
                `useHighResPerformanceTimers`{.variable}.

            3.  Let `environment`{.variable} be null.

            4.  If `request`{.variable} is a [non-subresource
                request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#non-subresource-request){#ref-for-non-subresource-request②
                link-type="dfn"}, then:

                1.  Set `environment`{.variable} to
                    `reservedClient`{.variable}.

            5.  Else:

                1.  Set `environment`{.variable} to `client`{.variable}.

            6.  Let `caches`{.variable} be the result of running [obtain
                a local storage bottle
                map](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#obtain-a-local-storage-bottle-map){#ref-for-obtain-a-local-storage-bottle-map②
                link-type="dfn"} with `environment`{.variable} and
                \"`caches`\".

            7.  [For
                each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-iterate){#ref-for-map-iterate⑥
                link-type="dfn"} `cacheName`{.variable} →
                `cache`{.variable} of `caches`{.variable}.

                1.  If
                    `source`{.variable}\[\"[`cacheName`{.idl}](#dom-routersourcedict-cachename){#ref-for-dom-routersourcedict-cachename①
                    link-type="idl"}\"\]
                    [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists){#ref-for-map-exists⑧
                    link-type="dfn"} and
                    `source`{.variable}\[\"[`cacheName`{.idl}](#dom-routersourcedict-cachename){#ref-for-dom-routersourcedict-cachename②
                    link-type="idl"}\"\]
                    [is](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#string-is){#ref-for-string-is
                    link-type="dfn"} not `cacheName`{.variable},
                    [continue](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-continue){#ref-for-iteration-continue⑨
                    link-type="dfn"}.

                2.  Let `requestResponses`{.variable} be the result of
                    running [Query
                    Cache](#query-cache){#ref-for-query-cache②
                    link-type="dfn"} with `request`{.variable}, a new
                    [`CacheQueryOptions`{.idl}](#dictdef-cachequeryoptions){#ref-for-dictdef-cachequeryoptions⑥
                    link-type="idl"}, and `cache`{.variable}.

                3.  If `requestResponses`{.variable} is an empty
                    [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list){#ref-for-list②③
                    link-type="dfn"}, return `timingInfo`{.variable}.

                4.  Else:

                    1.  Let `requestResponse`{.variable} be the first
                        element of `requestResponses`{.variable}.

                    2.  Let `response`{.variable} be
                        `requestResponse`{.variable}'s response.

                    3.  Let `globalObject`{.variable} be
                        `activeWorker`{.variable}'s [global
                        object](#dfn-service-worker-global-object){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-global-object①⓪
                        link-type="dfn"}.

                    4.  If `globalObject`{.variable} is null:

                        1.  Set `globalObject`{.variable} to the result
                            of running [Setup
                            ServiceWorkerGlobalScope](#setup-serviceworkerglobalscope){#ref-for-setup-serviceworkerglobalscope①
                            link-type="dfn"} with
                            `activeWorker`{.variable}.

                    5.  If `globalObject`{.variable} is null, return
                        `timingInfo`{.variable}.

                    [Note:]{.marker} This only creates a
                    ServiceWorkerGlobalScope because CORS checks require
                    that. It is not expected that implementations will
                    actually create a ServiceWorkerGlobalScope here.

                    1.  If `response`{.variable}'s
                        [type](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-type){#ref-for-concept-response-type③
                        link-type="dfn"} is \"`opaque`\", and
                        [cross-origin resource policy
                        check](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#cross-origin-resource-policy-check){#ref-for-cross-origin-resource-policy-check①
                        link-type="dfn"} with
                        `globalObject`{.variable}'s
                        [origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-origin){#ref-for-concept-settings-object-origin①⑥
                        link-type="dfn"}, `globalObject`{.variable},
                        \"\", and `response`{.variable}'s [internal
                        response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-internal-response){#ref-for-concept-internal-response①
                        link-type="dfn"} returns **blocked**, then
                        return `timingInfo`{.variable}.

                    2.  Set `timingInfo`{.variable}'s [worker final
                        router
                        source](#service-worker-timing-info-worker-final-router-source){#ref-for-service-worker-timing-info-worker-final-router-source①
                        link-type="dfn"} be set to
                        [`"cache"`{.idl}](#dom-routersourceenum-cache){#ref-for-dom-routersourceenum-cache①
                        link-type="idl"}.

                    3.  Set `result`{.variable}'s [service worker timing
                        info](#service-worker-timing-info){#ref-for-service-worker-timing-info②
                        link-type="dfn"} be set to
                        `timingInfo`{.variable}.

                    4.  Return `response`{.variable}.

            8.  Return `timingInfo`{.variable}.

        4.  Else if `source`{.variable} is
            [`"race-network-and-fetch-handler"`{.idl}](#dom-routersourceenum-race-network-and-fetch-handler){#ref-for-dom-routersourceenum-race-network-and-fetch-handler②
            link-type="idl"}, and `request`{.variable}'s
            [method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-method){#ref-for-concept-request-method⑤
            link-type="dfn"} is \``GET`\` then:

            1.  If `shouldSoftUpdate`{.variable} is true, then [in
                parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel){#ref-for-in-parallel③⑤
                link-type="dfn"} run the [Soft
                Update](#soft-update){#ref-for-soft-update③
                link-type="dfn"} algorithm with
                `registration`{.variable}.

            2.  Let `queue`{.variable} be an empty
                [queue](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#queue){#ref-for-queue①
                link-type="dfn"} of [race
                result](#dfn-race-result){#ref-for-dfn-race-result②
                link-type="dfn"}.

            3.  Let `raceFetchController`{.variable} be null.

            4.  Let `raceResponse`{.variable} be a [race
                response](#dfn-race-response){#ref-for-dfn-race-response①
                link-type="dfn"} whose
                [value](#race-response-value){#ref-for-race-response-value
                link-type="dfn"} is \"`pending`\".

            5.  Run the following substeps [in
                parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel){#ref-for-in-parallel③⑥
                link-type="dfn"}:

                1.  If `fetchController`{.variable}'s
                    [state](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-controller-state){#ref-for-fetch-controller-state
                    link-type="dfn"} is \"`terminated`\" or
                    \"`aborted`\", then set `raceResponse`{.variable} to
                    a [race
                    response](#dfn-race-response){#ref-for-dfn-race-response②
                    link-type="dfn"} whose
                    [value](#race-response-value){#ref-for-race-response-value①
                    link-type="dfn"} is null, and abort these steps.

                2.  Set `raceFetchController`{.variable} to the result
                    of calling
                    [fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch){#ref-for-concept-fetch①④
                    link-type="dfn"} given `request`{.variable}, with
                    [processResponse](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#process-response){#ref-for-process-response②
                    link-type="dfn"} set to the following steps given a
                    [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response){#ref-for-concept-response①②
                    link-type="dfn"}
                    `raceNetworkRequestResponse`{.variable}:

                    1.  Run these steps, but [abort
                        when](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#abort-when){#ref-for-abort-when
                        link-type="dfn"} `fetchController`{.variable}'s
                        [state](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-controller-state){#ref-for-fetch-controller-state①
                        link-type="dfn"} is \"`terminated`\" or
                        \"`aborted`\".

                        1.  Set `raceResponse`{.variable}'s
                            [value](#race-response-value){#ref-for-race-response-value②
                            link-type="dfn"} to
                            `raceNetworkRequestResponse`{.variable}.

                        2.  If `raceNetworkRequestResponse`{.variable}'s
                            [status](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-status){#ref-for-concept-response-status③
                            link-type="dfn"} is [ok
                            status](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#ok-status){#ref-for-ok-status②
                            link-type="dfn"}, then:

                            1.  Let `raceNetworkResult`{.variable} be a
                                [race
                                result](#dfn-race-result){#ref-for-dfn-race-result③
                                link-type="dfn"} whose [routed
                                response](#race-result-routed-response){#ref-for-race-result-routed-response①
                                link-type="dfn"} is
                                `raceNetworkRequestResponse`{.variable}
                                and [used
                                route](#race-result-used-route){#ref-for-race-result-used-route①
                                link-type="dfn"} is
                                [`"network"`{.idl}](#dom-routersourceenum-network){#ref-for-dom-routersourceenum-network②
                                link-type="idl"}.

                            2.  [Enqueue](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#queue-enqueue){#ref-for-queue-enqueue③
                                link-type="dfn"}
                                `raceNetworkResult`{.variable} to
                                `queue`{.variable}.

                    2.  [If
                        aborted](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#if-aborted){#ref-for-if-aborted
                        link-type="dfn"} and
                        `raceFetchController`{.variable} is not null,
                        then:

                        1.  [Abort](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-controller-abort){#ref-for-fetch-controller-abort②
                            link-type="dfn"}
                            `raceFetchController`{.variable}.

                        2.  Set `raceResponse`{.variable} to a [race
                            response](#dfn-race-response){#ref-for-dfn-race-response③
                            link-type="dfn"} whose
                            [value](#race-response-value){#ref-for-race-response-value③
                            link-type="dfn"} is null.

            6.  Resolve `preloadResponse`{.variable} with undefined.

            7.  Run the following substeps [in
                parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel){#ref-for-in-parallel③⑦
                link-type="dfn"}:

                1.  Let `fetchHandlerResponse`{.variable} be the result
                    of [Create Fetch Event and
                    Dispatch](#create-fetch-event-and-dispatch){#ref-for-create-fetch-event-and-dispatch
                    link-type="dfn"} with `request`{.variable},
                    `registration`{.variable},
                    `useHighResPerformanceTimers`{.variable},
                    `timingInfo`{.variable}, `workerRealm`{.variable},
                    `reservedClient`{.variable},
                    `preloadResponse`{.variable}, and
                    `raceResponse`{.variable}.

                2.  If `fetchHandlerResponse`{.variable} is not null and
                    not a [network
                    error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error){#ref-for-concept-network-error⑦
                    link-type="dfn"}, and
                    `raceFetchController`{.variable} is not null,
                    [abort](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-controller-abort){#ref-for-fetch-controller-abort③
                    link-type="dfn"} `raceFetchController`{.variable}.

                3.  Let `raceFetchHandlerResult`{.variable} be a [race
                    result](#dfn-race-result){#ref-for-dfn-race-result④
                    link-type="dfn"} whose [routed
                    response](#race-result-routed-response){#ref-for-race-result-routed-response②
                    link-type="dfn"} is
                    `fetchHandlerResponse`{.variable} and [used
                    route](#race-result-used-route){#ref-for-race-result-used-route②
                    link-type="dfn"} is
                    [`"fetch-event"`{.idl}](#dom-routersourceenum-fetch-event){#ref-for-dom-routersourceenum-fetch-event①
                    link-type="idl"}.

                4.  [Enqueue](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#queue-enqueue){#ref-for-queue-enqueue④
                    link-type="dfn"} `raceFetchHandlerResult`{.variable}
                    to `queue`{.variable}.

            8.  Wait until `queue`{.variable} is not empty.

            9.  Let `result`{.variable} be the result of
                [dequeue](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#queue-dequeue){#ref-for-queue-dequeue①
                link-type="dfn"} `queue`{.variable}.

            10. Let `routedResponse`{.variable} be `result`{.variable}'s
                [routed
                response](#race-result-routed-response){#ref-for-race-result-routed-response③
                link-type="dfn"}.

            11. If `routedResponse`{.variable} is null:

                1.  Return `timingInfo`{.variable}.

            12. If `result`{.variable}'s [used
                route](#race-result-used-route){#ref-for-race-result-used-route③
                link-type="dfn"} is
                [`"network"`{.idl}](#dom-routersourceenum-network){#ref-for-dom-routersourceenum-network③
                link-type="idl"}, then:

                1.  Set `routedResponse`{.variable}'s [service worker
                    timing
                    info](#service-worker-timing-info){#ref-for-service-worker-timing-info③
                    link-type="dfn"} be set to `timingInfo`{.variable}.

            13. Set `routedResponse`{.variable}'s [service worker timing
                info](#service-worker-timing-info){#ref-for-service-worker-timing-info④
                link-type="dfn"}'s [worker final router
                source](#service-worker-timing-info-worker-final-router-source){#ref-for-service-worker-timing-info-worker-final-router-source②
                link-type="dfn"} be set to `result`{.variable}'s [used
                route](#race-result-used-route){#ref-for-race-result-used-route④
                link-type="dfn"}.

            14. Return `routedResponse`{.variable}.

        5.  Assert: `source`{.variable} is
            \"[`fetch-event`{.idl}](#dom-routersourceenum-fetch-event){#ref-for-dom-routersourceenum-fetch-event②
            link-type="idl"}\"

14. Let `responseForAutoPreload`{.variable} be null.

15. If `request`{.variable} is a [navigation
    request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#navigation-request){#ref-for-navigation-request①
    link-type="dfn"}, `request`{.variable}'s
    [method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-method){#ref-for-concept-request-method⑥
    link-type="dfn"} is \``GET`\`, `registration`{.variable}'s [active
    worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker③⑨
    link-type="dfn"}'s [set of event types to
    handle](#dfn-set-of-event-types-to-handle){#ref-for-dfn-set-of-event-types-to-handle④
    link-type="dfn"}
    [contains](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-contain){#ref-for-list-contain③
    link-type="dfn"} `fetch`, and `registration`{.variable}'s [active
    worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker④⓪
    link-type="dfn"}'s [all fetch listeners are empty
    flag](#service-worker-all-fetch-listeners-are-empty-flag){#ref-for-service-worker-all-fetch-listeners-are-empty-flag②
    link-type="dfn"} is not set then:

    1.  If `registration`{.variable}'s [navigation preload enabled
        flag](#service-worker-registration-navigation-preload-enabled-flag){#ref-for-service-worker-registration-navigation-preload-enabled-flag③
        link-type="dfn"} is set then:

        [Note:]{.marker} If the above is true except
        `registration`{.variable}'s [active
        worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker④①
        link-type="dfn"}'s [set of event types to
        handle](#dfn-set-of-event-types-to-handle){#ref-for-dfn-set-of-event-types-to-handle⑤
        link-type="dfn"} **does not** contain `fetch`, then the user
        agent may wish to show a console warning, as the developer's
        intent isn't clear.

        1.  Let `preloadRequest`{.variable} be the result of
            [cloning](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-clone){#ref-for-concept-request-clone
            link-type="dfn"} the request `request`{.variable}.

        2.  Let `preloadRequestHeaders`{.variable} be
            `preloadRequest`{.variable}'s [header
            list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-header-list){#ref-for-concept-request-header-list①
            link-type="dfn"}.

        3.  Let `preloadResponseObject`{.variable} be a new
            [`Response`{.idl}](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response){#ref-for-response①④
            link-type="idl"} object associated with a new
            [`Headers`{.idl}](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#headers){#ref-for-headers②
            link-type="idl"} object whose
            [guard](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-guard){#ref-for-concept-headers-guard②
            link-type="dfn"} is \"`immutable`\".

        4.  [Append](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-list-append){#ref-for-concept-header-list-append
            link-type="dfn"} to `preloadRequestHeaders`{.variable} a new
            [header](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header){#ref-for-concept-header②
            link-type="dfn"} whose
            [name](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-name){#ref-for-concept-header-name②
            link-type="dfn"} is \``Service-Worker-Navigation-Preload`\`
            and
            [value](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-value){#ref-for-concept-header-value
            link-type="dfn"} is `registration`{.variable}'s [navigation
            preload header
            value](#service-worker-registration-navigation-preload-header-value){#ref-for-service-worker-registration-navigation-preload-header-value②
            link-type="dfn"}.

        5.  Set `preloadRequest`{.variable}'s [service-workers
            mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-service-workers-mode){#ref-for-request-service-workers-mode③
            link-type="dfn"} to \"`none`\".

        6.  Let `preloadFetchController`{.variable} be null.

        7.  Run the following substeps [in
            parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel){#ref-for-in-parallel③⑧
            link-type="dfn"}, but [abort
            when](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#abort-when){#ref-for-abort-when①
            link-type="dfn"} `fetchController`{.variable}'s
            [state](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-controller-state){#ref-for-fetch-controller-state②
            link-type="dfn"} is \"`terminated`\" or \"`aborted`\":

            1.  Set `preloadFetchController`{.variable} to the result of
                [fetching](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch){#ref-for-concept-fetch①⑤
                link-type="dfn"} `preloadRequest`{.variable}.

                To
                [processResponse](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#process-response){#ref-for-process-response③
                link-type="dfn"} for
                `navigationPreloadResponse`{.variable}, run these
                substeps:

                1.  If `navigationPreloadResponse`{.variable}'s
                    [type](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-type){#ref-for-concept-response-type④
                    link-type="dfn"} is \"`error`\", reject
                    `preloadResponse`{.variable} with a `TypeError` and
                    terminate these substeps.

                2.  Associate `preloadResponseObject`{.variable} with
                    `navigationPreloadResponse`{.variable}.

                3.  Resolve `preloadResponse`{.variable} with
                    `preloadResponseObject`{.variable}.

        8.  [If
            aborted](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#if-aborted){#ref-for-if-aborted①
            link-type="dfn"}, then:

            1.  Let `deserializedError`{.variable} be the result of
                [deserialize a serialized abort
                reason](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#deserialize-a-serialized-abort-reason){#ref-for-deserialize-a-serialized-abort-reason
                link-type="dfn"} given null and
                `workerRealm`{.variable}.

            2.  [Abort](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-controller-abort){#ref-for-fetch-controller-abort④
                link-type="dfn"} `preloadFetchController`{.variable}
                with `deserializedError`{.variable}.

    2.  Else if `timingInfo`{.variable}'s [worker matched router
        source](#service-worker-timing-info-worker-matched-router-source){#ref-for-service-worker-timing-info-worker-matched-router-source①
        link-type="dfn"} is not
        \"[`fetch-event`{.idl}](#dom-routersourceenum-fetch-event){#ref-for-dom-routersourceenum-fetch-event③
        link-type="idl"}\", a user agent may run the following substeps:

        [Note:]{.marker} A user agent may speculatively dispatch a
        network request in parallel with creating a fetch event in order
        to minimize the bootstrap cost.

        1.  Assert: `timingInfo`{.variable}'s [worker matched router
            source](#service-worker-timing-info-worker-matched-router-source){#ref-for-service-worker-timing-info-worker-matched-router-source②
            link-type="dfn"} is null.

        2.  Let `autoPreloadFetchController`{.variable} be null.

        3.  Set `responseForAutoPreload`{.variable} to be a [race
            response](#dfn-race-response){#ref-for-dfn-race-response④
            link-type="dfn"} whose
            [value](#race-response-value){#ref-for-race-response-value④
            link-type="dfn"} is \"`pending`\".

        4.  Run the following substeps [in
            parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel){#ref-for-in-parallel③⑨
            link-type="dfn"}, but [abort
            when](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#abort-when){#ref-for-abort-when②
            link-type="dfn"} `fetchController`{.variable}'s
            [state](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-controller-state){#ref-for-fetch-controller-state③
            link-type="dfn"} is \"`terminated`\" or \"`aborted`\":

            1.  Set `autoPreloadFetchController`{.variable} to the
                result of calling
                [fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch){#ref-for-concept-fetch①⑥
                link-type="dfn"} given `request`{.variable}, with
                [processResponse](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#process-response){#ref-for-process-response④
                link-type="dfn"} set to the following steps given a
                [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response){#ref-for-concept-response①③
                link-type="dfn"}
                `autoPreloadRequestResponse`{.variable}:

                1.  Set `responseForAutoPreload`{.variable}'s
                    [value](#race-response-value){#ref-for-race-response-value⑤
                    link-type="dfn"} to
                    `autoPreloadRequestResponse`{.variable}.

        5.  [If
            aborted](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#if-aborted){#ref-for-if-aborted②
            link-type="dfn"} and `autoPreloadFetchController`{.variable}
            is not null, then:

            1.  [Abort](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-controller-abort){#ref-for-fetch-controller-abort⑤
                link-type="dfn"}
                `autoPreloadFetchController`{.variable}.

            2.  Set `responseForAutoPreload`{.variable} to a [race
                response](#dfn-race-response){#ref-for-dfn-race-response⑤
                link-type="dfn"} whose
                [value](#race-response-value){#ref-for-race-response-value⑥
                link-type="dfn"} is null.

        6.  Resolve `preloadResponse`{.variable} with undefined.

16. Else, resolve `preloadResponse`{.variable} with undefined.

17. Let `fetchResult`{.variable} be the result of [Create Fetch Event
    and
    Dispatch](#create-fetch-event-and-dispatch){#ref-for-create-fetch-event-and-dispatch①
    link-type="dfn"} with `request`{.variable},
    `registration`{.variable}, `useHighResPerformanceTimers`{.variable},
    `timingInfo`{.variable}, `workerRealm`{.variable},
    `reservedClient`{.variable}, `preloadResponse`{.variable}, and
    `responseForAutoPreload`{.variable}.

18. If `timingInfo`{.variable}'s [worker final router
    source](#service-worker-timing-info-worker-final-router-source){#ref-for-service-worker-timing-info-worker-final-router-source③
    link-type="dfn"} is not an empty string:

    1.  Assert `timingInfo`{.variable}'s [worker final router
        source](#service-worker-timing-info-worker-final-router-source){#ref-for-service-worker-timing-info-worker-final-router-source④
        link-type="dfn"} is
        [`"network"`{.idl}](#dom-routersourceenum-network){#ref-for-dom-routersourceenum-network④
        link-type="idl"}.

    2.  If `fetchResult`{.variable} is null, then return
        `timingInfo`{.variable}.

    3.  Else:

        1.  Assert `fetchResult`{.variable}'s [service worker timing
            info](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response-service-worker-timing-info){#ref-for-response-service-worker-timing-info
            link-type="dfn"}'s [worker final router
            source](#service-worker-timing-info-worker-final-router-source){#ref-for-service-worker-timing-info-worker-final-router-source⑤
            link-type="dfn"} is
            [`"network"`{.idl}](#dom-routersourceenum-network){#ref-for-dom-routersourceenum-network⑤
            link-type="idl"}.

        2.  Set `fetchResult`{.variable}'s [service worker timing
            info](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response-service-worker-timing-info){#ref-for-response-service-worker-timing-info①
            link-type="dfn"}'s [worker final router
            source](#service-worker-timing-info-worker-final-router-source){#ref-for-service-worker-timing-info-worker-final-router-source⑥
            link-type="dfn"} to
            [`"fetch-event"`{.idl}](#dom-routersourceenum-fetch-event){#ref-for-dom-routersourceenum-fetch-event④
            link-type="idl"}.

19. Return `fetchResult`{.variable}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="Create Fetch Event and Dispatch"}
### [[Create Fetch Event and Dispatch]{#create-fetch-event-and-dispatch .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}]{.content}[](#create-fetch-event-and-dispatch-algorithm){.self-link} {#create-fetch-event-and-dispatch-algorithm .heading .settled}

Input

:   `request`{.variable}, a
    [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request){#ref-for-concept-request⑧
    link-type="dfn"}

:   `registration`{.variable}, a [service worker
    registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration⑤⑦
    link-type="dfn"}

:   `useHighResPerformanceTimers`{.variable}, a boolean

:   `timingInfo`{.variable}, a [service worker timing
    info](#service-worker-timing-info){#ref-for-service-worker-timing-info⑤
    link-type="dfn"}

:   `workerRealm`{.variable}, a [relevant
    realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-realm){#ref-for-concept-relevant-realm⑨
    link-type="dfn"} of the [global
    object](#dfn-service-worker-global-object){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-global-object①①
    link-type="dfn"}

:   `reservedClient`{.variable}, a [reserved
    client](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-reserved-client){#ref-for-concept-request-reserved-client①
    link-type="dfn"}

:   `preloadResponse`{.variable}, a
    [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects){#ref-for-sec-promise-objects③②
    link-type="dfn"}

:   `raceResponse`{.variable}, a [race
    response](#dfn-race-response){#ref-for-dfn-race-response⑥
    link-type="dfn"} or null

Output

:   a
    [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response){#ref-for-concept-response①④
    link-type="dfn"} or null

1.  Let `response`{.variable} be null.

2.  Let `eventCanceled`{.variable} be false.

3.  Let `client`{.variable} be `request`{.variable}'s
    [client](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-client){#ref-for-concept-request-client④
    link-type="dfn"}.

4.  Let `activeWorker`{.variable} be `registration`{.variable}'s [active
    worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker④②
    link-type="dfn"}.

5.  Let `eventHandled`{.variable} be null.

6.  Let `handleFetchFailed`{.variable} be false.

7.  Let `respondWithEntered`{.variable} be false.

8.  Let `networkError`{.variable} be a [network
    error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error){#ref-for-concept-network-error⑧
    link-type="dfn"}.

9.  If `raceResponse`{.variable} is not null:

    1.  Set `networkError`{.variable}'s [service worker timing
        info](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response-service-worker-timing-info){#ref-for-response-service-worker-timing-info②
        link-type="dfn"} to `timingInfo`{.variable}.

10. Let `shouldSoftUpdate`{.variable} be true if any of the following
    are true, and false otherwise:

    - `request`{.variable} is a [non-subresource
      request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#non-subresource-request){#ref-for-non-subresource-request③
      link-type="dfn"}.

    - `request`{.variable} is a [subresource
      request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#subresource-request){#ref-for-subresource-request②
      link-type="dfn"} and `registration`{.variable} is
      [stale](#service-worker-registration-stale){#ref-for-service-worker-registration-stale④
      link-type="dfn"}.

11. If the result of running the [Should Skip
    Event](#should-skip-event){#ref-for-should-skip-event③
    link-type="dfn"} algorithm with \"fetch\" and
    `activeWorker`{.variable} is true, then:

    1.  If `shouldSoftUpdate`{.variable} is true, then [in
        parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel){#ref-for-in-parallel④⓪
        link-type="dfn"} run the [Soft
        Update](#soft-update){#ref-for-soft-update④ link-type="dfn"}
        algorithm with `registration`{.variable}.

    2.  Return null.

12. If `activeWorker`{.variable}'s [all fetch listeners are empty
    flag](#service-worker-all-fetch-listeners-are-empty-flag){#ref-for-service-worker-all-fetch-listeners-are-empty-flag③
    link-type="dfn"} is set:

    1.  [In
        parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel){#ref-for-in-parallel④①
        link-type="dfn"}:

        1.  If `activeWorker`{.variable}'s
            [state](#dfn-state){#ref-for-dfn-state①⓪ link-type="dfn"} is
            \"activating\", then wait for `activeWorker`{.variable}'s
            [state](#dfn-state){#ref-for-dfn-state①① link-type="dfn"} to
            become \"activated\".

        2.  Run the [Run Service
            Worker](#run-service-worker){#ref-for-run-service-worker⑦
            link-type="dfn"} algorithm with `activeWorker`{.variable}.

        3.  If `shouldSoftUpdate`{.variable} is true, then run the [Soft
            Update](#soft-update){#ref-for-soft-update⑤ link-type="dfn"}
            algorithm with `registration`{.variable}.

    2.  Return null.

13. If `useHighResPerformanceTimers`{.variable} is true, then set
    `useHighResPerformanceTimers`{.variable} to
    `activeWorker`{.variable}'s [global
    object](#dfn-service-worker-global-object){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-global-object①②
    link-type="dfn"}'s [cross-origin isolated
    capability](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/workers.html#concept-workerglobalscope-cross-origin-isolated-capability){#ref-for-concept-workerglobalscope-cross-origin-isolated-capability①
    link-type="dfn"}.

14. Let `timingInfo`{.variable}'s [start
    time](#service-worker-timing-info-start-time){#ref-for-service-worker-timing-info-start-time
    link-type="dfn"} be the [coarsened shared current
    time](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dfn-coarsened-shared-current-time){#ref-for-dfn-coarsened-shared-current-time②
    link-type="dfn"} given `useHighResPerformanceTimers`{.variable}.

15. If `activeWorker`{.variable}'s
    [state](#dfn-state){#ref-for-dfn-state①② link-type="dfn"} is
    \"`activating`\", wait for `activeWorker`{.variable}'s
    [state](#dfn-state){#ref-for-dfn-state①③ link-type="dfn"} to become
    \"`activated`\".

16. If the result of running the [Run Service
    Worker](#run-service-worker){#ref-for-run-service-worker⑧
    link-type="dfn"} algorithm with `activeWorker`{.variable} is
    *failure*, then set `handleFetchFailed`{.variable} to true.

17. Else:

    1.  Set `workerRealm`{.variable} to the [relevant
        realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-realm){#ref-for-concept-relevant-realm①⓪
        link-type="dfn"} of the `activeWorker`{.variable}'s [global
        object](#dfn-service-worker-global-object){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-global-object①③
        link-type="dfn"}.

    2.  Set `eventHandled`{.variable} to [a new
        promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-new-promise){#ref-for-a-new-promise②⓪
        link-type="dfn"} in `workerRealm`{.variable}.

    3.  If `raceResponse`{.variable} is not null,
        [set](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-set){#ref-for-map-set③
        link-type="dfn"} `activeWorker`{.variable}'s [global
        object](#dfn-service-worker-global-object){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-global-object①④
        link-type="dfn"}'s [race response
        map](#serviceworkerglobalscope-race-response-map){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope-race-response-map
        link-type="dfn"}\[`request`{.variable}\] to
        `raceResponse`{.variable}.

    4.  [Queue a
        task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task){#ref-for-queue-a-task③④
        link-type="dfn"} `task`{.variable} to run the following
        substeps:

        1.  Let `e`{.variable} be the result of [creating an
            event](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event-create){#ref-for-concept-event-create④
            link-type="dfn"} with
            [`FetchEvent`{.idl}](#fetchevent){#ref-for-fetchevent④
            link-type="idl"}.

        2.  Let `abortController`{.variable} be a
            [new](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#new){#ref-for-new
            link-type="dfn"}
            [`AbortController`{.idl}](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#abortcontroller){#ref-for-abortcontroller
            link-type="idl"} object with `workerRealm`{.variable}.

        3.  Let `requestObject`{.variable} be the result of
            [creating](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-create){#ref-for-request-create
            link-type="dfn"} a
            [`Request`{.idl}](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request){#ref-for-request①④
            link-type="idl"} object, given `request`{.variable}, a new
            [`Headers`{.idl}](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#headers){#ref-for-headers③
            link-type="idl"} object's
            [guard](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-guard){#ref-for-concept-headers-guard③
            link-type="dfn"} which is \"`immutable`\",
            `abortController`{.variable}'s
            [signal](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#abortcontroller-signal){#ref-for-abortcontroller-signal
            link-type="dfn"}, and `workerRealm`{.variable}.

        4.  Initialize `e`{.variable}'s
            [`type`{.idl}](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-event-type){#ref-for-dom-event-type②
            link-type="idl"} attribute to
            [`fetch`{.idl}](#service-worker-global-scope-fetch-event){#ref-for-service-worker-global-scope-fetch-event⑦
            .idl-code link-type="event"}.

        5.  Initialize `e`{.variable}'s
            [`cancelable`{.idl}](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-event-cancelable){#ref-for-dom-event-cancelable
            link-type="idl"} attribute to true.

        6.  Initialize `e`{.variable}'s
            [`request`{.idl}](#dom-fetchevent-request){#ref-for-dom-fetchevent-request②
            link-type="idl"} attribute to `requestObject`{.variable}.

        7.  Initialize `e`{.variable}'s
            [`preloadResponse`{.idl}](#dom-fetchevent-preloadresponse){#ref-for-dom-fetchevent-preloadresponse②
            link-type="idl"} to `preloadResponse`{.variable}.

        8.  Initialize `e`{.variable}'s
            [`clientId`{.idl}](#dom-fetchevent-clientid){#ref-for-dom-fetchevent-clientid②
            link-type="idl"} attribute to `client`{.variable}'s
            [id](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-id){#ref-for-concept-environment-id③
            link-type="dfn"}.

        9.  If `request`{.variable} is a [non-subresource
            request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#non-subresource-request){#ref-for-non-subresource-request④
            link-type="dfn"} and `request`{.variable}'s
            [destination](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-destination){#ref-for-concept-request-destination⑤
            link-type="dfn"} is not
            [`"report"`{.idl}](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-requestdestination-report){#ref-for-dom-requestdestination-report①
            link-type="idl"}, initialize `e`{.variable}'s
            [`resultingClientId`{.idl}](#dom-fetchevent-resultingclientid){#ref-for-dom-fetchevent-resultingclientid②
            link-type="idl"} attribute to `reservedClient`{.variable}'s
            [id](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-id){#ref-for-concept-environment-id④
            link-type="dfn"}, and to the empty string otherwise.

        10. If `request`{.variable} is a [navigation
            request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#navigation-request){#ref-for-navigation-request②
            link-type="dfn"}, initialize `e`{.variable}'s
            [`replacesClientId`{.idl}](#dom-fetchevent-replacesclientid){#ref-for-dom-fetchevent-replacesclientid②
            link-type="idl"} attribute to `request`{.variable}'s
            [replaces client
            id](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-replaces-client-id){#ref-for-concept-request-replaces-client-id
            link-type="dfn"}, and to the empty string otherwise.

        11. Initialize `e`{.variable}'s
            [`handled`{.idl}](#dom-fetchevent-handled){#ref-for-dom-fetchevent-handled②
            link-type="idl"} to `eventHandled`{.variable}.

        12. Let `timingInfo`{.variable}'s [fetch event dispatch
            time](#service-worker-timing-info-fetch-event-dispatch-time){#ref-for-service-worker-timing-info-fetch-event-dispatch-time
            link-type="dfn"} to the [coarsened shared current
            time](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dfn-coarsened-shared-current-time){#ref-for-dfn-coarsened-shared-current-time③
            link-type="dfn"} given
            `useHighResPerformanceTimers`{.variable}.

        13. [Dispatch](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event-dispatch){#ref-for-concept-event-dispatch⑥
            link-type="dfn"} `e`{.variable} at
            `activeWorker`{.variable}'s [global
            object](#dfn-service-worker-global-object){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-global-object①⑤
            link-type="dfn"}.

        14. Invoke [Update Service Worker Extended Events
            Set](#update-service-worker-extended-events-set){#ref-for-update-service-worker-extended-events-set①
            link-type="dfn"} with `activeWorker`{.variable} and
            `e`{.variable}.

        15. If `e`{.variable}'s [respond-with entered
            flag](#fetchevent-respond-with-entered-flag){#ref-for-fetchevent-respond-with-entered-flag②
            link-type="dfn"} is set, set `respondWithEntered`{.variable}
            to true.

        16. If `e`{.variable}'s [wait to respond
            flag](#fetchevent-wait-to-respond-flag){#ref-for-fetchevent-wait-to-respond-flag③
            link-type="dfn"} is set, then:

            1.  Wait until `e`{.variable}'s [wait to respond
                flag](#fetchevent-wait-to-respond-flag){#ref-for-fetchevent-wait-to-respond-flag④
                link-type="dfn"} is unset.

            2.  If `e`{.variable}'s [respond-with error
                flag](#fetchevent-respond-with-error-flag){#ref-for-fetchevent-respond-with-error-flag③
                link-type="dfn"} is set, set
                `handleFetchFailed`{.variable} to true.

            3.  Else, set `response`{.variable} to `e`{.variable}'s
                [potential
                response](#fetchevent-potential-response){#ref-for-fetchevent-potential-response①
                link-type="dfn"}.

        17. If `response`{.variable} is null, `request`{.variable}'s
            [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body){#ref-for-concept-request-body
            link-type="dfn"} is not null, and `request`{.variable}'s
            [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body){#ref-for-concept-request-body①
            link-type="dfn"}'s
            [source](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-source){#ref-for-concept-body-source
            link-type="dfn"} is null, then:

            1.  If `request`{.variable}'s
                [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body){#ref-for-concept-request-body②
                link-type="dfn"} is
                [unusable](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#body-unusable){#ref-for-body-unusable
                link-type="dfn"}, set `handleFetchFailed`{.variable} to
                true.

            2.  Else,
                [cancel](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-cancel){#ref-for-readablestream-cancel
                link-type="dfn"} `request`{.variable}'s
                [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body){#ref-for-concept-request-body③
                link-type="dfn"} with undefined.

        18. If `response`{.variable} is not null, then set
            `response`{.variable}'s [service worker timing
            info](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response-service-worker-timing-info){#ref-for-response-service-worker-timing-info③
            link-type="dfn"} to `timingInfo`{.variable}.

        19. If `e`{.variable}'s [canceled
            flag](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#canceled-flag){#ref-for-canceled-flag
            link-type="dfn"} is set, set `eventCanceled`{.variable} to
            true.

        20. If `fetchController`{.variable}
            [state](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-controller-state){#ref-for-fetch-controller-state④
            link-type="dfn"} is \"`terminated`\" or \"`aborted`\", then:

            1.  Let `deserializedError`{.variable} be the result of
                [deserialize a serialized abort
                reason](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#deserialize-a-serialized-abort-reason){#ref-for-deserialize-a-serialized-abort-reason①
                link-type="dfn"} given `fetchController`{.variable}'s
                [serialized abort
                reason](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-controller-serialized-abort-reason){#ref-for-fetch-controller-serialized-abort-reason
                link-type="dfn"} and `workerRealm`{.variable}.

            2.  [Queue a
                task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task){#ref-for-queue-a-task③⑤
                link-type="dfn"} to [signal
                abort](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#abortcontroller-signal-abort){#ref-for-abortcontroller-signal-abort
                link-type="dfn"} on `abortController`{.variable} with
                `deserializedError`{.variable}.

        If `task`{.variable} is discarded, set
        `handleFetchFailed`{.variable} to true.

        The `task`{.variable} *must* use `activeWorker`{.variable}'s
        [event
        loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-loop){#ref-for-event-loop①⑤
        link-type="dfn"} and the [handle fetch task
        source](#dfn-handle-fetch-task-source){#ref-for-dfn-handle-fetch-task-source③
        link-type="dfn"}.
