18. Wait for `task`{.variable} to have executed or for
    `handleFetchFailed`{.variable} to be true.

19. If `shouldSoftUpdate`{.variable} is true, then [in
    parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel){#ref-for-in-parallel④②
    link-type="dfn"} run the [Soft
    Update](#soft-update){#ref-for-soft-update⑥ link-type="dfn"}
    algorithm with `registration`{.variable}.

20. If `activeWorker`{.variable}'s [global
    object](#dfn-service-worker-global-object){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-global-object①⑥
    link-type="dfn"}'s [race response
    map](#serviceworkerglobalscope-race-response-map){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope-race-response-map①
    link-type="dfn"}\[`request`{.variable}\]
    [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists){#ref-for-map-exists⑨
    link-type="dfn"},
    [remove](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-remove){#ref-for-map-remove⑤
    link-type="dfn"} `activeWorker`{.variable}'s [global
    object](#dfn-service-worker-global-object){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-global-object①⑦
    link-type="dfn"}'s [race response
    map](#serviceworkerglobalscope-race-response-map){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope-race-response-map②
    link-type="dfn"}\[`request`{.variable}\].

21. If `respondWithEntered`{.variable} is false, then:

    1.  If `eventCanceled`{.variable} is true, then:

        1.  If `eventHandled`{.variable} is not null, then
            [reject](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#reject){#ref-for-reject③
            link-type="dfn"} `eventHandled`{.variable} with a
            \"[`NetworkError`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#networkerror){#ref-for-networkerror
            link-type="idl"}\"
            [`DOMException`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException){#ref-for-idl-DOMException②⓪
            link-type="idl"} in `workerRealm`{.variable}.

        2.  Return `networkError`{.variable}.

    2.  If `eventHandled`{.variable} is not null, then
        [resolve](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#resolve){#ref-for-resolve
        link-type="dfn"} `eventHandled`{.variable}.

    3.  If `raceResponse`{.variable} is not null, and
        `raceResponse`{.variable}'s
        [value](#race-response-value){#ref-for-race-response-value⑦
        link-type="dfn"} is not null, then:

        1.  Wait until `raceResponse`{.variable}'s
            [value](#race-response-value){#ref-for-race-response-value⑧
            link-type="dfn"} is not \"`pending`\".

        2.  If `raceResponse`{.variable}'s
            [value](#race-response-value){#ref-for-race-response-value⑨
            link-type="dfn"} is a
            [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response){#ref-for-concept-response①⑤
            link-type="dfn"}, return `raceResponse`{.variable}'s
            [value](#race-response-value){#ref-for-race-response-value①⓪
            link-type="dfn"}.

    4.  Return null.

22. If `handleFetchFailed`{.variable} is true, then:

    1.  If `eventHandled`{.variable} is not null, then
        [reject](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#reject){#ref-for-reject④
        link-type="dfn"} `eventHandled`{.variable} with a
        \"[`NetworkError`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#networkerror){#ref-for-networkerror①
        link-type="idl"}\"
        [`DOMException`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException){#ref-for-idl-DOMException②①
        link-type="idl"} in `workerRealm`{.variable}.

    2.  Return `networkError`{.variable}.

23. If `eventHandled`{.variable} is not null, then
    [resolve](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#resolve){#ref-for-resolve①
    link-type="dfn"} `eventHandled`{.variable}.

24. Return `response`{.variable}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="Parse URL Pattern"}
### [[Parse URL Pattern]{#parse-url-pattern .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}]{.content}[](#parse-urlpattern-algorithm){.self-link} {#parse-urlpattern-algorithm .heading .settled}

Input

:   `rawPattern`{.variable}, a
    [`URLPatternCompatible`{.idl}](https://urlpattern.spec.whatwg.org/#typedefdef-urlpatterncompatible){#ref-for-typedefdef-urlpatterncompatible①
    link-type="idl"}

:   `serviceWorker`{.variable}, a [service
    worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker①⓪②
    link-type="dfn"}

Output

:   A [URL
    pattern](https://urlpattern.spec.whatwg.org/#url-pattern){#ref-for-url-pattern
    link-type="dfn"}

1.  Let `baseURL`{.variable} be `serviceWorker`{.variable}'s [script
    url](#dfn-script-url){#ref-for-dfn-script-url①⓪ link-type="dfn"}.

2.  Return the result of [building a URL pattern from a Web IDL
    value](https://urlpattern.spec.whatwg.org/#build-a-url-pattern-from-a-web-idl-value){#ref-for-build-a-url-pattern-from-a-web-idl-value
    link-type="dfn"} `rawPattern`{.variable} given `baseURL`{.variable}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="Verify Router Condition"}
### [[Verify Router Condition]{#verify-router-condition .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}]{.content}[](#verify-router-rule-algorithm){.self-link} {#verify-router-rule-algorithm .heading .settled}

Input

:   `condition`{.variable}, a
    [`RouterCondition`{.idl}](#dictdef-routercondition){#ref-for-dictdef-routercondition③
    link-type="idl"}

:   `serviceWorker`{.variable}, a [service
    worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker①⓪③
    link-type="dfn"}

Output

:   a boolean

1.  Let `hasCondition`{.variable} be false.

2.  If
    `condition`{.variable}\[\"[`urlPattern`{.idl}](#dom-routercondition-urlpattern){#ref-for-dom-routercondition-urlpattern
    link-type="idl"}\"\]
    [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists){#ref-for-map-exists①⓪
    link-type="dfn"}, then:

    1.  Let `rawPattern`{.variable} be
        `condition`{.variable}\[\"[`urlPattern`{.idl}](#dom-routercondition-urlpattern){#ref-for-dom-routercondition-urlpattern①
        link-type="idl"}\"\].

    2.  Let `pattern`{.variable} be the result of running the [Parse URL
        Pattern](#parse-url-pattern){#ref-for-parse-url-pattern
        link-type="dfn"} algorithm passing `rawPattern`{.variable} and
        `serviceWorker`{.variable}. If this throws an exception, catch
        it and return false.

    3.  If `pattern`{.variable} [has regexp
        groups](https://urlpattern.spec.whatwg.org/#url-pattern-has-regexp-groups){#ref-for-url-pattern-has-regexp-groups
        link-type="dfn"}, then return false.

        [Note:]{.marker} Since running a user-defined regular expression
        has a security concern, it is prohibited.

    4.  Set `hasCondition`{.variable} to true.

3.  If
    `condition`{.variable}\[\"[`requestMethod`{.idl}](#dom-routercondition-requestmethod){#ref-for-dom-routercondition-requestmethod
    link-type="idl"}\"\]
    [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists){#ref-for-map-exists①①
    link-type="dfn"}:

    1.  Let `method`{.variable} be
        `condition`{.variable}\[\"[`requestMethod`{.idl}](#dom-routercondition-requestmethod){#ref-for-dom-routercondition-requestmethod①
        link-type="idl"}\"\].

    2.  If `method`{.variable} is not a
        [method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-method){#ref-for-concept-method
        link-type="dfn"}, then return false.

    3.  If `method`{.variable} is a [forbidden
        method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#forbidden-method){#ref-for-forbidden-method
        link-type="dfn"}, then return false.

    4.  Set `hasCondition`{.variable} to true.

4.  If
    `condition`{.variable}\[\"[`requestMode`{.idl}](#dom-routercondition-requestmode){#ref-for-dom-routercondition-requestmode
    link-type="idl"}\"\]
    [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists){#ref-for-map-exists①②
    link-type="dfn"}, set `hasCondition`{.variable} to true.

5.  If
    `condition`{.variable}\[\"[`requestDestination`{.idl}](#dom-routercondition-requestdestination){#ref-for-dom-routercondition-requestdestination
    link-type="idl"}\"\]
    [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists){#ref-for-map-exists①③
    link-type="dfn"}, set `hasCondition`{.variable} to true.

6.  If
    `condition`{.variable}\[\"[`runningStatus`{.idl}](#dom-routercondition-runningstatus){#ref-for-dom-routercondition-runningstatus
    link-type="idl"}\"\]
    [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists){#ref-for-map-exists①④
    link-type="dfn"}, set `hasCondition`{.variable} to true.

7.  If
    `condition`{.variable}\[\"[`_or`{.idl}](#dom-routercondition-or){#ref-for-dom-routercondition-or
    link-type="idl"}\"\]
    [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists){#ref-for-map-exists①⑤
    link-type="dfn"}, then:

    1.  If `hasCondition`{.variable} is true, return false.

        [Note:]{.marker} For ease of understanding the router rule, the
        \"or\" condition is mutually exclusive with other conditions.

    2.  Let `orConditions`{.variable} be
        `condition`{.variable}\[\"[`_or`{.idl}](#dom-routercondition-or){#ref-for-dom-routercondition-or①
        link-type="idl"}\"\].

    3.  For each `orCondition`{.variable} of `orConditions`{.variable}:

        1.  If running the [Verify Router
            Condition](#verify-router-condition){#ref-for-verify-router-condition①
            link-type="dfn"} algorithm with `orCondition`{.variable} and
            `serviceWorker`{.variable} returns false, return false.

    4.  Set `hasCondition`{.variable} to true.

8.  If
    `condition`{.variable}\[\"[`not`{.idl}](#dom-routercondition-not){#ref-for-dom-routercondition-not
    link-type="idl"}\"\]
    [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists){#ref-for-map-exists①⑥
    link-type="dfn"}, then:

    1.  If `hasCondition`{.variable} is true, return false.

        [Note:]{.marker} For ease of understanding the router rule, the
        \"not\" condition is mutually exclusive with other conditions.

    2.  If running the [Verify Router
        Condition](#verify-router-condition){#ref-for-verify-router-condition②
        link-type="dfn"} algorithm with
        `condition`{.variable}\[\"[`not`{.idl}](#dom-routercondition-not){#ref-for-dom-routercondition-not①
        link-type="idl"}\"\] and `serviceWorker`{.variable} returns
        false, return false.

    3.  Set `hasCondition`{.variable} to true.

9.  Return `hasCondition`{.variable}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="Match Router Condition"}
### [[Match Router Condition]{#match-router-condition .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}]{.content}[](#match-router-condition-algorithm){.self-link} {#match-router-condition-algorithm .heading .settled}

Input

:   `condition`{.variable}, a
    [`RouterCondition`{.idl}](#dictdef-routercondition){#ref-for-dictdef-routercondition④
    link-type="idl"}

:   `serviceWorker`{.variable}, a [service
    worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker①⓪④
    link-type="dfn"}

:   `request`{.variable}, a
    [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request){#ref-for-concept-request⑨
    link-type="dfn"}

Output

:   a boolean

[Note:]{.marker} if there are multiple conditions (e.g. `urlPattern`,
`runningStatus`, and `requestMethod` are set), all conditions need to
match for true to be returned.

1.  If
    `condition`{.variable}\[\"[`or`{.idl}](#dom-routercondition-or){#ref-for-dom-routercondition-or②
    link-type="idl"}\"\]
    [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists){#ref-for-map-exists①⑦
    link-type="dfn"}, then:

    1.  Let `orConditions`{.variable} be
        `condition`{.variable}\[\"[`or`{.idl}](#dom-routercondition-or){#ref-for-dom-routercondition-or③
        link-type="idl"}\"\].

    2.  For each `orCondition`{.variable} of `orConditions`{.variable}:

        1.  If running the [Match Router
            Condition](#match-router-condition){#ref-for-match-router-condition
            link-type="dfn"} algorithm with `orCondition`{.variable},
            `serviceWorker`{.variable} and `request`{.variable} returns
            true, then return true.

    3.  Return false.

2.  If
    `condition`{.variable}\[\"[`not`{.idl}](#dom-routercondition-not){#ref-for-dom-routercondition-not②
    link-type="idl"}\"\]
    [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists){#ref-for-map-exists①⑧
    link-type="dfn"}, then:

    1.  If running the [Match Router
        Condition](#match-router-condition){#ref-for-match-router-condition①
        link-type="dfn"} algorithm with
        `condition`{.variable}\[\"[`not`{.idl}](#dom-routercondition-not){#ref-for-dom-routercondition-not③
        link-type="idl"}\"\], `serviceWorker`{.variable} and
        `request`{.variable} returns true, then return false.

    2.  Return true.

3.  Else:

    [Note:]{.marker} The [Verify Router
    Condition](#verify-router-condition){#ref-for-verify-router-condition③
    link-type="dfn"} algorithm guarantees that
    [`or`{.idl}](#dom-routercondition-or){#ref-for-dom-routercondition-or④
    link-type="idl"},
    [`not`{.idl}](#dom-routercondition-not){#ref-for-dom-routercondition-not④
    link-type="idl"}, and other conditions are mutually exclusive.

    1.  If
        `condition`{.variable}\[\"[`urlPattern`{.idl}](#dom-routercondition-urlpattern){#ref-for-dom-routercondition-urlpattern②
        link-type="idl"}\"\]
        [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists){#ref-for-map-exists①⑨
        link-type="dfn"}, then:

        1.  Let `rawPattern`{.variable} be
            `condition`{.variable}\[\"[`urlPattern`{.idl}](#dom-routercondition-urlpattern){#ref-for-dom-routercondition-urlpattern③
            link-type="idl"}\"\].

        2.  Let `pattern`{.variable} be the result of running the [Parse
            URL Pattern](#parse-url-pattern){#ref-for-parse-url-pattern①
            link-type="dfn"} algorithm passing `rawPattern`{.variable}
            and `serviceWorker`{.variable}.

        3.  If running
            [match](https://urlpattern.spec.whatwg.org/#url-pattern-match){#ref-for-url-pattern-match
            link-type="dfn"} with `pattern`{.variable} and
            `request`{.variable}'s
            [URL](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-url){#ref-for-concept-request-url①⓪
            link-type="dfn"} returns null, return false.

    2.  If
        `condition`{.variable}\[\"[`requestMethod`{.idl}](#dom-routercondition-requestmethod){#ref-for-dom-routercondition-requestmethod②
        link-type="idl"}\"\]
        [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists){#ref-for-map-exists②⓪
        link-type="dfn"}, then:

        1.  Let `method`{.variable} be
            `condition`{.variable}\[\"[`requestMethod`{.idl}](#dom-routercondition-requestmethod){#ref-for-dom-routercondition-requestmethod③
            link-type="idl"}\"\].

        2.  [Normalize](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-method-normalize){#ref-for-concept-method-normalize
            link-type="dfn"} `method`{.variable}.

        3.  If `request`{.variable}'s
            [method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-method){#ref-for-concept-request-method⑦
            link-type="dfn"} is not `method`{.variable}, return false.

    3.  If
        `condition`{.variable}\[\"[`requestMode`{.idl}](#dom-routercondition-requestmode){#ref-for-dom-routercondition-requestmode①
        link-type="idl"}\"\]
        [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists){#ref-for-map-exists②①
        link-type="dfn"}, then:

        1.  Let `mode`{.variable} be
            `condition`{.variable}\[\"[`requestMode`{.idl}](#dom-routercondition-requestmode){#ref-for-dom-routercondition-requestmode②
            link-type="idl"}\"\].

        2.  If `request`{.variable}'s
            [mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-mode){#ref-for-concept-request-mode
            link-type="dfn"} is not `mode`{.variable}, return false.

    4.  If
        `condition`{.variable}\[\"[`requestDestination`{.idl}](#dom-routercondition-requestdestination){#ref-for-dom-routercondition-requestdestination①
        link-type="idl"}\"\]
        [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists){#ref-for-map-exists②②
        link-type="dfn"}, then:

        1.  Let `destination`{.variable} be
            `condition`{.variable}\[\"[`requestDestination`{.idl}](#dom-routercondition-requestdestination){#ref-for-dom-routercondition-requestdestination②
            link-type="idl"}\"\].

        2.  If `request`{.variable}'s
            [destination](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-destination){#ref-for-concept-request-destination⑥
            link-type="dfn"} is not `destination`{.variable}, return
            false.

    5.  If
        `condition`{.variable}\[\"[`runningStatus`{.idl}](#dom-routercondition-runningstatus){#ref-for-dom-routercondition-runningstatus①
        link-type="idl"}\"\]
        [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists){#ref-for-map-exists②③
        link-type="dfn"}, then:

        1.  Let `runningStatus`{.variable} be
            `condition`{.variable}\[\"[`runningStatus`{.idl}](#dom-routercondition-runningstatus){#ref-for-dom-routercondition-runningstatus②
            link-type="idl"}\"\].

        2.  If `runningStatus`{.variable} is
            [`"running"`{.idl}](#dom-runningstatus-running){#ref-for-dom-runningstatus-running
            link-type="idl"}, and `serviceWorker`{.variable} is not
            [running](#service-worker-running){#ref-for-service-worker-running④
            link-type="dfn"}, return false.

        3.  If `runningStatus`{.variable} is
            [`"not-running"`{.idl}](#dom-runningstatus-not-running){#ref-for-dom-runningstatus-not-running
            link-type="idl"}, and `serviceWorker`{.variable} is
            [running](#service-worker-running){#ref-for-service-worker-running⑤
            link-type="dfn"}, return false.

    6.  Return true.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="Check Router Registration Limit"}
### [[Check Router Registration Limit]{#check-router-registration-limit .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}]{.content}[](#check-router-registration-limit-algorithm){.self-link} {#check-router-registration-limit-algorithm .heading .settled}

Input

:   `routerRules`{.variable}, a [list of router
    rules](#service-worker-list-of-router-rules){#ref-for-service-worker-list-of-router-rules③
    link-type="dfn"}

Output

:   a boolean

[Note:]{.marker} Router conditions can be complex and nested using
[`_or`{.idl}](#dom-routercondition-or){#ref-for-dom-routercondition-or⑤
link-type="idl"} and
[`not`{.idl}](#dom-routercondition-not){#ref-for-dom-routercondition-not⑤
link-type="idl"}. To prevent excessive processing, this algorithm
introduces two limits. First, the total number of conditions, counting
all nested conditions, cannot exceed 1024. Second, the nesting depth is
limited to 10 levels to avoid exponential computation.

1.  Let `result`{.variable} be a [count router condition
    result](#dfn-count-router-condition-result){#ref-for-dfn-count-router-condition-result
    link-type="dfn"}.

2.  Set `result`{.variable}'s [condition
    count](#dfn-count-router-condition-result-condition-count){#ref-for-dfn-count-router-condition-result-condition-count
    link-type="dfn"} to 1024.

3.  Set `result`{.variable}'s [quota
    exceeded](#dfn-dfn-count-router-condition-result-quota-exceeded){#ref-for-dfn-dfn-count-router-condition-result-quota-exceeded
    link-type="dfn"} to false.

4.  [For
    each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate){#ref-for-list-iterate①⑦
    link-type="dfn"} `rule`{.variable} of `routerRules`{.variable}:

    1.  Set `result`{.variable} to be the result of running [Count
        Router Inner
        Conditions](#count-router-inner-conditions){#ref-for-count-router-inner-conditions
        link-type="dfn"} with
        `rule`{.variable}\[\"[`condition`{.idl}](#dom-routerrule-condition){#ref-for-dom-routerrule-condition①
        link-type="idl"}\"\], `result`{.variable}, and 10.

    2.  If `result`{.variable}'s [quota
        exceeded](#dfn-dfn-count-router-condition-result-quota-exceeded){#ref-for-dfn-dfn-count-router-condition-result-quota-exceeded①
        link-type="dfn"} is true, return false.

5.  Return true.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="Count Router Inner Conditions"}
### [[Count Router Inner Conditions]{#count-router-inner-conditions .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}]{.content}[](#count-router-inner-conditions-algorithm){.self-link} {#count-router-inner-conditions-algorithm .heading .settled}

Input

:   `condition`{.variable}, a
    [`RouterCondition`{.idl}](#dictdef-routercondition){#ref-for-dictdef-routercondition⑤
    link-type="idl"}

:   `result`{.variable}, a [count router condition
    result](#dfn-count-router-condition-result){#ref-for-dfn-count-router-condition-result①
    link-type="dfn"}

:   `depth`{.variable}, a number

Output

:   `result`{.variable}, a [count router condition
    result](#dfn-count-router-condition-result){#ref-for-dfn-count-router-condition-result②
    link-type="dfn"}

1.  Decrement `result`{.variable}'s [condition
    count](#dfn-count-router-condition-result-condition-count){#ref-for-dfn-count-router-condition-result-condition-count①
    link-type="dfn"} by one.

2.  If `result`{.variable}'s [condition
    count](#dfn-count-router-condition-result-condition-count){#ref-for-dfn-count-router-condition-result-condition-count②
    link-type="dfn"} is zero, or `depth`{.variable} is zero, then:

    1.  Set `result`{.variable}'s [quota
        exceeded](#dfn-dfn-count-router-condition-result-quota-exceeded){#ref-for-dfn-dfn-count-router-condition-result-quota-exceeded②
        link-type="dfn"} to be true.

    2.  Return `result`{.variable}.

3.  If
    `condition`{.variable}\[\"[`_or`{.idl}](#dom-routercondition-or){#ref-for-dom-routercondition-or⑥
    link-type="idl"}\"\]
    [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists){#ref-for-map-exists②④
    link-type="dfn"}, then:

    1.  Decrement `depth`{.variable} by one.

    2.  For each `orCondition`{.variable} of
        `condition`{.variable}\[\"[`_or`{.idl}](#dom-routercondition-or){#ref-for-dom-routercondition-or⑦
        link-type="idl"}\"\]:

        1.  Set `result`{.variable} to be the result of running [Count
            Router Inner
            Conditions](#count-router-inner-conditions){#ref-for-count-router-inner-conditions①
            link-type="dfn"} with `orCondition`{.variable},
            `result`{.variable}, and `depth`{.variable}.

        2.  If `result`{.variable}'s [quota
            exceeded](#dfn-dfn-count-router-condition-result-quota-exceeded){#ref-for-dfn-dfn-count-router-condition-result-quota-exceeded③
            link-type="dfn"} is true, return `result`{.variable}.

4.  Else if
    `condition`{.variable}\[\"[`not`{.idl}](#dom-routercondition-not){#ref-for-dom-routercondition-not⑥
    link-type="idl"}\"\]
    [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists){#ref-for-map-exists②⑤
    link-type="dfn"}, then:

    1.  Decrement `depth`{.variable} by one.

    2.  Set `result`{.variable} to be the result of running [Count
        Router Inner
        Conditions](#count-router-inner-conditions){#ref-for-count-router-inner-conditions②
        link-type="dfn"} with
        `condition`{.variable}\[\"[`not`{.idl}](#dom-routercondition-not){#ref-for-dom-routercondition-not⑦
        link-type="idl"}\"\], `result`{.variable}, and
        `depth`{.variable}.

    3.  If `result`{.variable}'s [quota
        exceeded](#dfn-dfn-count-router-condition-result-quota-exceeded){#ref-for-dfn-dfn-count-router-condition-result-quota-exceeded④
        link-type="dfn"} is true, return `result`{.variable}.

5.  Return `result`{.variable}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="Get Router Source"}
### [[Get Router Source]{#get-router-source .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}]{.content}[](#get-router-source-algorithm){.self-link} {#get-router-source-algorithm .heading .settled}

Input

:   `serviceWorker`{.variable}, a [service
    worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker①⓪⑤
    link-type="dfn"}

:   `request`{.variable}, a
    [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request){#ref-for-concept-request①⓪
    link-type="dfn"}

Output

:   [`RouterSource`{.idl}](#typedefdef-routersource){#ref-for-typedefdef-routersource①
    link-type="idl"} or null

1.  [For
    each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate){#ref-for-list-iterate①⑧
    link-type="dfn"} `rule`{.variable} of `serviceWorker`{.variable}'s
    [list of router
    rules](#service-worker-list-of-router-rules){#ref-for-service-worker-list-of-router-rules④
    link-type="dfn"}:

    1.  If running the [Match Router
        Condition](#match-router-condition){#ref-for-match-router-condition②
        link-type="dfn"} algorithm with
        `rule`{.variable}\[\"[`condition`{.idl}](#dom-routerrule-condition){#ref-for-dom-routerrule-condition②
        link-type="idl"}\"\], `serviceWorker`{.variable} and
        `request`{.variable} returns true, then return
        `rule`{.variable}\[\"[`source`{.idl}](#dom-routerrule-source){#ref-for-dom-routerrule-source①
        link-type="idl"}\"\].

2.  Return null.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="Should Skip Event"}
### [[Should Skip Event]{#should-skip-event .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}]{.content}[](#should-skip-event-algorithm){.self-link} {#should-skip-event-algorithm .heading .settled}

Input

:   `eventName`{.variable}, a string

:   `serviceWorker`{.variable}, a [service
    worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker①⓪⑥
    link-type="dfn"}

Output

:   a boolean

[Note:]{.marker} To avoid unnecessary delays, this specification permits
skipping event dispatch when no event listeners for the event have been
deterministically added in the service worker's global during the very
first script execution.

1.  If `serviceWorker`{.variable}'s [set of event types to
    handle](#dfn-set-of-event-types-to-handle){#ref-for-dfn-set-of-event-types-to-handle⑥
    link-type="dfn"} does not
    [contain](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-contain){#ref-for-list-contain④
    link-type="dfn"} `eventName`{.variable}, then the user agent *may*
    return true.

2.  Return false.
:::

:::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="Fire Functional Event"}
### [[Fire Functional Event]{#fire-functional-event .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" export="" lt="Fire Functional Event|Fire a functional event"}]{.content}[](#fire-functional-event-algorithm){.self-link} {#fire-functional-event-algorithm .heading .settled}

Input

:   `eventName`{.variable}, a string

:   `eventConstructor`{.variable}, an event constructor that extends
    [`ExtendableEvent`{.idl}](#extendableevent){#ref-for-extendableevent①⑦
    link-type="idl"}

:   `registration`{.variable}, a [service worker
    registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration⑤⑧
    link-type="dfn"}

:   `initialization`{.variable}, optional property initialization for
    `event`{.variable}, constructed from `eventConstructor`{.variable}

:   `postDispatchSteps`{.variable}, optional steps to run on the [active
    worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker④③
    link-type="dfn"}'s event loop, with `dispatchedEvent`{.variable} set
    to the instance of `eventConstructor`{.variable} that was
    [dispatched](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event-dispatch){#ref-for-concept-event-dispatch⑦
    link-type="dfn"}.

Output

:   None

1.  Assert: `registration`{.variable}'s [active
    worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker④④
    link-type="dfn"} is not null.

2.  Let `activeWorker`{.variable} be `registration`{.variable}'s [active
    worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker④⑤
    link-type="dfn"}.

3.  If the result of running [Should Skip
    Event](#should-skip-event){#ref-for-should-skip-event④
    link-type="dfn"} with `eventName`{.variable} and
    `activeWorker`{.variable} is true, then:

    1.  If `registration`{.variable} is
        [stale](#service-worker-registration-stale){#ref-for-service-worker-registration-stale⑤
        link-type="dfn"}, then [in
        parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel){#ref-for-in-parallel④③
        link-type="dfn"} run the [Soft
        Update](#soft-update){#ref-for-soft-update⑦ link-type="dfn"}
        algorithm with `registration`{.variable}.

    2.  Return.

4.  If `activeWorker`{.variable}'s
    [state](#dfn-state){#ref-for-dfn-state①④ link-type="dfn"} is
    \"`activating`\", wait for `activeWorker`{.variable}'s
    [state](#dfn-state){#ref-for-dfn-state①⑤ link-type="dfn"} to become
    \"`activated`\".

5.  If the result of running the [Run Service
    Worker](#run-service-worker){#ref-for-run-service-worker⑨
    link-type="dfn"} algorithm with `activeWorker`{.variable} is
    *failure*, then:

    1.  If `registration`{.variable} is
        [stale](#service-worker-registration-stale){#ref-for-service-worker-registration-stale⑥
        link-type="dfn"}, then [in
        parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel){#ref-for-in-parallel④④
        link-type="dfn"} run the [Soft
        Update](#soft-update){#ref-for-soft-update⑧ link-type="dfn"}
        algorithm with `registration`{.variable}.

    2.  Return.

6.  [Queue a
    task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task){#ref-for-queue-a-task③⑥
    link-type="dfn"} `task`{.variable} to run these substeps:

    1.  Let `event`{.variable} be the result of [creating an
        event](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event-create){#ref-for-concept-event-create⑤
        link-type="dfn"} with `eventConstructor`{.variable} and the
        [relevant
        realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-realm){#ref-for-concept-relevant-realm①①
        link-type="dfn"} of `activeWorker`{.variable}'s [global
        object](#dfn-service-worker-global-object){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-global-object①⑧
        link-type="dfn"}.

    2.  If `initialization`{.variable} is not null, then initialize
        `event`{.variable} with `initialization`{.variable}.

    3.  [Dispatch](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event-dispatch){#ref-for-concept-event-dispatch⑧
        link-type="dfn"} `event`{.variable} on
        `activeWorker`{.variable}'s [global
        object](#dfn-service-worker-global-object){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-global-object①⑨
        link-type="dfn"}.

    4.  Invoke [Update Service Worker Extended Events
        Set](#update-service-worker-extended-events-set){#ref-for-update-service-worker-extended-events-set②
        link-type="dfn"} with `activeWorker`{.variable} and
        `event`{.variable}.

    5.  If `postDispatchSteps`{.variable} is not null, then run
        `postDispatchSteps`{.variable} passing `event`{.variable} as
        `dispatchedEvent`{.variable}.

    The `task`{.variable} *must* use `activeWorker`{.variable}'s [event
    loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-loop){#ref-for-event-loop①⑥
    link-type="dfn"} and the [handle functional event task
    source](#dfn-handle-functional-event-task-source){#ref-for-dfn-handle-functional-event-task-source③
    link-type="dfn"}.

7.  Wait for `task`{.variable} to have executed or been discarded.

8.  If `registration`{.variable} is
    [stale](#service-worker-registration-stale){#ref-for-service-worker-registration-stale⑦
    link-type="dfn"}, then [in
    parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel){#ref-for-in-parallel④⑤
    link-type="dfn"} run the [Soft
    Update](#soft-update){#ref-for-soft-update⑨ link-type="dfn"}
    algorithm with `registration`{.variable}.

::: {#example-cfaebd87 .example}
[](#example-cfaebd87){.self-link} To fire an \"`amazingthing`\" event
(which is of type `AmazingThingEvent`) on a particular
`serviceWorkerRegistration`{.variable}, and initialize the event
object's properties, the prose would be:

1.  [Fire Functional
    Event](#fire-functional-event){#ref-for-fire-functional-event①
    link-type="dfn"} \"`amazingthing`\" using `AmazingThingEvent` on
    `serviceWorkerRegistration`{.variable} with the following
    properties:

    propertyName

    :   value

    anotherPropertyName

    :   anotherValue

    Then run the following steps with `dispatchedEvent`{.variable}:

    1.  Do whatever you need to with `dispatchedEvent`{.variable} on the
        service worker's event loop.

Note that the initialization steps and post-dispatch steps are optional.
If they aren't needed, the prose would be:

1.  [Fire Functional
    Event](#fire-functional-event){#ref-for-fire-functional-event②
    link-type="dfn"} \"`whatever`\" using
    [`ExtendableEvent`{.idl}](#extendableevent){#ref-for-extendableevent①⑧
    link-type="idl"} on `serviceWorkerRegistration`{.variable}.
:::
::::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="Handle Service Worker Client Unload"}
### [[Handle Service Worker Client Unload]{#handle-service-worker-client-unload .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}]{.content}[](#on-client-unload-algorithm){.self-link} {#on-client-unload-algorithm .heading .settled}

The user agent *must* run these steps as part of when a [service worker
client](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client④⑤
link-type="dfn"} unloads via [unloading document cleanup
steps](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-lifecycle.html#unloading-document-cleanup-steps){#ref-for-unloading-document-cleanup-steps
link-type="dfn"} or
[termination](#terminate-service-worker){#ref-for-terminate-service-worker⑥
link-type="dfn"}.

Input

:   `client`{.variable}, a [service worker
    client](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client④⑥
    link-type="dfn"}

Output

:   None

1.  Run the following steps atomically.

2.  Let `registration`{.variable} be the [service worker
    registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration⑤⑨
    link-type="dfn"} [used](#dfn-use){#ref-for-dfn-use⑤ link-type="dfn"}
    by `client`{.variable}.

3.  If `registration`{.variable} is null, abort these steps.

4.  If any other [service worker
    client](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client④⑦
    link-type="dfn"} is [using](#dfn-use){#ref-for-dfn-use⑥
    link-type="dfn"} `registration`{.variable}, abort these steps.

5.  If `registration`{.variable} is
    [unregistered](#dfn-service-worker-registration-unregistered){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration-unregistered③
    link-type="dfn"}, invoke [Try Clear
    Registration](#try-clear-registration){#ref-for-try-clear-registration①
    link-type="dfn"} with `registration`{.variable}.

6.  Invoke [Try Activate](#try-activate){#ref-for-try-activate④
    link-type="dfn"} with `registration`{.variable}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="Handle User Agent Shutdown"}
### [[Handle User Agent Shutdown]{#handle-user-agent-shutdown .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}]{.content}[](#on-user-agent-shutdown-algorithm){.self-link} {#on-user-agent-shutdown-algorithm .heading .settled}

Input

:   None

Output

:   None

1.  [For
    each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate){#ref-for-list-iterate①⑨
    link-type="dfn"} `registration`{.variable} of [registration
    map](#dfn-scope-to-registration-map){#ref-for-dfn-scope-to-registration-map⑦
    link-type="dfn"}'s
    [values](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-getting-the-values){#ref-for-map-getting-the-values④
    link-type="dfn"}:

    1.  If `registration`{.variable}'s [installing
        worker](#dfn-installing-worker){#ref-for-dfn-installing-worker①④
        link-type="dfn"} is not null, then:

        1.  If `registration`{.variable}'s [waiting
            worker](#dfn-waiting-worker){#ref-for-dfn-waiting-worker①③
            link-type="dfn"} is null and `registration`{.variable}'s
            [active
            worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker④⑥
            link-type="dfn"} is null, invoke [Clear
            Registration](#clear-registration){#ref-for-clear-registration
            link-type="dfn"} with `registration`{.variable} and continue
            to the next iteration of the loop.

        2.  Else, set `registration`{.variable}'s [installing
            worker](#dfn-installing-worker){#ref-for-dfn-installing-worker①⑤
            link-type="dfn"} to null.

    2.  If `registration`{.variable}'s [waiting
        worker](#dfn-waiting-worker){#ref-for-dfn-waiting-worker①④
        link-type="dfn"} is not null, then [in
        parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel){#ref-for-in-parallel④⑥
        link-type="dfn"}:

        1.  Invoke [Activate](#activate){#ref-for-activate⑥
            link-type="dfn"} with `registration`{.variable}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="Update Service Worker Extended Events Set"}
### [[Update Service Worker Extended Events Set]{#update-service-worker-extended-events-set .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}]{.content}[](#update-service-worker-extended-events-set-algorithm){.self-link} {#update-service-worker-extended-events-set-algorithm .heading .settled}

Input

:   `worker`{.variable}, a [service
    worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker①⓪⑦
    link-type="dfn"}

:   `event`{.variable}, an
    [event](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event){#ref-for-concept-event⑦
    link-type="dfn"}

Output

:   None

1.  Assert: `event`{.variable}'s [dispatch
    flag](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#dispatch-flag){#ref-for-dispatch-flag②
    link-type="dfn"} is unset.

2.  For each `item`{.variable} of `worker`{.variable}'s [set of extended
    events](#dfn-set-of-extended-events){#ref-for-dfn-set-of-extended-events①
    link-type="dfn"}:

    1.  If `item`{.variable} is not
        [active](#extendableevent-active){#ref-for-extendableevent-active③
        link-type="dfn"},
        [remove](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-remove){#ref-for-list-remove①
        link-type="dfn"} `item`{.variable} from `worker`{.variable}'s
        [set of extended
        events](#dfn-set-of-extended-events){#ref-for-dfn-set-of-extended-events②
        link-type="dfn"}.

3.  If `event`{.variable} is
    [active](#extendableevent-active){#ref-for-extendableevent-active④
    link-type="dfn"},
    [append](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#set-append){#ref-for-set-append③
    link-type="dfn"} `event`{.variable} to `worker`{.variable}'s [set of
    extended
    events](#dfn-set-of-extended-events){#ref-for-dfn-set-of-extended-events③
    link-type="dfn"}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="Unregister"}
### [[Unregister]{#unregister .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}]{.content}[](#unregister-algorithm){.self-link} {#unregister-algorithm .heading .settled}

Input

:   `job`{.variable}, a [job](#dfn-job){#ref-for-dfn-job②⑦
    link-type="dfn"}

Output

:   none

1.  If the
    [origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-origin){#ref-for-concept-settings-object-origin①⑦
    link-type="dfn"} of `job`{.variable}'s [scope
    url](#dfn-job-scope-url){#ref-for-dfn-job-scope-url⑧
    link-type="dfn"} is not `job`{.variable}'s
    [client](#dfn-job-client){#ref-for-dfn-job-client②⓪
    link-type="dfn"}'s
    [origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-origin){#ref-for-concept-settings-object-origin①⑧
    link-type="dfn"}, then:

    1.  Invoke [Reject Job
        Promise](#reject-job-promise){#ref-for-reject-job-promise①⓪
        link-type="dfn"} with `job`{.variable} and
        \"[`SecurityError`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#securityerror){#ref-for-securityerror⑦
        link-type="idl"}\"
        [`DOMException`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException){#ref-for-idl-DOMException②②
        link-type="idl"}.

    2.  Invoke [Finish Job](#finish-job){#ref-for-finish-job①①
        link-type="dfn"} with `job`{.variable} and abort these steps.

2.  Let `registration`{.variable} be the result of running [Get
    Registration](#get-registration){#ref-for-get-registration②
    link-type="dfn"} given `job`{.variable}'s [storage
    key](#job-storage-key){#ref-for-job-storage-key④ link-type="dfn"}
    and `job`{.variable}'s [scope
    url](#dfn-job-scope-url){#ref-for-dfn-job-scope-url⑨
    link-type="dfn"}.

3.  If `registration`{.variable} is null, then:

    1.  Invoke [Resolve Job
        Promise](#resolve-job-promise){#ref-for-resolve-job-promise③
        link-type="dfn"} with `job`{.variable} and false.

    2.  Invoke [Finish Job](#finish-job){#ref-for-finish-job①②
        link-type="dfn"} with `job`{.variable} and abort these steps.

4.  [Remove](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-remove){#ref-for-map-remove⑥
    link-type="dfn"} [registration
    map](#dfn-scope-to-registration-map){#ref-for-dfn-scope-to-registration-map⑧
    link-type="dfn"}\[(`registration`{.variable}'s [storage
    key](#service-worker-registration-storage-key){#ref-for-service-worker-registration-storage-key①③
    link-type="dfn"}, `job`{.variable}'s [scope
    url](#dfn-job-scope-url){#ref-for-dfn-job-scope-url①⓪
    link-type="dfn"})\].

5.  Invoke [Resolve Job
    Promise](#resolve-job-promise){#ref-for-resolve-job-promise④
    link-type="dfn"} with `job`{.variable} and true.

6.  Invoke [Try Clear
    Registration](#try-clear-registration){#ref-for-try-clear-registration②
    link-type="dfn"} with `registration`{.variable}.

    [Note:]{.marker} If [Try Clear
    Registration](#try-clear-registration){#ref-for-try-clear-registration③
    link-type="dfn"} does not trigger [Clear
    Registration](#clear-registration){#ref-for-clear-registration①
    link-type="dfn"} here, [Clear
    Registration](#clear-registration){#ref-for-clear-registration②
    link-type="dfn"} is tried again when the last client
    [using](#dfn-use){#ref-for-dfn-use⑦ link-type="dfn"} the
    registration is
    [unloaded](#handle-service-worker-client-unload){#ref-for-handle-service-worker-client-unload②
    link-type="dfn"} or the [extend lifetime
    promises](#extendableevent-extend-lifetime-promises){#ref-for-extendableevent-extend-lifetime-promises⑧
    link-type="dfn"} for the registration's service workers settle.

7.  Invoke [Finish Job](#finish-job){#ref-for-finish-job①③
    link-type="dfn"} with `job`{.variable}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="Set Registration"}
### [[Set Registration]{#set-registration .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}]{.content}[](#set-registration-algorithm){.self-link} {#set-registration-algorithm .heading .settled}

Input

:   `storage key`{.variable}, a [storage
    key](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#storage-key){#ref-for-storage-key⑤
    link-type="dfn"}

:   `scope`{.variable}, a
    [URL](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url){#ref-for-concept-url①③
    link-type="dfn"}

:   `updateViaCache`{.variable}, an [update via cache
    mode](#dfn-update-via-cache){#ref-for-dfn-update-via-cache⑧
    link-type="dfn"}

Output

:   `registration`{.variable}, a [service worker
    registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration⑥⓪
    link-type="dfn"}

1.  Run the following steps atomically.

2.  Let `scopeString`{.variable} be
    [serialized](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-serializer){#ref-for-concept-url-serializer①③
    link-type="dfn"} `scope`{.variable} with the *exclude fragment flag*
    set.

3.  Let `registration`{.variable} be a new [service worker
    registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration⑥①
    link-type="dfn"} whose [storage
    key](#service-worker-registration-storage-key){#ref-for-service-worker-registration-storage-key①④
    link-type="dfn"} is set to `storage key`{.variable}, [scope
    url](#dfn-scope-url){#ref-for-dfn-scope-url①⑧ link-type="dfn"} is
    set to `scope`{.variable}, and [update via cache
    mode](#dfn-update-via-cache){#ref-for-dfn-update-via-cache⑨
    link-type="dfn"} is set to `updateViaCache`{.variable}.

4.  [Set](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-set){#ref-for-map-set④
    link-type="dfn"} [registration
    map](#dfn-scope-to-registration-map){#ref-for-dfn-scope-to-registration-map⑨
    link-type="dfn"}\[(`storage key`{.variable},
    `scopeString`{.variable})\] to `registration`{.variable}.

5.  Return `registration`{.variable}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="Clear Registration"}
### [[Clear Registration]{#clear-registration .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}]{.content}[](#clear-registration-algorithm){.self-link} {#clear-registration-algorithm .heading .settled}

Input

:   `registration`{.variable}, a [service worker
    registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration⑥②
    link-type="dfn"}

Output

:   None

1.  Run the following steps atomically.

2.  If `registration`{.variable}'s [installing
    worker](#dfn-installing-worker){#ref-for-dfn-installing-worker①⑥
    link-type="dfn"} is not null, then:

    1.  [Terminate](#terminate-service-worker){#ref-for-terminate-service-worker⑦
        link-type="dfn"} `registration`{.variable}'s [installing
        worker](#dfn-installing-worker){#ref-for-dfn-installing-worker①⑦
        link-type="dfn"}.

    2.  Run the [Update Worker
        State](#update-worker-state){#ref-for-update-worker-state⑧
        link-type="dfn"} algorithm passing `registration`{.variable}'s
        [installing
        worker](#dfn-installing-worker){#ref-for-dfn-installing-worker①⑧
        link-type="dfn"} and \"`redundant`\" as the arguments.

    3.  Run the [Update Registration
        State](#update-registration-state){#ref-for-update-registration-state⑥
        link-type="dfn"} algorithm passing `registration`{.variable},
        \"`installing`\" and null as the arguments.

3.  If `registration`{.variable}'s [waiting
    worker](#dfn-waiting-worker){#ref-for-dfn-waiting-worker①⑤
    link-type="dfn"} is not null, then:

    1.  [Terminate](#terminate-service-worker){#ref-for-terminate-service-worker⑧
        link-type="dfn"} `registration`{.variable}'s [waiting
        worker](#dfn-waiting-worker){#ref-for-dfn-waiting-worker①⑥
        link-type="dfn"}.

    2.  Run the [Update Worker
        State](#update-worker-state){#ref-for-update-worker-state⑨
        link-type="dfn"} algorithm passing `registration`{.variable}'s
        [waiting
        worker](#dfn-waiting-worker){#ref-for-dfn-waiting-worker①⑦
        link-type="dfn"} and \"`redundant`\" as the arguments.

    3.  Run the [Update Registration
        State](#update-registration-state){#ref-for-update-registration-state⑦
        link-type="dfn"} algorithm passing `registration`{.variable},
        \"`waiting`\" and null as the arguments.

4.  If `registration`{.variable}'s [active
    worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker④⑦
    link-type="dfn"} is not null, then:

    1.  [Terminate](#terminate-service-worker){#ref-for-terminate-service-worker⑨
        link-type="dfn"} `registration`{.variable}'s [active
        worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker④⑧
        link-type="dfn"}.

    2.  Run the [Update Worker
        State](#update-worker-state){#ref-for-update-worker-state①⓪
        link-type="dfn"} algorithm passing `registration`{.variable}'s
        [active worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker④⑨
        link-type="dfn"} and \"`redundant`\" as the arguments.

    3.  Run the [Update Registration
        State](#update-registration-state){#ref-for-update-registration-state⑧
        link-type="dfn"} algorithm passing `registration`{.variable},
        \"`active`\" and null as the arguments.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="Try Clear Registration"}
### [[Try Clear Registration]{#try-clear-registration .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}]{.content}[](#try-clear-registration-algorithm){.self-link} {#try-clear-registration-algorithm .heading .settled}

Input

:   `registration`{.variable}, a [service worker
    registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration⑥③
    link-type="dfn"}

Output

:   None

1.  Invoke [Clear
    Registration](#clear-registration){#ref-for-clear-registration③
    link-type="dfn"} with `registration`{.variable} if no [service
    worker
    client](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client④⑧
    link-type="dfn"} is [using](#dfn-use){#ref-for-dfn-use⑧
    link-type="dfn"} `registration`{.variable} and all of the following
    conditions are true:

    - `registration`{.variable}'s [installing
      worker](#dfn-installing-worker){#ref-for-dfn-installing-worker①⑨
      link-type="dfn"} is null or the result of running [Service Worker
      Has No Pending
      Events](#service-worker-has-no-pending-events){#ref-for-service-worker-has-no-pending-events②
      link-type="dfn"} with `registration`{.variable}'s [installing
      worker](#dfn-installing-worker){#ref-for-dfn-installing-worker②⓪
      link-type="dfn"} is true.

    - `registration`{.variable}'s [waiting
      worker](#dfn-waiting-worker){#ref-for-dfn-waiting-worker①⑧
      link-type="dfn"} is null or the result of running [Service Worker
      Has No Pending
      Events](#service-worker-has-no-pending-events){#ref-for-service-worker-has-no-pending-events③
      link-type="dfn"} with `registration`{.variable}'s [waiting
      worker](#dfn-waiting-worker){#ref-for-dfn-waiting-worker①⑨
      link-type="dfn"} is true.

    - `registration`{.variable}'s [active
      worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker⑤⓪
      link-type="dfn"} is null or the result of running [Service Worker
      Has No Pending
      Events](#service-worker-has-no-pending-events){#ref-for-service-worker-has-no-pending-events④
      link-type="dfn"} with `registration`{.variable}'s [active
      worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker⑤①
      link-type="dfn"} is true.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="Update Registration State"}
### [[Update Registration State]{#update-registration-state .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}]{.content}[](#update-registration-state-algorithm){.self-link} {#update-registration-state-algorithm .heading .settled}

Input

:   `registration`{.variable}, a [service worker
    registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration⑥④
    link-type="dfn"}

:   `target`{.variable}, a string (one of \"`installing`\",
    \"`waiting`\", and \"`active`\")

:   `source`{.variable}, a [service
    worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker①⓪⑧
    link-type="dfn"} or null

Output

:   None

1.  Let `registrationObjects`{.variable} be an array containing all the
    [`ServiceWorkerRegistration`{.idl}](#serviceworkerregistration){#ref-for-serviceworkerregistration①⑥
    link-type="idl"} objects associated with `registration`{.variable}.

2.  If `target`{.variable} is \"`installing`\", then:

    1.  Set `registration`{.variable}'s [installing
        worker](#dfn-installing-worker){#ref-for-dfn-installing-worker②①
        link-type="dfn"} to `source`{.variable}.

    2.  For each `registrationObject`{.variable} in
        `registrationObjects`{.variable}:

        1.  [Queue a
            task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task){#ref-for-queue-a-task③⑦
            link-type="dfn"} to set the
            [`installing`{.idl}](#dom-serviceworkerregistration-installing){#ref-for-dom-serviceworkerregistration-installing④
            link-type="idl"} attribute of
            `registrationObject`{.variable} to null if
            `registration`{.variable}'s [installing
            worker](#dfn-installing-worker){#ref-for-dfn-installing-worker②②
            link-type="dfn"} is null, or the result of [getting the
            service worker
            object](#get-the-service-worker-object){#ref-for-get-the-service-worker-object⑦
            link-type="dfn"} that represents `registration`{.variable}'s
            [installing
            worker](#dfn-installing-worker){#ref-for-dfn-installing-worker②③
            link-type="dfn"} in `registrationObject`{.variable}'s
            [relevant settings
            object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object){#ref-for-relevant-settings-object③①
            link-type="dfn"}.

3.  Else if `target`{.variable} is \"`waiting`\", then:

    1.  Set `registration`{.variable}'s [waiting
        worker](#dfn-waiting-worker){#ref-for-dfn-waiting-worker②⓪
        link-type="dfn"} to `source`{.variable}.

    2.  For each `registrationObject`{.variable} in
        `registrationObjects`{.variable}:

        1.  [Queue a
            task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task){#ref-for-queue-a-task③⑧
            link-type="dfn"} to set the
            [`waiting`{.idl}](#dom-serviceworkerregistration-waiting){#ref-for-dom-serviceworkerregistration-waiting④
            link-type="idl"} attribute of
            `registrationObject`{.variable} to null if
            `registration`{.variable}'s [waiting
            worker](#dfn-waiting-worker){#ref-for-dfn-waiting-worker②①
            link-type="dfn"} is null, or the result of [getting the
            service worker
            object](#get-the-service-worker-object){#ref-for-get-the-service-worker-object⑧
            link-type="dfn"} that represents `registration`{.variable}'s
            [waiting
            worker](#dfn-waiting-worker){#ref-for-dfn-waiting-worker②②
            link-type="dfn"} in `registrationObject`{.variable}'s
            [relevant settings
            object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object){#ref-for-relevant-settings-object③②
            link-type="dfn"}.

4.  Else if `target`{.variable} is \"`active`\", then:

    1.  Set `registration`{.variable}'s [active
        worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker⑤②
        link-type="dfn"} to `source`{.variable}.

    2.  For each `registrationObject`{.variable} in
        `registrationObjects`{.variable}:

        1.  [Queue a
            task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task){#ref-for-queue-a-task③⑨
            link-type="dfn"} to set the
            [`active`{.idl}](#dom-serviceworkerregistration-active){#ref-for-dom-serviceworkerregistration-active④
            link-type="idl"} attribute of
            `registrationObject`{.variable} to null if
            `registration`{.variable}'s [active
            worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker⑤③
            link-type="dfn"} is null, or the result of [getting the
            service worker
            object](#get-the-service-worker-object){#ref-for-get-the-service-worker-object⑨
            link-type="dfn"} that represents `registration`{.variable}'s
            [active
            worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker⑤④
            link-type="dfn"} in `registrationObject`{.variable}'s
            [relevant settings
            object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object){#ref-for-relevant-settings-object③③
            link-type="dfn"}.

    The
    [task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-task){#ref-for-concept-task⑨
    link-type="dfn"} *must* use `registrationObject`{.variable}'s
    [relevant settings
    object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object){#ref-for-relevant-settings-object③④
    link-type="dfn"}'s [responsible event
    loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop){#ref-for-responsible-event-loop①⑧
    link-type="dfn"} and the [DOM manipulation task
    source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source){#ref-for-dom-manipulation-task-source②④
    link-type="dfn"}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="Update Worker State"}
### [[Update Worker State]{#update-worker-state .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}]{.content}[](#update-state-algorithm){.self-link} {#update-state-algorithm .heading .settled}

Input

:   `worker`{.variable}, a [service
    worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker①⓪⑨
    link-type="dfn"}

:   `state`{.variable}, a [service
    worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker①①⓪
    link-type="dfn"} [state](#dfn-state){#ref-for-dfn-state①⑥
    link-type="dfn"}

Output

:   None

1.  Assert: `state`{.variable} is not \"`parsed`\".

    [Note:]{.marker} \"`parsed`\" is the initial state. A [service
    worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker①①①
    link-type="dfn"} is never updated to this state.

2.  Set `worker`{.variable}'s [state](#dfn-state){#ref-for-dfn-state①⑦
    link-type="dfn"} to `state`{.variable}.

3.  Let `settingsObjects`{.variable} be all [environment settings
    objects](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object){#ref-for-environment-settings-object②①
    link-type="dfn"} whose
    [origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-origin){#ref-for-concept-settings-object-origin①⑨
    link-type="dfn"} is `worker`{.variable}'s [script
    url](#dfn-script-url){#ref-for-dfn-script-url①① link-type="dfn"}'s
    [origin](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-origin){#ref-for-concept-url-origin⑨
    link-type="dfn"}.

4.  For each `settingsObject`{.variable} of
    `settingsObjects`{.variable}, [queue a
    task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task){#ref-for-queue-a-task④⓪
    link-type="dfn"} on `settingsObject`{.variable}'s [responsible event
    loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop){#ref-for-responsible-event-loop①⑨
    link-type="dfn"} in the [DOM manipulation task
    source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source){#ref-for-dom-manipulation-task-source②⑤
    link-type="dfn"} to run the following steps:

    1.  Let `objectMap`{.variable} be `settingsObject`{.variable}'s
        [service worker object
        map](#environment-settings-object-service-worker-object-map){#ref-for-environment-settings-object-service-worker-object-map①
        link-type="dfn"}.

    2.  If `objectMap`{.variable}\[`worker`{.variable}\] does not
        [exist](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists){#ref-for-map-exists②⑥
        link-type="dfn"}, then abort these steps.

    3.  Let `workerObj`{.variable} be
        `objectMap`{.variable}\[`worker`{.variable}\].

    4.  Set `workerObj`{.variable}'s
        [`state`{.idl}](#dom-serviceworker-state){#ref-for-dom-serviceworker-state④
        link-type="idl"} to `state`{.variable}.

    5.  [Fire an
        event](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event-fire){#ref-for-concept-event-fire②
        link-type="dfn"} named
        [`statechange`{.idl}](#eventdef-serviceworker-statechange){#ref-for-eventdef-serviceworker-statechange①
        .idl-code link-type="event"} at `workerObj`{.variable}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="Notify Controller Change"}
### [[Notify Controller Change]{#notify-controller-change .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}]{.content}[](#notify-controller-change-algorithm){.self-link} {#notify-controller-change-algorithm .heading .settled}

Input

:   `client`{.variable}, a [service worker
    client](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client④⑨
    link-type="dfn"}

Output

:   None

1.  Assert: `client`{.variable} is not null.

2.  If `client`{.variable} is an [environment settings
    object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object){#ref-for-environment-settings-object②②
    link-type="dfn"}, [queue a
    task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task){#ref-for-queue-a-task④①
    link-type="dfn"} to [fire an
    event](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event-fire){#ref-for-concept-event-fire③
    link-type="dfn"} named `controllerchange` at the
    [`ServiceWorkerContainer`{.idl}](#serviceworkercontainer){#ref-for-serviceworkercontainer①⑥
    link-type="idl"} object that `client`{.variable} is
    [associated](#serviceworkercontainer-service-worker-client){#ref-for-serviceworkercontainer-service-worker-client⑨
    link-type="dfn"} with.

The
[task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-task){#ref-for-concept-task①⓪
link-type="dfn"} *must* use `client`{.variable}'s [responsible event
loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop){#ref-for-responsible-event-loop②⓪
link-type="dfn"} and the [DOM manipulation task
source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source){#ref-for-dom-manipulation-task-source②⑥
link-type="dfn"}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="Match Service Worker Registration"}
### [[Match Service Worker Registration]{#match-service-worker-registration .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" export=""}]{.content}[](#scope-match-algorithm){.self-link} {#scope-match-algorithm .heading .settled}

Input

:   `storage key`{.variable}, a [storage
    key](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#storage-key){#ref-for-storage-key⑥
    link-type="dfn"}

:   `clientURL`{.variable}, a
    [URL](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url){#ref-for-concept-url①④
    link-type="dfn"}

Output

:   A [service worker
    registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration⑥⑤
    link-type="dfn"}

1.  Run the following steps atomically.

2.  Let `clientURLString`{.variable} be
    [serialized](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-serializer){#ref-for-concept-url-serializer①④
    link-type="dfn"} `clientURL`{.variable}.

3.  Let `matchingScopeString`{.variable} be the empty string.

4.  Let `scopeStringSet`{.variable} be an empty list.

5.  [For
    each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate){#ref-for-list-iterate②⓪
    link-type="dfn"} (`entry storage key`{.variable},
    `entry scope`{.variable}) of [registration
    map](#dfn-scope-to-registration-map){#ref-for-dfn-scope-to-registration-map①⓪
    link-type="dfn"}'s
    [keys](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-getting-the-keys){#ref-for-map-getting-the-keys⑤
    link-type="dfn"}:

    1.  If `storage key`{.variable}
        [equals](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#storage-key-equal){#ref-for-storage-key-equal⑤
        link-type="dfn"} `entry storage key`{.variable}, then
        [append](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-append){#ref-for-list-append⑧
        link-type="dfn"} `entry scope`{.variable} to the end of
        `scopeStringSet`{.variable}.

6.  Set `matchingScopeString`{.variable} to the longest value in
    `scopeStringSet`{.variable} which the value of
    `clientURLString`{.variable} starts with, if it exists.

    [Note:]{.marker} The URL string matching in this step is
    prefix-based rather than path-structural. E.g. a client URL string
    with \"https://example.com/prefix-of/resource.html\" will match a
    registration for a scope with \"https://example.com/prefix\". The
    URL string comparison is safe for the same-origin security as
    HTTP(S) URLs are always
    [serialized](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-serializer){#ref-for-concept-url-serializer①⑤
    link-type="dfn"} with a trailing slash at the end of the origin part
    of the URLs.

7.  Let `matchingScope`{.variable} be null.

8.  If `matchingScopeString`{.variable} is not the empty string, then:

    1.  Set `matchingScope`{.variable} to the result of
        [parsing](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-parser){#ref-for-concept-url-parser⑧
        link-type="dfn"} `matchingScopeString`{.variable}.

    2.  Assert: `matchingScope`{.variable}'s
        [origin](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-origin){#ref-for-concept-url-origin①⓪
        link-type="dfn"} and `clientURL`{.variable}'s
        [origin](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-origin){#ref-for-concept-url-origin①①
        link-type="dfn"} are [same
        origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#same-origin){#ref-for-same-origin⑥
        link-type="dfn"}.

9.  Return the result of running [Get
    Registration](#get-registration){#ref-for-get-registration③
    link-type="dfn"} given `storage key`{.variable} and
    `matchingScope`{.variable}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="Get Registration"}
### [[Get Registration]{#get-registration .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}]{.content}[](#get-registration-algorithm){.self-link} {#get-registration-algorithm .heading .settled}

Input

:   `storage key`{.variable}, a [storage
    key](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#storage-key){#ref-for-storage-key⑦
    link-type="dfn"}

:   `scope`{.variable}, a
    [URL](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url){#ref-for-concept-url①⑤
    link-type="dfn"}

Output

:   A [service worker
    registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration⑥⑥
    link-type="dfn"}

1.  Run the following steps atomically.

2.  Let `scopeString`{.variable} be the empty string.

3.  If `scope`{.variable} is not null, set `scopeString`{.variable} to
    [serialized](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-serializer){#ref-for-concept-url-serializer①⑥
    link-type="dfn"} `scope`{.variable} with the *exclude fragment flag*
    set.

4.  [For
    each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-iterate){#ref-for-map-iterate⑦
    link-type="dfn"} (`entry storage key`{.variable},
    `entry scope`{.variable}) → `registration`{.variable} of
    [registration
    map](#dfn-scope-to-registration-map){#ref-for-dfn-scope-to-registration-map①①
    link-type="dfn"}:

    1.  If `storage key`{.variable}
        [equals](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#storage-key-equal){#ref-for-storage-key-equal⑥
        link-type="dfn"} `entry storage key`{.variable} and
        `scopeString`{.variable} matches `entry scope`{.variable}, then
        return `registration`{.variable}.

5.  Return null.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="Get Newest Worker"}
### [[Get Newest Worker]{#get-newest-worker .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}]{.content}[](#get-newest-worker-algorithm){.self-link} {#get-newest-worker-algorithm .heading .settled}

Input

:   `registration`{.variable}, a [service worker
    registration](#dfn-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-registration⑥⑦
    link-type="dfn"}

Output

:   `newestWorker`{.variable}, a [service
    worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker①①②
    link-type="dfn"}

1.  Run the following steps atomically.

2.  Let `newestWorker`{.variable} be null.

3.  If `registration`{.variable}'s [installing
    worker](#dfn-installing-worker){#ref-for-dfn-installing-worker②④
    link-type="dfn"} is not null, set `newestWorker`{.variable} to
    `registration`{.variable}'s [installing
    worker](#dfn-installing-worker){#ref-for-dfn-installing-worker②⑤
    link-type="dfn"}.

4.  Else if `registration`{.variable}'s [waiting
    worker](#dfn-waiting-worker){#ref-for-dfn-waiting-worker②③
    link-type="dfn"} is not null, set `newestWorker`{.variable} to
    `registration`{.variable}'s [waiting
    worker](#dfn-waiting-worker){#ref-for-dfn-waiting-worker②④
    link-type="dfn"}.

5.  Else if `registration`{.variable}'s [active
    worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker⑤⑤
    link-type="dfn"} is not null, set `newestWorker`{.variable} to
    `registration`{.variable}'s [active
    worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker⑤⑥
    link-type="dfn"}.

6.  Return `newestWorker`{.variable}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="Service Worker Has No Pending Events"}
### [[Service Worker Has No Pending Events]{#service-worker-has-no-pending-events .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}]{.content}[](#service-worker-has-no-pending-events-algorithm){.self-link} {#service-worker-has-no-pending-events-algorithm .heading .settled}

Input

:   `worker`{.variable}, a [service
    worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker①①③
    link-type="dfn"}

Output

:   True or false, a boolean

1.  For each `event`{.variable} of `worker`{.variable}'s [set of
    extended
    events](#dfn-set-of-extended-events){#ref-for-dfn-set-of-extended-events④
    link-type="dfn"}:

    1.  If `event`{.variable} is
        [active](#extendableevent-active){#ref-for-extendableevent-active⑤
        link-type="dfn"}, return false.

2.  Return true.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="Create Client"}
### [[Create Client]{#create-client .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}]{.content}[](#create-client-algorithm){.self-link} {#create-client-algorithm .heading .settled}

Input

:   `client`{.variable}, a [service worker
    client](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client⑤⓪
    link-type="dfn"}

Output

:   `clientObject`{.variable}, a
    [`Client`{.idl}](#client){#ref-for-client①① link-type="idl"} object

1.  Let `clientObject`{.variable} be a new
    [`Client`{.idl}](#client){#ref-for-client①② link-type="idl"} object.

2.  Set `clientObject`{.variable}'s [service worker
    client](#dfn-service-worker-client-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client-service-worker-client①③
    link-type="dfn"} to `client`{.variable}.

3.  Return `clientObject`{.variable}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="Create Window Client"}
### [[Create Window Client]{#create-window-client .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}]{.content}[](#create-windowclient-algorithm){.self-link} {#create-windowclient-algorithm .heading .settled}

Input

:   `client`{.variable}, a [service worker
    client](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client⑤①
    link-type="dfn"}

:   `frameType`{.variable}, a string

:   `visibilityState`{.variable}, a string

:   `focusState`{.variable}, a boolean

:   `ancestorOriginsList`{.variable}, a list

Output

:   `windowClient`{.variable}, a
    [`WindowClient`{.idl}](#windowclient){#ref-for-windowclient①⓪
    link-type="idl"} object

1.  Let `windowClient`{.variable} be a new
    [`WindowClient`{.idl}](#windowclient){#ref-for-windowclient①①
    link-type="idl"} object.

2.  Set `windowClient`{.variable}'s [service worker
    client](#dfn-service-worker-client-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client-service-worker-client①④
    link-type="dfn"} to `client`{.variable}.

3.  Set `windowClient`{.variable}'s [frame
    type](#dfn-service-worker-client-frame-type){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client-frame-type①
    link-type="dfn"} to `frameType`{.variable}.

4.  Set `windowClient`{.variable}'s [visibility
    state](#dfn-service-worker-client-visibilitystate){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client-visibilitystate①
    link-type="dfn"} to `visibilityState`{.variable}.

5.  Set `windowClient`{.variable}'s [focus
    state](#dfn-service-worker-client-focusstate){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client-focusstate②
    link-type="dfn"} to `focusState`{.variable}.

6.  Set `windowClient`{.variable}'s [ancestor origins
    array](#windowclient-ancestor-origins-array){#ref-for-windowclient-ancestor-origins-array①
    link-type="dfn"} to a [frozen
    array](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-frozen-array-type){#ref-for-dfn-frozen-array-type④
    link-type="dfn"} created from `ancestorOriginsList`{.variable}.

7.  Return `windowClient`{.variable}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="Get Frame Type"}
### [[Get Frame Type]{#get-frame-type .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}]{.content}[](#get-frametype-algorithm){.self-link} {#get-frametype-algorithm .heading .settled}

Input

:   `navigable`{.variable}, a
    [navigable](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#navigable){#ref-for-navigable
    link-type="dfn"}

Output

:   `frameType`{.variable}, a string

1.  If `navigable`{.variable}'s
    [parent](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#nav-parent){#ref-for-nav-parent
    link-type="dfn"} is not null, then return \"`nested`\".

2.  If `navigable`{.variable}'s [active browsing
    context](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#nav-bc){#ref-for-nav-bc
    link-type="dfn"} is an [auxiliary browsing
    context](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#auxiliary-browsing-context){#ref-for-auxiliary-browsing-context
    link-type="dfn"}, then return \"`auxiliary`\".

3.  Return \"`top-level`\".
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="Resolve Get Client Promise"}
### [[Resolve Get Client Promise]{#resolve-get-client-promise .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}]{.content}[](#resolve-get-client-promise-algorithm){.self-link} {#resolve-get-client-promise-algorithm .heading .settled}

Input

:   `client`{.variable}, a [service worker
    client](#dfn-service-worker-client){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-client⑤②
    link-type="dfn"}

:   `promise`{.variable}, a
    [promise](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-promise-objects){#ref-for-sec-promise-objects③③
    link-type="dfn"}

Output

:   none

1.  If `client`{.variable} is an [environment settings
    object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object){#ref-for-environment-settings-object②③
    link-type="dfn"}, then:

    1.  If `client`{.variable} is not a [secure
        context](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#secure-context){#ref-for-secure-context⑤
        link-type="dfn"}, [queue a
        task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task){#ref-for-queue-a-task④②
        link-type="dfn"} to reject `promise`{.variable} with a
        \"[`SecurityError`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#securityerror){#ref-for-securityerror⑧
        link-type="idl"}\"
        [`DOMException`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException){#ref-for-idl-DOMException②③
        link-type="idl"}, on `promise`{.variable}'s [relevant settings
        object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object){#ref-for-relevant-settings-object③⑤
        link-type="dfn"}'s [responsible event
        loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop){#ref-for-responsible-event-loop②①
        link-type="dfn"} using the [DOM manipulation task
        source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source){#ref-for-dom-manipulation-task-source②⑦
        link-type="dfn"}, and abort these steps.

2.  Else:

    1.  If `client`{.variable}'s [creation
        URL](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-creation-url){#ref-for-concept-environment-creation-url⑨
        link-type="dfn"} is not a [potentially trustworthy
        URL](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-secure-contexts/#potentially-trustworthy-url){#ref-for-potentially-trustworthy-url①
        link-type="dfn"}, [queue a
        task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task){#ref-for-queue-a-task④③
        link-type="dfn"} to reject `promise`{.variable} with a
        \"[`SecurityError`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#securityerror){#ref-for-securityerror⑨
        link-type="idl"}\"
        [`DOMException`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException){#ref-for-idl-DOMException②④
        link-type="idl"}, on `promise`{.variable}'s [relevant settings
        object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object){#ref-for-relevant-settings-object③⑥
        link-type="dfn"}'s [responsible event
        loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop){#ref-for-responsible-event-loop②②
        link-type="dfn"} using the [DOM manipulation task
        source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source){#ref-for-dom-manipulation-task-source②⑧
        link-type="dfn"}, and abort these steps.

3.  If `client`{.variable} is an [environment settings
    object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object){#ref-for-environment-settings-object②④
    link-type="dfn"} and is not a [window
    client](#dfn-window-client){#ref-for-dfn-window-client①③
    link-type="dfn"}, then:

    1.  Let `clientObject`{.variable} be the result of running [Create
        Client](#create-client){#ref-for-create-client① link-type="dfn"}
        algorithm with `client`{.variable} as the argument.

    2.  [Queue a
        task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task){#ref-for-queue-a-task④④
        link-type="dfn"} to resolve `promise`{.variable} with
        `clientObject`{.variable}, on `promise`{.variable}'s [relevant
        settings
        object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object){#ref-for-relevant-settings-object③⑦
        link-type="dfn"}'s [responsible event
        loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop){#ref-for-responsible-event-loop②③
        link-type="dfn"} using the [DOM manipulation task
        source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source){#ref-for-dom-manipulation-task-source②⑨
        link-type="dfn"}, and abort these steps.

4.  Else:

    1.  Let `browsingContext`{.variable} be null.

    2.  If `client`{.variable} is an [environment settings
        object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object){#ref-for-environment-settings-object②⑤
        link-type="dfn"}, set `browsingContext`{.variable} to
        `client`{.variable}'s [global
        object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-global){#ref-for-concept-settings-object-global①①
        link-type="dfn"}'s [browsing
        context](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#browsing-context){#ref-for-browsing-context⑦
        link-type="dfn"}.

    3.  Else, set `browsingContext`{.variable} to `client`{.variable}'s
        [target browsing
        context](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-target-browsing-context){#ref-for-concept-environment-target-browsing-context②
        link-type="dfn"}.

    4.  Let `navigable`{.variable} be the
        [navigable](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#navigable){#ref-for-navigable①
        link-type="dfn"} with a [active browsing
        context](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#nav-bc){#ref-for-nav-bc①
        link-type="dfn"} of `browsingContext`{.variable}.

    5.  [Queue a
        task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task){#ref-for-queue-a-task④⑤
        link-type="dfn"} to run the following steps on
        `browsingContext`{.variable}'s [event
        loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-loop){#ref-for-event-loop①⑦
        link-type="dfn"} using the [user interaction task
        source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#user-interaction-task-source){#ref-for-user-interaction-task-source④
        link-type="dfn"}:

        1.  Let `frameType`{.variable} be the result of running [Get
            Frame Type](#get-frame-type){#ref-for-get-frame-type④
            link-type="dfn"} with `navigable`{.variable}.

        2.  Let `visibilityState`{.variable} be
