            `browsingContext`{.variable}'s [active
            document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#nav-document){#ref-for-nav-document①④
            link-type="dfn"}'s
            [`visibilityState`{.idl}](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/interaction.html#page-visibility){#ref-for-page-visibility⑥
            link-type="idl" refhint-key="d7e375d3"} attribute value.

        3.  Let `focusState`{.variable} be the result of running the
            [has focus
            steps](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/interaction.html#has-focus-steps){#ref-for-has-focus-steps④
            link-type="dfn"} with `browsingContext`{.variable}'s [active
            document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#nav-document){#ref-for-nav-document①⑤
            link-type="dfn"} as the argument.

        4.  Let `ancestorOriginsList`{.variable} be the empty list.

        5.  If `client`{.variable} is a [window
            client](#dfn-window-client){#ref-for-dfn-window-client①④
            link-type="dfn"}, set `ancestorOriginsList`{.variable} to
            `browsingContext`{.variable}'s [active
            document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#nav-document){#ref-for-nav-document①⑥
            link-type="dfn"}'s [relevant global
            object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-global){#ref-for-concept-relevant-global⑨
            link-type="dfn"}'s
            [`Location`{.idl}](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#location){#ref-for-location④
            link-type="idl"} object's [ancestor origins
            list](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#concept-location-ancestor-origins-list){#ref-for-concept-location-ancestor-origins-list④
            link-type="dfn"}'s associated list.

        6.  [Queue a
            task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task){#ref-for-queue-a-task④⑥
            link-type="dfn"} to run the following steps on
            `promise`{.variable}'s [relevant settings
            object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object){#ref-for-relevant-settings-object③⑧
            link-type="dfn"}'s [responsible event
            loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#responsible-event-loop){#ref-for-responsible-event-loop②④
            link-type="dfn"} using the [DOM manipulation task
            source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source){#ref-for-dom-manipulation-task-source③⓪
            link-type="dfn"}:

            1.  If `client`{.variable}'s [discarded
                flag](#service-worker-client-discarded-flag){#ref-for-service-worker-client-discarded-flag⑤
                link-type="dfn"} is set, resolve `promise`{.variable}
                with undefined and abort these steps.

            2.  Let `windowClient`{.variable} be the result of running
                [Create Window
                Client](#create-window-client){#ref-for-create-window-client④
                link-type="dfn"} with `client`{.variable},
                `frameType`{.variable}, `visibilityState`{.variable},
                `focusState`{.variable}, and
                `ancestorOriginsList`{.variable}.

            3.  Resolve `promise`{.variable} with
                `windowClient`{.variable}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="Query Cache"}
### [[Query Cache]{#query-cache .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}]{.content}[](#query-cache-algorithm){.self-link} {#query-cache-algorithm .heading .settled}

Input

:   `requestQuery`{.variable}, a
    [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request){#ref-for-concept-request①①
    link-type="dfn"}

:   `options`{.variable}, a
    [`CacheQueryOptions`{.idl}](#dictdef-cachequeryoptions){#ref-for-dictdef-cachequeryoptions⑦
    link-type="idl"} object, optional

:   `targetStorage`{.variable}, a [request response
    list](#dfn-request-response-list){#ref-for-dfn-request-response-list⑥
    link-type="dfn"}, optional

Output

:   `resultList`{.variable}, a [request response
    list](#dfn-request-response-list){#ref-for-dfn-request-response-list⑦
    link-type="dfn"}

1.  Let `resultList`{.variable} be an empty
    [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list){#ref-for-list②④
    link-type="dfn"}.

2.  Let `storage`{.variable} be null.

3.  If the optional argument `targetStorage`{.variable} is omitted, set
    `storage`{.variable} to the [relevant request response
    list](#dfn-relevant-request-response-list){#ref-for-dfn-relevant-request-response-list②
    link-type="dfn"}.

4.  Else, set `storage`{.variable} to `targetStorage`{.variable}.

5.  [For
    each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate){#ref-for-list-iterate②①
    link-type="dfn"} `requestResponse`{.variable} of
    `storage`{.variable}:

    1.  Let `cachedRequest`{.variable} be `requestResponse`{.variable}'s
        request.

    2.  Let `cachedResponse`{.variable} be
        `requestResponse`{.variable}'s response.

    3.  If [Request Matches Cached
        Item](#request-matches-cached-item){#ref-for-request-matches-cached-item
        link-type="dfn"} with `requestQuery`{.variable},
        `cachedRequest`{.variable}, `cachedResponse`{.variable}, and
        `options`{.variable} returns true, then:

        1.  Let `requestCopy`{.variable} be a copy of
            `cachedRequest`{.variable}.

        2.  Let `responseCopy`{.variable} be a copy of
            `cachedResponse`{.variable}.

        3.  Add `requestCopy`{.variable}/`responseCopy`{.variable} to
            `resultList`{.variable}.

6.  Return `resultList`{.variable}.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="Request Matches Cached Item"}
### [[Request Matches Cached Item]{#request-matches-cached-item .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" export=""}]{.content}[](#request-matches-cached-item-algorithm){.self-link} {#request-matches-cached-item-algorithm .heading .settled}

Input

:   `requestQuery`{.variable}, a
    [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request){#ref-for-concept-request①②
    link-type="dfn"}

:   `request`{.variable}, a
    [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request){#ref-for-concept-request①③
    link-type="dfn"}

:   `response`{.variable}, a
    [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response){#ref-for-concept-response①⑥
    link-type="dfn"} or null, optional, defaulting to null

:   `options`{.variable}, a
    [`CacheQueryOptions`{.idl}](#dictdef-cachequeryoptions){#ref-for-dictdef-cachequeryoptions⑧
    link-type="idl"} object, optional

Output

:   a boolean

1.  If
    `options`{.variable}\[\"[`ignoreMethod`{.idl}](#dom-cachequeryoptions-ignoremethod){#ref-for-dom-cachequeryoptions-ignoremethod
    link-type="idl"}\"\] is false and `request`{.variable}'s
    [method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-method){#ref-for-concept-request-method⑧
    link-type="dfn"} is not \``GET`\`, return false.

2.  Let `queryURL`{.variable} be `requestQuery`{.variable}'s
    [url](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-url){#ref-for-concept-request-url①①
    link-type="dfn"}.

3.  Let `cachedURL`{.variable} be `request`{.variable}'s
    [url](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-url){#ref-for-concept-request-url①②
    link-type="dfn"}.

4.  If
    `options`{.variable}\[\"[`ignoreSearch`{.idl}](#dom-cachequeryoptions-ignoresearch){#ref-for-dom-cachequeryoptions-ignoresearch
    link-type="idl"}\"\] is true, then:

    1.  Set `cachedURL`{.variable}'s
        [query](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-query){#ref-for-concept-url-query
        link-type="dfn"} to the empty string.

    2.  Set `queryURL`{.variable}'s
        [query](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-query){#ref-for-concept-url-query①
        link-type="dfn"} to the empty string.

5.  If `queryURL`{.variable} does not
    [equal](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-equals){#ref-for-concept-url-equals②
    link-type="dfn"} `cachedURL`{.variable} with the *exclude fragment
    flag* set, then return false.

6.  If `response`{.variable} is null,
    `options`{.variable}\[\"[`ignoreVary`{.idl}](#dom-cachequeryoptions-ignorevary){#ref-for-dom-cachequeryoptions-ignorevary
    link-type="idl"}\"\] is true, or `response`{.variable}'s [header
    list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-header-list){#ref-for-concept-response-header-list⑤
    link-type="dfn"} does not
    [contain](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#header-list-contains){#ref-for-header-list-contains
    link-type="dfn"} \``Vary`\`, then return true.

7.  Let `fieldValues`{.variable} be the
    [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list){#ref-for-list②⑤
    link-type="dfn"} containing the elements corresponding to the
    [field-values](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc7230#section-3.2){#ref-for-section-3.2②
    link-type="dfn"} of the
    [Vary](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc7231#section-7.1.4){#ref-for-section-7.1.4②
    link-type="dfn"} header for the
    [value](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-value){#ref-for-concept-header-value①
    link-type="dfn"} of the
    [header](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header){#ref-for-concept-header③
    link-type="dfn"} with
    [name](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-name){#ref-for-concept-header-name③
    link-type="dfn"} \``Vary`\`.

8.  For each `fieldValue`{.variable} in `fieldValues`{.variable}:

    1.  If `fieldValue`{.variable} matches \"`*`\", or the [combined
        value](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-list-combine){#ref-for-concept-header-list-combine
        link-type="dfn"} given `fieldValue`{.variable} and
        `request`{.variable}'s [header
        list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-header-list){#ref-for-concept-request-header-list②
        link-type="dfn"} does not match the [combined
        value](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-list-combine){#ref-for-concept-header-list-combine①
        link-type="dfn"} given `fieldValue`{.variable} and
        `requestQuery`{.variable}'s [header
        list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-header-list){#ref-for-concept-request-header-list③
        link-type="dfn"}, then return false.

9.  Return true.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="Batch Cache Operations"}
### [[Batch Cache Operations]{#batch-cache-operations .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}]{.content}[](#batch-cache-operations-algorithm){.self-link} {#batch-cache-operations-algorithm .heading .settled}

Input

:   `operations`{.variable}, a
    [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list){#ref-for-list②⑥
    link-type="dfn"} of [cache batch
    operation](#dfn-cache-batch-operation){#ref-for-dfn-cache-batch-operation③
    link-type="dfn"} objects

Output

:   `resultList`{.variable}, a [request response
    list](#dfn-request-response-list){#ref-for-dfn-request-response-list⑧
    link-type="dfn"}

1.  Let `cache`{.variable} be the [relevant request response
    list](#dfn-relevant-request-response-list){#ref-for-dfn-relevant-request-response-list③
    link-type="dfn"}.

2.  Let `backupCache`{.variable} be a new [request response
    list](#dfn-request-response-list){#ref-for-dfn-request-response-list⑨
    link-type="dfn"} that is a copy of `cache`{.variable}.

3.  Let `addedItems`{.variable} be an empty
    [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list){#ref-for-list②⑦
    link-type="dfn"}.

4.  Try running the following substeps atomically:

    1.  Let `resultList`{.variable} be an empty
        [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list){#ref-for-list②⑧
        link-type="dfn"}.

    2.  [For
        each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate){#ref-for-list-iterate②②
        link-type="dfn"} `operation`{.variable} in
        `operations`{.variable}:

        1.  If `operation`{.variable}'s
            [type](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-type){#ref-for-dfn-cache-batch-operation-type③
            link-type="dfn"} matches neither \"`delete`\" nor \"`put`\",
            [throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw){#ref-for-dfn-throw①④
            link-type="dfn"} a `TypeError`.

        2.  If `operation`{.variable}'s
            [type](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-type){#ref-for-dfn-cache-batch-operation-type④
            link-type="dfn"} matches \"`delete`\" and
            `operation`{.variable}'s
            [response](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-response){#ref-for-dfn-cache-batch-operation-response②
            link-type="dfn"} is not null,
            [throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw){#ref-for-dfn-throw①⑤
            link-type="dfn"} a `TypeError`.

        3.  If the result of running [Query
            Cache](#query-cache){#ref-for-query-cache③ link-type="dfn"}
            with `operation`{.variable}'s
            [request](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-request){#ref-for-dfn-cache-batch-operation-request③
            link-type="dfn"}, `operation`{.variable}'s
            [options](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-options){#ref-for-dfn-cache-batch-operation-options①
            link-type="dfn"}, and `addedItems`{.variable} [is not
            empty](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-is-empty){#ref-for-list-is-empty④
            link-type="dfn"},
            [throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw){#ref-for-dfn-throw①⑥
            link-type="dfn"} an
            \"[`InvalidStateError`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#invalidstateerror){#ref-for-invalidstateerror①⓪
            link-type="idl"}\"
            [`DOMException`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException){#ref-for-idl-DOMException②⑤
            link-type="idl"}.

        4.  Let `requestResponses`{.variable} be an empty
            [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list){#ref-for-list②⑨
            link-type="dfn"}.

        5.  If `operation`{.variable}'s
            [type](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-type){#ref-for-dfn-cache-batch-operation-type⑤
            link-type="dfn"} matches \"`delete`\", then:

            1.  Set `requestResponses`{.variable} to the result of
                running [Query
                Cache](#query-cache){#ref-for-query-cache④
                link-type="dfn"} with `operation`{.variable}'s
                [request](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-request){#ref-for-dfn-cache-batch-operation-request④
                link-type="dfn"} and `operation`{.variable}'s
                [options](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-options){#ref-for-dfn-cache-batch-operation-options②
                link-type="dfn"}.

            2.  [For
                each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate){#ref-for-list-iterate②③
                link-type="dfn"} `requestResponse`{.variable} in
                `requestResponses`{.variable}:

                1.  [Remove](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-remove){#ref-for-list-remove②
                    link-type="dfn"} the
                    [item](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-item){#ref-for-list-item①①
                    link-type="dfn"} whose value matches
                    `requestResponse`{.variable} from
                    `cache`{.variable}.

        6.  Else if `operation`{.variable}'s
            [type](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-type){#ref-for-dfn-cache-batch-operation-type⑥
            link-type="dfn"} matches \"`put`\", then:

            1.  If `operation`{.variable}'s
                [response](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-response){#ref-for-dfn-cache-batch-operation-response③
                link-type="dfn"} is null,
                [throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw){#ref-for-dfn-throw①⑦
                link-type="dfn"} a `TypeError`.

            2.  Let `r`{.variable} be `operation`{.variable}'s
                [request](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-request){#ref-for-dfn-cache-batch-operation-request⑤
                link-type="dfn"}'s associated
                [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request){#ref-for-concept-request-request①⓪
                link-type="dfn"}.

            3.  If `r`{.variable}'s
                [url](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-url){#ref-for-concept-request-url①③
                link-type="dfn"}'s
                [scheme](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-scheme){#ref-for-concept-url-scheme⑤
                link-type="dfn"} is not one of \"`http`\" and
                \"`https`\",
                [throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw){#ref-for-dfn-throw①⑧
                link-type="dfn"} a `TypeError`.

            4.  If `r`{.variable}'s
                [method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-method){#ref-for-concept-request-method⑨
                link-type="dfn"} is not \``GET`\`,
                [throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw){#ref-for-dfn-throw①⑨
                link-type="dfn"} a `TypeError`.

            5.  If `operation`{.variable}'s
                [options](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-options){#ref-for-dfn-cache-batch-operation-options③
                link-type="dfn"} is not null,
                [throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw){#ref-for-dfn-throw②⓪
                link-type="dfn"} a `TypeError`.

            6.  Set `requestResponses`{.variable} to the result of
                running [Query
                Cache](#query-cache){#ref-for-query-cache⑤
                link-type="dfn"} with `operation`{.variable}'s
                [request](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-request){#ref-for-dfn-cache-batch-operation-request⑥
                link-type="dfn"}.

            7.  [For
                each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate){#ref-for-list-iterate②④
                link-type="dfn"} `requestResponse`{.variable} of
                `requestResponses`{.variable}:

                1.  [Remove](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-remove){#ref-for-list-remove③
                    link-type="dfn"} the
                    [item](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-item){#ref-for-list-item①②
                    link-type="dfn"} whose value matches
                    `requestResponse`{.variable} from
                    `cache`{.variable}.

            8.  [Append](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-append){#ref-for-list-append⑨
                link-type="dfn"} `operation`{.variable}'s
                [request](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-request){#ref-for-dfn-cache-batch-operation-request⑦
                link-type="dfn"}/`operation`{.variable}'s
                [response](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-response){#ref-for-dfn-cache-batch-operation-response④
                link-type="dfn"} to `cache`{.variable}.

            9.  If the cache write operation in the previous two steps
                failed due to exceeding the granted quota limit,
                [throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw){#ref-for-dfn-throw②①
                link-type="dfn"} a
                [`QuotaExceededError`{.idl}](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#quotaexceedederror){#ref-for-quotaexceedederror①
                link-type="idl"}.

            10. [Append](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-append){#ref-for-list-append①⓪
                link-type="dfn"} `operation`{.variable}'s
                [request](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-request){#ref-for-dfn-cache-batch-operation-request⑧
                link-type="dfn"}/`operation`{.variable}'s
                [response](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-response){#ref-for-dfn-cache-batch-operation-response⑤
                link-type="dfn"} to `addedItems`{.variable}.

        7.  [Append](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-append){#ref-for-list-append①①
            link-type="dfn"} `operation`{.variable}'s
            [request](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-request){#ref-for-dfn-cache-batch-operation-request⑨
            link-type="dfn"}/`operation`{.variable}'s
            [response](#dfn-cache-batch-operation-response){#ref-for-dfn-cache-batch-operation-response⑥
            link-type="dfn"} to `resultList`{.variable}.

    3.  Return `resultList`{.variable}.

5.  And then, if an exception was
    [thrown](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw){#ref-for-dfn-throw②②
    link-type="dfn"}, then:

    1.  [Remove](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-remove){#ref-for-list-remove④
        link-type="dfn"} all the
        [items](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-item){#ref-for-list-item①③
        link-type="dfn"} from the [relevant request response
        list](#dfn-relevant-request-response-list){#ref-for-dfn-relevant-request-response-list④
        link-type="dfn"}.

    2.  [For
        each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate){#ref-for-list-iterate②⑤
        link-type="dfn"} `requestResponse`{.variable} of
        `backupCache`{.variable}:

        1.  [Append](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-append){#ref-for-list-append①②
            link-type="dfn"} `requestResponse`{.variable} to the
            [relevant request response
            list](#dfn-relevant-request-response-list){#ref-for-dfn-relevant-request-response-list⑤
            link-type="dfn"}.

    3.  [Throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw){#ref-for-dfn-throw②③
        link-type="dfn"} the exception.

    [Note:]{.marker} When an exception is
    [thrown](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw){#ref-for-dfn-throw②④
    link-type="dfn"}, the implementation does undo (roll back) any
    changes made to the cache storage during the batch operation job.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="Is Async Module"}
### [[Is Async Module]{#is-async-module .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}]{.content}[](#is-async-module-algorithm){.self-link} {#is-async-module-algorithm .heading .settled}

Input

:   `record`{.variable}, a [Module
    Record](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-abstract-module-records){#ref-for-sec-abstract-module-records
    link-type="dfn"}

:   `moduleMap`{.variable}, a [module
    map](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#module-map){#ref-for-module-map
    link-type="dfn"}

:   `base`{.variable}, a
    [URL](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url){#ref-for-concept-url①⑥
    link-type="dfn"}

:   `seen`{.variable}, a
    [set](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ordered-set){#ref-for-ordered-set⑦
    link-type="dfn"} of
    [URLs](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url){#ref-for-concept-url①⑦
    link-type="dfn"}

Output

:   a boolean

1.  If `record`{.variable} is not a [Cyclic Module
    Record](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-cyclic-module-records){#ref-for-sec-cyclic-module-records
    link-type="dfn"}, then:

    1.  Return false.

2.  If `record`{.variable}.\[\[Async\]\] is true, then:

    1.  Return true.

3.  [For
    each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate){#ref-for-list-iterate②⑥
    link-type="dfn"} string `requested`{.variable} of
    `record`{.variable}.\[\[RequestedModules\]\]:

    1.  Let `url`{.variable} be the result of [resolving a module
        specifier](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#resolve-a-module-specifier){#ref-for-resolve-a-module-specifier
        link-type="dfn"} given `base`{.variable} and
        `requested`{.variable}.

    2.  Assert: `url`{.variable} is never failure, because [resolving a
        module
        specifier](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#resolve-a-module-specifier){#ref-for-resolve-a-module-specifier①
        link-type="dfn"} must have been previously successful with these
        same two arguments.

    3.  If `seen`{.variable} does not
        [contain](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-contain){#ref-for-list-contain⑤
        link-type="dfn"} `url`{.variable}, then:

        1.  [Append](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#set-append){#ref-for-set-append④
            link-type="dfn"} `url`{.variable} to `seen`{.variable}.

        2.  If `moduleMap`{.variable}\[`url`{.variable}\] does not have
            a
            [record](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-script-record){#ref-for-concept-script-record①
            link-type="dfn"}, then:

            1.  Return false.

        3.  If [Is Async
            Module](#is-async-module){#ref-for-is-async-module①
            link-type="dfn"} for
            `moduleMap`{.variable}\[`url`{.variable}\]\'s
            [record](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-script-record){#ref-for-concept-script-record②
            link-type="dfn"}, `moduleMap`{.variable}, `base`{.variable},
            and `seen`{.variable} is true, then:

            1.  Return true.

4.  Return false.
:::

::: {.section .algorithm algorithm="Lookup Race Response"}
### [[Lookup Race Response]{#lookup-race-response .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" export=""}]{.content}[](#lookup-race-response-algorithm){.self-link} {#lookup-race-response-algorithm .heading .settled}

Input

:   `request`{.variable}, a
    [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request){#ref-for-concept-request①④
    link-type="dfn"}

Output

:   a
    [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response){#ref-for-concept-response①⑦
    link-type="dfn"} or null

1.  Let `registration`{.variable} be null.

2.  If `request`{.variable} is a [non-subresource
    request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#non-subresource-request){#ref-for-non-subresource-request⑤
    link-type="dfn"}, then:

    1.  If `request`{.variable}'s [reserved
        client](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-reserved-client){#ref-for-concept-request-reserved-client②
        link-type="dfn"} is null, return null.

    2.  Let `storage key`{.variable} be the result of running [obtain a
        storage
        key](https://storage.spec.whatwg.org/#obtain-a-storage-key){#ref-for-obtain-a-storage-key①⓪
        link-type="dfn"} given `request`{.variable}'s [reserved
        client](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-reserved-client){#ref-for-concept-request-reserved-client③
        link-type="dfn"}.

    3.  Set `registration`{.variable} to the result of running [Match
        Service Worker
        Registration](#match-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-match-service-worker-registration⑧
        link-type="dfn"} given `storage key`{.variable} and
        `request`{.variable}'s
        [url](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-url){#ref-for-concept-request-url①④
        link-type="dfn"}.

3.  Else if `request`{.variable} is a [subresource
    request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#subresource-request){#ref-for-subresource-request③
    link-type="dfn"}, then:

    1.  Let `client`{.variable} be `request`{.variable}'s
        [client](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-client){#ref-for-concept-request-client⑤
        link-type="dfn"}.

    2.  If `client`{.variable}'s [active service
        worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker){#ref-for-concept-environment-active-service-worker③⓪
        link-type="dfn"} is null, return null.

    3.  Set `registration`{.variable} to `client`{.variable}'s [active
        service
        worker](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-active-service-worker){#ref-for-concept-environment-active-service-worker③①
        link-type="dfn"}'s [containing service worker
        registration](#dfn-containing-service-worker-registration){#ref-for-dfn-containing-service-worker-registration②⓪
        link-type="dfn"}.

4.  Otherwise, return null.

5.  Let `activeWorker`{.variable} be `registration`{.variable}'s [active
    worker](#dfn-active-worker){#ref-for-dfn-active-worker⑤⑦
    link-type="dfn"}.

6.  Let `map`{.variable} be `activeWorker`{.variable}'s [global
    object](#dfn-service-worker-global-object){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker-global-object②⓪
    link-type="dfn"}'s [race response
    map](#serviceworkerglobalscope-race-response-map){#ref-for-serviceworkerglobalscope-race-response-map③
    link-type="dfn"}.

7.  If `map`{.variable}\[`request`{.variable}\]
    [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists){#ref-for-map-exists②⑦
    link-type="dfn"}, then:

    1.  Let `entry`{.variable} be
        `map`{.variable}\[`request`{.variable}\].

    2.  Wait until `entry`{.variable}'s
        [value](#race-response-value){#ref-for-race-response-value①①
        link-type="dfn"} is not \"`pending`\"

    3.  If `entry`{.variable}'s
        [value](#race-response-value){#ref-for-race-response-value①②
        link-type="dfn"} is
        [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response){#ref-for-concept-response①⑧
        link-type="dfn"}, return `entry`{.variable}'s
        [value](#race-response-value){#ref-for-race-response-value①③
        link-type="dfn"}.

8.  Return null.
:::
::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::

:::::::::: section
## [Appendix B: Extended HTTP headers]{.content}[](#extended-http-headers){.self-link} {#extended-http-headers .heading .settled}

::: section
### [Service Worker Script Request]{.content}[](#service-worker-script-request){.self-link} {#service-worker-script-request .heading .settled}

An HTTP request to
[fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch){#ref-for-concept-fetch①⑦
link-type="dfn"} a [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker①①④
link-type="dfn"}'s [script
resource](#dfn-script-resource){#ref-for-dfn-script-resource⑧
link-type="dfn"} will include the following
[header](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header){#ref-for-concept-header④
link-type="dfn"}:

\`[`Service-Worker`]{#service-worker .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}\`

:   Indicates this request is a [service
    worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker①①⑤
    link-type="dfn"}'s [script
    resource](#dfn-script-resource){#ref-for-dfn-script-resource⑨
    link-type="dfn"} request.

    [Note:]{.marker} This header helps administrators log the requests
    and detect threats.
:::

::::::: section
### [Service Worker Script Response]{.content}[](#service-worker-script-response){.self-link} {#service-worker-script-response .heading .settled}

An HTTP response to a [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker①①⑥
link-type="dfn"}'s [script
resource](#dfn-script-resource){#ref-for-dfn-script-resource①⓪
link-type="dfn"} request can include the following
[header](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header){#ref-for-concept-header⑤
link-type="dfn"}:

\`[`Service-Worker-Allowed`]{#service-worker-allowed .dfn .dfn-paneled dfn-type="dfn" noexport=""}\`

:   Indicates the user agent will override the path restriction, which
    limits the maximum allowed [scope
    url](#dfn-scope-url){#ref-for-dfn-scope-url①⑨ link-type="dfn"} that
    the script can [control](#dfn-control){#ref-for-dfn-control③
    link-type="dfn"}, to the given value.

    [Note:]{.marker} The value is a URL. If a relative URL is given, it
    is parsed against the script's URL.

::: {#example-3c0435b3 .example}
[](#example-3c0435b3){.self-link} Default scope:

``` highlight
// Maximum allowed scope defaults to the path the script sits in
// "/js/" in this example
navigator.serviceWorker.register("/js/sw.js").then(() => {
  console.log("Install succeeded with the default scope '/js/'.");
});
```
:::

::: {#example-7c01199a .example}
[](#example-7c01199a){.self-link} Upper path without
Service-Worker-Allowed header:

``` highlight
// Set the scope to an upper path of the script location
// Response has no Service-Worker-Allowed header
navigator.serviceWorker.register("/js/sw.js", { scope: "/" }).catch(() => {
  console.error("Install failed due to the path restriction violation.");
});
```
:::

::: {#example-4ac10046 .example}
[](#example-4ac10046){.self-link} Upper path with Service-Worker-Allowed
header:

``` highlight
// Set the scope to an upper path of the script location
// Response included "Service-Worker-Allowed : /"
navigator.serviceWorker.register("/js/sw.js", { scope: "/" }).then(() => {
  console.log("Install succeeded as the max allowed scope was overriden to '/'.");
});
```
:::

::: {#example-e39a8279 .example}
[](#example-e39a8279){.self-link} A path restriction voliation even with
Service-Worker-Allowed header:

``` highlight
// Set the scope to an upper path of the script location
// Response included "Service-Worker-Allowed : /foo"
navigator.serviceWorker.register("/foo/bar/sw.js", { scope: "/" }).catch(() => {
  console.error("Install failed as the scope is still out of the overriden maximum allowed scope.");
});
```
:::
:::::::

::: section
### [Syntax]{.content}[](#syntax){.self-link} {#syntax .heading .settled}

[ABNF](#biblio-rfc5234 "Augmented BNF for Syntax Specifications: ABNF"){link-type="biblio"}
for the values of the headers used by the [service
worker](#dfn-service-worker){#ref-for-dfn-service-worker①①⑦
link-type="dfn"}'s [script
resource](#dfn-script-resource){#ref-for-dfn-script-resource①①
link-type="dfn"} requests and responses:

    Service-Worker = %x73.63.72.69.70.74 ; "script", case-sensitive

[Note:]{.marker} The validation of the Service-Worker-Allowed header's
values is done by URL parsing algorithm (in Update algorithm) instead of
using ABNF.
:::
::::::::::

::: section
## [8. ]{.secno}[Acknowledgements]{.content}[](#acknowledgements){.self-link} {#acknowledgements .heading .settled level="8"}

Deep thanks go to Andrew Betts for organizing and hosting a small
workshop of like-minded individuals including: Jake Archibald, Jackson
Gabbard, Tobie Langel, Robin Berjon, Patrick Lauke, Christian Heilmann.
From the clarity of the day's discussions and the use-cases outlined
there, much has become possible. Further thanks to Andrew for raising
consciousness about the offline problem. His organization of EdgeConf
and inclusion of Offline as a persistent topic there has created many
opportunities and connections that have enabled this work to progress.

Anne van Kesteren has generously lent his encyclopedic knowledge of Web
Platform arcana and standards development experience throughout the
development of the service worker. This specification would be
incomplete without his previous work in describing the real-world
behavior of URLs, HTTP Fetch, Promises, and DOM. Similarly, this
specification would not be possible without Ian Hickson's rigorous Web
Worker spec. Much thanks to him.

In no particular order, deep gratitude for design guidance and
discussion goes to: Jungkee Song, Alec Flett, David Barrett-Kahn, Aaron
Boodman, Michael Nordman, Tom Ashworth, Kinuko Yasuda, Darin Fisher,
Jonas Sicking, Jesús Leganés Combarro, Mark Christian, Dave Hermann,
Yehuda Katz, François Remy, Ilya Grigorik, Will Chan, Domenic Denicola,
Nikhil Marathe, Yves Lafon, Adam Barth, Greg Simon, Devdatta Akhawe,
Dominic Cooney, Jeffrey Yasskin, Joshua Bell, Boris Zbarsky, Matt
Falkenhagen, Tobie Langel, Gavin Peters, Ben Kelly, Hiroki Nakagawa,
Jake Archibald, Josh Soref, Jinho Bang, Yutaka Hirano, Michael(tm)
Smith, isonmad, Ali Alabbas, Philip Jägenstedt, Mike Pennisi, and Eric
Willigers.

Jason Weber, Chris Wilson, Paul Kinlan, Ehsan Akhgari, and Daniel Austin
have provided valuable, well-timed feedback on requirements and the
standardization process.

The authors would also like to thank Dimitri Glazkov for his scripts and
formatting tools which have been essential in the production of this
specification. The authors are also grateful for his considerable
guidance.

Thanks also to Vivian Cromwell, Greg Simon, Alex Komoroske, Wonsuk Lee,
and Seojin Kim for their considerable professional support.
:::
